012025-10-27

止血作用Hemostasis

止血作用在肿瘤发生中的作用 IRole of Hemostasis in Carcinogenesis

一、止血定义与功能 Hemostasis: Definition and Function

止血是维持血流的过程，通过精细调节以防止（1）失血和（2）血管堵塞。

Hemostasis is the process involved in the maintenance of blood flow, which is finely regulated to prevent (i) blood loss and (ii) vessel occlusion.

止血需保持平衡，既要防止出血，也要防止血栓形成和血管堵塞。

Maintaining hemostatic balance is essential to both avoid bleeding and to prevent thrombosis and vessel obstruction.

二、止血复杂性的核心 Hemostasis: An Irreducible Complexity

止血是时空高度同步的连续事件。

Hemostasis consists of sequential events that are perfectly synchronized in time and space.

止血参与者包括：

血管壁（内皮细胞及其下方结构）

血细胞（主要是血小板）

血浆蛋白（凝血因子）

The key players in hemostasis include:

Vessel wall (endothelial cells and the sub-endothelial space)

Blood cells (mainly platelets)

Plasma proteins (coagulation factors)

老师强调，多个环节和组分共同参与，每一步严格协调，不能早也不能晚，确保只在损伤时形成血块，既不提前也不滞后。

The teacher emphasized that many components and steps are involved in hemostasis, all rigorously orchestrated, ensuring that clots form only at the right time—namely during injury, not before or after.

三、止血的三个阶段 Three Phases of Hemostasis

止血主要分为三个阶段，时间顺序和事件协同推进，但实际中部分环节会重叠进行。

Hemostasis comprises three major phases, which proceed sequentially and overlap in practice.

1
初级止血 Primary Hemostasis

血小板被激活和聚集，形成最初的血小板血栓，3-5 分钟完成。

Platelet activation and aggregation lead to initial platelet plug formation, completed within 3–5 minutes.

2
凝血 Coagulation

一系列酶促反应，生成纤维蛋白以加固血小板血栓，所需时间约为5-10分钟。

A cascade of enzymatic reactions leads to fibrin formation, stabilizing the platelet plug over 5–10 minutes.

3
纤溶 Fibrinolysis

通过酶促反应降解纤维蛋白，把已形成血栓最终消除，过程持续48–72小时。

Fibrinolysis, also an enzymatic cascade, degrades fibrin and removes the clot, generally over 48–72 hours.

老师补充，纤溶只会在愈合基本完成后才启动，和修复同步，避免过早溶解导致再次出血。

The instructor added fibrinolysis is strictly timed to begin only after healing is mostly complete, thus ensuring clot removal won’t cause re-bleeding.

四、初级止血的要素及流程 Key Points of Primary Hemostasis

什么（WHAT）：

血小板血栓形成，足够稳定以止血。

Formation of a platelet plug, stable enough to stop blood loss.

哪里（WHERE）：

在损伤部位，血管腔内，并锚定于血管壁上。

At the injury site, in the vessel lumen, anchored to the vessel wall.

谁参与（WHO）：

血管壁、细胞外基质黏附蛋白、血浆蛋白以及血小板。

Vessel wall, adhesive proteins in the extracellular matrix, plasma proteins, and platelets.

过程（HOW）：

1
损伤部位发生后，血管收缩，减少血流。
2
血小板粘附于暴露的基质成分。
3
血小板被激活并释放信号分子，进一步吸引并激活更多血小板。
4
聚集的血小板形成堵塞小孔的“血小板血栓”。
1
After injury, the vessel constricts to limit blood flow.
2
Platelets adhere to revealed sub-endothelial components.
3
Activated platelets release signals that recruit and activate more platelets.
4
Aggregated platelets plug the hole, forming a platelet plug.

老师强调，纯血小板血栓易受血流冲刷，必须有纤维蛋白网格加固。

The teacher emphasized that the initial platelet plug is fragile and must be stabilized by a fibrin mesh.

五、图片/图示补充说明 Illustration Supplement

页面图片显示出血时血管壁破裂时流血、血小板堵孔后止血、以及血管堵塞形成血栓等状态。

The images show the states of vessel rupture with bleeding, platelet plug formation arresting bleeding, and vessel blockage by thrombosis.

平衡强调止血需兼顾防止失血与防止血管堵塞，偏离都会造成严重后果。

The balance illustration underscores that hemostasis must prevent both bleeding and vessel occlusion, as deviations in either direction have serious consequences.

六、老师讲解补充内容 Teacher’s Additional Points

止血涉及的三个阶段根据受伤部位和血管大小具体进程会有所差异，静脉和动脉、大小血管愈合时间不同。

The three phases of hemostasis vary in duration and detail depending on the injury site and vessel size; arteries, veins, and vessel calibers heal at different rates.

所有反应必须精确协同，否则可能导致过度出血或异常血栓。

All these steps must coordinate precisely, or it may result in excessive bleeding or abnormal clotting.

初级止血（系列PPT）Primary Hemostasis (PPT Series)

1
完整血管壁结构及功能 Intact Vessel Wall Structure and Function
血管壁由三层结构组成
平滑肌层、细胞外基质（胶原纤维）、内皮层（内皮细胞）。

The vessel wall has three layers: layer of smooth muscle cells, extracellular matrix (collagen fibers), and endothelium (endothelial cells).

红细胞（RBC）和血小板（platelets）处于血管腔内。

Red blood cells (RBCs) and platelets are inside the vessel lumen.

正常内皮细胞持续释放前列环素和一氧化氮以抑制血小板激活，维持血管开放。

Endothelial cells constantly release prostacyclin and nitric oxide to inhibit platelet activation and keep vessels open.

老师强调，内皮层是唯一完整覆盖血管腔的细胞层，起到屏障作用，防止血液直接接触下方胶原。

The teacher highlighted that the endothelium forms a continuous barrier separating blood from the underlying collagen.

2
血管损伤后的早期反应 Early Response to Vascular Injury

血管损伤导致内皮连续性破裂，暴露出细胞外基质（以胶原为主）。

Vascular injury breaks the continuity of the endothelium, exposing the extracellular matrix (mainly collagen).

损伤区的内皮细胞丢失，血液与基质直接接触。

At the injury site, endothelial cells are lost and blood comes into direct contact with the matrix.

此时前列环素和一氧化氮的释放减少，局部血小板抑制作用减弱。

At this point, prostacyclin and nitric oxide release decrease, reducing local inhibition on platelet activation.

老师补充，受损区域血管出现短暂收缩，有助于减少出血并为后续止血反应创造条件。

The teacher added that the injured site undergoes transient vasoconstriction, helping limit blood loss and facilitate ensuing hemostatic responses.

3
血小板与胶原的接触及激活 Platelet Contact with Collagen and Activation

损伤后血液中的血小板与暴露的胶原直接接触，从休眠状态转变为激活状态。

After injury, circulating platelets contact exposed collagen, transforming from a resting to an activated state.

激活前，血小板呈扁平状，激活后迅速变圆并形成伪足。

Prior to activation, platelets are flat; upon activation, they round up rapidly and extend pseudopods.

胶原是强有力的血小板激活因子，直接介导这一形态和功能变化。

Collagen acts as a powerful platelet activator, directly mediating these shape and functional changes.

老师讲到，内皮层损伤越大，胶原暴露就越多，血小板激活越明显。

The teacher noted that the larger the endothelial damage, the more collagen exposure and the stronger the platelet activation.

4
Von Willebrand因子作用及血小板粘附 Von Willebrand Factor's Role and Platelet Adhesion

Von Willebrand因子（vWF）由内皮细胞分泌，存在于血浆，可结合于胶原或血小板，促进血小板粘附到血管壁。

Von Willebrand Factor (vWF) is secreted by endothelial cells and present in plasma; it binds to collagen or platelets and promotes platelet adhesion to the vessel wall.

vWF的作用是初期弱粘附，后续被胶原介导的强粘附所增强。

vWF mediates an initial weak adhesion, which is then reinforced by collagen-mediated strong adhesion.

在损伤部位，vWF帮助血小板牢牢附着于暴露的胶原。

At the injury site, vWF helps platelets firmly adhere to exposed collagen.

老师特别提出，vWF对初级止血至关重要，缺乏时可导致止血障碍。

The teacher specifically highlighted that vWF is vital for primary hemostasis and its deficiency can cause bleeding disorders.

5
血小板聚集和血小板血栓形成 Platelet Aggregation and Platelet Plug Formation

激活的血小板释放信号分子（如ADP、血清素、Ca2+），吸引更多血小板聚集。

Activated platelets secrete signaling molecules (such as ADP, serotonin, Ca2+), recruiting more platelets to aggregate.

血小板通过表面受体与纤维蛋白原形成连接，建立血小板间的“桥梁”，最终封堵破口，形成血小板血栓。

Platelets connect through surface receptors and fibrinogen to form bridges, eventually plugging the breach with a platelet plug.

电镜图示证实，正常血小板为圆盘状，激活后变为刺突形，最终形成致密的血小板团块。

Electron micrographs confirm that normal platelets are disc-shaped, become spiky upon activation, and ultimately form dense platelet clumps.

6
总结和机制要点 Summary and Key Mechanism Points

初级止血是由血小板识别和修复血管损伤引发的一系列快速反应，其核心步骤包括血小板的激活、粘附及聚集，最终生成初步止血栓。

Primary hemostasis is a series of rapid responses initiated by platelets recognizing and repairing vascular injury, with key steps including activation, adhesion, and aggregation, ultimately resulting in the initial hemostatic plug.

所有流程均依赖内皮完整性与局部信号调控，任何环节异常都会导致止血功能障碍或异常血栓。

All these processes depend on endothelial integrity and local signal regulation; abnormalities at any step can lead to impaired hemostasis or abnormal thrombosis.

老师强调，每一环节有独特分子参与，理解结构-功能关系对判断止血障碍很重要。

The instructor emphasized that each step involves unique molecules, and understanding structure-function relationships is key for assessing hemostatic disorders.

初级止血Primary Hemostasis

内皮损伤的结果
血管收缩与血小板血栓形成

Endothelium injury consequences: vasoconstriction and platelet plug formation

内皮损伤后，首先导致血管收缩，其次诱发血小板血栓形成。

When endothelium is injured, it first causes vasoconstriction and then triggers platelet plug formation.

2
正常完整血管壁的分子机制

Molecular mechanisms of intact vessel wall

正常情况下，内皮细胞　持续分泌一氧化氮与前列环素。
答案 CE
Under normal conditions, endothelial cells 　 constantly secrete nitric oxide and prostacyclin.
答案 CE

这些分子维持血管舒张，并抑制血小板激活。

These molecules maintain vasodilation and prevent platelet activation.

老师补充，持续的血管舒张和血小板静止有利于血液流动、防止不必要的血栓。

The lecturer added that continuous vasodilation and platelet quiescence are essential for smooth blood flow and preventing unnecessary thrombosis.

3
损伤后血管收缩机制

Mechanisms of vasoconstriction after injury

内皮细胞受损时，失去对一氧化氮与前列环素的分泌，同时激活内皮细胞分泌血管收缩素（内皮素）。

When endothelial cells are injured, they lose secretion of nitric oxide and prostacyclin, while activated ECs start releasing endothelin (a vasoconstrictor).

内皮素作用于血管平滑肌，引起局部血管平滑肌细胞收缩，使受损部位血管快速收缩。

Endothelin acts on vascular smooth muscle, causing rapid contraction at the injury site.

老师强调这一步骤的目的是短暂减少局部血流，为止血创造条件。

The lecturer emphasized this step transiently reduces local blood flow, creating a window for efficient hemostasis.

4
血小板血栓的形成与三步过程

Formation of the platelet plug: the three-step process

血小板血栓形成分为三个阶段
1. 粘附 2. 激活 3. 聚集。

Platelet plug formation proceeds in three stages: 1. Adhesion 2. Activation 3. Aggregation.

血液中的静息态血小板在接触损伤外暴露的胶原后，先粘附于损伤区。

Resting platelets in the blood adhere to the exposed collagen at the injury site.

粘附后迅速被激活，发生形态改变（如伸出伪足），并开始释放活化信号。

After adhesion, they are rapidly activated, change shape (form pseudopods), and begin releasing activation signals.

激活的血小板通过表面受体彼此连接，聚集成血小板团块，最终封堵破口，形成血小板血栓。

Activated platelets link together via surface receptors to form a platelet mass, ultimately sealing the breach and forming a platelet plug.

老师明确讲到，胶原是血小板激活的强大信号，一旦血小板接触胶原，这一串反应迅速启动。

The lecturer pointed out that collagen is a potent activator for platelets, and this cascade is rapidly triggered upon contact.

5
机制总结与调控意义

Summary of mechanisms and significance of regulation

血管正常完整时，防止血小板激活与血管收缩，保护血液平稳流动。

When vessels are intact, platelet activation and vasoconstriction are inhibited, ensuring smooth blood flow.

损伤时，局部血管迅速收缩并激活血小板，为有效止血创造基础。

Upon injury, local vasoconstriction and rapid platelet activation set up the foundation for effective hemostasis.

所有步骤高度有序、时空协作，保证只在需要的部位和时间形成血栓，防止过度或异常止血反应。

All processes are highly ordered and coordinated in time and space, ensuring clot formation only where and when needed, and preventing excessive or inappropriate hemostatic responses.

初级止血过程Primary Hemostasis Process

1
血小板血栓=三步过程 Platelet plug = 3 step process
步骤一
粘附 Step 1: Adhesion
步骤二
激活（细胞骨架重组、分泌）Step 2: Activation (cytoskeleton remodeling, secretion)
步骤三
聚集 Step 3: Aggregation

【一】粘附 Adhesion

静息态血小板通过血管内皮暴露的胶原粘附于损伤部位。 Resting platelets adhere to the injury site via collagen exposed by vascular endothelial damage.

von Willebrand因子（vWF）由内皮细胞分泌，游离于血浆。 Von Willebrand factor (vWF) is secreted by endothelial cells and circulates in plasma.

血流剪切力使球状vWF展开延长，vWF与胶原结合后暴露与血小板GPIb的结合位点（初始弱结合）。 Shear stress causes globular vWF to unfold and elongate; upon binding to collagen, vWF exposes a binding site for platelet GPIb (initial weak interaction).

血小板与胶原的初步粘附后，诱导GPVI受体表达，GPVI介导血小板与胶原的牢固结合。 After the initial platelet-collagen attachment, GPVI expression is induced on platelets, mediating firm binding to collagen.

【二】激活（细胞骨架重组、分泌）Activation (cytoskeleton remodeling, secretion)

血小板受到粘附后的信号刺激，激活胞内信号通路。 Platelets are stimulated by adhesion-triggered signals, activating intracellular signaling pathways.

血小板内部F-肌动蛋白重排，形态由扁平变为球形并产生伪足。 F-actin in platelets rearranges, changing their shape from flat to round with pseudopods.

激活的血小板释放内含物，即颗粒（α颗粒和致密颗粒）进入血浆。 Activated platelets release their contents—α-granules and dense granules—into the plasma.

α颗粒
vWF、纤维蛋白原、血小板因子4；致密颗粒：ADP、5-羟色胺（Serotonin）、钙离子。 α-granules: vWF, fibrinogen, platelet factor 4; dense granules: ADP, serotonin, calcium ions.

分泌内容物具有旁分泌和自分泌作用，即激活其他血小板或自我放大激活信号。 Granule contents act in paracrine and autocrine fashion: activating other platelets or amplifying the activation of the same platelet.

【三】ADP途径及血小板放大反馈 ADP pathway and platelet amplification feedback

ADP能与血小板表面受体P2Y12和P2Y1结合，激活信号通路，进一步促血小板激活。 ADP binds to platelet surface receptors P2Y12 and P2Y1, activating signaling pathways and further promoting platelet activation.

颗粒分泌的ADP通过旁分泌和自分泌方式，激活周围或自身血小板，产生同步放大效应。 Granule-secreted ADP activates surrounding or self platelets via paracrine or autocrine mechanisms, producing a synchronized amplification effect.

【四】翻转与TXA2释放 Flip-Flop and TXA2 secretion

激活的血小板外膜发生磷脂“翻转”，使磷脂酰丝氨酸暴露于外侧，提供促凝表面。 Activated platelets undergo membrane "flip-flop", exposing phosphatidylserine on the outer layer, providing a procoagulant surface.

同时细胞膜磷脂在磷脂酶A2作用下，产生花生四烯酸，经COX（环氧化酶）和TX合酶生成血栓烷A2（TXA2）。 At the same time, membrane phospholipids are converted into arachidonic acid by phospholipase A2, which, via COX (cyclo-oxygenase) and TX synthase, produces thromboxane A2 (TXA2).

TXA2通过被动扩散出胞，结合血小板表面TP受体，进一步促进血小板激活及正反馈放大。 TXA2 passively diffuses out of the cell, binds to the TP receptor on the platelet surface, and further promotes platelet activation and positive feedback amplification.

老师强调COX（环氧化酶）是抗血小板药物作用靶点。 The instructor emphasized that COX (cyclo-oxygenase) is a target of anti-platelet drugs.

【五】聚集 Aggregation

激活的血小板表面暴露GPIIb/IIIa受体，此受体在纤维蛋白原的桥接下，将多个血小板连接成团。 Activated platelets expose their GPIIb/IIIa receptors, which, via fibrinogen bridges, connect multiple platelets into a clump.

血小板聚集完成后，初级止血栓形成，短时间内封堵伤口止血。 After aggregation, the primary hemostatic plug is formed, quickly sealing the wound to stop bleeding.

【六】生理意义与调控 Clinical implication and regulation

初级止血反应时空高度协调，确保止血栓只在需要时、需要处生成，过度或缺陷均可导致疾病。 Primary hemostasis is tightly regulated in time and space to form clots only when and where needed; both excessive an

血液凝固Coagulation

1
凝固的本质与定位 What Is Coagulation and Where Does It Happen

凝固是指纤维蛋白网络的形成，聚合的纤维蛋白形成网状结构，稳固血小板聚集。

Coagulation refers to the formation of a fibrin network; polymerized fibrin forms a mesh to stabilize the platelet aggregate.

这一过程发生于损伤部位的血管腔内，特别是在已被激活的血小板表面。

This process takes place at the site of injury, within the vessel lumen, especially on the surface of activated platelets.

参与者包括凝血因子、血管壁和血小板。

The main participants are coagulation factors, the vessel wall, and platelets.

老师补充，只有这样定位，才能确保血栓形成精准、高效，同时不会在全身广泛发生不必要的凝固。

The lecturer emphasized that such precise localization ensures efficient and targeted clotting without unnecessary systemic coagulation 16.

2
凝固反应分期（阶段解析）Phases of Coagulation (Stage Analysis)

（1）起始阶段（慢阶段）Initiation Phase (Slow)

起始阶段始于内皮连续性破坏，暴露亚内皮细胞于血流。

Initiation begins when endothelial continuity is disrupted and sub-endothelial cells are exposed to blood flow.

组织因子（TF）在血流暴露后成为发挥作用的关键分子。

Tissue Factor (TF) becomes a key molecule when exposed to blood flow.

FVII/FVIIa与TF结合，启动凝血级联反应。

Activated factor VII (FVIIa) binds to TF, triggering the coagulation cascade.

老师强调，正常情况下组织因子从不接触血液，只有损伤后暴露，起始凝血。

The lecturer noted that tissue factor never contacts blood under normal conditions; exposure only occurs upon injury, initiating coagulation 1218.

（2）酶促反应顺序及局部性 Enzymatic Reaction Sequence and Localized Activation

FVIIa-TF复合物依次激活FIX（FIXa）、FX（FXa），然后进一步激活FII（前凝血酶，变为凝血酶FIIa）。

The FVIIa-TF complex sequentially activates factor IX (FIXa) and factor X (FXa), which then activates factor II (prothrombin) into thrombin (FIIa).

所有酶促反应都是缓慢的，且局限于组织因子暴露周围。

All enzymatic reactions proceed slowly and are tightly localized to the region around tissue factor exposure.

这一阶段仅产生痕量凝血酶（FIIa），为后续放大做准备。

This stage produces only trace amounts of thrombin, priming the system for subsequent amplification.

老师特别强调此慢速反应阶段对保障凝血时空精准调控至关重要。

The lecturer stressed that this slow, local reaction phase is vital for precise temporal and spatial control of coagulation 1619.

（3）调节机制 Regulation

TFPI(组织因子途径抑制物)负责负反馈调控，抑制TF-FVIIa复合物活性，从而减缓级联反应速度。

Tissue Factor Pathway Inhibitor (TFPI) provides negative feedback regulation, inhibiting the TF-FVIIa complex and slowing the cascade.

这一机制保证即使有少量因子“漏出”，也不会在全身范围内诱发凝血。

This mechanism ensures that even if some factors "leak," it does not trigger systemic coagulation 1621.

3
酶级联图示与重点 Enzymatic Cascade Illustration and Key Points

图片展示，随着级联反应进行，从TF开始依次激活FVII、FIX、FX，最终生成凝血酶和纤维蛋白。

The diagram shows that as the cascade progresses, TF sequentially activates FVII, FIX, FX, ultimately generating thrombin and fibrin.

SEM实物图可见，激活的血小板与聚合的纤维蛋白密切交织成稳定血栓。

The SEM image demonstrates dense integration of activated platelets and polymerized fibrin forming a stable thrombus.

老师补充，高倍显微运行证实纤维蛋白网是真正实现血栓结构稳定和抗剪切的核心。

The lecturer added that high-magnification imagery confirms the fibrin mesh is the structural core providing clot stability and shear resistance 16.

4
重难点提示 Key Learning Points

凝固“起始-放大”是两阶段，前者慢并局限，后者快并广泛，便于精准启动、快速止血。

Coagulation features "initiation-amplification" phase division: the former is slow and localized, the latter rapid and widespread—allowing precise start and quick hemostasis.

初始微量凝血酶产生为后续大量凝血过程创造条件，空间定位依赖组织因子暴露部位。

Initial trace thrombin production paves the way for massive downstream coagulation, with spatial restriction to the tissue factor–exposed area.

TFPI等调节因子确保反应不过度、不全身扩散。

Regulators like TFPI ensure reaction is not excessive nor body-wide.

凝血酶的结构与功能Coagulation Enzymes Structure and Function

1
合成与前体形式Synthesis and Zymogen Form

所有凝血因子都由肝细胞合成，最初以无活性前体（酶原，zymogen）形式存在。

All coagulation factors are synthesized by hepatocytes and initially exist as inactive precursors (zymogens).

常见凝血酶原包括
FVII、FIX、FX、FII等。

Common coagulation zymogens include: FVII, FIX, FX, and FII.

2
蛋白酶激活与结构变化Protease Activation and Structural Changes

经蛋白水解切割后，酶原转变为具有催化活性的酶（蛋白酶）。

After proteolytic cleavage, zymogens are converted into catalytically active enzymes (proteases).

激活后的酶通常由两条肽链组成（轻链LC和重链HC），通过二硫键连接，部分酶如FIIa含有额外结构域。

The activated enzymes usually consist of two polypeptide chains (light chain LC and heavy chain HC) linked by disulfide bonds; some, like FIIa, contain additional domains.

3
结构组织的共同性Common Structural Organization

所有凝血因子的结构高度相似，均含有蛋白酶结构域，催化底物切割反应。

All coagulation factors share a highly similar structure, each containing a protease domain responsible for catalyzing substrate cleavage.

老师特别指出酶原激活后的结构变化（如“口张开”表示获得活性），图示体现了单链蛋白转变为双链活性酶的过程。

The lecturer emphasized the structural changes upon zymogen activation (the "mouth open" indicates acquired activity), and the diagram illustrates the conversion from single-chain protein to double-chain active enzyme.

4
Gla结构域与生物学意义Gla Domain and Biological Significance

Gla结构域是指谷氨酰残基经翻译后修饰，形成γ-羧基谷氨酸（Gla）。

The Gla domain refers to glutamate residues that are modified post-translationally to form gamma-carboxyglutamic acid (Gla).

该修饰依赖于维生素K，因此称为维生素K依赖性因子（如FII、VII、IX、X）。

This modification depends on vitamin K, and the corresponding factors (e.g., FII, VII, IX, X) are known as vitamin K-dependent factors.

在Ca2+存在下，Gla结构域形成Ω环，暴露疏水基团并提高对脂质双层的亲和力。

In the presence of Ca2+, the Gla domain adopts an omega-loop conformation that exposes hydrophobic residues and increases its affinity for lipid bilayers.

Gla结构域使凝血因子高效锚定于细胞膜表面，为酶促反应提供空间定位。

The Gla domain allows efficient anchoring of clotting factors to the cell membrane, providing spatial localization for enzymatic reactions.

5
Gla结构域的功能与优势Gla Domain Functions and Advantages

Gla结构域促使凝血因子紧密结合在细胞膜表面，实现2D空间（膜表面）分布，而不是3D空间（血浆）分散，提高有效浓度。

The Gla domain promotes tight binding of clotting factors to the membrane surface, achieving 2D (membrane) rather than 3D (plasma) distribution, thus increasing effective local concentration.

这种机制不仅加快局部酶促反应速率，同时限制了酶活性扩散，防止全身性凝固反应。

This mechanism accelerates local enzymatic reaction rates while restricting enzymatic activity, preventing systemic coagulation.

老师强调，维生素K缺乏或Gla结构域缺陷会显著影响凝血功能，是抗凝药（如华法林）的作用靶点。

The lecturer emphasized that vitamin K deficiency or Gla domain defects greatly impair coagulation and are the target of anticoagulants (e.g., warfarin).

6
激活与功能定位Activation and Functional Localization

激活的凝血酶通过Gla结构域锚定于损伤部位的细胞膜，确保凝血反应局限于受损区域，避免“跑偏”。

Activated coagulation enzymes localize to the damaged site membrane via the Gla domain, ensuring reactions are confined to the site of injury, preventing unwanted spread.

这种时空定位机制，是凝血反应高效且安全启动和调控的分子基础。

This spatiotemporal localization provides the molecular basis for the efficient and safe initiation and regulation of coagulation.

凝血酶的结构与激活机制Coagulation Enzymes Structure and Molecular Activation Mechanism

1
凝血因子的多结构域组成Coagulation factors are multi-domain proteins

典型凝血因子（如FVII, FIX, FX, FII）包含以下结构域：Gla结构域、EGF样结构域、以及蛋白酶催化结构域。

Typical coagulation factors (such as FVII, FIX, FX, FII) contain the following domains: Gla domain, EGF-like domains, and a protease catalytic domain.

Gla结构域负责膜结合，依赖于钙离子与磷脂膜作用。

The Gla domain is responsible for membrane binding and requires calcium ions to interact with the phospholipid membrane.

EGF样结构域位于Gla和蛋白酶结构域之间，起到空间间隔作用。

EGF-like domains are located between the Gla and protease domains, acting as spacers.

最末端是蛋白酶结构域，决定了其蛋白切割酶功能。

The terminal part is the protease domain, which determines its proteolytic function.

2
酶原与酶的激活形式Zymogen and enzyme active forms

凝血因子作为酶原（无活性），血浆中以单链蛋白形式存在（FVII, FIX, FX, FII）。

Coagulation factors as zymogens (inactive) are present in plasma as single-chain proteins (FVII, FIX, FX, FII).

经过上游蛋白酶特异性切割后，酶原转变为活性酶，通常由两条链（重链和轻链）构成，通过二硫键连接。

After specific cleavage by upstream proteases, zymogens become active enzymes, usually composed of a heavy and light chain linked by disulfide bonds.

活性酶的重链包含蛋白酶催化结构域，轻链包含Gla和EGF样结构域。

The heavy chain of the active enzyme contains the protease catalytic domain, while the light chain contains the Gla and EGF-like domains.

图示“口张开”表示激活酶获得了蛋白水解活性。

In the diagram, the "open mouth" indicates that the activated enzyme has gained proteolytic activity.

3
EGF样结构域的作用Functions of EGF-like domains

EGF样结构域（EGF-1/EGF-2）因结构上与表皮生长因子（EGF）类似而得名。

EGF-like domains (EGF-1/EGF-2) are so called due to their structural similarity to Epidermal Growth Factor (EGF).

EGF样结构域位于Gla结构域与蛋白酶结构域之间，起到间隔和连接作用，使酶的催化结构域与细胞膜保持最优距离。

EGF-like domains act as spacers between the Gla and catalytic domains, keeping the catalytic domain optimally distanced from the cell membrane.

其β折叠片结构，使蛋白具有紧密核心，但N端和C端具有高度柔性，方便蛋白在膜表面定向、调整空间位置。

Their β-sheet structure ensures a compact core, while the N- and C-terminal regions are highly flexible, facilitating spatial adjustment and orientation on the membrane surface.

这有利于催化结构域高效催化底物，同时保证定位在膜表面。

This favors efficient catalysis of substrates while maintaining precise localization at the membrane surface.

4
分子激活机制Molecular mechanism of catalytic domain activation

酶原激活第一步为蛋白酶切割，产生新的N端。

The first step in zymogen activation is proteolytic cleavage, generating a new N-terminus.

新N端引发蛋白空间构象重排，酶催化活性位点暴露，获得蛋白酶活性。

The new N-terminus triggers conformational rearrangement of the protein, exposing the catalytic site and conferring protease activity.

激活过程包括两个关键步骤
1）蛋白裂解，2）空间构象激活（催化活性中心开放）。

The activation process involves two key steps: 1) proteolytic cleavage, 2) conformational activation (opening of the catalytic center).

老师强调，所有凝血蛋白酶共享类似激活机制，催化结构域均分布于重链。

The instructor emphasized that all coagulation proteases share a similar activation mechanism, and their catalytic domains reside in the heavy chain.

小结Summary

多结构域蛋白设计保障了凝血酶高效、精准定位与调控。

The multi-domain design ensures efficient and precise localization and regulation of coagulation enzymes.

EGF样结构域作为空间支架，保障酶活性结构域最佳暴露，保证酶反应在膜表面顺利进行。

EGF-like domains act as spatial scaffolds, ensuring optimal exposure of the active domain on the membrane surface for efficient catalysis.

酶原→酶转变为特殊蛋白酶活性的分子基础，调控凝血反应时空专一性。

The zymogen-to-enzyme transformation forms the molecular basis for specific protease activity and spatiotemporal control of coagulation.

FVII活化的分子机制Molecular mechanism of FVII activation

1
一般凝血酶原激活机制General mechanism of zymogen activation
大多数凝血酶原的激活包括两个步骤
蛋白水解切割和构象激活。

Activation of most coagulation zymogens involves two steps: proteolytic cleavage and conformational activation.

第一步是蛋白水解切割，生成新的N端，该步骤为限速步骤，需要有催化剂存在。

The first step is proteolytic cleavage, creating a new N-terminus; it is the rate-limiting step and requires a catalyst.

第二步是构象激活，蛋白内部结构像弹簧一样释放，自发完成，使酶获得活性。

The second step is conformational activation, where the protein’s internal structure (like a spring) is spontaneously released, endowing the enzyme with catalytic activity.

老师强调，这种双步骤激活方式几乎适用于所有凝血因子（99.9%），只有极少数例外。

The lecturer emphasized that this two-step activation is typical for nearly all coagulation factors (99.9%), with very few exceptions.

2
FVII的特殊激活机制Special activation mechanism of FVII

FVII的激活机制与一般凝血因子不同，体内以一种“预活化-无活性”中间状态存在。

The activation of FVII differs from other coagulation factors; in plasma, it mainly circulates in a "pre-activated–inactive" intermediate state.

第一步蛋白水解切割为“自发完成”，无需催化剂，因此FVII可自发形成轻微切割的FVII*。

The first step—proteolytic cleavage—occurs spontaneously, a process called "auto-activation", so FVII can be partially cleaved (FVII*) without a catalyst.

但第二步构象激活不是自发的，需要协同因子（即组织因子TF）介导，激活速率受此步骤限制。

However, the second step, conformational activation, does not occur spontaneously and is strictly dependent on the cofactor (tissue factor, TF); this step is rate-limiting.

换言之，FVIIa只有在与组织因子结合后，才能实现活性位点开放，获得真正的蛋白酶活性。

In other words, FVIIa only attains an open, catalytically active site after binding to tissue factor (TF).

这种机制使得FVII在循环中以预活化形式存在，但只有暴露于损伤部位、与TF结合时才真正活化，促进凝血级联反应，并实现凝血反应的空间精准定位。

This allows FVII to circulate as a pre-activated form but become functionally active only upon encountering TF at a site of injury—ensuring spatial precision of coagulation activation.

老师补充，正因FVII需TF参与后才能高效激活，下游凝血反应才能被严格局限和调控，避免全身性异常凝血。

The lecturer added that, because FVII requires TF for full activation, the downstream coagulation cascade is spatially restricted and stringently regulated, preventing unwanted systemic clotting.

3
小结Summary

大多数凝血酶原激活依赖于限速蛋白水解+自发构象变化；而FVII的自发蛋白水解和TF依赖的构象激活，确保其活性严格受组织因子调控。

Activation of most zymogens relies on a rate-limiting proteolytic step plus spontaneous conformational change; for FVII, spontaneous cleavage and TF-dependent conformational activation together ensure its activity is tightly controlled by tissue factor.

该分子机制是凝血反应空间、时序专一性和安全性的基础。

This molecular mechanism underpins both the spatial and temporal specificity and overall safety of the coagulation response.

凝血过程的启动与放大阶段Coagulation: Initiation and Amplification

1
启动阶段Initiation phase

启动阶段由组织因子（TF）暴露于血液后开始。

The initiation phase starts after tissue factor (TF) is exposed to the blood.

此时酶促反应速率较慢，仅产生极微量凝血酶（FIIa）。

At this stage, enzymatic reactions are slow and only trace amounts of thrombin (FIIa) are produced.

大部分凝血因子以酶原形式存在，向活性酶的转化速度缓慢。

Most coagulation factors exist as zymogens and are converted to active enzymes at a slow rate.

所有的酶促反应都高度局限在组织因子暴露的局部区域，避免凝血反应扩散至全身。

All enzymatic reactions are highly localized near the site of tissue factor exposure, preventing coagulation from spreading systemically.

老师补充
此阶段主导反应为TF与FVIIa结合，先后激活FIX与FX生成FXa，进而有限度地产生极微量凝血酶（FIIa），这为后续放大反应做准备。
The teacher added
The main reaction at this stage is the binding of TF and FVIIa, sequentially activating FIX and FX to generate FXa. This results in only a small amount of thrombin (FIIa), which prepares the system for the subsequent amplification phase.
放大阶段第一步
凝血酶激活辅因子Amplification phase 1: Thrombin activates cofactors

极少量FIIa（凝血酶）激活FV与FVIII（产生FVa和FVIIIa），并激活FXI为FXIa。

The trace amount of FIIa (thrombin) activates FV and FVIII (generating FVa and FVIIIa), and also activates FXI to FXIa.

这些被激活的因子（即FVa和FVIIIa）作为后续级联反应的强大辅因子。

These activated factors (FVa and FVIIIa) act as powerful cofactors in the following cascade.

老师强调，这是放大反应的第一步，酶促反应很快接力放大。

The teacher emphasized that this is the first step of amplification, where enzymatic reactions are primed for rapid escalation.

放大阶段第二步
酶-辅因子复合物极大增强反应效率Amplification phase 2: Enzyme–cofactor complexes dramatically enhance reactivity

激活的FIXa与FVIIIa在血小板表面形成复合物，大大提高FX转化为FXa的效率。

Activated FIXa and FVIIIa form a complex on the platelet surface, greatly enhancing the efficiency of FX to FXa conversion.

同理，FXa与FVa结合形成复合物，高效催化FII变为大量FIIa（凝血酶）。

Similarly, FXa combines with FVa to form a complex that efficiently catalyzes the conversion of FII to large amounts of FIIa (thrombin).

这种酶-辅因子复合物的组装使酶促反应速率比启动阶段提高千倍以上，导致血小板表面产生大量凝血酶。

The assembly of these enzyme–cofactor complexes increases reaction rates by over a thousand-fold compared to the initiation phase, producing massive amounts of thrombin on the platelet surface.

老师特别指出，这样的定位不仅大幅度提升反应效率，还保证凝血限定在损伤部位，防止全身凝固。

The instructor specifically noted that such localization not only boosts efficiency but also confines coagulation to the site of injury, preventing systemic clotting.

4
小结Summary

启动阶段产生极微量凝血酶，主要在TF暴露处局部反应，反应速率慢，为放大反应做准备。

The initiation phase produces only trace amounts of thrombin, with localized and slow reactions at the site of TF exposure, preparing for amplification.

放大阶段，辅因子被激活，形成复合物，催化反应效率极大提升，血小板表面局部产生大量凝血酶。

In the amplification phase, cofactors are activated and form complexes, which massively boost catalytic efficiency and generate large quantities of thrombin on the platelet surface.

这种分步、定位、级联放大反应机制，是血液凝固高效精准和防止异常扩散的基础。

This stepwise, localized, cascading amplification mechanism ensures efficient, precise coagulation and prevents abnormal systemic clotting.

酶-辅因子复合物与膜组成Enzyme-cofactor complexes and Membrane composition

1
酶-辅因子复合物的组装机制Mechanism of Enzyme-cofactor Complexes Assembly

酶-辅因子复合物如“Tenase复合物（FIXa+FVIIIa+FX）”和“凝血酶原酶复合物（FXa+FVa+FII）”在细胞膜表面组装。

Enzyme-cofactor complexes, such as the "Tenase complex (FIXa + FVIIIa + FX)" and the "Prothrombinase complex (FXa + FVa + FII)", assemble on the cell membrane surface.

这一过程依赖Gla结构域（需要Ca2+介导）把凝血因子锚定在细胞膜上。

This process relies on the Gla domain (requiring Ca2+) to anchor coagulation factors onto the membrane.

在膜表面，凝血因子的作用区域由三维（血浆）变为二维（膜表面），反应物局部浓度大幅提升。

On the membrane surface, the functional area of coagulation factors shifts from three-dimensional (plasma) to two-dimensional (membrane surface), dramatically increasing the local concentration of reactants.

局部高浓度显著提高了酶促反应速率，加速凝血反应进行。

The high local concentration significantly increases enzymatic reaction rates, accelerating the coagulation process.

酶促活性被严格局限在膜表面，阻止酶促活性在体内扩散，防止凝血反应全身性蔓延（防止扩散）。

Enzymatic activity is strictly confined to the membrane surface, preventing its spread throughout the body and avoiding systemic coagulation (prevent dissemination).

老师强调，正是这种复合物的表面组装与空间局限性，使凝血反应快速而精确定位于损伤点，保证止血的高效和安全。

The instructor emphasized that this surface assembly and spatial confinement of complexes ensures rapid and precise hemostasis at the injury site, guaranteeing both efficiency and safety.

2
膜组成与复合物形成的必要条件Membrane Composition and Requirements for Complex Formation

静息细胞膜的外层为磷脂酰胆碱（PC，电中性），内层为磷脂酰丝氨酸（PS，带负电），这种分布需要消耗能量维持。

In resting cells, the membrane outer leaflet contains phosphatidylcholine (PC, neutral), and the inner leaflet has phosphatidylserine (PS, acidic/negative); this arrangement is maintained by an energy-consuming process.

细胞激活或损伤时，PS和PC在膜两层随机分布，PS暴露于外表面。

Upon cell activation or damage, PS and PC are randomly distributed, exposing PS on the extracellular membrane surface.

复合物的形成严格依赖于PS在膜外层暴露，PS提供负电荷，与凝血酶Gla结构域的钙形成桥接，实现锚定与组装。

The formation of enzyme-cofactor complexes strictly depends on PS exposure on the external face, as PS offers negative charges that bridge with the Ca2+-bound Gla domains for anchoring and assembly.

因此，仅在被激活的血小板或受损伤的内皮细胞膜上可形成凝血复合物。

Therefore, these coagulation complexes can form only on activated platelets or damaged endothelial cell membranes.

老师补充解释，PS外翻是高效止血反应的结构基础，不会在健康细胞表面异常发生，保证凝血反应专一性。

The instructor further explained that the externalization of PS is the structural basis for efficient hemostasis and does not occur on healthy cell surfaces, ensuring the specificity of the coagulation response.

3
小结Summary

酶-辅因子复合物必须在富含PS的膜表面（受损或活化细胞）组装，依赖局部高浓度和Ca2+依赖性锚定，高效且局限地产生凝血酶活性。

Assembly of enzyme-cofactor complexes requires PS-rich membrane surfaces (damaged or activated cells), depends on local high concentrations and Ca2+-dependent anchoring, leading to efficient and spatially-restricted generation of coagulation activity.

这种表面组装和定位机制，是防止全身性异常凝血的关键保障。

This surface assembly and localization mechanism is a key safeguard against abnormal systemic coagulation

凝血放大阶段与纤维蛋白血栓形成Coagulation Amplification Phase and Fibrin Clot Formation

1
酶-辅因子复合物的功能Function of enzyme-cofactor complexes

酶-辅因子复合物（如FIXa+FVIIIa及FXa+FVa）在活化的血小板表面组装。

Enzyme-cofactor complexes, such as FIXa+FVIIIa and FXa+FVa, assemble on the surface of activated platelets.

这些复合物能极大提升凝血酶的反应速度，促使下游凝血因子的高效激活。

These complexes dramatically increase the rate of thrombin generation, enabling efficient downstream activation of coagulation factors.

酶-辅因子复合物的本地化，确保凝血反应局限于受损血管和血小板表面，防止全身性凝血。

The localization of enzyme-cofactor complexes ensures that coagulation is confined to injured vessel sites and the platelet surface, preventing systemic clotting.

老师强调，这种本地化可防止酶促反应扩散，是机体凝血精准性的关键机制。

The teacher emphasized that such localization prevents dissemination of enzymatic activity and is key for precise coagulation control.

2
放大反应阶段及其后果Amplification phase and its consequences

在放大阶段，生成大量活化酶（如FIIa/凝血酶）。

During the amplification phase, a large amount of active enzymes (mainly FIIa/thrombin) is produced.

FIXa和FVIIIa组成的“Tenase复合物”加速FX转化为FXa。

The FIXa and FVIIIa “tenase complex” accelerates the conversion of FX to FXa.

FXa和FVa形成的“凝血酶原酶复合物”催化FII转化为大量FIIa（凝血酶）。

The FXa and FVa “prothrombinase complex” catalyzes the production of massive amounts of FIIa (thrombin) from FII.

放大结果是在极短时间内于损伤部位产生大量凝血酶，为后续纤维蛋白血栓形成提供充足条件。

The result of amplification is the rapid formation of large amounts of thrombin at the site of injury, setting the stage for fibrin clot formation.

3
纤维蛋白血栓的形成Fibrin clot formation

凝血酶（FIIa）促使可溶性的纤维蛋白原转化为不溶性纤维蛋白单体。

Thrombin (FIIa) converts soluble fibrinogen into insoluble fibrin monomers.

纤维蛋白单体进一步聚合形成三维网状结构，包埋和稳定血小板栓子。

Fibrin monomers then polymerize into a 3D meshwork, embedding and stabilizing the platelet plug.

纤维蛋白网与血小板共同构成致密而稳定的血栓，有效阻止血液持续外渗。

The fibrin mesh and platelets together form a dense, stable thrombus that effectively halts further bleeding.

老师补充
有了纤维蛋白的“胶水”作用，初级血小板血栓被牢固固定，不再易被血流冲刷脱落。

The lecturer highlighted that the “glue” effect of fibrin firmly secures the primary platelet thrombus, preventing it from being washed away by blood flow.

4
小结Summary

酶-辅因子复合物的高效和定位组装，是快速局限性凝血放大反应的基础。

The efficient, localized assembly of enzyme-cofactor complexes underpins the rapid and confined amplification of coagulation.

放大反应产生大量凝血酶，推动纤维蛋白血栓形成，实现止血过程的最终稳定化。

The amplification phase generates large quantities of thrombin, leading to fibrin clot formation and final stabilization of hemostasis.

凝血过程的终止与调控Coagulation Extinction and Regulation

1
PCa抗凝途径PCa anticoagulant pathway

在凝血终止（3）阶段，体内通过PCa抗凝途径对凝血进行调控。 During the extinction phase (step 3), coagulation is regulated in the body via the PCa anticoagulant pathway.

关键步骤为
FIIa（凝血酶）与TM（血栓调节蛋白）结合，激活PC（蛋白C），PCa（活化蛋白C）结合PS（蛋白S，辅因子）。 The key steps are: FIIa (thrombin) binds to TM (thrombomodulin), activates PC (protein C), and then PCa (activated protein C) binds PS (protein S, cofactor).

PCa-PS复合物可降解FVIIIa和FVa（两大凝血级联反应辅因子），从而迅速终止凝血放大反应。 The PCa–PS complex degrades FVIIIa and FVa (the two main cofactors of the coagulation cascade), rapidly terminating the amplification phase of coagulation.

（补充）这种调控仅发生在靠近血管内皮处，因为只有内皮细胞表面表达TM，保证局部精确灭活凝血。 (Addition) This regulation occurs only near the endothelium as only endothelial cells express TM, ensuring local and precise coagulation inactivation.

2
FIIa在TM结合后的功能转变Functional switch of FIIa after binding TM

FIIa（凝血酶）在游离状态具有强烈促凝功能，激活多种凝血因子及血小板。 FIIa (thrombin), in free form, strongly promotes coagulation by activating various coagulation factors and platelets.

但其与TM结合后，促凝活性丧失，转为激活蛋白C的抗凝酶。 However, once bound to TM, it loses its procoagulant activity and becomes an anticoagulant enzyme that activates protein C.

这一转变保证局部发生凝血、远端可及时关停防止血栓蔓延，体现体内凝血与抗凝动态平衡。 This switch ensures local clotting and timely shutdown at a distance, preventing thrombus propagation and maintaining coagulation–anticoagulation balance in vivo.

3
抗凝酶（AT）的参与及机制Role and mechanism of antithrombin (AT)

AT（抗凝酶）是血浆中重要的凝血酶及多种凝血因子（FIXa, FXa, FIIa等）的天然抑制剂。 AT (antithrombin) is a major plasma inhibitor of thrombin (FIIa) and several coagulation proteases (including FIXa, FXa, etc.).

AT在体液中持续“巡逻”，但只有在高浓度FIIa或暴露于肝素/肝素硫酸时，抑制作用显著增强。 AT continuously "patrols" in plasma, but only in high FIIa concentrations or in the presence of heparin/heparan sulfates is its inhibitory effect strongly potentiated.

血管内皮表面富含肝素硫酸，可极大增强AT活性，加速其灭活凝血酶和相关活化酶的能力。 Endothelial cells are rich in heparan sulfates, which dramatically increase AT's activity and its ability to inactivate thrombin and related enzymes.

（补充）AT优先抑制游离态凝血酶和活化因子——当凝血复合物被破坏、辅因子被PCa降解后，游离的酶才能被AT灭活。 (Addition) AT preferentially inactivates free thrombin and active factors—only after complexes are dismantled and cofactors degraded by PCa can AT efficiently terminate free enzymes.

4
肝素硫酸对AT的协同增强作用Potentiation of AT by heparan sulfates

肝素硫酸作为内皮细胞表面的糖胺聚糖，显著提升AT抑制所有促凝酶的效率。 Heparan sulfates, glycosaminoglycans on endothelial surfaces, significantly potentiate AT’s inhibition of all procoagulant enzymes.

解释了“离开损伤点、临近健康内皮区域后，凝血反应迅速终止”的机制。 This explains why, once away from the injury and near healthy endothelium, coagulation rapidly shuts off.

5
接触途径引发的凝血Contact system pathway induced coagulation

（补充）除了常规的组织因子途径，某些电负性表面（如LPS、医用器械、硅胶、凹凸棒石等）暴露于血液也能激活凝血，形成“接触系统途径”。 (Addition) Apart from the conventional tissue factor pathway, certain electronegative surfaces (like LPS, medical devices, silica, kaolin, etc.) exposed to blood can also activate coagulation, forming the "contact system pathway".

该途径不依赖组织损伤与TF暴露，相关因子包括FXII、FXI和FIX。 This pathway is independent of tissue damage and TF exposure, involving factors FXII, FXI, and FIX.

（实验中常用此途径作为体外激活凝血模型或解释某些临床异常血栓形成病例。） (This pathway is often used experimentally as an in vitro coagulation activation model or to explain specific clinical thrombotic phenomena.)

6
小结Summary

机体具有多重终止凝血机制，包括PCa-蛋白S-通路、抗凝酶-肝素硫酸、以及调控FIIa功能转变，确保凝血局限且及时终止，减少血栓风险。 The body has multiple extinction mechanisms for coagulation, including the PCa–protein S pathway, antithrombin–heparan sulfate system, and functional switches in FIIa, ensuring coagulation is localized and promptly terminated to minimize thrombosis risk.

“接触激活途径”则提示实验和病理状况下还可通过非经典方式激活凝血级联。 The "contact activation pathway" illustrates that, under experimental or pathological conditions, coagulation cascades can be triggered by non-classical mechanisms.

纤溶作用Fibrinolysis

1
纤溶作用的基本定义和意义Basic definition and significance of fibrinolysis

纤溶作用是指纤维蛋白血块的降解，以防止血流阻塞。 Fibrinolysis refers to the degradation of the fibrin clot, to prevent blood flow obstruction.

这一过程保证血管被修复后，血块能够被及时清除，避免血栓长期存在导致栓塞。 This process ensures that after the blood vessel is repaired, the clot can be removed in time, preventing persistent thrombus and embolism.

2
发生部位与关键催化者Site and main catalyst

纤溶作用发生在血栓（血块）表面，由纤维蛋白本身作为触发平台。 Fibrinolysis occurs on the surface of the thrombus (clot), and fibrin itself serves as the platform for triggering this process.

3
参与者Participants

在此过程中，血浆蛋白和内皮细胞分泌的蛋白共同参与。 Both plasma proteins and proteins secreted by endothelial cells participate in this process.

4
过程启动与核心步骤Initiation and main steps

纤溶作用由tPA（组织型纤溶酶原激活物）启动。 Fibrinolysis is initiated by tPA (tissue plasminogen activator).

tPA只有在纤维蛋白存在时才能获得其酶活性。 tPA requires the presence of fibrin to acquire its enzymatic activity.

tPA将纤溶酶原（plasminogen, Pgn）激活为纤溶酶（plasmin, Pn）。 tPA then activates plasminogen (Pgn) into plasmin (Pn).

纤溶酶将不溶性的纤维蛋白降解为可溶性的降解产物，使血块消散。 Plasmin degrades insoluble fibrin into soluble degradation products, thus dissolving the clot.

5
负向调控与保护机制Negative regulation and protective mechanisms

纤溶作用受到酶抑制剂调控，主要的抑制分子为PAI-1和α2-AP（α2-抗纤溶酶）。 Fibrinolysis is regulated by enzyme inhibitors, mainly PAI-1 (plasminogen activator inhibitor 1) and α2-AP (alpha-2 antiplasmin).

PAI-1抑制tPA，α2-AP抑制纤溶酶，避免纤溶过程过度，保护正常止血和血管修复。 PAI-1 inhibits tPA, and α2-AP inhibits plasmin, thus preventing excessive fibrinolysis and ensuring proper hemostasis and vessel healing.

6
纤维蛋白的中心调控作用Central regulatory role of fibrin

一切始于纤维蛋白，也终于纤维蛋白：只有形成纤维蛋白后纤溶才能被激活；一旦纤维蛋白降解殆尽，纤溶过程自动终止。 Everything starts and ends with fibrin: fibrinolysis is only initiated with the presence of fibrin, and as soon as all fibrin is degraded, the process automatically stops.

7
小结Summary

纤溶作用在血管修复中的作用是清除血块，恢复正常血流，且过程高度受控，防止异常溶栓或再出血。 The role of fibrinolysis in vessel repair is to clear the clot and restore normal blood flow; the process is tightly regulated to prevent abnormal thrombolysis or re-bleeding.

血液止凝过程总结Summary of Hemostasis and Coagulation

1
止凝过程总览Overview of the process
止血包括三个阶段
初级止血、凝血（包括启动、放大、终止），以及纤溶。 Hemostasis consists of three phases: primary hemostasis, coagulation (including initiation, amplification, and extinction), and fibrinolysis.
2
初级止血 = 血小板聚集Primary hemostasis = platelet aggregation

受损血管内皮暴露后，血小板聚集于损伤处形成初步血小板血栓。 After vascular endothelial injury and exposure, platelets aggregate at the injury site to form a primary platelet plug.

3
凝血反应三阶段The three phases of coagulation

（1）启动阶段：凝血因子的激活 (1) Initiation: activation of clotting factors

起始时，组织因子暴露，触发一系列凝血因子的级联激活反应。 At the beginning, tissue factor exposure triggers a cascade activation of coagulation factors.

（2）放大阶段：血栓形成 (2) Amplification: thrombus formation

凝血过程加速，活化的酶与辅因子形成复合物，大量生成凝血酶，促使纤维蛋白聚合，血块形成。 The process is accelerated, activated enzymes form complexes with cofactors, generating large amounts of thrombin and promoting fibrin polymerization and clot formation.

（3）终止（灭活）阶段： (3) Extinction (inactivation) phase:

通过抗凝系统的作用，迅速阻止血栓继续扩展，抑制凝血酶及相关酶的蔓延。 Through the action of anticoagulant systems, thrombus propagation is quickly halted, and dissemination of thrombin and related enzymes is suppressed.

这一阶段预防血栓蔓延（血管阻塞）和因凝血因子扩散导致的弥散性血管内凝血（DIC）。 This stage prevents thrombus propagation (vessel obstruction) and the development of disseminated intravascular coagulation (DIC).

4
纤溶作用 = 血栓清除、伤口愈合Fibrinolysis = thrombus resorption, wound healing

血管修复后，纤溶系统启动，降解纤维蛋白，溶解血块，恢复正常血流并促进愈合。 After vessel repair, the fibrinolytic system degrades fibrin, dissolves the clot, restores normal blood flow, and promotes healing.

5
止凝的动态平衡Dynamic balance of hemostasis
止凝系统中存在四类因子
促凝因子、抗凝因子、抗纤溶因子和促纤溶因子。 Four types of factors exist in the hemostatic system: procoagulant, anticoagulant, antifibrinolytic, and profibrinolytic factors.

生理稳态需要这些因子的精确平衡。 Physiological homeostasis requires a precise balance among these factors.

一旦偏向某一方，即可导致出血（促凝/抗纤溶因子缺陷）或血栓（抗凝/促纤溶因子缺陷）。 A tilt toward one side leads to bleeding (deficit in procoagulant/antifibrinolytic factors) or thrombosis (deficit in anticoagulant/profibrinolytic factors).

6
具体重要因子和抑制剂Key factors and inhibitors

促凝因子包括TF、FVIIa、FIXa、FXa、FVIIIa、FVa、FIIa；抗凝以PCa、PS、TM、TFPI、AT等为代表。 Procoagulant factors include TF, FVIIa, FIXa, FXa, FVIIIa, FVa, and FIIa; anticoagulant factors include PCa, PS, TM, TFPI, and AT.

抗纤溶剂如PAI-1、α2-AP，促纤溶剂如tPA、Pn。 Antifibrinolytic agents include PAI-1 and α2-AP, while profibrinolytic agents include tPA and plasmin (Pn).

课程重点强调
各种因子的平衡问题直接决定止血或致栓倾向。 The lecture emphasizes that the balance of these factors directly determines the propensity for bleeding or thrombosis.
7
总结结论Key summary conclusion

止血和凝血是高度动态、受多因素精确调控的过程。 Hemostasis and coagulation are highly dynamic processes under precise multi-factorial regulation.

系统失衡将导致出血或血栓性疾病。 System imbalance will result in hemorrhagic or thrombotic disorders.

老师强调三阶段模型和各类因子（特别是调控网络）理解对于临床和今后学习都极为重要。 The instructor emphasized that understanding the three-stage model and various factor networks (especially regulatory systems) is essential for clinical work and further studies.

022025-10-31

Hemostasis与出血的诊断及表现 Hemostasis and Bleeding: Diagnosis and Manifestations

一、止血作用 Hemostasis

止血（Hemostasis）是防止出血的重要生理过程，如果调节失常，会导致出血或血栓。 Hemostasis is the physiological process preventing bleeding; its dysregulation leads to bleeding or thrombosis.

失衡表现包括
一端为出血，一端为血栓形成。 Imbalance presents as either bleeding on one end or thrombosis on the other.

在实际临床中，既可能是在出现出血事件时需要检查止血功能，也常在术前为预防出血做相关评估。 In clinical practice, hemostasis is assessed both when bleeding occurs and preventively before surgery.

二、出血原因 Bleeding Causes

1
原发性止血障碍 Defective primary hemostasis

原发性止血障碍包括血小板减少（数量不足）或血小板功能障碍（功能异常）。 Defective primary hemostasis includes thrombocytopenia (not enough platelets) or platelet dysfunction.

von Willebrand因子缺乏也属于原发性止血障碍的一个重要原因。 Von Willebrand factor deficiency is another major cause of primary hemostasis defect.

2
凝血障碍 Defective coagulation

凝血障碍指凝血因子的缺乏，可以是单个或多个凝血因子缺乏。 Defective coagulation refers to deficiencies of coagulation factors, which can be single or multiple factor deficiencies.

三、临床表现 Clinical Manifestations

（一）原发性止血障碍表现 Defective Primary Hemostasis Manifestations

出血多局限于微血管，如皮下或黏膜 Bleedings are mostly localized in microvasculature, such as skin or mucosa.

皮下出血
表现为紫癜或瘀斑等。 Subcutaneous bleeding: shows as purpura or ecchymosis.
黏膜出血
包括鼻出血、月经过多、牙龈出血等。 Mucosal bleedings: include epistaxis, menorrhagia, gingivorrhagia.

四、诊断检测 Diagnosis Tests

1
原发性止血障碍检查 Primary Hemostasis Investigations
血细胞计数　：特别是血小板计数 CBC (Complete Blood Count): Especially platelet count
答案 BC

血小板正常范围为150-400 G/L，低于150 G/L为血小板减少，低于50 G/L出血风险明显升高。 Platelet count normal range is 150-400 G/L; less than 150 G/L is thrombocytopenia, less than 50 G/L gives significant bleeding risk.

血小板功能分析仪（PFA）： Platelet Function Analyzer (PFA):

让血液流经包被胶原的膜，正常情况下胶原激活血小板使之聚集，最终膜被堵塞。记录堵塞所需时间。 Blood flows across a collagen-coated membrane; collagen normally activates platelets to aggregate and block the membrane. The time to occlusion is recorded.

时间明显延长提示原发性止血存在障碍。 Significantly prolonged time indicates primary hemostasis defect.

凝血障碍的临床表现与诊断 Defective Coagulation: Clinical Manifestations and Diagnosis

一、临床表现 Clinical Manifestations

1
凝血障碍时的出血特点 Features of Bleeding in Defective Coagulation

“凝血障碍可以导致任何类型的出血，无特定部位限制。” Defective coagulation can cause any kind of bleeding, without restriction to specific locations.

因为凝血因子的作用是生成纤维蛋白，使止血牢固，一旦缺陷，出血不仅局限在微血管，也可大量出现在任何部位。 Since coagulation factors are essential for stable clot formation via fibrin, their deficiency enables bleeding not only in microvessels but at any site.

表现可以包括皮肤、黏膜、内脏、大关节腔出血，外伤后或手术出血量多，有时可见血尿、消化道出血、甚至颅内等严重出血。 Manifestations include skin, mucosa, visceral, or large joint bleeding; bleeding is often severe after trauma or surgery, and can present as hematuria, gastrointestinal, or even intracranial hemorrhage.

二、诊断检测 Diagnosis Tests

1
凝血障碍相关的实验检查 Laboratory Tests for Defective Coagulation

“凝血功能可通过凝血时间（aPTT/PT）检测。” Coagulation function can be evaluated by clotting time tests (aPTT/PT).

存在两种不同激活方式，对应两种凝血时间测试： There are two different ways to activate coagulation, corresponding to two clotting time tests:

“PT（凝血酶原时间）” PT (Prothrombin Time)

“aPTT（活化部分凝血活酶时间）” aPTT (Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time)

2
检测流程及原理 Test Process and Principle
检查流程
取抗凝剂柠檬酸钠预充管抽血，离心后去除血细胞，取等离子。 Sample is drawn into a citrate-Na tube, centrifuged, cells discarded, and plasma is collected.

在等离子中加入特定激活物（不同激活物对应PT或aPTT），启动凝血反应，用秒表记录到纤维蛋白生成的时间。 Add a specific activator to plasma (differing for PT or aPTT), trigger coagulation, and record time until fibrin formation.

三、凝血时间的两种检测方法 Two Kinds of Clotting Time Assays

1
PT（凝血酶原时间） PT (Prothrombin Time)

“PT通过加组织因子（TF）和Ca²⁺激活外源性凝血途径。” PT activates the extrinsic coagulation pathway by adding tissue factor (TF) and Ca²⁺.

步骤（正文配图与流程） Steps (matching diagram and flow):

1
组织因子（TF）与Ⅶ因子（FVIIa）结合激活 Lipidated TF binds with Factor VIIa (FVIIa) to activate it
2
该复合物激活Ⅹ因子（FX）产生FXa The TF/FVIIa complex activates Factor X (FX) to FXa
3
FXa进一步激活Ⅱ因子（FII，凝血酶原）为FIIa（凝血酶） FXa then activates Factor II (FII, prothrombin) to FIIa (thrombin)
4
FIIa激活Ⅴ因子为FVa，最终形成“凝血酶酶原酶”复合物 FIIa activates Factor V to FVa, thus forming the "prothrombinase" complex
5
FXa与FVa形成的复合物加速FIIa生成 The FXa-FVa complex speeds up FIIa formation
6
足够FIIa生成后，将纤维蛋白原转化为纤维蛋白，形成血凝块 Once enough FIIa is formed, it converts fibrinogen to fibrin, forming the fibrin clot
2
aPTT（活化部分凝血活酶时间） aPTT (Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time)

“aPTT通过加XII因子激活物（如高岭土、硅胶）激活内源性凝血途径。” aPTT activates the intrinsic coagulation pathway by adding a Factor XII activator (e.g., kaolin, silica).

步骤 Steps:

1
激活XII因子（FXII）→ FXIIa，进而激活FXI、FIX，持续级联激活FX FXII is activated to FXIIa and then activates FXI, FIX, continuing cascade activation to FX
2
“几分钟后加入Ca²⁺和磷脂囊泡以继续反应。” After a few minutes, Ca²⁺ and PL vesicles are added to proceed.
3
形成“Tenase”和“凝血酶酶原酶”复合物，加快FX活化与FIIa形成 "Tenase" and "prothrombinase" complexes form, accelerating FX activation and FIIa generation.
4
这些复合物显著加快FIIa生成 These complexes greatly increase the rate of FIIa generation
5
足够FIIa后，将纤维蛋白原转化为纤维蛋白，最终出凝块 When enough FIIa is produced, it converts fibrinogen into fibrin, forming the clot
3
PT与aPTT的关系与图示 PT and aPTT Pathways Illustrated

PT主要反映外源性及共同途径，aPTT主要反映内源性及共同途径。 PT mainly reflects extrinsic and common pathways; aPTT mainly reflects intrinsic and common pathways.

两者时间延长提示相关凝血因子的缺陷或功能障碍。 Prolongation of either indicates deficiency or dysfunction of related coagulation factors.

小结 Summary

凝血障碍可致全身任何部位出血，常严重且无局限性。 Coagulation defects can cause general bleeding anywhere in the body, often severe and not localized.

实验室通过 PT 和 aPTT 两种方法，检测不同机制导致的凝血障碍。 Laboratory diagnoses use PT and aPTT to detect different mechanisms of coagulation defects.

准确区分内源性和外源性途径有助于溯源具体因子问题，指导治疗。 Clear distinction between intrinsic and extrinsic pathways helps trace specific factor problems and guide therapy.

凝血时间测定的不同方法与凝血功能监测 Different Methods for Clotting Time Measurement and Coagulation Monitoring

一、凝血时间测定的不同方法

Different Methods for Clotting Time Measurement

1
机械法 Mechanical Method

机械法检测通过在装有等离子体的试管中放入磁性小球，并用磁力搅拌器保持小球旋转。 The mechanical method involves putting a magnetic marble into a plasma-containing tube, keeping it moving with a magnetic stirrer.

在未形成血凝块时，小球可以在等离子体中自由转动。 When no fibrin clot forms, the marble moves freely in the plasma.

血纤维蛋白形成后，小球被纤维蛋白网格卡住，无法再转动，机器检测到运动停止后计时。 When a fibrin clot forms, the marble becomes trapped in the mesh, stops moving, and the device measures the time until this happens.

2
光学法 Photo-optical Method

光学法是利用光源和光敏接收器检测等离子体溶液的透明度变化。 The photo-optical method uses a light source and photoreceptor to detect changes in plasma clarity.

在等离子体未发生凝固时，光线可以直线通过，进入光敏接收器。 When the plasma is liquid, light passes straight through to the photoreceptor.

随着纤维蛋白形成，光线在纤维网格中被散射，进入接收器的光减少，机器以此判定凝血时间。 As fibrin forms, light is dispersed by the fibrin mesh, reducing the light reaching the receptor—this drop marks clotting time.

二、凝血功能监测 Coagulation Monitoring

PT
凝血酶原时间 PT: Prothrombin Time
检测内容
反映外源性凝血途径和共同途径功能。 PT tests the extrinsic and common pathways of coagulation.
检测方法
加脂化组织因子（TF）和Ca²⁺激活凝血过程。 The assay is initiated by adding lipidated tissue factor (TF) and Ca²⁺.
PT依赖的凝血因子包括
FVII, FX, FV, FII, 纤维蛋白原。 PT reflects the function of FVII, FX, FV, FII, and fibrinogen.
正常参考范围
10-15秒，病人PT/正常PT的比值在0.8-1.2之间为正常。 Normal range: 10-15 seconds; ratio of patient PT to normal PT should be between 0.8 and 1.2.
aPTT
活化部分凝血活酶时间 aPTT: Activated Partial Thromboplastin Time
检测内容
反映内源性凝血途径和共同途径功能。 aPTT assesses the intrinsic and common pathways of coagulation.
检测方法
加FXII激活剂（如高岭土或硅胶）启动反应，后续加入PL囊泡与Ca²⁺。 Test is triggered with a FXII activator (kaolin or silica), followed by phospholipid vesicles and Ca²⁺.
aPTT依赖的凝血因子包括
FXII, FXI, FIX, FVIII, FX, FV, FII, 纤维蛋白原。 aPTT reflects the function of FXII, FXI, FIX, FVIII, FX, FV, FII, and fibrinogen.
正常参考范围
25-35秒，病人aPTT/正常aPTT的比值在0.8-1.2之间为正常。 Normal range: 25-35 seconds; ratio of patient aPTT to normal aPTT should be between 0.8 and 1.2.
3
检测要点与意义 Key Points and Significance

通过PT和aPTT，可以分别筛查外源性、内源性及共同通路的凝血因子异常。 PT and aPTT help screen extrinsic, intrinsic, and common pathway abnormalities.

两者均正常基本排除主要凝血因子缺陷，有助于术前和出血患者的快速评估。 If both tests are normal, it effectively rules out major factor deficiencies, aiding preoperative and bleeding risk assessment.

如有异常，则进一步通过比值和具体延长时间，定位是哪个凝血因子的障碍。 If abnormal, the specific ratio and time help pinpoint which factor or pathway is defective.

4
公式表达 Formula Expression
PT比值计算
PT ratio calculation: 病人PT比值 = 病人PT / 正常平均PT PT ratio = Patient PT / Mean normal PT
aPTT比值计算
aPTT ratio calculation: 病人aPTT比值 = 病人aPTT / 正常平均aPTT aPTT ratio = Patient aPTT / Mean normal aPTT

肝病疑似病例专题研究 Case Study #1: Suspected Liver Disease

65岁女性，因疑似肝病（腹部肿胀、皮肤和眼睛发黄）住院治疗。 A 65-year-old woman hospitalized for suspected liver disease (swelling of the abdomen, yellowing of the skin and eyes).

一、凝血实验结果 Coagulation Assays

PT = 22秒 (r = 1.7) PT = 22 seconds (r = 1.7)

aPTT = 43秒 (r = 1.5) aPTT = 43 seconds (r = 1.5)

纤维蛋白原1.5 g/L（正常范围2~4 g/L） Fibrinogen 1.5 g/L (normal range 2–4 g/L)

二、血常规 CBC

血小板数 = 110 G/L Platelets = 110 G/L

三、结果解读 Interpretation

1
PT和aPTT均延长，且纤维蛋白原减少。 Both PT and aPTT are prolonged, and fibrinogen is decreased.
2
由于既有外源性、又有内源性通路被影响，提示共同途径或多个因子不足，而不是单一因子缺陷。 Since both extrinsic and intrinsic pathways are affected, this suggests a defect in the common pathway or multiple factor deficiencies, not a single factor deficiency.
3
结合血小板轻度减少，说明患者整体凝血功能减弱，出血风险增加。 Mild thrombocytopenia plus decreased coagulation suggests overall impaired hemostasis and increased bleeding risk.

四、需要进一步检测 Next Steps

需进一步确定具体缺乏的凝血因子，可以采用共同通路因子检测，即分别测定每种因子的活性。 It is necessary to determine exactly which coagulation factors are deficient, by assaying common pathway factors individually.

实际操作中常用混合实验（Mixing Study）：病人血浆与缺乏某种因子的标准血浆混合，观察凝血时间恢复情况，可确定缺陷因子。 In practice, a mixing study is used: mix patient plasma with factor-deficient plasma, observe clotting time restoration to identify the deficient factor.

五、技术与经济考虑 Technical and Economic Considerations

从技术和经济角度看，联合检测FVII和FX的残余活性比分别测定每个因子的活性更容易且便宜。 For technical and economic reasons, it is much easier and less expensive to determine combined residual activity of FVII and FX than to measure each factor separately.

六、预防及处理建议 Prevention and Management

由于存在凝血功能障碍和出血风险，必须在有创操作或手术前采取预防措施，可以补充纤维蛋白原、新鲜冰冻血浆或目标凝血因子。 Due to coagulation dysfunction and bleeding risk, prophylactic measures are needed before invasive procedures, such as supplementing fibrinogen, fresh frozen plasma, or targeted factor concentrates.

必须随时监测凝血功能，及时调整治疗方案。 Coagulation function must be monitored constantly with timely adjustment of therapy.

七、总结 Summary

该病例为典型的多因子联合缺陷所致凝血功能障碍，见于肝病患者。 This is a typical case of combined (multiple) coagulation factor deficiency seen in liver disease patients.

评估出血风险应综合PT、aPTT、纤维蛋白原、血小板等多项指标，必要时分步查明具体缺陷因子。 Bleeding risk assessment should integrate PT, aPTT, fibrinogen, platelet count, and proceed stepwise to identify specific deficiencies as needed.

肝病疑似病例专题研究Case Study #1: Suspected Liver Disease

一、病例与出血风险评估 Case scenario and bleeding risk evaluation

65岁女性，因怀疑肝病（腹水、皮肤和眼睛发黄）住院。 A 65-year-old woman hospitalized for suspected liver disease (ascites, yellow skin and eyes).

完整血常规检查发现血小板为110 G/L，虽低于正常，但未达到高危出血阈值（重度血小板减少为50 G/L以下）。 Complete blood count showed platelets at 110 G/L, lower than normal but not at the high-risk threshold (severe thrombocytopenia is below 50 G/L).

二、凝血实验与意义 Coagulation assays and significance

PT = 22秒，PT比值 = 1.7（正常比值0.8–1.2）。 PT = 22 seconds, PT ratio = 1.7 (normal ratio 0.8–1.2).

aPTT = 43秒，aPTT比值 = 1.5（正常比值0.8–1.2）。 aPTT = 43 seconds, aPTT ratio = 1.5 (normal ratio 0.8–1.2).

纤维蛋白原1.5 g/L（正常2~4 g/L），明显低于正常。 Fibrinogen 1.5 g/L (normal 2–4 g/L), well below normal.

由于PT和aPTT均延长，表明外源性、内源性及共同通路均受损，且不只是单一因子缺乏。 Prolonged PT and aPTT indicate abnormalities in extrinsic, intrinsic, and common pathways, not a single factor deficiency.

纤维蛋白原降低提示合成障碍或消耗增加，常见于肝功能减退。 Decreased fibrinogen suggests decreased synthesis or increased consumption, often seen in hepatic dysfunction.

三、进一步因子检测 Further factor evaluation

检测FII=45%，FV=41%，FVII+FX=43%（均低于正常值70~140%）。 Factor assay showed FII=45%, FV=41%, FVII+X=43% (all below normal 70–140%).

聚合分析显示为多因子缺乏，为肝病典型表现；其他肝脏合成因子如FVIII、FIX亦常受累。 Combined factor deficiency is typical for liver disease; other liver-synthesized factors like FVIII, FIX are often also affected.

一般不必检测所有因子，只需检测关键共同通路因子，根据是否均降低判断为合成障碍，节省成本和时间。 It is usually unnecessary to test all factors; just assay key common pathway factors, and if all are decreased, assume synthetic defect—this is time- and cost-efficient.

四、机制分析 Mechanism analysis

多因子联合缺乏的主要原因
1) 肝合成功能障碍（最常见）；2) 消耗增多（如DIC），但本例无急性DIC表现，肝合成功能障碍为主。 Main mechanisms: 1) impaired factor synthesis by the liver (most common); 2) increased consumption (such as DIC), but absence of acute DIC here supports synthesis defect.

五、治疗建议 Treatment suggestions

若有出血或需手术，应补充新鲜冰冻血浆（FFP），一次性补足所有凝血因子，维持止血及抗凝平衡。 For bleeding or surgery, administer fresh frozen plasma (FFP) to replenish all coagulation factors at once—maintaining both hemostatic and anticoagulant equilibrium.

不推荐逐一补充各单因子或单补某一项，否则容易打破凝血-抗凝平衡，增加血栓风险。 Do not supplement each factor individually, or just one, as this would disrupt the balance between coagulation and anticoagulation, increasing thrombosis risk.

同时需维持正常纤维蛋白原，可酌情给予纤维蛋白原制剂，并持续监测血小板。 Maintain normal fibrinogen levels if needed, using fibrinogen concentrate, and keep platelet counts under monitoring.

六、小结 Summary

肝病引起的为典型的多因子缺乏，凝血功能障碍，全球性凝血风险升高。 Liver disease causes typical multifactorial deficiencies and global coagulation dysfunction, increasing bleeding risk.

治疗主张全因素同时补充，预防和处理出血风险。 Treatment emphasizes replacement of all factors at once to prevent and manage bleeding risk.

公式专区 Formula section

PT比值 = 患者PT / 正常PT

PT ratio = Patient PT / Normal PT

aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT / 正常aPTT

aPTT ratio = Patient aPTT / Normal aPTT

病例分析#2Case Study #2

一、基本信息及检验结果 Basic Information and Test Results

一名孕妇（孕33周），麻醉前需进行凝血功能检测。 A pregnant woman (33rd week of amenorrhea); coagulation tests are required before anesthesia.

凝血实验指标如下
Coagulation assay results are as follows:

PT = 18秒（比值r = 1.4） PT = 18 seconds (ratio r = 1.4)

aPTT = 30秒（比值r = 1.0） aPTT = 30 seconds (ratio r = 1.0)

纤维蛋白原 = 3 g/L（正常范围2–4 g/L） Fibrinogen = 3 g/L (normal range 2–4 g/L)

血小板 = 220 G/L Platelets = 220 G/L

二、进一步因子检测结果 Further Factor Assays

FII = 90%（正常范围70–140%） FII = 90% (normal range 70–140%)

FV = 95%（正常范围70–140%） FV = 95% (normal range 70–140%)

FVII+FX = 24%（正常范围70–140%） FVII+FX = 24% (normal range 70–140%)

FX = 92%（正常范围70–140%） FX = 92% (normal range 70–140%)

FVII = 15%（正常范围70–140%） FVII = 15% (normal range 70–140%)

三、分析与定位 Analysis and Localization

PT延长，aPTT正常，因此主要是外源性通路（包括FVII）异常。 PT is prolonged while aPTT is normal, so the abnormality is mainly in the extrinsic pathway (involving FVII).

进一步检测显示FVII显著低下，仅15%，而FX正常。 Further testing shows FVII is significantly decreased (only 15%) while FX is normal.

其他各因子（FII、FV、FX、纤维蛋白原）正常，提示是单一FVII缺乏。 Other factors (FII, FV, FX, fibrinogen) are normal, indicating isolated FVII deficiency.

四、临床意义与风险评估 Clinical Significance and Risk Assessment

血小板和纤维蛋白原均正常，无原发性止血缺陷或整体凝血障碍。 Platelets and fibrinogen are normal, so there is no primary hemostasis defect or generalized coagulopathy.

FVII轻度缺乏通常只导致轻度或无明显出血风险。 Mild FVII deficiency generally causes mild or no obvious bleeding risk.

孕妇凝血因子的微小变化可能受生理状态影响，但本例明显为病理性FVII缺乏。 Physiological changes in coagulation factors occur during pregnancy, but in this case, the FVII decrease is clearly pathological.

五、处理建议与教师补充讲解 Recommendations and Instructor’s Supplement

发生实际出血或需手术、麻醉时，采取按需替代治疗。 If actual bleeding occurs or for procedures/anesthesia, adopt "on-demand" substitution therapy.

常用重组FVIIa（rFVIIa）或特定凝血因子制剂补充。 Recombinant FVIIa (rFVIIa) or specific factor concentrate is commonly administered.

没有必要常规预防性输注，仅在有创操作、有明确出血风险或实际出血时使用。 Routine prophylactic transfusion is unnecessary; use only for invasive procedures, proven bleeding risk, or actual bleeding.

教师特别强调，单一因子缺乏可由遗传或获得性（如自身免疫）因素引起，但多因子缺陷基本排除如本例单独FVII缺乏的情况。 The instructor emphasized that single-factor deficiency may be genetic or acquired (autoimmune, etc.), but multifactorial deficiency is essentially excluded here because only FVII is affected.

六、总结及公式 Summary and Formula

此病例为典型的单一因子（FVII）缺乏导致的外源性通路异常，临床风险较低，仅在手术时需警惕。 This is a classic case of single-factor (FVII) deficiency leading to extrinsic pathway abnormalities, with low clinical risk and alert only needed for surgical situations.

公式：PT比值 = 患者PT / 正常PT Formula: PT ratio = patient’s PT / normal PT

公式：aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT / 正常aPTT Formula: aPTT ratio = patient’s aPTT / normal aPTT
