维生素K依赖性凝血因子缺乏相关病例分析Case study #3: Vitamin K-dependent coagulation factor deficiency

病例简介与初步检验结果 Case summary and initial laboratory findings

4岁男孩，出现急性出血（尿和粪便中带血）。 A 4-year-old boy presenting with acute bleeding (blood in urine and stools).

凝血实验结果
PT 32秒（比值2.5），aPTT 62秒（比值2.1），血浆纤维蛋白原2.5 g/L（正常值2-4 g/L）。 Coagulation results: PT 32 sec (ratio 2.5), aPTT 62 sec (ratio 2.1), plasma fibrinogen 2.5 g/L (normal 2–4 g/L).
全血细胞计数
血小板350 G/L，数值正常，排除原发性止血障碍。 CBC: Platelets 350 G/L, normal value, excluding primary hemostatic disorder.

进一步因子检测与定位 Additional factor assays and diagnostic localization

FII 10%（正常范围70–140%），FV 85%（正常范围70–140%），FVII+FX 6%（正常范围70–140%）。 FII 10% (normal 70–140%), FV 85% (normal 70–140%), FVII+FX 6% (normal 70–140%).

PT和aPTT均显著延长，提示外源性、内源性及共同通路均受累。 Both PT and aPTT significantly prolonged, indicating abnormality in extrinsic, intrinsic, and common pathways.

多个凝血因子活性显著下降，尤其是FII、FVII、FX。 Several coagulation factors are significantly decreased, especially FII, FVII, and FX.

根据分子机制，FV正常或轻度减少，而FII、FVII、FX、FIX等均低下，需寻找共同原因。 According to molecular mechanism, FV is normal or mildly reduced, but FII, FVII, FX, and FIX are decreased, so a shared reason must be sought.

共性分析与鉴别诊断 Common point analysis and differential diagnosis

FII、FVII、FIX、FX均为维生素K依赖性凝血因子。 FII, FVII, FIX, and FX are all vitamin K-dependent coagulation factors.

共同点：这些因子在谷氨酸残基需维持羧基化，该过程依赖于维生素K。 Common feature: These factors require gamma-carboxylation of glutamate residues in a vitamin K-dependent process.

所见为维生素K依赖性因子缺乏症的典型表现，且FIX也可预计降低（虽未检测）。 This is typical for vitamin K-dependent factor deficiency, and FIX is also expected to be low (although not tested).

发病机制与临床原因 Etiology and clinical settings

维生素K缺乏因素可分为以下几类
Causes of vitamin K deficiency can be classified as follows:
1
治疗性应用维生素K拮抗剂（如华法林）。 Therapeutic use of vitamin K antagonists (e.g., warfarin).
2
误服维生素K拮抗剂（如儿童误服成人药物或灭鼠药中毒）。 Accidental ingestion of vitamin K antagonists (child taking medications or rodenticide poisoning).
3
肠道菌群改变（长期抗生素治疗，影响细菌合成维生素K）。 Alteration of intestinal microbiota (prolonged antibiotic therapy affecting bacterial vitamin K synthesis).
4
营养不良（严重营养缺乏导致维生素K摄入不足）。 Nutritional deficiency (severe malnutrition causing insufficient vitamin K intake).

治疗策略与要点 Treatment strategies and key points

出血时需立即补充维生素K依赖性因子浓缩物（凝血酶原复合物）。 In case of bleeding, immediately administer vitamin K-dependent factor concentrates (prothrombin complex concentrate).

同时补充维生素K，但因其参与因子合成起效较慢，不能作为急救手段的唯一措施。 Administer vitamin K as well, but since its effect depends on synthesis of new factors and is delayed, it is not sufficient alone for acute rescue.

补充剂作用快，可直接纠正因子缺乏，是急性出血的首选。 Factor concentrate acts rapidly, correcting deficiencies directly—first choice for acute bleeding management.

必须查找缺乏维生素K的具体原因，根本上解决问题。 It is essential to identify and address the underlying cause of vitamin K deficiency for definitive management.

相关公式、检验解释 Related formulas and test interpretation

PT比值 = 患者PT / 正常PT PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT / 正常aPTT aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

小结 Summary

本例为典型维生素K依赖因子缺乏。PT与aPTT同时延长提示全局性凝血障碍，凝血酶原复合物及急性维K补充是关键。 This case is a typical vitamin K-dependent factor deficiency. Simultaneous PT and aPTT prolongation indicate global coagulopathy, with prothrombin complex concentrate and urgent vitamin K supplementation as key interventions.

老年髋关节置换术后出血病例分析Case Study #4: Bleeding After Hip Arthroplasty in the Elderly

病例及基础化验结果 Case Description and Basic Assays

76岁男性，髋关节置换术后出现引流管出血。 A 76-year-old man with bleeding after total hip arthroplasty (in the outflow from the drain).

凝血实验
PT = 15 秒（比值r = 1.2），aPTT = 60 秒（比值r = 2.1）。 Coagulation assays: PT = 15 sec (ratio r = 1.2), aPTT = 60 sec (ratio r = 2.1).

纤维蛋白原2.1 g/L（正常范围2-4 g/L）。 Fibrinogen 2.1 g/L (normal range 2–4 g/L).

全血计数
血小板正常。 CBC: normal platelet count.

实验结果解读 Interpretation of Results

PT基本正常，aPTT明显延长，提示内源性通路异常。 PT is nearly normal, while aPTT is markedly prolonged, indicating an intrinsic pathway abnormality.

Fibrinogen和血小板正常，排除原发性止血障碍和纤维蛋白原异常。 Normal fibrinogen and platelet count rule out primary hemostatic disorder and fibrinogen problem.

需重点考虑凝血因子缺陷，尤其是内源性通路因子（如VIII、IX、XI、XII）。 Consider clotting factor deficiency, especially intrinsic pathway factors (e.g., VIII, IX, XI, XII).

进一步因子检测及结果 Further Factor Assays and Results

FVIII = 75%（正常范围50-150%） FVIII = 75% (normal range = 50–150%)

FIX = 85%（正常范围60-120%） FIX = 85% (normal range = 60–120%)

FXI = 78%（正常范围60-120%） FXI = 78% (normal range = 60–120%)

FXII = 80% FXII = 80%

所有相关凝血因子均在正常范围，无显著因子缺乏。 All relevant clotting factors are within normal ranges; there is no significant factor deficiency.

aPTT延长不能用凝血因子缺乏解释。 Prolonged aPTT cannot be explained by factor deficiency.

aPTT和抗凝治疗的敏感性 Sensitivity of aPTT and Role of Anticoagulant Therapy

aPTT对抗凝治疗敏感，常用于监测抗凝药物（如肝素）。 aPTT is sensitive to anticoagulant therapy and is often used to monitor drugs like heparin.

临床实际中，术后患者常常接受抗凝剂预防血栓，因此aPTT延长可能反映抗凝药影响而非因子缺陷。 In clinical practice, post-operative patients are often on anticoagulants to prevent thrombosis, so a prolonged aPTT may reflect drug effect, not factor deficiency.

老师强调，在真正临床中，不要一开始就盲目检测所有因子，应优先考虑抗凝剂影响。 The instructor stressed that in real practice, do not hastily measure all factors; consider anticoagulant effect first.

混合实验与鉴别诊断 Mixing Study and Differential Diagnosis

混合实验可区分因子缺乏与抑制剂（如抗凝剂）作用，应在做因子定量前先进行。 Mixing studies help distinguish between clotting factor deficiency and inhibitors (e.g., anticoagulants) and should be performed before factor assays.

方法：患者血浆与正常血浆1:1混合，重复测量aPTT。 Method: Mix patient plasma with normal plasma (1:1), repeat aPTT measurement.

本例混合后aPTT为55秒（比值1.9），仍长于正常，提示存在抑制剂（即抗凝剂肝素）。 Here, after mixing, aPTT = 55 sec (ratio 1.9), still prolonged, suggesting the presence of an inhibitor (i.e., heparin).

结论与操作要点 Conclusion and Practical Points

aPTT延长但无因子缺陷，结合混合实验和临床用药史，说明为抗凝药物导致。 Prolonged aPTT without factor deficiency, supported by mixing study and clinical history, points to anticoagulant effect.

临床操作中，先结合药物史和混合实验分析aPTT延长原因，避免无效和浪费性检测。 In practice, use drug history and mixing studies to analyze prolonged aPTT before unnecessary and costly assays.

公式与简明算法 Formulas and Summary Algorithm

PT比值 = 患者PT / 正常PT PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT / 正常aPTT aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

混合实验判读
混合后aPTT矫正→因子缺乏；混合后不矫正→抑制剂（如肝素、抗体等）。 Mixing study interpretation: Corrected aPTT after mixing = factor deficiency; uncorrected aPTT = inhibitor (e.g., heparin, antibody).

该病例主要考查了抗凝治疗对凝血实验的影响、混合实验在鉴别诊断中的作用，以及合理检测流程的实际操作关键点。 This case mainly covers anticoagulant effect on coagulation tests, the role of mixing studies in differential diagnosis, and key points for rational diagnostic workflows.

Case study #5——18月龄儿童出血症状病例分析Case study #5: Bleeding Symptoms in an 18-Month-Old Child

病例基本情况与临床表现 Case description and clinical presentation

18个月大儿童，表现为出血症状（舌系带、肘部和膝部血肿，易瘀伤）。 An 18-month-old child with signs of bleeding (lingual frenulum, elbow and knee hematoma, easy bruising).

基础化验结果 Basic laboratory results

PT=12秒（比值r=1），正常范围。 PT=12 sec (ratio r=1), within normal range.

aPTT=100秒（比值r=3.3），显著延长。 aPTT=100 sec (ratio r=3.3), markedly prolonged.

混合实验aPTT=35秒（比值r=1.2），混合后可纠正。 Mixing study aPTT=35 sec (ratio r=1.2), correction seen after mixing.

纤维蛋白原3.2 g/L（正常范围2–4 g/L）。 Fibrinogen 3.2 g/L (normal range 2–4 g/L).

全血计数
血小板计数正常。 CBC: normal platelet count.

实验结果初步解读 Interpretation of initial results

PT正常，aPTT显著延长，提示内源性凝血通路异常。 Normal PT and prolonged aPTT indicate an intrinsic pathway abnormality.

混合实验可纠正aPTT，说明是凝血因子缺乏导致，而非抑制剂作用。 Correction in mixing study suggests factor deficiency, not inhibitor.

进一步凝血因子检测 Further coagulation factor assays

FVIII＜1%（正常范围50–150%），极度缺乏。 FVIII＜1% (normal range 50–150%), severe deficiency.

FIX=110%（正常范围60–120%），正常。 FIX=110% (normal range 60–120%), normal.

FXI=95%（正常范围60–120%），正常。 FXI=95% (normal range 60–120%), normal.

FXII=80%，正常。 FXII=80%, normal.

实验结果总结 Summary of laboratory findings

存在严重的FVIII缺乏，出血风险极高。 There is a severe FVIII deficiency, indicating high bleeding risk.

其余内源性凝血因子（FIX、FXI、FXII）都正常，排除联合因子缺乏或其他形式的血友病。 All other intrinsic pathway factors (FIX, FXI, FXII) are normal, excluding combined deficiency or other types of hemophilia.

鉴别诊断与分子机制 Differential diagnosis and molecular mechanism

FVIII缺乏的可能原因包括血友病A或vWF病。 Potential causes of FVIII deficiency are hemophilia A or von Willebrand disease.

必须与vWF病鉴别（vWF有助于稳定FVIII）。 Differential diagnosis with von Willebrand disease is essential (vWF stabilizes FVIII).

vWF测定（活性及血浆水平）=98%，正常。 vWF assays (activity and plasma level) = 98%, normal.

所以可诊断为重型血友病A。 Therefore, diagnosis is severe hemophilia A.

老师讲课补充 Teacher’s lecture points and supplements

血友病A为FVIII缺乏症，表现为深部、关节和软组织出血。 Hemophilia A is FVIII deficiency, typically causing deep tissue, joint, and soft tissue bleeding.

vWF在体内稳定FVIII，若vWF降低则FVIII也会下降。 vWF physiologically stabilizes FVIII, so decreased vWF leads to low FVIII.

必须区分是FVIII本身生成减少还是因vWF下降间接导致FVIII下降。 It's necessary to distinguish between primary FVIII deficiency and secondary decrease due to low vWF.

实验应包括FVIII抗原和活性检测，以及vWF检测。 Laboratory workup involves FVIII antigen and activity, plus vWF determination.

治疗建议 Treatment recommendations

补充疗法为首选，即给予FVIII浓缩物。 Substitutive therapy is preferred, i.e., FVIII concentrate supplementation.

近年来可选用Emicizumab（Emilbra，2018年FDA批准），不依赖FVIII通路激活凝血。 In recent years, Emicizumab (Emilbra, FDA approved in 2018) offers an alternative, bypassing FVIII.

应在医生指导下评估和选择具体治疗方案。 Treatment plan must be tailored and supervised by clinicians.

扩展知识点 Extended points from lecture

FVIII缺乏常为遗传性（X连锁隐性），可表现为男性患儿反复出血。 FVIII deficiency is often X-linked recessive, presenting as recurrent bleeding in boys.

出血表现主要为关节腔、肌肉或软组织深部出血，区别于原发性止血障碍（多为表浅黏膜出血）。 The bleeding tends to be into joints, muscles, or deep tissues, in contrast to primary hemostatic disorders (usually mucocutaneous bleeding).

检查异常只见于aPTT延长，PT及血小板均正常。 Prolonged aPTT, but normal PT and platelet count, is characteristic.

常用公式与检验 interpretative formulae and summary

PT比值=患者PT/正常PT PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值=患者aPTT/正常aPTT aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

该病例为典型重型血友病A，具有显著FVIII缺乏和高出血风险，治疗及鉴别诊断需规范进行。 This is a classic severe hemophilia A case with marked FVIII deficiency and high bleeding risk, requiring standard treatment and differential diagnosis procedures.

范科-威尔布兰德病病例分析Case Study #6：反复鼻出血与月经过多的少女

病例基本信息与临床表现 Case details and clinical presentation

13岁女孩，反复鼻出血（双侧）和月经过多，属于黏膜-皮肤出血表现。 A 13-year-old girl with recurrent bilateral nosebleeds and heavy periods (menorrhagia), showing mucocutaneous bleeding.

初步实验室检查结果 Initial laboratory results

凝血实验
Coagulation assays: PT = 14秒（比值r = 1.1），正常； PT = 14 sec (ratio r = 1.1), normal; aPTT = 60秒（比值r = 2），延长； aPTT = 60 sec (ratio r = 2), prolonged; 纤维蛋白原4 g/L（正常范围2-4 g/L），正常。 Fibrinogen 4 g/L (normal range 2-4 g/L), normal.
血常规
CBC: 血小板=350 G/L，正常。 Platelets = 350 G/L, normal.

PFA-100延长。 PFA-100 lengthened.

老师课堂补充（实验分析与思路） Teacher’s detailed explanation (laboratory reasoning)

出血部位主要为黏膜、皮肤，结合月经过多，是典型原发性止血障碍表现。 Site of bleeding (mucosa, skin, heavy menstruation) suggests a primary hemostasis disorder.

实验结果显示PT正常，仅aPTT延长，提示有内源性凝血通路问题。 PT is normal and only aPTT prolonged, indicating intrinsic pathway involvement.

PFA-100显著延长，说明血小板功能或vWF相关问题。 Markedly prolonged PFA-100 suggests platelet function or von Willebrand factor (vWF) related abnormality.

血小板数量正常，排除血小板减少症，需关注功能异常或血浆相关因子。 Platelet count is normal, excluding thrombocytopenia—focus needed on functional defect or plasma factors.

需优先考虑vWF病或轻型FVIII缺乏。 Clinically prioritize consideration of vWF disease or mild FVIII deficiency.

混合实验与凝血因子检测 Mixing study and clotting factor determination

混合实验
aPTT为32秒（r=1.2），校正，提示因子缺乏非抑制剂引起。 Mixing study: aPTT corrects to 32 sec (r=1.2), indicating factor deficiency, not inhibitor.
因子检测结果
FVIII < 35%（正常50-150%），轻中度缺乏； FVIII < 35% (normal 50–150%), mild to moderate deficiency; FIX=100%，FXI=80%，FXII=78%，均正常。 FIX = 100%, FXI = 80%, FXII = 78%, all normal.

仅FVIII略低，结合出血部位特征，仍需进一步排查vWF病。 Only FVIII is reduced, and given bleeding pattern, vWF disease must still be excluded.

老师讲解要点（机制与临床分析） Teacher’s key analysis (mechanism and clinical perspective)

PFA-100延长且FVIII缺乏，提示既有原发性止血障碍，也有部分凝血障碍。 Prolonged PFA-100 and FVIII reduction indicate both a primary hemostatic defect and minor coagulation defect.

FVIII缺乏较轻，不能单独解释所有黏膜出血。 Mild FVIII deficiency cannot alone explain all mucocutaneous bleeding.

结合前述，优先考虑vWF病（vWF生理上稳定FVIII，缺乏导致两者皆降）。 Given this, consider vWF disease predominantly (vWF physiologically stabilizes FVIII—deficiency reduces both).

范-威尔布兰德因子检测与结果 von Willebrand factor testing and result

vWF功能检测是诊断关键。 vWF functional testing is key for diagnosis.

vWF活性21%（正常50-150%），抗原25%（正常50-150%），均显著降低。 vWF activity 21% (normal 50–150%), antigen 25% (normal 50–150%), both markedly decreased.

活性与抗原均低，说明为定量缺陷。 Both activity and antigen are low, indicating a quantitative defect.

老师强调定量vs定性
定量型表现为活性与抗原同时下降，定性型仅活性下降而抗原正常。 Teacher emphasized quantitative vs qualitative (quantitative defects: both activity and antigen ↓; qualitative: activity ↓ but antigen normal).

vWF的生物学功能与机制 Biology and mechanism of vWF

vWF是FVIII的分子伴侣，防止其在血浆中降解。 vWF acts as a chaperone for FVIII, preventing its plasma degradation.

vWF参与血小板与损伤血管壁的黏附，是原发性止血的关键分子。 vWF mediates platelet adhesion to injured vessel walls, crucial in primary hemostasis.

vWF主要由血管内皮细胞和巨核细胞合成。 vWF is mainly synthesized by endothelial cells and megakaryocytes.

诊断与结论 Diagnosis and summary

该病例为定量缺陷型范-威尔布兰德病。 This case is a quantitative defect form of von Willebrand disease.

临床表现与机制紧密相关，实验诊断必须结合混合实验、vWF活性与抗原检测、PFA-100等多项指标。 Clinical features and underlying mechanism are interlinked; laboratory diagnosis relies on mixing studies, vWF activity/antigen, and PFA-100 among others.

附加知识点与公式 Additional points and formulas

PT比值=患者PT/正常PT PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值=患者aPTT/正常aPTT aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

vWF定量缺陷（Type 1）：活性与抗原均降低。 Quantitative vWF defect (Type 1): Both activity and antigen decreased.

vWF定性缺陷（Type 2）：活性降低，抗原正常。 Qualitative vWF defect (Type 2): Activity decreased, antigen normal.

结论：该病例涉及原发性止血障碍和轻度凝血因子下降的混合，但机制核心为定量型范-威尔布兰德病。

Summary
This case involves a mixed primary hemostatic defect and minor coagulation deficiency, but fundamentally is quantitative von Willebrand disease.

弥散性血管内凝血在转移性前列腺癌男性患者中的表现Disseminated Intravascular Coagulation (DIC) in a Man with Metastatic Prostate Cancer

病例简介

65岁男性，转移性前列腺癌。

A 65-year-old man with metastatic prostate cancer.

初步实验室检查

PT = 30秒（比值r = 2.1），显著延长。

PT = 30 sec (ratio r = 2.1), markedly prolonged.

aPTT = 55秒（比值r = 1.4），延长。

aPTT = 55 sec (ratio r = 1.4), prolonged.

纤维蛋白原0.5 g/L（正常范围2–4 g/L），显著降低。

Fibrinogen 0.5 g/L (normal range 2–4 g/L), markedly decreased.

血小板 = 80 G/L，明显减少。

Platelet count = 80 G/L, significantly decreased.

进一步凝血因子检测

FII = 65%（正常范围70–140%），中度减少。

FII = 65% (normal range 70–140%), moderately reduced.

FV = 40%（正常范围70–140%），明显减少。

FV = 40% (normal range 70–140%), markedly reduced.

FVII + FX = 53%（正常范围70–140%），均减少。

FVII + FX = 53% (normal range 70–140%), both decreased.

实验结果解析与老师讲解要点

多项凝血因子均明显降低，伴随PT、aPTT延长，纤维蛋白原极低，血小板减少，提示为全球性凝血障碍。

Multiple coagulation factors are decreased, with prolonged PT/aPTT, severe hypofibrinogenemia, and thrombocytopenia, indicating a global coagulation disorder.

这种模式常见于两种情况
一是凝血因子的合成减少（如肝功能衰竭），二是凝血因子的过度消耗（如弥散性血管内凝血，DIC）。

This pattern is commonly seen in two scenarios: decreased production (e.g., liver failure) or excessive consumption (e.g., disseminated intravascular coagulation, DIC).

恶性肿瘤，尤其转移和炎症状态，容易诱发DIC。肿瘤细胞表达组织因子，进入血流后广泛激活凝血级联反应。

Malignancy, especially metastatic and inflammatory conditions, tends to precipitate DIC. Tumor cells express tissue factor, inducing widespread coagulation when entering circulation2.

DIC中不仅凝血因子被消耗，抗凝蛋白如蛋白C、蛋白S、ATIII等也减少，导致既有出血风险，也有血栓风险。

In DIC, both coagulation factors and inhibitors like protein C, S, and ATIII are depleted, presenting risks for both bleeding and thrombosis2.

鉴别步骤及关键实验

根据结果有两种假设：

1
合成减少（如肝病）
2
消耗过多（如DIC）

With these results, two hypotheses are considered:

1
Decreased synthesis (e.g., liver disease)
2
Excessive consumption (e.g., DIC)2.

为鉴别可测D-二聚体（纤维蛋白降解产物）水平。

D-dimer assay (fibrin degradation products) is used for discrimination.

D-二聚体=8 μg/mL（正常<1 μg/mL），显著升高，提示体内存在广泛纤溶与凝血激活。

D-dimers = 8 μg/mL (normal <1 μg/mL), strongly elevated, indicating extensive fibrinolysis and intravascular coagulation2.

此异常强烈支持“因子过度消耗”而非“合成减少”，确诊为DIC。

This supports excessive consumption rather than decreased production, confirming DIC2.

DIC发病机制补充与老师讲解

DIC病理核心为病理性组织因子暴露，激活凝血级联，导致全身性微血栓形成，同时耗竭凝血因子和血小板。

Core mechanism in DIC: pathological tissue factor exposure triggers coagulation cascade, resulting in systemic microthrombi and depletion of both clotting factors and platelets.

癌症、感染、手术等状态均可引起组织因子进入血流扩散，说明疾病具有全身性及消耗性特点。

Cancer, sepsis, surgery all lead to tissue factor entering circulation, highlighting systemic and consumptive nature2.

治疗原则与注意事项

DIC患者需警惕“既出血又血栓”的双重风险。

DIC patients face dual risk of bleeding and thrombosis.

治疗以去除原发诱因、支持治疗（如新鲜冰冻血浆补充）、必要时补充血小板和凝血因子为主，避免单独过多补充引发血栓。

Treatment focuses on managing underlying cause, supportive therapy (e.g., fresh frozen plasma), supplementing platelets and factors as needed, but avoiding excessive transfusion to prevent thrombosis2.

核心公式与诊断总结

PT比值 = 患者PT / 正常PT

PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT / 正常aPTT

aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

DIC的特征
多因子降低+高D-二聚体+低血小板+低纤维蛋白原。
Hallmark of DIC
multiple factor deficiency + high D-dimer + low platelets + low fibrinogen.

结论

本病例为典型肿瘤伴发弥散性血管内凝血（DIC），以凝血因子消耗和纤溶激活为主。

This case represents secondary DIC due to metastatic cancer, centered on consumptive coagulopathy and fibrinolysis activation.

弥散性血管内凝血（DIC）与凝血检测思路Disseminated Intravascular Coagulation (DIC) and Approach to Coagulation Testing

1
弥散性血管内凝血机制图Disseminated Intravascular Coagulation Mechanism Diagram

“TF暴露于血流” “TF exposure in the blood flow”

组织因子（TF）异常暴露可激活外源性凝血级联，进而激活多种凝血因子（如VII、IX、X等）。 Abnormal exposure of tissue factor (TF) activates the extrinsic coagulation cascade, which further activates multiple clotting factors (such as VII, IX, X, etc).

病理条件包括
Pathological conditions include:

癌症

Cancer

炎症（如败血症）

Inflammation (such as sepsis)

手术后（如前列腺细胞释放）

After surgery (e.g., prostate cell release)

激活凝血酶后，纤维蛋白原被转化为纤维蛋白，并与活化血小板形成血栓。 After thrombin activation, fibrinogen is converted into fibrin, which, together with activated platelets, forms thrombi.

天然抗凝物如抗凝血酶（AT）、蛋白C（PC）、蛋白S（PS）等也被消耗。 Natural anticoagulants such as antithrombin (AT), protein C (PC), and protein S (PS) are also consumed.

最终临床上同时存在出血风险和血栓风险。 Ultimately, there is both a risk of bleeding and of thrombosis clinically.

老师补充解释
肿瘤细胞、炎症或手术使组织因子进入血流，从而在体内激发广泛的凝血和纤溶反应，导致多因子与抗凝蛋白消耗，属于消耗性凝血障碍。 Teacher's supplement: Tumor cells, inflammation, or surgery lead to tissue factor entering the circulation, triggering widespread coagulation and fibrinolysis in the body, resulting in consumption of multiple factors and anticoagulant proteins, known as consumptive coagulopathy26.
2
PT延长的判读路径图PT Prolongation Interpretation Flowchart
PT延长时的诊断路径如下
When PT is prolonged, the diagnostic pathway is:

aPTT正常时，考虑FVII缺乏（如病例2），出血风险高（<20%）。 If aPTT is normal, consider FVII deficiency (e.g., Case #2), which carries high bleeding risk (<20%).

aPTT也延长时，进行共同通路因子检测。 If aPTT is also prolonged, perform common pathway factor assays.

单因子缺乏
存在出血风险（<20%）。 Single factor deficiency: Risk of bleeding (<20%).
多因子缺乏需进一步分层
Multiple factor deficiency needs further categorization:
所有因子均低
要区分生成减少（如肝病）或消耗过度（如DIC，见病例1与7）。 All factors low: Discriminate between decreased production (e.g., liver disease) or excessive consumption (e.g., DIC; see Case #1 and #7).
Vitamin K依赖性因子下降
提示维生素K缺乏（如病例3）。 Vitamin K-dependent factors low: Suggests vitamin K deficiency (e.g., Case #3).

不论是哪种情况，均存在出血风险。 In all these situations, bleeding risk exists.

老师讲课补充
单因子缺乏常为遗传或免疫原发原因；多因子全低常见于严重肝病或DIC等消耗性病理。 Teacher supplement: Single factor deficiency usually stems from genetic or immune causes; global deficiency of multiple factors is typical of severe liver disease or consumptive pathologies like DIC613.
3
aPTT延长的判读路径图aPTT Prolongation Interpretation Flowchart
aPTT延长的诊断思路
Diagnostic approach for prolonged aPTT:

若PT正常，应做混合实验（mixing study）。 If PT is normal, perform a mixing study.

混合能纠正aPTT，提示内源性因子缺乏（如FVIII、FIX、FXI，病例5和6，<30%存在出血风险；如果是FXII缺乏则无出血风险）。 If aPTT is corrected by mixing, it suggests intrinsic pathway factor deficiency (e.g., FVIII, FIX, FXI; Cases 5 and 6: <30% have bleeding risk; FXII deficiency has no bleeding risk).

混合不能纠正aPTT，则需排查抗凝治疗（如肝素，见病例4）或抗磷脂抗体（狼疮抗凝物/磷脂抗体，无出血但有血栓风险），还需警惕因子抑制物（有出血风险）。 If aPTT is not corrected by mixing, consider anticoagulant therapy (e.g., heparin, Case #4), lupus anticoagulant (anti-phospholipid Ab, prothrombotic risk without bleeding), or factor inhibitors (anti-factor Ab, with bleeding risk).

老师讲授
混合实验是判别因子缺乏还是有抑制物的关键，能纠正即为缺乏，不能纠正多为抑制物或抗凝。 Teacher's note: The mixing study is the key to distinguish factor deficiency from inhibitors; correction means deficiency, no correction suggests inhibitors or anticoagulant effect73.
4
常用凝血公式Key Coagulation Formulas

PT比值 = 患者PT ÷ 正常PT PT ratio = patient PT / normal PT

aPTT比值 = 患者aPTT ÷ 正常aPTT aPTT ratio = patient aPTT / normal aPTT

5
概要总结Overall Summary

DIC是典型的多因子消耗性凝血障碍，见于严重的癌症、炎症或手术后，齐发出血与血栓风险，PT和aPTT皆延长，伴因子/纤维蛋白原/血小板下降。 DIC is a typical consumptive coagulopathy involving multiple factor depletion, seen with severe cancer, inflammation, or post-surgery, presenting both bleeding and thrombotic risk, with prolonged PT and aPTT, plus factor, fibrinogen, and platelet reductions.

鉴别多因子下降时应首查肝功能、D-二聚体等，判断是生成障碍还是消耗过度。 When multiple factor deficiencies are found, liver function tests and D-dimer help distinguish synthetic defects from excessive consumption.

老师强调合理选择检测路径，混合实验与相关因子检测至关重要。 Teachers emphasize the rational choice of diagnostic pathway; mixing study and targeted factor testing are crucial234.

032025-11-14

血栓形成
发病生理 Thrombosis: physiopathology

一、血栓形成的基本概念 Basic Concepts of Thrombosis

血栓形成是止血的一部分，可以阻止出血。 Thrombosis is part of hemostasis that prevents bleeding.

正常情况下，血管壁、细胞外基质和内皮细胞共同维持血液不凝固。 Under physiological conditions, vessel wall, extracellular matrix, and endothelial cells work together to keep blood from clotting.

二、生理性止血机制 Physiological Hemostasis Mechanisms

生理性止血包括两部分
Physiological hemostasis contains two stages:
初级止血
血小板塞形成。 Primary hemostasis: Formation of a platelet plug.
凝血过程
纤维蛋白网架的构建。 Coagulation: Construction of a fibrin network.

三、血栓的两类功能 Two Functions/Types of Thrombi

在生理状态下，血栓形成止血，有益于身体。 Under physiological states, thrombosis stops bleeding, which is beneficial.

在病理状态下，血栓造成血流阻断，导致组织低灌注和缺血。 Pathologically, thrombosis blocks blood flow, leading to hypoperfusion and ischemia.

四、血栓的定义与危害 Definition and Harm of Thrombus

血栓意味着血流阻断，从而导致组织缺血。 Thrombus means blood flow obstruction, which leads to tissue ischemia.

五、病理性血栓和生理性止血的区别 Difference Between Pathological Thrombosis and Physiological Hemostasis

两者都包括血小板塞和纤维蛋白网的形成。 Both include platelet plug formation and fibrin network construction.

区别在于病理性血栓最终是造成血流阻塞，危害机体。 The difference is that pathological thrombosis results in blood flow blockage, which harms the body.

六、止血与抗栓药物的应用 Application of Antiplatelet and Anticoagulant Drugs

针对初级止血障碍（血小板塞），应用抗血小板药物。 For primary hemostasis (platelet plug), antiplatelet drugs are used.

针对凝血过程障碍（纤维蛋白网），应用抗凝药物。 For coagulational process (fibrin network), anticoagulant drugs are used.

七、动脉与静脉血栓的区别 Difference Between Arterial and Venous Thrombosis

动脉血栓通常发生在动脉硬化损伤的血管，剪切力高，血栓富含血小板，需用抗血小板药物治疗。 Arterial thrombosis usually occurs at atherosclerotic injured vessels, is exposed to high shear forces, is platelet-rich, and needs antiplatelet drugs for treatment.

静脉血栓不与动脉粥样硬化相关，剪切力低，血栓富含纤维蛋白，需用抗凝药物治疗。 Venous thrombosis is not associated with atherosclerosis, has low shear forces, is fibrin-rich, and requires anticoagulant drugs for treatment.

八、血栓形成的临床意义与诊断 Clinical Significance and Diagnosis of Thrombosis

血栓可导致重要器官的缺血或梗死，需早期诊断和干预。 Thrombosis can cause ischemia or infarction in critical organs, requiring early diagnosis and intervention.

九、血栓的诊断方法 Diagnosis Methods of Thrombosis

体格检查
包括病史和临床背景、血栓可见体征（如肿胀、组织或温度改变）、心跳和血压测量。 Physical exam: Includes medical history and clinical context, visible signs of thrombosis (such as swelling, tissue or temperature changes), heart rate, and blood pressure.
影像学检查
包括血管X光（血管造影或静脉造影）、超声（多普勒）、磁共振成像（MRI）。 Imaging tests: Include X-rays of blood vessels (angiogram or venogram), ultrasound (Doppler), and magnetic resonance imaging (MRI).
血液检测
血小板计数，D-二聚体检测。 Blood tests: Platelet count and D-Dimer assessment.

十、不同类型血栓的指向性诊断 Targeted Diagnosis for Different Types of Thrombus

体格检查和血液检测有助于区分动脉血栓和静脉血栓。 Physical exam and blood tests help distinguish between arterial and venous thrombosis.

动脉血栓 Arterial Thrombosis

发生于血管壁损伤、动脉粥样硬化，高剪切力，富含血小板，用抗血小板药物治疗。 Occurs with vessel wall injury, atherosclerosis, high shear forces, platelet rich, treated with antiplatelet drugs.

静脉血栓 Venous Thrombosis

无粥样硬化、低剪切力，富含纤维蛋白，用抗凝药治疗。 No atherosclerosis, low shear forces, fibrin rich, treated with anticoagulant drugs.

静脉血栓症Venous thrombosis

一、静脉血栓栓塞性疾病（VTE）及下肢深静脉血栓形成（DVT）

Venous thromboembolic disease (VTE) and Deep Vein Thrombosis (DVT)

静脉血栓主要发生在下肢深静脉，包括股静脉、腘静脉和腓肠静脉。 Venous thrombosis mainly occurs in the deep veins of the lower limbs, including the femoral, popliteal, and calf veins.

按部位分为近端DVT和远端DVT。 DVT can be classified as proximal or distal according to location.

常见临床症状包括
Common clinical symptoms include:

下肢肿胀 Leg swelling

小腿开始的腿部疼痛、抽筋或不适 Leg pain, cramping or soreness that often starts in the calf

皮肤颜色变化（变红或变紫） Change in skin color on the leg (red or purple)

受累肢体感觉温热 A feeling of warmth on the affected leg

二、DVT的生命威胁并发症Pulmonary Embolism (PE): the Life-Threatening Complication of DVT

深静脉血栓可脱落，经静脉回流至右心房后进入肺动脉，形成肺栓塞（PE）。 Detached DVT can travel through venous return to the right atrium and then to the pulmonary arteries, forming pulmonary embolism (PE).

肺栓塞是DVT最严重的并发症，具有生命威胁性。 PE is the most severe and life-threatening complication of DVT.

DVT本身多数情况下不会导致局部组织缺血，因为静脉回流阻断并不导致局部供氧中断，但PE则可能危及生命。 DVT itself usually does not cause local tissue ischemia since venous blockage does not interrupt local oxygen supply, but PE can be life-threatening.

三、静脉血栓发生的机制及Virchow三联征Mechanisms and Virchow's Triad

Virchow三联征包括三方面
Virchow's triad includes three factors:
1
血流异常（如静脉扩张或瓣膜损伤导致血流淤滞） Abnormal blood flow (such as blood stasis caused by dilated veins or damaged valves)
2
血管内皮损伤（如手术、外伤、炎症、癌症或氧化应激） Endothelial injury (such as from surgery, trauma, inflammation, cancer, or oxidative stress)
3
高凝状态（如抗凝因子缺陷、凝血因子突变或抗磷脂综合征等） Hypercoagulability (such as deficiency in anticoagulant factors, coagulation factor mutations, or antiphospholipid syndrome)

静脉血液淤滞是最常见的血栓风险之一，可由老化、活动受限（如长期卧床、手术、石膏固定）、肥胖等引起。 Venous stasis is one of the most frequent thrombosis risks, and can be caused by aging, immobilization (such as bed rest, surgery, limb casting), obesity, etc.

四、静脉血栓风险因素Risk Factors for Venous Thromboembolism

获得性风险因素包括
Acquired risk factors include:

手术 Surgery

年龄 Age

长期不动（长途旅行、住院、卧床等） Immobilization (travel, hospitalization, bed rest, etc.)

肥胖 Obesity

肿瘤 Cancer

妊娠 Pregnancy

激素治疗 Hormonal treatment

遗传性风险因素包括
Genetic risk factors include:

抗凝血酶（AT）、蛋白C（PC）、蛋白S（PS）缺陷 Deficiency in antithrombin (AT), protein C (PC), protein S (PS)

第五因子Leiden突变（FV Leiden） Factor V Leiden mutation (FV Leiden)

第二因子G20210A突变（FII Mutation） Factor II G20210A mutation (FII Mutation)

五、生物性血栓倾向相关的风险分层Risk Stratification of Thrombophilia by Biological Markers

生物学性血栓倾向导致的风险因年龄而异
The risk associated with biological thrombophilia varies with age:
成人
FII 20210A杂合、FV Leiden杂合、PC或PS缺陷，风险增加3-5倍 Adults: FII 20210A heterozygotes, FV Leiden heterozygotes, PC or PS deficiency, 3-5 fold increased risk
青年人
AT缺陷杂合、FV Leiden纯合、FV Leiden合并FII 20210A、FV Leiden合并AT/PC/PS缺陷，风险增加10-20倍 Young adults: AT deficiency heterozygotes, FV Leiden homozygotes, FV Leiden + FII 20210A, FV Leiden + AT/PC/PS deficiency, 10-20 fold increased risk
新生儿
PC、PS或AT纯合缺陷，风险大于100倍 Newborns: Homozygous deficiency in PC, PS or AT, risk over 100-fold

六、老师补充强调内容 Teacher’s Additional Emphases

DVT常见于下肢深静脉，尤其是腘静脉和股静脉，但更危险的是血栓脱落引起肺栓塞，因PE可以短时间内危及生命，要高度关注和早期干预。 DVT is common in the deep veins of the lower limbs, especially the popliteal and femoral veins, but the greater danger is embolism causing PE, which can threaten life quickly—thus early detection and intervention are critical.

单纯依赖症状不足以确诊静脉血栓，需结合影像学等客观检查，如超声和D-二聚体检测。 Diagnosis of venous thrombosis cannot rely on symptoms alone; objective tests such as ultrasound and D-dimer analysis are necessary.

七、相关危险因素的发生概率及OR值 Occurrence Rates and Odds Ratios of Risk Factors

部分典型获得性危险因素的血栓OR值
Typical odds ratios (OR) for acquired risk factors:
下肢石膏固定
OR 36 Casts of the lower limbs: OR 36
骨科手术
OR 16 Orthopedic surgery: OR 16
长期卧床或不动
OR 7 Immobilization: OR 7
长途旅行
OR 1.6 Long travel: OR 1.6

八、Virchow三联征提出的重要意义 Significance of Virchow's Triad

Virchow三联征自1858年提出，至今仍指导血栓形成机制和临床预防措施。 Since its proposal by Dr. Virchow in 1858, the Virchow’s triad remains key for understanding thrombosis and guiding prevention in clinical practice.

DVT诊断与治疗 DVT diagnosis and treatments

一、DVT临床评分与诊断流程 Clinical Score and Diagnostic Pathway for DVT

深静脉血栓形成（DVT）的诊断需结合临床评分系统。 The diagnosis of deep vein thrombosis (DVT) requires utilization of clinical scoring systems.

Wells评分系统用于评估DVT的可能性，各临床变量赋予不同分值。 The Wells score is used to evaluate the clinical probability of DVT, assigning points to different clinical variables.

活动性肿瘤（正在治疗或6月内/姑息）1分 Active cancer (treatment ongoing or within previous 6 months or palliative): 1 point

下肢瘫痪、麻痹或近期石膏固定 1分 Paralysis, paresis, or recent plaster immobilization of the lower extremities: 1 point

近3天或以上卧床，或12周内经历大手术 1分 Recently bedridden for 3 days or more, or major surgery within previous 12 weeks requiring general or regional anesthesia: 1 point

深静脉沿走向的局部压痛 1分 Localized tenderness along the distribution of the deep venous system: 1 point

整条腿肿胀 1分 Entire leg swelling: 1 point

患侧小腿肿胀较对侧大3cm以上（距胫骨结节下10cm处测量）1分 Calf swelling at least 3 cm larger than that on the asymptomatic leg (measured 10 cm below tibial tuberosity): 1 point

限于患肢的凹陷性水肿 1分 Pitting edema confined to the symptomatic leg: 1 point

表浅侧支静脉（非静脉曲张）1分 Collateral superficial veins (nonvaricose): 1 point

既往DVT 1分 Previously documented DVT: 1 point

其他诊断至少与DVT同样可能 -2分 Alternative diagnosis at least as likely as DVT: -2 points

评分结果风险分层
Risk stratification based on total score:

低概率 ≤ 0分 Weak probability: ≤ 0

中等概率 1-2分 Moderate probability: 1–2

高概率 ≥ 3分 High probability: ≥ 3

二、DVT的影像学与检验诊断 Imaging and Laboratory Diagnosis of DVT

高概率患者应首选超声多普勒（Venous echo-Doppler）检查以确认DVT。 In patients with high clinical probability, venous echo-Doppler ultrasound should be the first diagnostic step.

如果超声未见DVT，但临床怀疑度高，继续行静脉造影（phlebography）进一步排查。 If Doppler is negative while clinical suspicion remains high, phlebography (venography) should be performed for further evaluation.

低或中等概率者，首先检测D-二聚体（D-dimer）。 For weak or moderate probability cases, D-dimer testing is done first.

若D-dimer≤500μg/L，可排除DVT。 If D-dimer ≤ 500 μg/L: DVT can be excluded.

若D-dimer>500μg/L，推荐进一步做超声多普勒检查。 If D-dimer > 500 μg/L: venous echo-Doppler is recommended.

Doppler若阴性，基本可排除DVT；若阳性，确诊DVT。 If Doppler is negative, DVT can be ruled out; if positive, DVT is confirmed.

三、特殊情况下的生物性血栓倾向检测 When to Search for Biological Thrombophilia

以下情况需评估是否存在生物性血栓倾向
Testing for biological thrombophilia should be considered in the following situations:

年轻人（<45岁）发生血栓 Thrombosis in young subjects (<45 years)

复发性血栓 Recurrent thrombosis

血栓发生部位不寻常 Thrombosis of unusual localization

血栓形成为自发性 Spontaneous thrombosis, particularly when other risk factors are absent

有家族史 Family history of DVT

四、DVT治疗原则 Principles of DVT Treatment

抗凝药物作用于凝血过程，可以防止血栓进一步增长，但不能溶解已形成血栓。 Anticoagulants act on coagulation, preventing further clot growth, but do not dissolve established thrombi.

肺栓塞（PE）可能需要溶栓治疗（纤溶治疗），属于危及生命时的急诊措施。 Pulmonary embolism (PE) may require thrombolytic therapy (fibrinolysis) as an emergency life-saving measure.

五、三类抗凝治疗药物 Three Classes of Anticoagulant Therapies

肝素类（静脉注射/皮下注射） Heparins (intravenous or subcutaneous)

维生素K拮抗剂（VKA，口服） Vitamin K antagonists (VKA, oral)

直接口服抗凝药（DOAC，口服） Direct Oral AntiCoagulants (DOAC, oral)

六、老师补充强调 Teacher's Additional Remarks

判断DVT风险时，首要应用临床评分，但确诊仍需结合客观检查（D-二聚体、超声等）。 Initial DVT risk stratification relies on clinical scoring, but objective tests (D-dimer, ultrasound) are required for definitive diagnosis.

对于一些高风险或特殊病例，还需关注遗传/生物学性因素是否影响抗凝治疗策略。 For high-risk or special cases, underlying genetic or biological factors may alter anticoagulation management.

抗凝治疗的主要目标是防止血栓进展、并发症（如PE），而非立即溶解栓子。 The main aim of anticoagulation is to prevent clot extension and complications (especially PE), not to immediately dissolve existing thrombus.

抗凝药物Anticoagulants

一、主要抗凝药物的作用机制 Mechanism of Main Anticoagulants

抗凝药物通过减少FIIa（凝血酶）的生成，抑制血栓的进一步生长。 Anticoagulants reduce the generation of FIIa (thrombin) and thus inhibit further clot growth.

主要抗凝药物包括维生素K拮抗剂（VKA）、肝素类（Heparins）、直接口服抗凝药（DOAC）。 The main anticoagulants include Vitamin K antagonists (VKA), heparins, and direct oral anticoagulants (DOAC).

VKA通过抑制凝血因子FII、FVII、FIX、FX的合成，间接减少凝血酶（FIIa）的产生。 VKA inhibits synthesis of FII, FVII, FIX, and FX, thus indirectly reducing FIIa (thrombin) production.

肝素类通过激活抗凝血酶（AT），增强其对FXa和FIIa的抑制作用。 Heparins enhance the activity of antithrombin (AT), promoting its inhibition of FXa and FIIa.

DOAC直接抑制FXa或FIIa活性，迅速阻断凝血级联反应。 DOAC directly inhibits FXa or FIIa activity, rapidly interrupting the coagulation cascade.

所有抗凝药物的核心作用是减少FIIa生成，从而减少血块增大。 The key role of all anticoagulants is to reduce the amount of FIIa produced and thus restrict clot enlargement.

二、肝素类药物作用与分类 Heparins: Effect and Classification

肝素类药物为硫酸化多糖，经静脉或皮下注射使用。 Heparins are sulfated polysaccharides, administered intravenously (IV) or subcutaneously (SC)23.

肝素通过与抗凝血酶（AT）结合，增强其对FIIa与FXa的抑制作用。 Heparin binds antithrombin (AT), strongly potentiating the inhibition of FIIa and FXa23.

按分子量和链长分为三类
Heparins are classified by molecular weight and chain length:

未分级肝素（UFH）：长链，平均分子量14-18 kD Unfractionated heparin (UFH): long chain, MW 14-18 kD

低分子量肝素（LMWH）：较短链，平均分子量约4 kD Low molecular weight heparin (LMWH): shorter chain, MW ~4 kD

磺达肝癸钠（Fondaparinux, Arixtra®）：最短的链，分子量1.7 kD Fondaparinux (Arixtra®): shortest chain able to bind AT, MW 1.7 kD

UFH和LMWH均为动物来源，Arixtra为化学产品。 UFH and LMWH are animal products, while Arixtra is a chemical product23.

三、肝素作用的分子机制 Molecular Mechanism of Heparin Activity

肝素通过其带负电的硫酸基（SO₃⁻）与抗凝血酶正电氨基结合，依赖多点离子相互作用。 Heparin binds AT via its negatively charged sulfate groups (SO₃⁻) and the cationic sites on AT, involving multiple ionic interactions.

结合肝素后，AT暴露活性位点（Arg 393），显著增强对靶酶的抑制活性。 After heparin binding, AT exposes its active site (Arg 393), greatly strengthening inhibition of the target enzymes.

结构学显示肝素结合使AT构象改变，P1位点暴露，利于与FXa、FIIa结合。 Structural studies show conformational change in AT upon heparin binding, exposing the P1 site for efficient FXa/FIIa inhibition23.

四、链长决定抑制效果 Effect of Chain Length on Inhibition

短链肝素（如Arixtra）仅增强AT对FXa的抑制（即主要抗FXa），但对FIIa无促进作用。 Short heparin chains (such as Arixtra) mainly enhance AT–FXa inhibition (anti-FXa effect only), with no effect on FIIa23.

低分子量肝素（LMWH）以抗FXa为主，长链肝素（UFH）可并行增强对FIIa和FXa的抑制。 LMWH mainly exerts anti-FXa effect, while long-chain UFH potentiates both anti-FIIa and anti-FXa effects23.

五、分子机制补充 Detailed Mechanism Supplement

短链肝素仅通过别构（allosteric）机制，AT对FXa抑制率提升约300倍，但对FIIa无显著增强。 Short-chain heparin works via allosteric mechanism, increases AT–FXa inhibition up to 300-fold, but has minimal effect on AT–FIIa inhibition.

长链肝素可引入“桥接”效应（bridging mechanism），促进AT和FIIa同时结合及反应，AT对FIIa抑制率增加4000倍，对FXa提升约600倍。 Long-chain heparin enables a "bridging" effect, facilitating simultaneous binding and reaction of AT and FIIa. Inhibition rate increases 4000-fold for FIIa and about 600-fold for FXa.

所以，UFH既有抗FIIa又有抗FXa作用，LMWH主要抗FXa，Arixtra仅抗FXa。 Thus, UFH has both anti-FIIa and anti-FXa effect, LMWH mainly anti-FXa, and Arixtra only anti-FXa23.

六、老师讲解强调 Teacher’s Key Teaching Points

肝素类药物通过AT活化而实现抗凝，不同链长决定选择临床适应症及监测需求。 Heparins function via AT activation, and different chain lengths determine clinical applications and monitoring needs.

长链肝素须静脉注射且需监测，因为生物利用度和个体差异大；短链和化学合成肝素皮下注射、个体差异小。 Long-chain heparins require IV administration and monitoring due to low bioavailability and high variability; short-chain/chemical heparins are given SC with less variability.

七、分类总结 Summary Table

UFH（长链，动物来源）：抗FIIa和FXa UFH (long chain, animal): anti-FIIa and anti-FXa

LMWH（较短链，动物来源）：主要抗FXa LMWH (shorter chain, animal): mainly anti-FXa

Arixtra（最短链，化学产品）：仅抗FXa Arixtra (shortest chain, chemical): only anti-FXa23.

抗凝药物Anticoagulants

一、肝素类抗凝药物（静脉注射或皮下注射）Heparins (IV or SC)

未分级肝素（UFH）半衰期短，仅1-2小时。 Unfractionated heparin (UFH) has a short half-life, only 1-2 hours.

低分子量肝素（LMWH）半衰期约4小时。 Low molecular weight heparin (LMWH) has a half-life of about 4 hours.

磺达肝癸钠（Fondaparinux）半衰期为15小时。 Fondaparinux has a half-life of 15 hours.

UFH生物利用度低（约10%），且个体差异大。 UFH has low bioavailability (~10%) and high interindividual variability.

LMWH生物利用度高（>90%），个体差异小。 LMWH has high bioavailability (>90%) and low interindividual variability.

磺达肝癸钠生物利用度100%，个体差异极小。 Fondaparinux has 100% bioavailability and no interindividual variability.

UFH需强制监测APTT（活化部分凝血活酶时间）。 UFH requires mandatory monitoring by APTT (activated partial thromboplastin time).

LMWH仅在肾功能衰竭、极端体重或出血时需监测。 LMWH monitoring is only required if there is renal failure, extreme weight, or hemorrhage.

磺达肝癸钠同理，仅在肾衰、极端体重或出血时需监测。 Fondaparinux requires monitoring only in cases of renal failure, extreme weight, or hemorrhage.

UFH经肾脏与细胞共同清除。 UFH is eliminated by both kidneys and cellular mechanisms.

LMWH主要经肾脏清除。 LMWH is mainly eliminated by the kidneys.

磺达肝癸钠仅经肾脏清除。 Fondaparinux is eliminated only by the kidneys.

UFH和LMWH都可致肝素相关性血小板减少症。 Both UFH and LMWH may cause heparin-induced thrombocytopenia.

磺达肝癸钠无特效解毒剂，出血时无法特异性逆转。 Fondaparinux has no antidote; bleeding cannot be specifically reversed.

二、维生素K拮抗剂（VKA，口服）Vitamin K Antagonists (VKA, oral route)

维生素K是合成功能性Gla结构域所必需。 Vitamin K is required for functional Gla domain synthesis.

VKA抑制依赖维生素K的凝血因子的合成。 VKAs impair the synthesis of vitamin K-dependent factors.

主要影响因子有FVII、FIX、FX、FII、蛋白C（PC）、蛋白S（PS）。 Main targets are FVII, FIX, FX, FII, protein C (PC), and protein S (PS).

维生素K从醌态（VK Quinone）经还原为KH2形式，再被γ-羧化酶用于Glu到Gla的转化。 Vitamin K cycles from quinone (VK Quinone) to hydroquinone (KH2), then is used by γ-carboxylase to convert Glu to Gla.

VKAs通过阻断VKOR（维生素K环氧还原酶），阻断该循环，导致凝血因子无法羧化成熟。 VKAs block VKOR (vitamin K epoxide reductase), blocking recycling and preventing functional factor production.

三、香豆素类（如华法林，醋硫香豆素）和氟马隆 Coumarins (warfarin, acenocoumarol) and fluindion

作用延迟，需要3-4天才能起效（与作用机制相关）。 There is a delayed onset, taking 3-4 days to work due to their mechanism.

代谢依赖CYP酶，经各种食物和药物影响，个体差异大。 Metabolism is CYP-dependent, resulting in many food/drug interactions and high interindividual differences.

需定期监测INR（国际标准化比值，PT病人/PT正常血浆）。 Monitoring via INR (International Normalized Ratio; PT patient/PT normal plasma) is required.

预防DVT或PE的靶INR为2-3。 The target INR for prevention of DVT or PE is 2-3.

INR3时出血风险增加。 If INR 3, bleeding risk rises.

逆转措施包括补充维生素K依赖因子浓缩物或补充维生素K。 Reversal includes vitamin K-dependent factor concentrate and/or vitamin K.

四、老师补充讲解要点 Teacher's additional key points

UFH需密切监测，因为其生物利用度低且个体反应差异大。 UFH requires close monitoring due to low and unpredictable bioavailability.

LMWH和磺达肝癸钠多用于门诊，更安全，监测要求较低。 LMWH and fondaparinux are more suitable for outpatient use, with lower monitoring requirements and a better safety profile.

VKAs作用机制决定起效慢，且用药期间需保持多因素稳定。 The slow onset of VKAs means therapy must be managed carefully, maintaining stable conditions during use.

任何抗凝药物使用都需权衡出血风险。 The bleeding risk of any anticoagulant must always be weighed.

磺达肝癸钠无特异性解毒剂，遇大出血时临床措施有限。 Fondaparinux has no specific antidote, so clinical response to serious bleeding is limited.

抗凝药物——DOAC类抗凝药物Anticoagulants—DOAC Class Anticoagulants

一、DOAC的定义和作用机制Definition and Mechanism of DOACs

DOAC指直接口服抗凝药（Direct Oral AntiCoagulants, oral route）。 DOAC means Direct Oral AntiCoagulants (oral route).

DOAC是一类直接、可逆且竞争性抑制凝血酶或凝血因子Xa的酶抑制剂。 DOACs are direct, reversible, and competitive enzyme inhibitors targeting thrombin or factor Xa.

其作用机制为直接结合凝血酶（FIIa）或FXa活性位点，阻止底物结合，抑制凝血瀑布反应。 Their mechanism is direct binding to the active site of thrombin (FIIa) or FXa, blocking substrate access and inhibiting the coagulation cascade.

结构上，DOAC分子通过空间互补，与靶酶活性口袋密切结合，并且这种结合是可逆的。 Structurally, DOAC molecules fit into the enzyme's active pocket, and this interaction is reversible.

二、主要DOAC药物种类与靶点Types and Targets of Main DOACs

达比加群（Dabigatran, Pradaxa）直接抑制凝血酶（FIIa）。 Dabigatran (Pradaxa) directly inhibits thrombin (FIIa).

利伐沙班（Rivaroxaban, Xarelto）、阿哌沙班（Apixaban, Eliquis）、依度沙班（Edoxaban, Lixiana）均属于“Xaban类”，直接抑制FXa。 Rivaroxaban (Xarelto), Apixaban (Eliquis), Edoxaban (Lixiana) all belong to the “Xaban” class, directly inhibiting FXa.

“Xaban”类名称来源于抑制对FXa的选择性。 The “Xaban” suffix comes from their selectivity for FXa inhibition.

三、DOAC相较于VKA的优势Advantages of DOACs vs. VKA

DOAC起效迅速，峰浓度达成时间（t_max）约3小时。 DOACs have rapid onset, with time to peak concentration (t_max) around 3 hours.

食物和药物相互作用极少。 There are very few food and drug interactions.

个体间反应差异小。 Inter-individual variability is low.

通过肾脏排泄，药代动力学可预测。 They are eliminated via the kidneys, and have predictable pharmacokinetics.

一般无需常规生化监测，除特殊情况如肾衰、严重出血或需大手术时。 No routine biochemical monitoring required except in special cases such as renal failure, severe bleeding, or major surgery.

INR检测不适用于DOAC，也不反映抗FXa或抗FIIa活性。 INR assays are not suitable for DOAC, nor do they reflect anti-FXa or anti-FIIa activity.

四、DOAC的主要用途及临床风险Clinical Uses and Risks of DOACs

DOAC用于深静脉血栓（DVT）和肺栓塞（PE）急性期治疗。 DOACs are used for acute-phase treatment of deep vein thrombosis (DVT) and pulmonary embolism (PE).

可用于DVT/PE长期预防（根据具体临床情况）；短期可用肝素类。 They are used in DVT/PE long-term prevention (depending on context); heparins for short-term.

房颤患者脑卒中预防也推荐长期应用DOAC。 For stroke prevention in atrial fibrillation (AF), long-term DOAC is recommended.

但DOAC与VKA一样存在同等的出血风险。 However, DOACs have a similar bleeding risk compared with VKA.

五、DOAC的逆转剂Reversal Agents for DOACs

达比加群（Dabigatran）专属逆转剂为伊达鲁珠单抗（Idarucizumab, Paxbind），为人源化单克隆抗体，可强力置换达比加群与FIIa的结合，几乎不可逆。 Idarucizumab (Paxbind) is a humanized monoclonal antibody specific for dabigatran, potently displacing it from FIIa binding, and is almost irreversible.

对于“Xaban类”药物（利伐沙班、阿哌沙班、依度沙班），逆转剂为安达昔单抗（Andexanet-alpha, Ondexxya），为重组、无Gla结构域的“假”FXa，可竞争性结合药物，从而解除其抗凝作用。 For “Xaban” drugs (rivaroxaban, apixaban, edoxaban), the reversal agent is Andexanet-alpha (Ondexxya), a recombinant inactive Gla domain-less FXa decoy that binds the drug and neutralizes the anticoagulant effect.

六、DOAC的主要适应症和选择Indications and Selection of DOACs

DVT/PE急性期
可选肝素类或DOAC。 DVT/PE acute phase: choose heparin or DOAC.
DVT/PE预防
如需长期，选DOAC或VKA，短期选肝素。 DVT/PE prevention: for long-term, use DOAC or VKA; for short term, use heparin.
房颤卒中预防
长期DOAC（若已用VKA则继续VKA）。 Stroke prevention in AF: long-term DOAC (continue VKA if already on VKA).
肾衰透析患者
首选肝素类，不建议DOAC。 Kidney failure/dialysis: heparin is preferred, DOACs not recommended.

七、老师补充讲解关键点Teacher’s Key Teaching Points

DOAC为口服小分子，直接靶向酶活性位点，起效快，且大多数情况下无需监测。 DOACs are oral small molecules that directly target enzyme active sites, act quickly, and generally do not require monitoring.

DOAC使用后，如遇大出血，不能用INR监测，只能用特殊检测方法或相应逆转药。 INR is useless for DOACs during bleeding events; specific assays or reversal agents must be used.

DOAC已有专门逆转药物，极大提升安全性，但仍需关注肾功能。 The availability of specific reversal drugs increases their safety, but renal function must always be considered.

临床上DOAC正在逐步取代VKA，用于各种血栓性疾病的长期管理。 Clinically, DOACs are gradually replacing VKAs in long-term management of various thrombotic disorders.

血栓症
诊断与动脉性血栓的处理Thrombosis: Diagnosis and Management of Arterial Thrombosis

一、血栓症的诊断方法 Diagnosis of Thrombosis

体格检查 Physical exam

询问病史和临床背景 Medical history/Clinical context

观察血栓形成的可见体征（如肿胀、组织或温度变化） Visible signs of thrombosis (swelling, tissue or temperature changes)

测量心率和血压 Heart pulse, blood pressure

影像学检查 Imaging tests

血管X线检查（血管造影或静脉造影） X-rays of blood vessels (angiogram or venogram)

超声检查（多普勒） Ultrasound (Doppler)

磁共振成像（MRI） Magnetic resonance imaging (MRI)

血液检查 Blood tests

血小板计数 Platelets count

D-二聚体检测 D-Dimer

老师补充讲解要点 Teacher’s Additional Points

老师强调深静脉血栓形成的症状多不特异，例如腿部肿胀、疼痛、皮肤颜色或温度变化等，并提醒诊断需借助客观检测手段。 The teacher emphasized that symptoms of deep vein thrombosis (DVT) are often non-specific, such as swelling, pain, skin color or temperature changes in the leg, and stressed the necessity of objective diagnostic tools.

重点是静脉血栓的最大威胁在于血栓脱落引起的肺栓塞，临床需高度警惕。 A key point is that the greatest danger of venous thrombosis is pulmonary embolism caused by thrombus dislodgement; clinicians must be vigilant.

二、动脉性血栓及其临床表现 Arterial Thrombosis and Clinical Manifestations

冠状动脉血栓 → 心肌梗死（MI） Coronary arteries thrombosis → Myocardial infarction (MI)

脑动脉血栓 → 缺血性脑卒中 Brain arteries thrombosis → Ischemic Stroke

下肢动脉血栓 → 肢体缺血/外周动脉疾病（PAD） Leg arteries thrombosis → Limb ischemia/Peripheral Artery Disease (PAD)

老师补充讲解要点 Teacher’s Additional Points

动脉血栓的临床后果取决于血栓所在的器官，心脏致冠心病，脑部致卒中，肢体可引发坏死。 Clinical outcomes of arterial thrombosis depend on the affected organ: heart (coronary disease), brain (stroke), or limb (necrosis).

三、动脉性血栓的急诊处理 Emergency Treatment of Arterial Thrombosis

溶栓治疗（纤溶）：用于心梗、脑卒中等急症 Thrombolytic therapy (fibrinolysis): Emergency treatment for heart attacks, strokes, etc.

血栓切除术
用于急性心梗、卒中等 Thrombectomy: Emergency treatment for heart attacks, strokes, etc.

经皮冠状动脉介入（PCI）：针对心肌梗死的紧急处理 Percutaneous coronary intervention (PCI): Emergency treatment for myocardial infarction

老师补充讲解要点 Teacher’s Additional Points

急症溶栓和血管再通手段必须争分夺秒，否则缺血可致组织不可逆损伤。 Rapid application of lytic/reperfusion measures is critical in emergencies; otherwise, ischemia leads to irreversible tissue damage.

四、动脉性血栓的二级预防与抗血小板药物 Secondary Prevention and Antiplatelet Therapy for Arterial Thrombosis

动脉斑块破裂与高剪切力共同导致动脉血栓形成 Arterial thrombosis is caused by plaque rupture and high shear force

动脉血栓以血小板为主，抗血小板药物为防治核心 Arterial thrombosis is platelet-rich; thus, antiplatelet drugs are the mainstay of prevention and treatment

阻断3大靶点（COX-1、P2Y12、GPIIbIIIa）的抗血小板药物广泛用于高危患者 Antiplatelet drugs targeting 3 main pathways (COX-1, P2Y12, GPIIbIIIa) are widely used in high-risk patients

老师补充讲解要点 Teacher’s Additional Points

动脉硬化易损斑块破裂是动脉血栓的根本原因，且预防复发需长期抗血小板药物。 Vulnerable plaque rupture is the root cause of arterial thrombosis; long-term antiplatelet therapy is mandatory for secondary prevention.

有需多联合用药以多靶点强化抑制，以降低再发风险。 Multi-drug (dual or triple) therapy may be required to simultaneously block multiple targets and minimize recurrence risk.

五、动脉血栓与静脉血栓的区别 Difference between Arterial and Venous Thrombosis

动脉血栓多为白色血栓，以血小板为主；静脉血栓多为红色血栓，以纤维蛋白和红细胞为主。 Arterial thrombosis is usually white (platelet-rich); venous thrombosis is usually red (fibrin and RBC-rich).

动脉血栓预防主要靠抗血小板药；静脉血栓则侧重抗凝药。 Arterial thrombosis prevention relies on antiplatelet agents, while venous thrombosis relies on anticoagulants.

初级止血过程Primary Hemostasis

一、血小板激活的三步过程Three-step Process of Platelet Activation

血小板激活分为三个主要步骤
1.黏附；2.激活（细胞骨架重塑与分泌）；3.聚集。 Platelet activation consists of three major steps: 1. Adhesion; 2. Activation (cytoskeleton remodeling and secretion); 3. Aggregation.
二、第一步
血小板黏附 Step 1: Platelet Adhesion

静息血小板最初通过表面的GPIb受体与血浆中的vWF（血管性血友病因子）进行弱相互作用，实现初步黏附。 Resting platelets initially adhere via GPIb receptors on their surface to plasma von Willebrand factor (vWF), forming a weak interaction.

此过程中，剪切力使得vWF结构由球状变为拉长状，暴露结合位点，促进血小板黏附于受损血管。 In this process, shear stress causes globular vWF to unfold and elongate, exposing binding sites and enhancing platelet adhesion to damaged vessels.

黏附后，GPIb-vWF的结合诱导血小板表达GPVI受体，随后GPVI与胶原实现紧密结合，增强黏附强度。 Upon adhesion, GPIb-vWF binding induces expression of GPVI, which then binds firmly to collagen, strengthening adhesion.

三、第二步
血小板激活 Step 2: Platelet Activation

血小板激活涉及细胞骨架重塑，使血小板由扁平变为圆形并伸展伪足。 Platelet activation involves cytoskeleton remodeling, transforming platelets from flat to round shapes with pseudopod extensions.

激活的血小板通过胞内信号通路启动颗粒释放。 Activated platelets trigger granule secretion via intracellular signaling pathways.

分泌物包括α颗粒（含vWF, 纤维蛋白原, PF4）和致密颗粒（含ADP, 5-羟色胺, Ca2+）。 Secreted materials include α-granules (vWF, fibrinogen, PF4) and dense granules (ADP, serotonin, Ca2+).

ADP等分泌物可自分泌（自分泌正反馈激活自身），也通过旁分泌激活周围血小板，形成放大效应。 Molecules like ADP act in both autocrine (self-amplification) and paracrine (activating nearby platelets) ways, amplifying the response.

ADP作用于血小板表面P2Y12、P2Y1受体，进一步促进激活和聚集信号级联。 ADP binds to platelet surface P2Y12 and P2Y1 receptors, further promoting signal cascade for activation and aggregation.

四、血小板激活伴随过程Concomitant Events with Platelet Activation

除了分泌作用，血小板激活还包括“翻转”现象（Flip-Flop），即磷脂酰丝氨酸（PS）暴露在血小板表面，形成促凝血的磷脂表面。 Besides secretion, platelet activation includes membrane “flip-flop” exposing phosphatidylserine (PS), creating a procoagulant surface.

激活过程中，血小板合成并释放血栓素A2（TXA2），可通过被动扩散作用于自身（自分泌）和临近血小板（旁分泌），通过TP受体介导信号转导。 During activation, platelets synthesize and release thromboxane A2 (TXA2), which passively diffuses to act on itself (autocrine) and on nearby platelets (paracrine), signaling via TP receptors.

TXA2的合成途径为
磷脂酶A2使膜磷脂产生花生四烯酸，后者经COX（环氧合酶）和TX合酶变为TXA2。 TXA2 is synthesized as follows: phospholipase A2 liberates arachidonic acid from membrane phospholipids, which is then converted to TXA2 via COX (cyclo-oxygenase) and TX synthase.
五、第三步
血小板聚集 Step 3: Platelet Aggregation

激活的血小板表面表达GPIIbIIIa受体，可与血浆中的纤维蛋白原结合，通过GPIIbIIIa/纤维蛋白原复合物相互连接，实现血小板聚集。 Activated platelets express GPIIbIIIa receptors that bind plasma fibrinogen, forming GPIIbIIIa/fibrinogen complexes that cross-link platelets and drive aggregation.

这个聚集过程是初级止血形成血小板血栓的关键环节。 This aggregation is a key step for primary hemostatic plug formation.

六、老师补充讲解Teacher’s Additional Explanation

老师强调，血小板激活既有放大效应（不断招募和激活更多血小板），也是止血的启动事件，为后续凝血级联反应提供促凝表面。 The teacher emphasized that platelet activation has an amplification effect (recruiting and activating more platelets continuously) and is the initiating event of hemostasis, providing a procoagulant surface for the subsequent coagulation cascade.

血小板单独形成初级止血塞，但只有在纤维蛋白网和凝血系统协作下，才能实现稳固的止血。 Platelets alone form the primary hemostatic plug, but stable hemostasis is achieved only through cooperation with the fibrin network and coagulation system.

抗血小板药物Antiplatelet drugs

一、P2Y12受体抑制剂（ADP受体）P2Y12 inhibitors (ADP receptor)

该类药物通过抑制血小板表面的P2Y12受体，减少ADP介导的血小板聚集。 These drugs inhibit the P2Y12 receptor on platelet surface, reducing ADP-mediated platelet aggregation.

代表药物有氯吡格雷、普拉格雷和替格瑞洛。 Representative agents include clopidogrel, prasugrel, and ticagrelor.

二、普拉格雷与氯吡格雷对比Prasugrel vs. Clopidogrel

普拉格雷抑制血小板聚集效果更强，并且个体差异较小，新陈代谢过程更简单。 Prasugrel inhibits platelet aggregation more potently, with lower interindividual variability and a simpler metabolism.

图中显示在64名患者中，普拉格雷对ADP诱导的血小板聚集抑制率更高，分布更集中，减少了背景变异。 The figure shows, among 64 patients, prasugrel achieves higher and more consistent inhibition of ADP-induced platelet aggregation, reducing background variability.

但老师补充指出，更强的抗血小板效果也带来更高的出血风险，因此用药时必须充分评估出血倾向。 However, the teacher emphasized that the stronger antiplatelet effect comes with a higher risk of bleeding, so bleeding tendency must be carefully assessed when prescribing.

三、临床研究结论与安全性Clinical trial outcomes and Safety

大型研究表明，普拉格雷能进一步降低心血管死亡/非致死性心梗或脑卒中的发生率（12.1%降至9.9%，显著优于氯吡格雷）。 Large clinical trials show prasugrel further reduces the incidence of cardiovascular death/non-fatal MI/stroke (from 12.1% to 9.9%), significantly better than clopidogrel.

但与此同时，普拉格雷组严重出血事件略高于氯吡格雷（2.4%对1.8%）。 At the same time, major bleeding was slightly higher in the prasugrel group compared to clopidogrel (2.4% vs 1.8%).

老师强调
临床上需平衡强力抑制与安全风险，尤其关注高危出血患者。 The teacher highlighted that clinical practice must balance potent inhibition with safety, especially in high-risk bleeding patients.

四、替格瑞洛与氯吡格雷对比Ticagrelor vs. Clopidogrel

替格瑞洛抗血小板效果更强，且总体安全性与氯吡格雷相当。 Ticagrelor provides stronger antiplatelet effect, with overall safety similar to clopidogrel.

临床数据显示，替格瑞洛组死亡率低于氯吡格雷（11.7%对9.8%，p<0.001），但出血事件比例无显著差异。 Clinical data show ticagrelor reduces mortality compared with clopidogrel (11.7% vs 9.8%, p<0.001), while bleeding rates show no significant difference.

替格瑞洛具有多效性，除抗血小板作用外，还可通过腺苷相关途径产生心脏保护等多种效应。 Ticagrelor has pleiotropic effects, including cardioprotection via adenosine-mediated mechanisms, in addition to antiplatelet action.

但这种多效性同时可能导致心律异常（如室性停搏、呼吸困难等），推测与ADP增加相关。 However, these multiple effects may also cause side effects (ventricular pauses, dyspnea, etc.), likely related to increased ADP.

老师讲解
服用替格瑞洛时需警惕特殊不良反应，尤其是心脏和呼吸系统异常，并加强监测。 The teacher explained that ticagrelor use requires monitoring for specific adverse effects, especially cardiac and respiratory disturbances.

五、双重抗血小板治疗Dual antiplatelet therapy：P2Y12抑制剂+阿司匹林

双药治疗常采用P2Y12受体抑制剂联合阿司匹林，分别阻断ADP途径与环氧酶（COX-1）/TXA2途径。 Dual therapy commonly combines a P2Y12 inhibitor with aspirin, blocking both the ADP pathway and the cyclooxygenase-1 (COX-1) / TXA2 pathway.

不同机制的药物联用，产生叠加抗血小板效应，降低血栓形成风险。 Combining drugs with different mechanisms yields an additive antiplatelet effect, reducing thrombosis risk.

CAPRIE及CURE等大型研究证实
双联疗法在降低心脑血管事件发生率方面优于单一治疗，但出血风险也相应升高。 Large studies such as CAPRIE and CURE confirmed that dual antiplatelet therapy is more effective than monotherapy in reducing cardiovascular event incidence, but also increases bleeding risk.

CAPRIE研究提示氯吡格雷与阿司匹林单药疗效相近；CURE研究显示阿司匹林+氯吡格雷可显著减少心血管事件（11.4%降至9.3%，p<0.001），但严重出血风险也上升（2.7%升至3.7%，p<0.001）。 The CAPRIE study showed clopidogrel and aspirin monotherapy have similar efficacy; the CURE study indicated aspirin plus clopidogrel significantly reduces cardiovascular events (from 11.4% to 9.3%, p<0.001), with increased major bleeding risk (from 2.7% to 3.7%, p<0.001).

老师特别提醒
实际方案需个体化，既要最大限度防止血栓，还要严防出血并发症。 The teacher specially reminded that the real-world regimen must be individualized to maximally prevent thrombosis and carefully avoid bleeding complications.

六、总结与用药注意 Summary and Clinical Considerations

P2Y12受体抑制剂是动脉性血栓防治的核心药物，尤其合用阿司匹林可增强疗效。 P2Y12 inhibitors are central drugs for arterial thrombosis prevention and treatment, especially when combined with aspirin for enhanced effect.

具体药物选择需综合疗效、代谢差异以及潜在风险，根据患者具体情况制定个体化治疗方案。 Drug selection must consider efficacy, metabolic differences, and potential risks, with individualized plans for each patient.

老师反复强调
抗血小板治疗的原则是在有效防栓的同时，严格监控和管理出血风险。 The teacher repeatedly emphasized that the principle of antiplatelet therapy is to ensure effective thromboprophylaxis while rigorously monitoring and managing bleeding risk.

抗血小板药物Antiplatelet drugs

一、GPIIbIIIa抑制剂（纤维蛋白原结合）GPIIbIIIa inhibitors (fibrinogen binding)

该类药物通过抑制血小板表面的GPIIbIIIa受体，阻断纤维蛋白原介导的血小板聚集。 These drugs inhibit the GPIIbIIIa receptor on platelets, blocking fibrinogen-mediated platelet aggregation.

阻断GPIIbIIIa实际上就是阻断了激活状态下的血小板之间形成桥接，最终抑制血小板的聚集和血栓形成。 Blocking GPIIbIIIa prevents cross-linking between activated platelets, thereby inhibiting platelet aggregation and thrombus formation.

二、主要药物及机制Main drugs and mechanisms

Abciximab（瑞普罗®）：单克隆抗GPIIbIIIa抗体，通过阻断RGD结合位点实现作用，且为不可逆抑制。 Abciximab (ReoPro®): A monoclonal anti-GPIIbIIIa antibody that blocks the RGD binding site, causing irreversible inhibition.

Eptifibatide（整合理宁®）：由蛇毒蛋白衍生的多肽，含KGD序列，可以与RGD结合位点竞争纤维蛋白原，抑制为可逆。 Eptifibatide (Integrilin®): A peptide derived from rattlesnake venom with a KGD sequence that competes with fibrinogen at the RGD site, and its inhibition is reversible.

Tirofiban（通立达®）：非肽类分子，结构模拟RGD片段，同样与RGD结合位点结合竞争纤维蛋白原，作用是可逆的。 Tirofiban (Aggrastat®): A non-peptidic molecule mimicking the RGD motif, binding to and competing for the RGD site, also with reversible inhibition.

RGD结合位点为GPIIb和GPIIIa两个亚单位界面处，是纤维蛋白原结合的关键区域。 The RGD binding site is located at the interface between GPIIb and GPIIIa subunits, which is critical for fibrinogen binding.

三、用药方式与适应症Usage and Indications

此类药物均为静脉注射，仅适用于特殊状况和急性阶段。 All drugs in this class are administered intravenously and are used only in special situations and acute phases.

目前主要用于心肌梗死介入治疗（PCI）或重大支架血栓事件的“救命”抢救。 Currently, they are mainly used as salvage therapy in percutaneous coronary intervention (PCI) for myocardial infarction or in major stent thrombosis events.

由于强效和高风险，不作为常规长期用药，现已逐渐被P2Y12受体抑制剂所替代。 Due to their potency and high risk, they are not used for long-term therapy and have been largely replaced by P2Y12 inhibitors.

四、不良反应与注意事项Adverse Effects and Precautions

主要不良反应包括血小板减少症（特别是免疫反应引起的）和出血等。 Major adverse effects include thrombocytopenia (especially immune-mediated) and bleeding.

老师着重讲到，这类药物风险较高，尤其要留意血小板变化和出血症状。 The teacher stressed that these drugs carry high risks, especially regarding platelet count changes and bleeding signs.

五、适应症总结Indication summary

稳定性冠心病
长期口服阿司匹林（过敏者用氯吡格雷）。 Stable coronary artery disease: Long-term oral aspirin (clopidogrel for allergy).
急性心梗介入术后
先用阿司匹林+普拉格雷或阿司匹林+替格瑞洛一年，再长期阿司匹林。 PCI following MI: Aspirin + prasugrel or aspirin + ticagrelor for 1 year, then aspirin long term.
稳定性冠心病介入
阿司匹林+氯吡格雷6-12个月，再长期阿司匹林。 PCI in stable coronary artery disease: Aspirin + clopidogrel for 6-12 months, then long-term aspirin.
缺血性卒中后
长期阿司匹林（或氯吡格雷）。 After ischemic stroke: Long-term aspirin (or clopidogrel).
外周动脉疾病
长期阿司匹林（或氯吡格雷）。 PAD: Long-term aspirin (or clopidogrel).

六、老师补充讲解Teacher’s Additional Points

老师补充，GPIIbIIIa抑制剂操作难度较大，只能静脉注射且用量需严控，主要留作特殊抢救/介入时使用。 The teacher added that GPIIbIIIa inhibitors are technically demanding, IV only, and strictly dose-controlled, reserved for specific rescue/intervention settings.

因P2Y12类口服药逐渐普及，GPIIbIIIa抑制剂今后主要为特殊抢救用药。 As oral P2Y12 inhibitors become more widely used, GPIIbIIIa inhibitors will mainly serve as emergency rescue drugs in the future.

结合临床，老师再次强调使用任何抗血小板药物都需严密监控出血并发症及血小板水平。 In clinical practice, the teacher reiterated that all antiplatelet drug therapies must be closely monitored for bleeding complications and platelet levels.

vWF疾病 vWF Disease

vWF疾病概述 vWF Disease Overview

vWF疾病最早由Erik A von Willebrand于1926年在一名有出血症状的年轻女孩身上描述。 vWF disease was first described by Erik A von Willebrand in 1926 in a young girl with a bleeding disorder.

1972年，vWF被成功纯化。 In 1972, vWF was purified.

1986年，vWF基因测序完成。 In 1986, the vWF gene was sequenced.

vWF病是普通人群中最常见的遗传性出血疾病。 vWF disease is the most common inherited bleeding disorder in the general population.

患病率估算为1%。 Prevalence is estimated to be 1%.

具有临床显著出血的患者约占0.01%。 Patients with clinically significant bleeding account for about 0.01%.

初级止血 Primary Hemostasis

过程及vWF的作用 Process and Role of vWF

在血管损伤部位，止血过程包括三个阶段：1.粘附 2.激活 3.聚集。 At the site of vessel injury, hemostasis involves three phases: 1. Adhesion 2. Activation 3. Aggregation.

静息状态的血小板在内皮细胞损伤暴露下与vWF结合，完成粘附。 Resting platelets adhere to exposed subendothelial matrix via vWF after endothelial cell injury.

内皮细胞分泌球状vWF至血浆，在剪切应力下vWF展开并延伸，暴露其胶原结合位点及GP1b结合位点。 Endothelial cells secrete globular vWF into plasma. Under shear stress, vWF unfolds and elongates, exposing its collagen binding sites and GP1b binding sites.

血小板表面的GP1b与vWF弱结合，GPVI与胶原强结合，协同完成血小板粘附和激活。 Platelet GP1b weakly binds vWF, while GPVI firmly binds collagen, enabling platelet adhesion and activation.

vWF缺陷的影响 Effect of vWF Deficiency

vWF缺陷时，初级止血功能障碍，血小板无法正常粘附。 In vWF deficiency, primary hemostasis is defective and platelets cannot adhere properly.

易导致临床上的出血倾向。 This predisposes to a clinical bleeding tendency.

vWD的出血类型 Types of Bleeding in vWD

以黏膜-皮肤出血为主 Predominantly Mucocutaneous Bleeding

常见的出血包括
Common bleedings include:

月经过多、分娩期间/之后的出血（女性突出） Heavy periods and bleeding during/after labour (prominent in females)

频繁或持续的鼻出血 Frequent or long-lasting nosebleeds

轻微割伤/小伤口出血过多 Excessive bleeding from minor cuts/wounds

牙龈出血 Bleeding gums

牙齿拔除或外科手术后出血持久 Persistent bleeding after tooth removal or surgery

皮肤易出现大面积淤青 Large bruises or easy bruising

胃肠道出血 Gastrointestinal bleeding

关节和肌肉出血较罕见 Joint and muscle bleeds are rarer

出血部位多样，严重程度和发生频率异质，不易量化。 Bleeding sites are heterogeneous, and severity and frequency vary, making quantification difficult.

临床特点 Clinical Features

临床上，vWD主要影响女性，以妇科出血表现为主，例如月经过多、分娩后出血等。 Clinically, vWD primarily affects females, mainly presenting as gynecological bleeding such as menorrhagia and postpartum hemorrhage.

男性中较罕见，特别是典型的妇科相关出血。 It is less common in males, especially with typical gynecological bleeding.

老师补充讲解内容 Teacher’s Supplementary Explanation

vWD首次被描述是因为女性患者表现出不寻常的出血，而一般体表出血性疾病常见于男性，因此这一发现显得特别。 vWD was first described because a female patient exhibited unusual bleeding, whereas most classic bleeding disorders are common in males, making this observation noteworthy.

虽然男女均可患病，但在临床上通常主要表现为女性疾病，多与妇科出血有关。 Although both males and females can have the disease, it is primarily seen in females clinically, usually associated with gynecological bleeding.

患者可能在相同性别、相同性病理、相同vWF水平下出现不同的出血严重程度和频率。 Patients may show different bleeding severities and frequencies even with the same gender, pathology, and vWF levels.

出血可表现为断断续续和不易预测，尤其是外科手术和妇科情况下要特别注意。 Bleeding can be intermittent and unpredictable, requiring special attention in surgery and obstetric cases.

由于出血部位和表现的多样性和异质性，vWD的量化和评估较为复杂。 The heterogeneous and diverse locations and manifestations of bleeding in vWD make its quantification and evaluation quite complex.

vWF结构与合成 vWF Structure and Synthesis

vWF结构和分子域 vWF Structure and Domains

vWF蛋白由若干结构域组成，包括D1、D2、D'、D3、A1、A2、A3、D4、C1-C6和CK。 The vWF protein is composed of several domains: D1, D2, D', D3, A1, A2, A3, D4, C1-C6, and CK.

分子由N端至C端依次排列，N端为前肽，C端为成熟亚基。 The domains are arranged from N-terminal to C-terminal, with the N-terminal as the propeptide and the C-terminal as the mature subunit.

合成细胞与贮存 Synthesis and Storage

vWF主要由内皮细胞合成，部分由巨核细胞（血小板前体）合成。 vWF is primarily synthesized by endothelial cells, and partly by megakaryocytes (precursors of platelets).

合成后，vWF分子以多结构域糖蛋白形式存在，分子量约250 kDa。 After synthesis, vWF exists as a multidomain glycoprotein with a molecular weight of approximately 250 kDa.

vWF在内皮细胞中的Weibel-Palade小体和血小板的α颗粒中储存。 vWF is stored in Weibel-Palade bodies in endothelial cells and α-granules in platelets.

多聚体形成过程 Multimerization Process

vWF单体首先通过C端形成二聚体（尾-尾连接），随后通过N端形成多聚体（头-头连接），连接方式为二硫键。 vWF monomers first form dimers through C-terminal (tail-to-tail) connections, then form multimers through N-terminal (head-to-head) connections via disulfide bonds.

多聚体化后，vWF具有完全止血活性。 Once multimerized, vWF has full hemostatic activity.

血浆中的多聚体分布及止血活性 Distribution of Multimers and Hemostatic Activity in Plasma

血浆中的vWF呈多聚体分布，分为超大（UL，>10,000 kDa）、高（5,500–10,000 kDa）、中（3,000–5,000 kDa）、低（500–2,500 kDa）分子量形式。 vWF in plasma exists as multimers, classified as ultra-large (UL, >10,000 kDa), high (5,500–10,000 kDa), intermediate (3,000–5,000 kDa), and low (500–2,500 kDa) molecular weights.

分子量越高的多聚体，止血活性越强。 Multimers with higher molecular weights have stronger hemostatic activity.

电泳分析显示，vWF多聚体呈多带型，反映其多聚体的存在。 Electrophoresis shows vWF as multiple bands, reflecting the multimeric pattern.

vWF多聚体大小调控 vWF Multimer Size Regulation

ADAMTS13蛋白酶的作用 Role of ADAMTS13 Protease

ADAMTS13是一种蛋白酶，能切割展开/延长的vWF（作用于A2结构域）。 ADAMTS13 is a protease that cleaves the unfolded/elongated vWF (acting on the A2 domain).

剪切可减小vWF多聚体的大小，从而降低其活性，调控血小板血栓的形成并维持初级止血的平衡。 Cleavage reduces the size of vWF multimers, lowering their activity and regulating platelet plug formation, maintaining balance in primary hemostasis.

ADAMTS13功能异常的临床后果 Clinical Consequences of ADAMTS13 Dysfunction

ADAMTS13缺陷时，超大vWF多聚体累积，易致血栓性疾病。 When ADAMTS13 is deficient, ultra-large vWF multimers accumulate, leading to thrombotic disorders.

若ADAMTS13过度活跃，vWF降解增加，引发出血性疾病，如VWD2A。 If ADAMTS13 is overactive, increased vWF degradation results in bleeding disorders such as VWD2A.

老师补充讲解内容 Teacher’s Supplementary Explanation

vWF是单链蛋白，经过复杂的多聚化过程形成具有功能的多聚体。 vWF is a single-chain protein that forms functional multimers through a complex multimerization process.

其结构域之间通过二硫键连接，保证了多聚体的结构完整性。 The domains are connected by disulfide bonds, ensuring the structural integrity of the multimers.

vWF以多聚体形式分布于血浆，各种分子量多聚体决定不同的止血活性。 vWF is distributed in plasma as multimers, and multimers of different molecular weights determine different hemostatic activities.

ADAMTS13调控vWF多聚体的大小，上调易致血栓，下调可致出血倾向。 ADAMTS13 regulates the size of vWF multimers. Decreased activity causes thrombosis, increased activity leads to bleeding tendency.

本部分主要聚焦

vWF结构与功能 vWF Structure and Function

生理条件下的vWF特点 vWF Features in Physiological Conditions

vWF以多聚体蛋白的形式在血浆中循环。 vWF circulates in plasma as a multimeric protein.

在正常情况下，vWF为球状/失活构象，不与血小板结合。 It circulates in a globular/inactive conformation (no platelet binding).

vWF作为凝血因子VIII（主要为D'-D3结构域）的载体。 vWF is the carrier of coagulation factor VIII (mainly D'-D3 domains).

vWF单体包含多个结构域
从N端到C端依次为D'、D3、A1、A2、A3、D4、C1-6、CK。 The vWF monomer includes several domains from N- to C-terminal: D', D3, A1, A2, A3, D4, C1-6, CK.

D'-D3区域为FVIII的结合位点。 The D'-D3 domains serve as the binding site for FVIII.

血管损伤部位的vWF作用 vWF Function at Site of Vascular Injury

在血管损伤部位，vWF受高剪切力或血流变化作用，结构由球状被动状态伸展开，成为活化状态。 At the site of vascular injury, vWF unfolds and acquires an elongated/active conformation due to high shear stress or flow changes.

vWF的A3结构域与胶原结合、A1结构域与血小板GPIb结合。 The A3 domain of vWF binds collagen, and the A1 domain binds platelet GPIb.

vWF还可以通过C4结构域与血小板GPIIbIIIa结合，促进血小板聚集。 vWF can also bind platelet GPIIbIIIa via its C4 domain to promote platelet aggregation.

这种构象变化保证了vWF在止血时的活化和功能实现。 This conformational change ensures vWF activation and its function in hemostasis.

分子水平作用机制 Molecular Mechanism of Action

球状（失活）的vWF在没有血管损伤时不与血小板结合，防止非特异性止血。 Globular (inactive) vWF does not bind platelets in the absence of vascular injury, preventing nonspecific hemostasis.

损伤发生后，vWF暴露其关键结合位点，通过与胶原和血小板的结合启动止血过程。 Upon injury, vWF exposes its critical binding sites and initiates hemostasis through binding to collagen and platelets.

老师补充讲解相关内容 Teacher’s Supplementary Explanation

vWF基础结构为单链蛋白，各结构域决定其多样功能。 vWF is fundamentally a single polypeptide chain, and its various domains define its multiple functions.

vWF的主要生理功能是在血管损伤时有效链接血小板和内皮下基质，激活止血反应。 The primary physiological function of vWF is to link platelets and subendothelial matrix at vascular injury sites to activate the hemostatic reaction.

vWF作为FVIII的载体，对于防止FVIII降解、维持凝血活性至关重要。 As a carrier of FVIII, vWF is vital for preventing FVIII degradation and maintaining coagulation activity.

小结 Summary

vWF在正常血浆中为球状多聚体，失活状态；损伤时变为活化长链，执行止血功能。 vWF exists as a globular, inactive multimer in plasma and becomes an active, elongated chain for hemostasis when injury occurs.

vWF各结构域实现与血小板、胶原及FVIII的多重结合，是初级止血核心分子。 The domains of vWF enable binding to platelets, collagen, and FVIII, making it a key molecule in primary hemostasis.

vWD的分型与分子基础 vWD Classification and Molecular Basis

von Willebrand Disease (vWD)简介 Introduction to von Willebrand Disease (vWD)

vWD由vWF基因突变引起，目前已知>700种基因变异。 vWD is caused by mutations in the vWF gene, with over 700 variants currently identified.

vWD可分为定量缺陷和定性缺陷两大类。 vWD can be divided into two major categories: quantitative defects and qualitative defects.

定量缺陷 Quantitative Defect

vWD 1型 Type 1
约占所有vWD患者的50-80%，主要表现为vWF合成或分泌减少，或清除加快。 Type 1 accounts for 50-80% of all vWD patients, mainly due to reduced vWF synthesis/secretion or increased clearance.
vWD 3型 Type 3
<5%的患者，几乎无vWF合成/分泌，表现为最严重出血倾向。 Type 3 is found in less than 5% of patients, with almost absent vWF synthesis/secretion, leading to the most severe bleeding.

定性缺陷 Qualitative Defect

定性缺陷由vWF基因变异导致，影响其与配体结合功能，有多种亚型。 Qualitative defects result from vWF gene variants that affect its interaction with ligands, with several subtypes.

vWD 2型 Type 2
约占20%的患者，进一步细分为： Type 2 accounts for around 20% of patients and is further subdivided into:
2A型 Type 2A
vWF合成/包装异常或被ADAMTS13过度剪切，多聚体减少，止血活性降低。 Abnormal synthesis/packaging or excessive cleavage by ADAMTS13, leading to reduced multimers and decreased activity.
2B型 Type 2B
构象更易延长/活化，可自发结合血小板，导致血小板减少和ADAMTS13剪切增加。 More elongated/active conformation, capable of spontaneous platelet binding, causing increased platelet clearance and ADAMTS13 cleavage.
2M型 Type 2M
vWF与血小板GPIb（或胶原）结合能力降低。 Decreased affinity for platelet GPIb (or collagen).
2N型 Type 2N
vWF与凝血因子VIII（FVIII）亲和力降低，导致FVIII迅速被清除。 Decreased affinity for FVIII, resulting in rapid FVIII clearance.

vWD的具体分型与分子机制 vWD Detailed Classification and Molecular Mechanisms

1型 Type 1
vWF抗原5-50%、活性降低或正常，多聚体谱正常，表现多样，常为AD遗传，机制为合成/分泌减少或清除增加。 vWF:Ag 5-50%, activity ↓ or normal, normal multimer profile, variable phenotype, mostly autosomal dominant, reduced synthesis/secretion or increased clearance.
3型 Type 3
vWF<5%、活性极低、多聚体缺失，表现为重度出血，常为AR遗传，机制为无合成/分泌。 vWF<5%, activity very low, no multimers, severe bleeding, usually autosomal recessive, no synthesis/secretion.
2A型 Type 2A
vWF功能/多聚体谱异常，低分子多聚体为主，表现为重度，常为AD遗传，机制为多聚体合成/包装异常或蛋白酶分解增加。 Abnormal function/multimers (mainly low MW), severe bleeding, mostly AD, abnormal multimer synthesis/packaging or increased proteolysis.
2B型 Type 2B
vWF倾向结合血小板，活性↓、多聚体谱低MW、多血小板减少，AD遗传，机制为vWF对血小板亲和力↑，血小板清除↑。 Tendency to bind platelets, activity ↓, low MW multimers, thrombocytopenia, AD inheritance, ↑ affinity for GPIb, ↑ platelet clearance.
2M型 Type 2M
vWF抗原↓、活性↓、多聚体谱正常，表现轻-中度，AD，机制为GPIb/胶原亲和力降低。 vWF:Ag ↓, activity ↓, normal multimer profile, mild-moderate, AD, ↓ affinity for GPIb/collagen.
2N型 Type 2N
vWF抗原正常或↓，活性正常，多聚体谱正常，FVIII水平低下，表现轻-中度，AR，机制为FVIII亲和力↓。 vWF:Ag normal or ↓, normal multimer, low FVIII, mild-moderate, AR, ↓ affinity for FVIII.
健康人vWF
Ag参考范围50-200%。 Normal vWF:Ag in healthy population: 50-200%.

AD=常染色体显性，AR=常染色体隐性。 AD = autosomal dominant, AR = autosomal recessive.

vWF基因突变分布及蛋白功能域 VWF Genetic Variants Distribution and Protein Functional Domains

已鉴定>750种独特vWF基因变异，涵盖所有分型。 Over 750 unique vWF genetic variants have been identified, covering all subtypes.

不同分型的变异聚集于vWF蛋白不同结构域，影响其与FVIII、GPIbα、ADAMTS13、胶原、αIIbβ3等配体结合。 Variants in different subtypes cluster in various vWF protein domains, affecting binding to FVIII, GPIbα, ADAMTS13, collagen, αIIbβ3, etc.

例如，2N型突变主要在D’/D3区域（影响FVIII结合），2A/2B/2M突变多见于A1-A3区域（影响GPIb/胶原结合）。 For example, type 2N mutations predominantly occur in the D’/D3 region (affecting FVIII binding), while types 2A/2B/2M are mainly found in the A1-A3 regions (affecting GPIb/collagen binding).

了解这些分子机制有助于精确诊断与个体化治疗。 Understanding these molecular mechanisms supports accurate diagnosis and personalized therapy.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Key Commentary

各分型可通过表型、遗传学和功能测定区分，患者临床表现高度异质，血浆vWF测定和活性检测对于类型区分及治疗至关重要。 The subtypes can be differentiated using phenotype, genetics and function tests; clinical manifestations are highly heterogeneous, and plasma vWF assays and function testing are crucial for subclassification and therapy.

2N型常需与血友病A鉴别。 Type 2N should be differentiated from hemophilia A.

多聚体分析（电泳）能直观反映2A、2B类型多聚体异常，用于确诊和分型。 Multimer analysis (electrophoresis) visually demonstrates multimer abnormality in types 2A and 2B, aiding diagnosis and classification.

vWD的治疗选择 vWD
Therapeutic options

主要治疗策略——增加/补充血浆vWF Main strategy: increase/replenish plasmatic VWF

DDAVP（去氨加压素，血管加压素类似物）：适用于按需（出血/手术），可静脉注射、鼻喷、皮下注射。 DDAVP (desmopressin, analogue of vasopressin): for on demand use (bleeding/surgery), administered by iv, nasal spray, or subcutaneously.

1型有效 Type 1 OK

2型部分有效 Type 2 +/-

3型无效 Type 3 NO

老师补充
DDAVP通过刺激血管内皮细胞释放贮存的vWF，适用于有残余vWF储备的患者，尤其是1型。如患者vWF储备极低（如3型），刺激无效；2型因分子缺陷不同，反应不一，需监测。 Teacher’s explanation: DDAVP stimulates the release of stored vWF from endothelial cells, working for patients with some vWF stores (especially type 1). When vWF stores are extremely low (type 3), DDAVP is ineffective; type 2 response varies due to different molecular defects, and monitoring is needed.

血浆来源或重组vWF补充 Therapy with plasma-derived or recombinant vWF

静脉补充血浆来源vWF（可合用FVIII）或重组vWF，按需给药（出血/手术）或作为预防，静脉途径专用。 Infusion of plasma-derived vWF (with or without FVIII) or recombinant vWF, for on demand (bleeding/surgery) or prophylaxis; iv route only.

1型有效 Type 1 OK

2型有效 Type 2 OK

3型有效 Type 3 OK

老师补充
血浆来源或重组vWF可用于各种类型（包括最重的3型），为替代治疗，能迅速提高血浆vWF水平，适用于不对DDAVP反应的患者。 Teacher’s explanation: Plasma-derived or recombinant vWF is suitable for all types (including severe type 3), is a replacement therapy, can quickly increase plasma vWF, and is used for patients unresponsive to DDAVP.

其他辅助治疗 Other adjuvant strategies

抗纤溶药（如氨甲环酸）：用于粘膜出血、重度月经出血等情形。 Antifibrinolytics (e.g., Tranexamic Acid): for mucocutaneous bleeds and heavy menstrual bleeding.

激素治疗
用于管理月经过多。 Hormonal therapy: for management of heavy periods.

治疗要点总结 Key therapeutic summary

1型vWD首选DDAVP，大多数患者有效；2型个体差异大，需试验性应用和检测反应；3型DDAVP无效，仅能依赖替代治疗。 DDAVP is first choice for type 1 (most respond); type 2 responses vary, requiring trial and testing; type 3 is unresponsive to DDAVP and relies only on replacement therapy.

替代治疗适用于所有类型，尤其在重症/手术/不耐受DDAVP时。 Replacement therapy applies to all types, especially in severe cases, surgery, or when DDAVP is not tolerated.

辅助药物用于特殊出血部位或女性生理期出血多者。 Adjuvant drugs are used for specific bleed sites or for women with heavy menstrual bleeding.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s additional notes

DDAVP或替代疗法实施后需检测vWF活性和血浆水平以指导后续用药。 After DDAVP or replacement therapy, vWF activity and plasma levels should be measured to guide further dosing.

DDAVP对3型无效的原因是3型患者几乎无内源性vWF贮存。 DDAVP is ineffective in type 3 because these patients have almost no endogenous vWF stores.

替代治疗需要注意病毒安全和剂量调整，防止过度凝血。 Replacement therapy requires monitoring for viral safety and dosing, to avoid hypercoagulability.

抗纤溶及激素类疗法常为辅助控制，不能单独替代vWF补充。 Antifibrinolytics and hormonal therapies are adjuncts and cannot replace vWF supplementation alone.

治疗选择应综合分型、临床表现及个体对治疗的反应决定。 Therapy choice should be based on vWD type, clinical presentation, and individual response to treatment.

血友病及其遗传和发病机制 Hemophilia, Inheritance and Pathophysiology

血友病 Hemophilia

血友病被称为“皇室病”。 Hemophilia is known as the "royal disease".

最早由维多利亚女王（携带者）遗传至欧洲多个皇室家族。 It was inherited from Queen Victoria (a carrier) and spread throughout various European royal families.

包括末代俄国沙皇尼古拉二世之子等王室成员均受其影响。 Members affected include the son of Nicholas II, the last Russian tsar.

血友病患者和携带者家族谱系突出显示其遗传特点。 The pedigree of hemophilia patients and carriers highlights the hereditary pattern.

血友病
发病机制 Hemophilia: Pathophysiology

血友病分为A型（因FVIII缺乏）和B型（因FIX缺乏）。 Hemophilia is classified as type A (due to FVIII deficiency) and type B (due to FIX deficiency).

这两种凝血因子均参与凝血酶复合体（tenase complex）的形成。 Both factors are involved in the formation of the tenase complex.

凝血因子缺陷导致凝血酶复合物功能障碍，进而影响下游凝血反应。 Defects in these factors cause malfunction of the tenase complex and downstream coagulation.

最终结果是纤维蛋白生成不足，导致出血性疾病。 The final result is insufficient fibrin production, resulting in hemorrhagic disorders.

患者由于纤维蛋白形成减少，形成的血小板-纤维蛋白血栓稀疏。 Because of reduced fibrin formation, the resulting platelet-fibrin thrombus is loose.

电镜下可见正常与血友病患者纤维蛋白网的明显差异，后者网孔更大、更疏松。 Electron microscopy reveals a denser fibrin mesh in normal plasma, while hemophilia patients show larger pores and looser mesh.

血友病的X染色体连锁遗传 Hemophilia X-linked Inheritance

FVIII或FIX的编码基因位于X染色体上。 The genes coding for FVIII or FIX are localized on the X chromosome.

女性为携带者，通常无症状，因两个X染色体保证凝血因子水平大于50%。 Females are carriers and are often asymptomatic, since two X chromosomes ensure factor levels above 50%.

男性若仅有一个X染色体且带有突变，凝血因子水平远低于50%，容易出血发病。 Males with the mutated X chromosome (and only one X) have much lower factor levels and are clinically affected with bleeding.

血友病遗传概率 Genetics of Hemophilia Inheritance

携带者女性X染色体有一半可能传递突变，子代25%为发病男性，50%为携带女性。 A female carrier has a 50% chance of passing on the mutation: 25% affected males, 50% carrier females.

患者男性与正常女性生育，所有女儿为携带者，儿子均正常。 Affected males with normal females have all daughters as carriers and all sons normal.

血友病X连锁遗传临床特点 Hemophilia X-linked Clinical Features

先天性出血性疾病，X连锁隐性，多经母亲遗传。 Congenital bleeding disorder, X-linked recessive, usually inherited from the mother.

临床表现取决于凝血因子水平
Clinical manifestations depend on factor level:
轻型
凝血因子>5%，无自发性出血，仅外伤或手术后出血时间延长。 Mild: factor level >5%, no spontaneous bleeding, but prolonged bleeding after injury/surgery.
中度
凝血因子1-5%，罕见自发出血，易瘀斑、出血，伤后/术后出血时间延长。 Moderate: factor level 1-5%, rare spontaneous bleeding, easy bruising, prolonged post-injury/surgery bleeding.
重型
凝血因子<1%，频繁自发性出血。 Severe: factor level <1%, frequent spontaneous bleeding.

正常人凝血因子水平约为50-150%。 Normal factor level in healthy individuals is about 50-150%.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Commentary

女王维多利亚本人无症状，但作为携带者通过代际传递使血友病广泛影响欧洲王室。 Queen Victoria herself was asymptomatic but as a carrier spread hemophilia widely in European royalty.

女性仅有一个X染色体突变时，因另一条正常X染色体补偿，故通常症状不明显。 Females with only one mutated X chromosome are usually symptom-free due to compensatory normal X.

男性仅需一条X染色体带突变便会发病（无正常X补偿）。 Males only require one mutated X chromosome to be affected (no compensation).

轻型患者日常生活受影响小，重型患者可出现频繁自发性出血及合并症，对生活质量影响大。 Mild cases have little impact on daily life; severe cases experience frequent spontaneous bleeding and complications that markedly reduce quality of life.

血友病的临床表现与治疗 Hemophilia: Clinical Manifestations and Therapy

血友病的临床表现 Hemophilia, clinical manifestations

关节出血常见于踝、膝、肘等大关节，会导致关节积血和血友病性关节病。 Joint bleeds typically occur in ankles, knees, and elbows, leading to hemarthrosis and hemophilic arthropathy.

关节出血后，关节肿胀、肌肉萎缩、慢性疼痛，并导致活动受限。 After joint bleeding, joints become swollen, muscles atrophy, chronic pain develops, and movement becomes limited.

肌肉出血多见于小腿、大腿、上臂，表现为肿胀、温热、疼痛，病变肌肉逐渐无力、瘢痕化、变短，丧失对关节的保护作用。 Muscle bleeds are common in the calf, thigh, and upper arm, showing swelling, warmth, pain; the affected muscle becomes weak, scarred, shorter, and can no longer protect the joint.

长期反复出血可导致关节、肌肉及神经永久性损伤，影响患者站立、行走、坐等日常生活。 Repeated bleeding results in permanent damage to joints, muscles, and nerves, affecting the ability to sit, stand, and walk.

血友病流行病学 Hemophilia, prevalence

美国血友病患者约有3万-3.3万人。 There are about 30,000–33,000 people living with hemophilia in the US.

其中60%为重型血友病，15%为中度，25%为轻型。 Among them, 60% have severe hemophilia, 15% moderate, and 25% mild.

血友病A（FVIII缺乏）发病率约为1/6000，血友病B（FIX缺乏）约为1/30000。 The incidence of Hemophilia A (FVIII deficiency) is about 1 in 6,000 males; Hemophilia B (FIX deficiency) is about 1 in 30,000.

约50%为重型血友病。 About 50% of patients have severe hemophilia.

漏诊现象明显，仅约25%的血友病患者被确诊且能获得规范治疗。 Underdiagnosis is significant; only about 25% of hemophilia patients are diagnosed and have access to treatment.

年治疗费用约为20万美元/人。 The annual treatment cost is about $200,000 per patient.

血友病治疗 Hemophilia therapy

主要治疗方式是凝血因子的替代治疗，包括按需（出血/手术时）或预防性用药。 The main treatment is replacement therapy with coagulation factors, either on-demand (during bleeding/surgery) or prophylactically.

可选用血浆来源因子或重组因子制剂，仅限静脉注射。 Plasma-derived or recombinant factors can be chosen, with IV administration only.

凝血因子的半衰期较短，FVIII约12小时，FIX约24小时，因此需每周静脉注射2-3次。 Coagulation factors have short half-lives (FVIII ~12 hours, FIX ~24 hours); thus, frequent IV administration (2-3 times/week) is necessary.

部分患者在外源性因子替代治疗过程中会产生中和性抗体（抑制物），导致疗效下降。 Some patients receiving exogenous factor develop neutralizing alloantibodies ("inhibitors"), reducing therapy efficacy.

约30-40%的血友病A患者、3-4%血友病B患者会形成抑制物。 Approximately 30-40% of Hemophilia A and 3-4% of Hemophilia B patients develop inhibitors.

替代治疗旨在形成血小板-纤维蛋白血栓，防止出血。 Replacement therapy aims to form a stable platelet-fibrin thrombus to prevent bleeding.

延长半衰期的因子制剂 Extended half-life (long-lasting) factor
