通过化学修饰（如PEG化）、融合蛋白（与白蛋白或Fc片段融合）等方式延长因子半衰期，FDA已批准相关制剂。 Chemical modifications (e.g., PEGylation), fusion proteins (albumin or Fc-fusion) enable extended half-life; FDA has approved such products.

目的是减少降解、增强稳定性、减缓肾脏清除，提高血液中有效药物浓度。 The goals are to decrease degradation, increase stability, reduce kidney clearance, and maintain effective plasma levels.

FVIII延长至约24小时，FIX可达96小时，显著延长给药间隔。 FVIII half-life extends to ~24h and FIX to ~96h, notably lengthening dosing intervals.

基因治疗 Gene therapy

血友病属单基因病，凝血因子由肝细胞合成，可通过AAV载体转导肝细胞实现基因治疗。 Hemophilia is a monogenic disease with coagulation factors synthesized by hepatocytes, allowing gene transfer with liver-targeted AAV vectors.

优势包括提高因子水平、降低年出血率（ABR）、减少对常规治疗的依赖、改善生活质量。 Advantages: increases factor levels, decreases annual bleeding rate (ABR), reduces reliance on other therapies, improves patient quality of life.

局限性包括肝炎、效果难以预测、抗AAV抗体/抗因子抗体生成、疗效随时间缓慢降低。 Limitations: risks of liver inflammation, unpredictable response, potential development of anti-AAV or anti-factor antibodies, gradual decrease of efficacy over 8-10 years.

近年已有Roctavian（FVIII, HA, 2022）和Hemgenix（FIX, HB, 2023）获批上市。 Roctavian (FVIII for HA, 2022) and Hemgenix (FIX for HB, 2023) have been approved.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Key Commentary

重型血友病患者易反复自发性关节和肌肉出血，导致长期、不可逆的功能障碍。 Severe hemophilia patients are prone to recurrent spontaneous joint and muscle bleeds, resulting in lasting, irreversible disability.

由于诊治率有限，绝大多数患者难以获得规范治疗，导致并发症高发和生活质量下降。 Due to low diagnosis/treatment rates, most patients lack proper care, facing high complication rates and reduced quality of life.

替代治疗的最大挑战是高昂费用和抑制物的产生，需权衡使用频度和患者依从性。 Big challenges in replacement therapy include high costs and the risk of inhibitor formation, requiring careful adjustment of treatment frequency and adherence.

新型长效因子和基因治疗有望改善患者长期生存和生活质量，但远期疗效及安全性需持续随访。 Novel long-acting factors and gene therapies may improve long-term outcomes, but ongoing follow-up on effectiveness and safety is essential.

血友病治疗 | Hemophilia Therapy

基因治疗 Gene therapy

血友病是单基因疾病。 Hemophilia is a monogenic disease.

凝血因子由肝细胞合成，因此可通过肝细胞靶向AAV介导的基因转移实现治疗。 Coagulation factors are synthesized by hepatocytes, enabling gene transfer by liver-targeted AAV vectors for therapy.

肝细胞对于AAV载体有很强的嗜性。 Hepatocytes have strong tropism for the AAV vector.

优点 Benefits

可提高体内凝血因子的水平。 Increases plasma levels of clotting factor.

能降低年出血率(ABR)。 Decreases the annual bleeding rate (ABR).

能减少对其它治疗方法的需求。 Reduces the use of other therapies.

明显改善患者生活质量(QOL)。 Significantly improves quality of life (QOL) for treated patients.

局限性 Limits

有肝脏炎症风险。 Risk of liver inflammation.

疗效不可预测。 Non-predictable effect.

可能产生抗AAV抗体（仅出现一次）。 Possible development of anti-AAV antibodies (can happen only once).

可能产生抗因子抗体。 Potential to develop anti-factor antibodies.

随时间(8-10年)疗效逐渐下降。 Slow decrease in efficiency over time (8-10 years).

进展 Progress

Roctavian（FVIII, HA型）2022年获批；Hemgenix（FIX, HB型）2023年获批。 Roctavian (FVIII for HA) approved in 2022; Hemgenix (FIX for HB) approved in 2023.

旁路制剂（Bypassing agents）

部分患者因体内产生抑制因子无法用常规替代疗法，此时需用旁路制剂。 Some patients develop inhibitors to factor replacements and require bypassing agents.

旁路制剂具有非特异性促凝活性，仅用于按需（急性）止血。 Bypassing agents have non-specific procoagulant activity, used only on demand for acute bleeding.

常用于有抑制物的患者。 Used for patients with inhibitors.

半衰期短。 Very short half-life.

常用旁路制剂 Examples

重组活化FVIIa（rFVIIa, Eptacog-α, Novoseven等） Recombinant activated FVII (rFVIIa, Eptacog-α, Novoseven, etc.)

激活的维生素K依赖性因子浓缩物（如FEIBA） Activated Vitamin K-dependent factor concentrate (e.g. FEIBA)

rFVIIa半衰期约2小时，FEIBA等6-8小时。 rFVIIa has a half-life of about 2 hours, FEIBA around 6-8 hours.

非替代疗法
Emicizumab（Hemlibra）Non-replacement therapy: Emicizumab (Hemlibra)

Emicizumab为人源化双特异性抗体，靶向FIX(a)和FX(a)，模拟FVIII功能。 Emicizumab is a humanized bispecific antibody targeting FIX(a) and FX(a), acting as an FVIII mimetic.

可用于A型血友病，适用于有无抑制物患者。 Suitable for hemophilia A, for patients with or without inhibitors.

2018年获批上市。 Approved in 2018.

皮下注射，每周1次，4周后每月1-2次。 Administered subcutaneously: once a week for 4 weeks, then 1-2 times/month.

半衰期约30天。 Half-life is about 30 days.

明显改善了患者的生活质量。 Significantly improves quality of life for patients.

只适用于A型，不适用于B型；仅用于预防，急性出血时无效；极少数患者有血栓事件报告。 Approved only for hemophilia A; does not work for acute bleeding, and rare cases of thrombosis have been reported.

FVIIIa与Emicizumab (ACE910)功能机制比较 Mechanistic comparison: FVIIIa vs Emicizumab (ACE910)

FVIIIa多个结合位点，酶/底物亲和力高（纳摩尔级），对FIXa和FX高度特异，具有完全辅因子活性（促进磷脂结合、稳定FIXa活性位点、桥联FIXa-FX）。 FVIIIa has multiple specific binding sites, high affinity (nanomolar), is highly specific for FIXa/FX, with full cofactor activity (phospholipid binding, FIXa stabilization, bridging FIXa-FX).

FVIIIa具备开关机制和高自我调节能力。 Has on/off mechanism and high self-regulation.

Emicizumab仅有单一结合位点，酶/底物亲和力低（微摩尔），不能区分酶原与酶，仅有部分辅因子活性（桥联FIXa-FX），无开关机制，自我调节水平低。 Emicizumab: single binding site, low affinity (micromolar), no distinction between zymogen/enzyme, only partial cofactor activity (bridging FIXa-FX), no on/off mechanism, low self-regulation.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Additional Explanation

基因治疗能极大减少凝血因子替代频率，长期维持更高凝血因子水平，但疗效个体差异大且长期安全性尚需监测。 Gene therapy can greatly reduce need for factor replacement, maintains higher clotting factor levels, but efficacy varies individually and long-term safety requires ongoing evaluation.

旁路制剂只在有抑制物且急需止血时使用，出血风险与促凝风险需权衡。 Bypassing agents are reserved for inhibitor-positive and acute bleeding, balancing risk of bleeding and thrombosis.

Emicizumab长期预防效果佳，用药方案简便显著改善依从性。 Emicizumab is effective for long-term prophylaxis, simple dosing and greatly improves compliance.

新疗法尤其适合难治型和常规治疗失败者。 These new therapies are especially valuable for refractory cases and conventional therapy failures.

③非替代疗法
Mim8，新一代抗体 Non-replacement therapy: Mim8, new generation Ab

Mim8是一种新型双特异性抗体，其作用机制与Emicizumab类似。 Mim8 is a novel bispecific antibody with a mechanism of action similar to Emicizumab.

Mim8相比Emicizumab，对膜结合因子（FIX、FIXa、FX、FXa）有更高亲和力。 Compared with Emicizumab, Mim8 has better affinity for membrane-bound factors (FIX, FIXa, FX, FXa).

Mim8在体外能极大促进FIXa的蛋白酶活性，实现>20000倍的激活效果。 Mim8 greatly stimulates the proteolytic activity of FIXa in vitro, with over 20,000-fold activation.

Mim8能有效增加血浆内凝血酶生成，达到类似或优于Emicizumab的止血效果。 Mim8 can significantly enhance thrombin generation in plasma, with equal or better hemostatic effect than Emicizumab.

Mim8在小鼠模型中，表现出剂量依赖的止血效果，并能显著减少血液丢失。 In mouse models, Mim8 shows dose-dependent reduction in blood loss and effective bleeding control.

Mim8对FX激活效率更高，有望在更低剂量下起效。 Mim8 has better efficiency for FX activation and works at lower doses.

Mim8的设计使其主要在活化血小板表面促进复合物组装与FX活化，不易在溶液中非特异结合，降低副作用风险。 Mim8 is designed to promote efficient complex assembly and FX activation mainly on the activated platelet surface, not in solution, reducing off-target risks.

Mim8目前处于Ⅲ期临床试验阶段，显示其潜力作为未来血友病新疗法。 Mim8 is currently in ongoing phase 3 clinical trials, showing promise as a next-generation hemophilia therapy.

老师强调Mim8的剂量更低、效率更高，且专一于局部激活减少血栓风险，代表未来非替代治疗发展趋势。 The teacher emphasized that Mim8 works at even lower doses and with higher specificity for local activation, representing the trend in non-replacement therapy for hemophilia.

④再平衡疗法
抑制天然抗凝物 Rebalancing therapies: inhibition of natural anticoagulants

再平衡疗法的原理是通过抑制体内天然抗凝因子，恢复血液凝固与抗凝的平衡，从而缓解因凝血因子缺乏导致的出血倾向。 The principle of rebalancing therapy is to restore the balance between coagulation and anticoagulation by inhibiting natural anticoagulants, thus alleviating bleeding caused by factor deficiency.

正常情况下，止血系统包括促凝和抗凝两大系统，维持动态平衡。 Normally, the hemostatic system includes procoagulant and anticoagulant pathways in dynamic equilibrium.

血友病患者因缺乏FVIII/FIX，促凝侧显著削弱，抗凝机制占优势，导致易出血。 Hemophilia patients lack FVIII/FIX, so the procoagulant side is weakened and the anticoagulant side dominates, causing a bleeding tendency.

再平衡疗法通过抑制TFPI、AT、PCa等抗凝分子，让残存的凝血途径能生成更多凝血酶，从而形成有效的血小板-纤维蛋白血栓。 Rebalancing therapies inhibit anticoagulants like TFPI, AT, and PCa, allowing more thrombin to be generated from the remaining coagulation pathways and forming stable platelet-fibrin thrombi.

目前靶向的主要抗凝因子包括
The main natural anticoagulant targets include:

组织因子途径抑制物（TFPI） Tissue factor pathway inhibitor (TFPI)

抗凝血酶（AT） Antithrombin (AT)

活化蛋白C（PCa） Activated protein C (PCa)

作用方式包括抗体阻断和RNA干扰抑制等。 Modes of action include antibody blockade and RNA interference.

代表性药物有
Representative drugs include:
抗TFPI单抗
Concinzumab (诺和诺德), Marstacimab (辉瑞), Befovaciamb (拜耳) Anti-TFPI monoclonal antibodies: Concinzumab (Novo Nordisk), Marstacimab (Pfizer), Befovaciamb (Bayer)
抗AT siRNA
Fitusiran (赛诺菲) Anti-AT siRNA: Fitusiran (Sanofi)
PCa抑制剂
SerpinPC PCa inhibitor: SerpinPC

这类疗法的机制是在一定程度上“放松”体内天然抗凝屏障，使残存的凝血反应被最大化。 The mechanism of these therapies is to "relax" the natural anticoagulant barriers to maximize the potential of residual coagulation.

再平衡疗法极大拓展了血友病治疗思路，为常规替代无效和有抑制物患者带来新选择，但也需警惕潜在的促凝/血栓风险。 Rebalancing therapies expand the choices for hemophilia treatment, especially in cases refractory to replacement or with inhibitors, but carry potential thrombotic risks that require close monitoring.

老师补充，目前这些药物大多处于临床试验阶段，长期安全性与适应症尚需进一步评价。 The teacher added that most of these drugs are still in clinical trials, and long-term safety and indications require further studies.

再平衡疗法
抑制天然抗凝物Rebalancing therapies: inhibition of natural anticoagulants

总体思路 General Concept

再平衡疗法通过抑制体内天然抗凝分子（如TFPI、AT、活化蛋白C）来“放松”抗凝屏障，增强残余的凝血功能，从而纠正因凝血因子缺乏导致的出血倾向。 Rebalancing therapy works by inhibiting natural anticoagulants (such as TFPI, AT, activated protein C), “relaxing” endogenous anticoagulant pathways to maximize residual coagulation and thus counteract bleeding due to factor deficiency.

该思路显著拓展了血友病的治疗选择，尤其针对常规替代疗法无效或有抑制物的患者。 This approach greatly expands treatment options for hemophilia, especially for patients where conventional replacement fails or for those with inhibitors.

针对靶点及方法 Key Targets and Methods

靶点包括
TFPI（组织因子途径抑制物）、AT（抗凝血酶）、PCa（活化蛋白C）。 Main targets include: TFPI (Tissue Factor Pathway Inhibitor), AT (Antithrombin), PCa (Activated Protein C).
方法包括
单克隆抗体、siRNA干扰、分子抑制剂等。 Approaches: monoclonal antibodies, RNA interference (siRNA), molecular inhibitors.

代表药物及作用机制 Representative Drugs and Mechanisms

TFPI靶向药物 TFPI-inhibiting agents

单克隆抗体代表
Concizumab（诺和诺德）、Marstacimab（辉瑞）、Befovaciamb（拜耳）。
代表药理作用
通过高亲和力结合TFPI，防止TFPI对FVIIIa和FXa的抑制，增强促凝活性。

Representative mAbs: Concizumab (Novo Nordisk), Marstacimab (Pfizer), Befovaciamb (Bayer).

Mechanism
Bind tightly to TFPI, blocking its inhibition on FVIIIa/FXa, thereby boosting coagulation activity.

基本用于皮下注射（Concizumab每日一次，Marstacimab每周一次）。 Subcutaneous administration (Concizumab once daily, Marstacimab once weekly).

2024年FDA预计批准用于伴或不伴抑制物的A/B型血友病。 FDA approval expected in 2024 for HA/HB with or without inhibitors.

约10%案例产生抗药抗体　，可能影响药效。 Around 10% cases produce ADA (anti-drug antibody), possibly neutralizing drug effect.
答案 AD

AT靶向药物 AT-targeting agents

代表药物Fitusiran（赛诺菲），为siRNA分子。

Fitusiran (Sanofi), an siRNA therapeutic.

机制为
为siRNA共轭N-乙酰半乳糖胺，通过肝细胞表面Asialoglycoprotein受体特异性递送进入肝细胞，沉默AT基因表达，降低AT蛋白合成与水平。
Mechanism
siRNA conjugated with N-acetyl-galactosamine, specifically targeted to hepatocytes via the asialoglycoprotein receptor, internalized by endocytosis; inside cytoplasm, combines with RISC, targets AT mRNA and represses AT protein synthesis.
作用效果
降低AT血浆水平至30%以下，减少年出血率，有效提高凝血酶生成。 Results: Decreases plasma AT level (<30%), lowers annual bleeding rate, increases thrombin generation.

优点：2个月一次皮下注射，疗效持久，疗效可预期、波动小，适应A/B型带或不带抑制物患者。 Benefits: Subcutaneous injection every 2 months, long-lasting effect, predictable with low inter-individual variation, effective for HA/HB with or without inhibitors.

不良反应
约6%血栓风险、胆囊疾病，停药后作用持续，起效有滞后（0.5-1个月），长期安全性需警惕。 Adverse effects: ~6% thrombosis risk, gallbladder disease, long effect after discontinuation, onset delayed (0.5-1 month), long-term safety to be monitored.

预计2025年获FDA批准，用于带或不带抑制物的A/B型血友病（商品名Qfiltia）。 FDA approval anticipated in 2025 for HA/HB (with/without inhibitors) under trade name Qfiltia.

PCa靶向药物 PCa-targeting agents

代表药物SerpinPC，为活化蛋白C（PCa）抑制剂。

Example
SerpinPC, a PCa inhibitor.

机制为直接抑制PCa活性，从而减少FVIII/FV的降解，增强凝血。 Mechanism: Directly inhibits PCa activity, reducing FVIII/FV degradation and improving coagulation.

相关药物尚处于临床开发阶段。 Drugs of this category are still under clinical development.

老师重点补充 Teacher’s Emphasis

再平衡疗法可适用于A/B型血友病，无论是否有抑制物，对重型或顽固型患者意义重大。 Rebalancing therapies apply to both hemophilia A and B, with or without inhibitors, especially benefiting severe or refractory patients.

这些疗法实质通过抑制抗凝作用，最大化残存的凝血系统，但需密切监控血栓风险。 These drugs essentially enhance coagulation by suppressing natural anticoagulation and must be used with careful thrombosis risk monitoring.

老师强调此类新机制药物为血友病患者带来了全新治疗希望，但长期效果、安全性与适应证仍需持续评价。 Teacher highlighted that these new agents open up promising options for hemophilia, but long-term results, safety and indications remain under evaluation.

PCa抑制剂及再平衡策略新靶点PCa inhibitor and New Targets for Rebalancing Strategy

PCa抑制剂机制与设计原则 Mechanism and Design Principles of PCa Inhibitor

凝血酶与抗凝酶（APC，活化蛋白C）在结构和功能上高度同源。 Thrombin and anticoagulant enzymes (APC, activated protein C) share high structural and functional homology.

挑战在于设计能够特异抑制PCa（抗凝酶）而不影响其他促凝酶的抑制剂。 The challenge is to design an inhibitor that specifically targets PCa (anticoagulant enzyme) without cross-reacting with other procoagulant enzymes.

通过对凝血因子结构-功能关系的深入了解，实现对PCa选择性抑制剂（如SerpinPC）的生物工程化改造。 Selective PCa inhibitor (e.g. SerpinPC) is engineered by leveraging detailed knowledge of coagulation factor structure-function relationships.

SerpinPC的分子特异性与靶点 Molecular Specificity and Targeting of SerpinPC

表格数据提示KRK α1AT突变体（SerpinPC）对APC有强力抑制作用，对凝血酶、FXa和FXIa等促凝酶基本无抑制。 Data show that the KRK α1AT mutant (SerpinPC) potently inhibits APC, but has negligible effect on thrombin, FXa, and FXIa.

SerpinPC实现对PCa高度特异的功能控制，有望成为理想再平衡治疗工具。 SerpinPC provides highly specific control over PCa, emerging as a promising tool for rebalancing therapy.

SerpinPC的前临床研究 Preclinical Evaluation of SerpinPC

在健康供者和血友病A型患者血浆中添加SerpinPC，可显著提升凝血酶生成量。 Addition of SerpinPC to plasma from healthy donors or HA patients significantly increases thrombin generation.

小鼠模型（FVIII-/-）经SerpinPC处理后出血量显著减少，表现出剂量依赖性止血效果。 In FVIII-/- mouse models, SerpinPC administration leads to dose-dependent reduction in blood loss, confirming hemostatic efficiency.

SerpinPC的临床评价 Clinical Evaluation of SerpinPC

2023年启动II期临床，招募HA/HB型、有无抑制物的患者，给予每月1次皮下注射。 Phase II clinical trial started in 2023, including HA/HB patients with or without inhibitors, dosed subcutaneously once per month.

初步结果显示
ABR（年出血率）下降，未见血栓事件，不中和ADA（非中和抗药抗体）发生率低于10%。 Preliminary data: ABR decreases in treated patients, no thrombotic events, non-neutralizing ADA in less than 10% of cases.

2024年因市场竞争，研发暂停，但前期结果显示SerpinPC疗效与安全性均具潜力。 Development was halted in 2024 due to market competition, but SerpinPC showed promise for efficacy and safety in early trials.

再平衡新靶点
抗凝通路 Other New Targets for Rebalancing Strategy: Anticoagulant Pathways

PCa/PS通路 PCa/PS pathway

PS为PCa辅因子，PS缺乏可引起血栓风险上升。 PS acts as a cofactor for PCa; deficiency increases thrombosis risk.

动物模型显示FVIII-/-PS+/-可纠正出血表型，抗PS siRNA正在开发并通过前临床验证。 In FVIII-/- PS+/- mice, the hemorrhagic phenotype is corrected; anti-PS siRNA is under preclinical development.

PZ/ZPI系统 PZ/ZPI system

ZPI为酶抑制物，专门靶向FXIa与FXa，缺乏ZPI不伴血栓风险。 ZPI is an enzyme inhibitor targeting FXIa and FXa, with no thrombosis risk upon deficiency.

动物模型（FVIII-/-ZPI-/-）显示可纠正出血表型，抗PZ抗体促进凝血酶生成。 In FVIII-/- ZPI-/- mice, bleeding phenotype is corrected; anti-PZ Ab promotes thrombin/clotting in patient plasma.

蛋白酶Nexin-1 Protease Nexin-1（PN-1）

PN-1由活化血小板分泌，特异抑制FXIa和FIIa。PN-1缺乏无血栓风险。 PN-1 is secreted by activated platelets, selectively inhibiting FXIa and FIIa; deficiency does not increase thrombosis risk.

动物模型（FVIII-/-PN-1-/-）也显示可纠正出血表型，抗PN-1抗体可促进止血。 In FVIII-/- PN-1-/- mice, hemorrhagic phenotype is corrected; anti-PN-1 Ab supports clotting in human blood samples.

老师补充讲解 Teacher’s Additional Notes

通过特异性抑制抗凝途径内元素（如PCa、PS、ZPI、PN-1等），可最大化残余凝血反应，为各类型血友病尤其有抑制物患者提供新解决方案。 Selectively targeting anticoagulant pathway elements (PCa, PS, ZPI, PN-1) amplifies residual coagulation, expanding options for all hemophilia types, especially with inhibitors.

动物实验及早期临床显示上述新型靶点均有良好纠正出血倾向的效果与可控风险。 Animal and early clinical results show these new targets can effectively correct bleeding with manageable risk.

再平衡疗法新靶点开发，将持续推动血友病等出血性疾病精准治疗进步。 Ongoing development of these targets will advance precision management of hemophilia and bleeding disorders.

052025-11-19

免疫学——宿主对病原体的防御

Immunology - Host defense against pathogens

免疫系统的主要功能是防御外来的病原体（如细菌、病毒、寄生虫等），维持机体健康。 The main function of the immune system is to defend the host against external pathogens (such as bacteria, viruses, and parasites) and maintain health.

机体通过物理屏障（如皮肤和黏膜）、分子机制和细胞反应来实现对病原体的防护。 The body achieves protection against pathogens through physical barriers (such as skin and mucosa), molecular mechanisms, and cellular responses.

免疫应答机制

Immune response mechanisms

免疫应答分为天然免疫和适应性免疫两类，二者协同工作抵御“危险”（如病原体）的入侵。 Immune responses are divided into innate immunity and adaptive immunity, both working together to defend against "danger" (such as pathogens).

外部的“危险”首先被皮肤和黏膜屏障阻挡，若突破这些屏障，机体内部的天然免疫系统会被激活。 The external "danger" is first blocked by the skin and mucosal barriers; if these barriers are breached, the body's innate immune system is activated.

天然免疫作为第一道应答，随后会激活适应性免疫。 Innate immunity acts as the first response and subsequently activates adaptive immunity.

无论哪种免疫应答，终极目标都是消除病原体，恢复体内平衡。 Regardless of which response, the ultimate goal is to eliminate pathogens and return to homeostasis.

天然免疫——危险信号的识别

Innate response - Detection of danger

先天免疫细胞（如巨噬细胞和树突状细胞）通过表面受体识别病原体的危险信号。 Innate immune cells (such as macrophages and dendritic cells) detect pathogen-derived danger signals via surface receptors.

巨噬细胞和树突状细胞是主要的感知细胞，能快速识别并响应入侵的病原体。 Macrophages and dendritic cells are the principal sensing cells that rapidly recognize and respond to invading pathogens.

天然免疫——循环细胞的招募

Innate response - Recruitment of circulating cells

巨噬细胞和树突状细胞在识别危险信号后，释放信号分子召集其他免疫细胞（如中性粒细胞）到感染部位。 After detecting danger signals, macrophages and dendritic cells release molecular signals to recruit other immune cells (such as neutrophils) to the site of infection.

中性粒细胞是最早到达感染区域的主要杀菌细胞，具有很强的吞噬和杀伤能力。 Neutrophils are the primary microbicidal cells to arrive early at the infection site and possess strong phagocytic and killing abilities.

天然免疫——病原体的清除

Innate response - Destruction of the pathogen

被招募的中性粒细胞、巨噬细胞和树突状细胞共同参与杀灭并清除入侵的病原体。 Recruited neutrophils, macrophages, and dendritic cells work together to destroy and clear the invading pathogens.

各类细胞通过包裹、吞噬、以及释放杀菌物质，直接消灭病原体。 These cells kill pathogens through engulfment, phagocytosis, and release of antimicrobial substances.

天然免疫——激活适应性免疫

Innate response - Starting adaptive response

病原体被吞噬处理后，树突状细胞被激活，携带抗原信息迁移至淋巴结等次级淋巴器官。 After pathogen uptake and processing, dendritic cells are activated and carry antigen information to secondary lymphoid organs such as lymph nodes.

树突状细胞在淋巴系统中启动并激活特异性的适应性免疫应答。 Dendritic cells initiate and activate specific adaptive immune responses within the lymphatic system.

淋巴循环在免疫细胞迁移和抗原传递中起关键作用。 Lymphatic circulation plays a key role in immune cell migration and antigen delivery.

补充讲解内容（结合课堂录音）

Supplementary explanation (from lecture audio)

天然免疫的首要步骤是通过受体识别“危险”，即病原体入侵信号。 The first step of innate immunity is the recognition of "danger" (signals of pathogen invasion) through receptors.

如果细胞没有相关受体，就无法感知威胁，也无法启动免疫反应。 If a cell lacks corresponding receptors, it cannot perceive threats and will not initiate an immune response.

巨噬细胞和树突状细胞是最重要的天然免疫感知细胞。 Macrophages and dendritic cells are the most important innate immune sensing cells.

在感知危险并发送警报信号后，它们会招募更多免疫细胞（尤其是中性粒细胞）到达感染部位。 Upon danger detection and alarm signaling, they recruit more immune cells (especially neutrophils) to the infection site.

中性粒细胞是免疫系统中最具攻击性的细胞之一，专门负责快速消灭入侵微生物。 Neutrophils are among the most aggressive immune cells, dedicated to rapidly destroying invading microbes.

这些应答为后续适应性免疫系统的启动奠定基础。 These responses lay the groundwork for activation of the subsequent adaptive immune system.

先天应答简述Innate response in a nutshell

先天免疫反应具有即时发生的特点。 The innate immune response is characterized by its immediacy.

先天免疫反应并不针对特定抗原，因此不具有抗原特异性。 The innate immune response is not antigen-specific.

先天免疫无记忆功能，同一种威胁多次暴露，反应强度不会增强。 The innate immune response has no memory; repeated exposures do not enhance the response.

先天免疫有三大核心目标
The innate immune response has three main goals:

触发炎症，发出报警信号。 To trigger inflammation and alarm.

杀灭与清除入侵病原体。 To destroy and clear invading pathogens.

激活并启动后续的适应性免疫反应。 To initiate the subsequent adaptive immune response.

教师补充说明，先天免疫细胞已完成分化，遇到病原体时做好了随时处理的准备，不会再增殖或发生本质改变，而是凭借既有“方案”快速启动反应。 The lecturer emphasized that innate immune cells are in a terminally differentiated state; they are ready to act immediately when encountering pathogens, without further proliferation or essential changes, relying on pre-established "programs."

各种细胞（如中性粒细胞、巨噬细胞、树突状细胞）按预定方式分工，对病原体作出迅速反应；比如，中性粒细胞是免疫系统最具攻击性的细胞之一，专门负责迅速消灭入侵微生物。 Various cells (such as neutrophils, macrophages, and dendritic cells) react swiftly to pathogens according to their predetermined roles. For example, neutrophils are among the most aggressive immune cells, dedicated to rapidly destroying invading microbes.

适应性应答组成成分Adaptive response components

适应性免疫系统角色可分为细胞成分与分子成分。 The adaptive immune system consists of cellular components and molecular components.

细胞成分包括
Cellular components include:

T淋巴细胞（T细胞），其中包括CD4+ T细胞和CD8+ T细胞。 T lymphocytes (T cells), including CD4+ T cells and CD8+ T cells.

B淋巴细胞（B细胞）。 B lymphocytes (B cells).

分子成分包括
Molecular components include:

细胞因子（由辅助性T细胞产生）。 Cytokines (produced by helper T lymphocytes).

抗体（由B细胞分化的浆细胞产生）。 Antibodies (produced by plasma cells differentiated from B lymphocytes).

细胞毒分子（由细胞毒性T细胞分泌）。 Cytotoxic molecules (secreted by cytotoxic T lymphocytes).

T细胞介导的细胞适应性应答Cellular adaptive response = T lymphocytes

细胞适应性免疫的主要执行者是T淋巴细胞。 T lymphocytes are the main effectors of the cellular adaptive immune response.

CD4+ T细胞激活后可分化为辅助性T细胞（如Th1），分泌细胞因子（如IL-2、IFN-γ），协助其他免疫细胞作战。 After activation, CD4+ T cells differentiate into helper cells (such as Th1), secreting cytokines (e.g., IL-2, IFN-γ) to assist other immune cells.

CD8+ T细胞激活后成为细胞毒性T淋巴细胞（CTL），能够识别并杀伤特异性靶细胞（如被病毒感染的细胞）。 After activation, CD8+ T cells become cytotoxic T lymphocytes (CTL), which recognize and kill specific target cells (such as virus-infected cells).

过程包括
The process includes:
1.抗原呈递细胞　向初始CD4+ T细胞呈递抗原，促使其激活与分化。 Dendritic cells (DCs) present antigens to naive CD4+ T cells, leading to their activation and differentiation.
答案 CD
2
DC也可激活初始CD8+ T细胞，形成CTL。 DCs also activate naive CD8+ T cells, leading to CTL formation.
3
CTL识别并裂解靶细胞，实现杀伤作用。 CTLs recognize and lyse target cells to exert cytotoxicity.

体液性适应性应答 = B淋巴细胞Humoral adaptive response = B lymphocytes

体液性免疫主要由B淋巴细胞执行，通过产生抗体消除病原体。 Humoral immunity is mainly mediated by B lymphocytes, which eliminate pathogens by producing antibodies.

过程包括
The process includes:
1
DC将抗原呈递给初始T细胞，促其激活。 DCs present antigens to naive T cells for activation.
2
B细胞识别并结合未加工的抗原（native antigen）。 B cells recognize and bind native antigens.
3
活化的T细胞和B细胞在生发中心中相互作用，实现T-B协同。 Activated T and B cells interact in the germinal center, achieving T-B cooperation.
4
B细胞分化为浆细胞，产生并分泌特异性抗体。 B cells differentiate into plasma cells, which produce and secrete specific antibodies.

记忆免疫应答Memory response

记忆应答体现为B淋巴细胞在初次和再次遇到抗原时分泌的免疫球蛋白水平不同，二次应答更加快速且IgG水平更高。 Memory responses are exemplified by the difference in immunoglobulin secretion by B lymphocytes; a secondary response is faster and has higher IgG levels.

适应性免疫应答过程中，B和T细胞能生成免疫记忆细胞，为再次感染提供更强保护。 During adaptive immune responses, B and T cells generate immunological memory cells, providing stronger protection against reinfection.

记忆细胞能长期存在，遇到相同抗原可迅速而有效地产生应答。 Memory cells can persist long-term and mount a rapid and effective response upon encountering the same antigen again.

记忆应答Memory response

记忆应答指的是免疫系统对同一样本抗原再次暴露时产生更快、更强烈的应答。 Memory response refers to the immune system mounting a faster and stronger response upon re-exposure to the same antigen.

图示说明
B淋巴细胞记忆应答可通过血中免疫球蛋白的变化表现出来。 The figure depicts: B lymphocyte memory response is demonstrated by the change in blood immunoglobulin levels.

初次应答（Primary response）时，IgM产生最早且量最多，IgG随后上升但量较低。 In the primary response, IgM is produced first and most abundantly, with IgG rising later but remaining at a lower level.

二次应答（Secondary response）时，IgG产生迅速且显著升高，IgM也会升高但幅度有限。 In the secondary response, IgG production is rapid and markedly increased, while IgM also rises but to a lesser extent.

二次暴露的高IgG水平体现了免疫记忆的快速和高效特点。 The high IgG level on secondary exposure demonstrates the speed and efficiency of immunological memory.

B细胞和T细胞在初次激活后均能生成免疫记忆细胞，为机体提供长效保护。 Both B cells and T cells can generate memory cells after initial activation, conferring long-term protection for the organism.

记忆细胞包括
辅助性T细胞（Helper T cell）、B细胞（B cell）、细胞毒性T细胞（CTL）。 Memory cells include: helper T cells, B cells, and cytotoxic T lymphocytes (CTL).

适应性免疫应答简述Adaptive response in a nutshell

适应性免疫属于延迟性应答。 The adaptive immune response is delayed.

适应性免疫具有高度抗原特异性。 Adaptive immunity is highly antigen-specific.

其效应器包括抗体、辅助性T细胞和细胞毒性T细胞。 Its effectors include antibodies, helper T cells, and cytotoxic T cells.

适应性免疫具备免疫记忆能力。 Adaptive immunity possesses memory capacity.

适应性免疫有两个核心目标
The adaptive immune response has two main goals:
1
直接或通过激活免疫机制消灭病原体。 To destroy pathogens, directly or via activation of immune mechanisms.
2
建立针对未来感染的保护机制（免疫记忆）。 To set up protection against future infections (memory).

免疫应答的差异Differences in immune responses

先天免疫特点
Features of innate immunity:

反应迅速，即时启动。 Acts immediately and rapidly.

模式识别受体（PRR）可识别广泛的危险信号，而非针对单一抗原。 Pattern recognition receptors (PRRs) detect a wide range of danger signals, not restricted to single antigens.

没有记忆功能。 Possesses no memory.

适应性免疫特点
Features of adaptive immunity:

反应延迟，需要时间启动。 The response is delayed and needs time to start.

对抗原高度特异。 Highly specific to antigens.

具备免疫记忆能力。 Has memory capacity.

免疫缺陷的后果
Consequences of immune deficiencies:

先天免疫缺陷常导致细菌或真菌感染。 Deficiency in innate immunity often leads to bacterial or fungal infections.

适应性免疫缺陷
Deficiencies in adaptive immunity:

体液免疫（B细胞）缺陷容易引发细菌感染。 Humoral (B cell) deficiency predisposes to bacterial infections.

细胞免疫（T细胞）缺陷常引发病毒、真菌或寄生虫感染。 Cellular (T cell) deficiency predisposes to viral, fungal, or parasitic infections.

补充讲解内容（结合课堂录音）

Supplementary explanation (from lecture audio)

记忆应答的核心在于有一部分效应细胞在初次感染消退后保留下来，这些细胞在机体消灭危险信号后不被彻底清除，而是被保留为记忆细胞。 The essence of memory response is that some effector cells survive after the first infection subsides; after elimination of danger signals, these cells are not completely cleared but are retained as memory cells.

二次感染时，记忆T细胞和B细胞能迅速扩增并发挥效应功能，显著缩短反应时间、提高应答强度，这在体液免疫表现为IgG的显著升高。 Upon secondary infection, memory T and B cells expand rapidly and exert effector functions, greatly reducing response time and amplifying intensity, notably shown as a marked IgG increase in humoral immunity.

这种免疫记忆基础上，疫苗接种才能实现长期免疫保护，适应性免疫能记住“见过的敌人”。 This immunological memory underpins the long-term protection of vaccination: adaptive immunity "remembers" previous invaders.

免疫病理学Immunopathology → 相关课件解读与听课笔记

免疫系统功能异常会导致两种极端情形
功能减弱（不足）和功能增强（过度）。 Dysfunction of the immune system leads to two extremes: insufficient function (not enough) and excessive function (too much).

功能不足时主要表现为易感染，各类感染（细菌、病毒、真菌、寄生虫）频发、加重，且感染的类型和严重程度取决于缺陷所在的免疫分支。 When function is insufficient, the main outcome is susceptibility to infections (bacterial, viral, fungal, parasitic) or increased infection severity. The specific manifestations depend on which immune component is deficient.

功能过度时主要见于自身免疫、过敏、器官移植排斥反应和单克隆免疫球蛋白病等。 Excessive immune function is mainly seen in autoimmunity, allergy, graft rejection, and monoclonal gammopathies.

只有当免疫系统维持在最佳平衡区间，机体才能保持健康。 Only when the immune system maintains an optimal balance, can the body remain healthy.

教师讲解
免疫系统失调可能表现为易感、持续感染（功能低下），或者过度炎症和组织损伤（功能过度）。例如器官移植排斥反应时，其实免疫反应“太有效”，此时需要药物抑制。 According to the lecturer: Immune dysregulation may lead to increased infection susceptibility (deficiency), or to excessive inflammation/tissue damage (overactivity). For example, organ graft rejection reflects an "overefficient" immune reaction, which requires immunosuppressant medication.
教师强调
临床上也有免疫相关慢性疾病如过敏和自身免疫病，这类疾病并非感染所致，而是免疫系统“攻击”了自体组织或对无害物质产生异常反应。 The lecturer also highlighted that there are immune-related chronic diseases such as allergy and auto-immune diseases—not due to infection, but because the immune system attacks the body itself or reacts abnormally to harmless substances.

免疫系统相关的临床工具Clinical tools related with the immune system

抗微生物作用（防御感染）对应工具：疫苗、疫苗佐剂、单克隆抗体、免疫球蛋白疗法。 Antimicrobial function (defense against infection): Vaccines, vaccine adjuvants, monoclonal antibodies, immunoglobulin therapies.

抗肿瘤作用（肿瘤监视与清除）对应工具：免疫治疗（如免疫检查点抑制剂、CAR-T细胞）、癌症疫苗。 Antitumoral function (tumor surveillance and elimination): Immunotherapies (such as checkpoint inhibitors, CAR-T cells), cancer vaccines.

移植医学（管理移植排斥）对应工具：免疫抑制剂、耐受诱导治疗。 Transplantation (managing graft rejection): Immunosuppressants, tolerance induction therapies.

造血干细胞移植（HSCT，重建免疫系统）是用于极端免疫缺陷等严重疾病的手段，包含同种异体和自体移植。 Hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT, immune system restoration) is used for severe immunodeficiencies and similar disorders; it includes both allogeneic and autologous transplantation.

教师补充
许多临床干预直接针对免疫功能的“失衡”，比如感染高发需增强免疫力，移植和自身免疫疾病需抑制部分免疫活性。 Lecturer’s note: Many clinical interventions directly address immune imbalance—for example, immune enhancement for infections; suppression for transplant rejection and autoimmune syndromes.

课程内容提要Course Outline

课程将系统介绍免疫学基础知识，包括先天免疫与适应性免疫。 This course will systematically cover basic immunology, including innate and adaptive immunity.

将深入讨论免疫病理学，包括过敏、移植、免疫缺陷、自身免疫病，以及抗体相关诊断方法。 The course will also discuss immunopathology, including allergy, grafts, immune deficiencies, autoimmune diseases, and diagnostic methods using antibodies.

教师说明
学好免疫学不仅是了解疾病产生原因，也为未来临床工具的选择和应用奠定基础。 Teacher’s remark: Mastering immunology not only helps understand disease mechanisms but also provides the foundation for choosing and applying clinical tools in the future.

防御的第一道防线First lines of defense → 黏膜和皮肤屏障Mucocutaneous Barriers

机体防御的第一道防线由物理屏障和化学屏障共同构成，能有效阻挡病原体入侵。 The first line of defense in the body consists of physical and chemical barriers, which effectively prevent pathogen invasion.

皮肤
形成物理屏障，其酸性环境和共生微生物进一步强化防御。 Skin: Serves as a physical barrier; its acidity and commensal microorganisms further enhance the defense.
口咽黏膜
唾液及共生菌群共同参与局部防护。 Oropharynx mucosa: Saliva and commensal flora participate in local defense.
呼吸道黏膜
存在粘液、纤毛细胞，有助于清除吸入的病原体。 Airways mucosa: Contains mucus and ciliated cells, aiding in the removal of inhaled pathogens.
肠道黏膜
由粘液、肠道菌群及蠕动运动构成立体防线。 Intestinal mucosa: Forms a comprehensive barrier with mucus, intestinal flora, and peristalsis.

泪液、唾液等体液具清洗功能，胃强酸环境可杀灭绝大多数微生物。 Tears, saliva, and other body fluids provide a washing effect; stomach acidity kills most microbes.

感染本质=黏膜-皮肤屏障破坏Infection = breach of muco-cutaneous barrier

当物理和化学屏障被突破（如创伤），病原体即有机会侵入体内，随即激活炎症反应。 When physical and chemical barriers are breached (e.g., by injury), pathogens gain entry into the body, immediately triggering the inflammatory response.

炎症实际上是一种“入侵报警”，其目标是快速招募免疫细胞、清除入侵者、限制病灶扩展。 Inflammation acts as an “intrusion alarm,” aiming to quickly recruit immune cells, eliminate invaders, and limit the spread.

炎症的作用Roles of inflammation

炎症的三大核心目标
Inflammation has three core objectives:

消灭（Destroy）：通过分子和细胞效应器清除致病因子。 Destruction: Remove pathogens via molecular and cellular effectors.

局限（Limit）：局部凝血限制感染扩散。 Limitation: Local coagulation to restrict infection spread.

修复（Repair）：促进受损组织修复和新血管生成。 Repair: Encourage tissue repair and angiogenesis.

若炎症反应持续超过6周，即为慢性炎症，可造成组织损伤。 If inflammation lasts more than 6 weeks, it becomes chronic and may cause tissue lesions.

炎症与免疫的临床表现Clinical manifestations of Immunity & Inflammation

典型炎症体征包括
发热、发红、肿胀、疼痛、功能障碍。 The classic signs of inflammation include: heat, redness, swelling, pain, and loss of function.

先天免疫应答的成分Innate response components

细胞成分
Cellular components:

定居组织的哨兵细胞（树突状细胞、巨噬细胞）；其它包括肥大细胞、上皮细胞、内皮细胞、固有免疫淋巴细胞。 Sentinel cells residing in tissues (dendritic cells, macrophages); others include mast cells, epithelial cells, endothelial cells, innate lymphoid cells.

招募细胞主要为粒细胞（尤其是中性粒细胞）、单核细胞和NK淋巴细胞。 Recruited cells include granulocytes (especially neutrophils), monocytes, and NK lymphocytes.

分子成分
Molecular components:

补体系统 Complement system

细胞因子（初期以促炎为主，消退期则分泌抗炎因子） Cytokines (pro-inflammatory at the onset; anti-inflammatory during resolution)

抗微生物分子 Antimicrobial molecules

血管活性分子 Vasoactive molecules

教师补充讲解（录音结合）：

Additional notes from the lecture (combined with recording):

教师强调，炎症反应不只是局部免疫激活，更具有系统报警和资源调配功能，通过强有力的“报警信号”协调机体其他防御力量。 The lecturer emphasized that inflammation is not just a local immune activation, but also a systemic alarm that coordinates the body’s defensive resources through robust “alarm signals.”

例如中性粒细胞，是最具攻击性的免疫细胞之一。一旦感知危险，会优先被招募至感染部位，快速消灭病原体。 For example, neutrophils are among the most aggressive immune cells. Once danger is sensed, they are quickly recruited to the infection site to destroy pathogens.

炎症不仅导致白细胞聚集，亦激活局部血管内皮，促使浆液成分渗出，起到稀释和限制感染的作用。 Inflammation not only leads to leukocyte accumulation, but also activates local vascular endothelium, promotes exudation to dilute and limit infection.

哨兵细胞如树突状细胞　感知危险后，会启动“搬运警报”，将致病信号带至次级淋巴器官以激活后续的适应性免疫。 Sentinel cells, such as dendritic cells (DC), after sensing danger, act as “alarm messengers” by transporting pathogenic signals to secondary lymphoid organs to activate adaptive immunity.
答案 CD
问先天免疫细胞如何识别非自身？IHow do innate cells recognize non-self?
答

先天免疫细胞需能够区分“自身”(Self)和“非自身”(Non-self)，这是启动免疫反应的第一步。 Innate immune cells must distinguish "self" from "non-self"—this is the first step in initiating an immune response.

只有当先天免疫细胞识别到非自身成分时（如微生物或受损细胞），才会被激活并参与防御。 Innate cells are only activated and participate in defense when they recognize non-self elements such as microbes or damaged cells.

“危险”信号与识别Danger signal and recognition

危险信号包括两类： Danger signals are divided into two categories:

1
PAMP/MAMP（病原体相关分子模式/微生物相关分子模式）——如LPS（脂多糖）、病毒RNA，主要源于病原体。 PAMP/MAMP (Pathogen/Microbe-Associated Molecular Patterns)—e.g., LPS, viral RNA; mainly from pathogens.
2
DAMP（损伤相关分子模式）——如DNA、ATP、HMGB1，源于受损或死亡细胞。 DAMP (Damage-Associated Molecular Patterns)—e.g., DNA, ATP, HMGB1; originate from damaged or dying cells.

先天免疫细胞通过模式识别受体（PRR, Pattern Recognition Receptors）感知PAMP和DAMP信号。 Innate immune cells sense both PAMPs and DAMPs via pattern recognition receptors (PRRs).

PRR主要分布于细胞膜和胞质： PRRs are mainly distributed on membranes and in the cytosol:

膜上的PRR：如Toll样受体(TLR)，C型凝集素受体。 Membrane PRRs: e.g., Toll-like receptors (TLRs), C-type lectin receptors.

胞质内PRR：如NOD样受体(NLR)、RIG样受体(RLR)。 Cytosolic PRRs: e.g., NOD-like receptors (NLRs), RIG-like receptors (RLRs).

PRR识别危险信号后，启动细胞激活和细胞因子分泌，触发炎症应答。 Upon recognizing danger signals, PRRs trigger cell activation and cytokine secretion, initiating the inflammatory response.

这种辨别方式保证了先天免疫既能够快速应对病原体入侵，也能对组织损伤做出反应。 This method ensures innate immunity can rapidly respond both to pathogen invasion and tissue injury.

PRR举例Examples of PRRs

不同类型的PRR能识别不同类别的分子模式： Different types of PRRs recognize distinct molecular patterns:

TLR1/6、TLR2：识别细菌脂蛋白（如来自革兰阳性菌的分子）。 TLR1/6, TLR2: Detect bacterial lipoproteins (e.g., from gram-positive bacteria).

TLR4：识别LPS（革兰阴性菌细胞壁主要成分）。 TLR4: Recognizes LPS (a major component of gram-negative bacteria cell walls).

TLR5：识别鞭毛蛋白（flagellin）。 TLR5: Recognizes flagellin (bacterial flagellar protein).

NOD1/NOD2：识别分枝杆菌等的肽聚糖片段。 NOD1/NOD2: Recognizes peptidoglycan fragments from mycobacteria, etc.

TLR3/7/9 等：识别病毒和细菌的核酸成分。 TLR3/7/9 etc.: Recognize nucleic acids from viruses and bacteria.

教师强调：PRR不针对特定抗原，而是识别“危险模式”、共同特征，确保应对范围广泛。 Lecturer's note: PRRs do not target specific antigens but detect "danger patterns" or shared features, ensuring a broad response capability.

炎症应答的细胞参与Cellular participants in inflammatory response

组织受损或病原体进入后，肥大细胞和巨噬细胞等第一时间被激活。 Upon tissue injury or pathogen entry, mast cells and macrophages are activated first.

肥大细胞释放组胺，导致血管扩张、血管通透性增加。 Mast cells release histamine, causing vasodilation and increased vascular permeability.

巨噬细胞产生TNF-α、IL-1等，激活血管内皮细胞，提升局部炎症环境。 Macrophages produce TNF-α and IL-1, activating endothelial cells and enhancing the local inflammatory milieu.

血管反应阶段Vascular phase of inflammation

炎症早期以血管反应为主： Early inflammation is dominated by vascular responses:

血管扩张，增加局部血流。 Vasodilation increases local blood flow.

血管通透性增强，促使免疫分子与细胞渗出到组织间隙。 Increased vascular permeability allows immune cells and molecules to exude into tissues.

内皮细胞表达归巢分子，招募更多白细胞至炎症部位。 Endothelial cells express homing molecules to recruit more leukocytes to the inflammation site.

教师补充：这些现象导致局部红、肿、热、痛与功能障碍，是典型炎症症状。 Lecturer’s addition: These phenomena cause local redness, swelling, heat, pain, and loss of function—classic inflammation signs.

整合讲解提示Integrated teacher's points:

先天免疫识别能力依赖于是否具备功能性PRR，如细胞缺失受体则无法识别危险信号，也不会启动炎症。 The ability of innate immunity to recognize danger relies on the presence of functional PRRs; cells lacking these receptors cannot sense danger signals and will not trigger inflammation.

危险信号识别后的“报警”会启动一系列信号级联，“调用”白细胞至感染或损伤区域，共同消灭入侵者，并为后续适应性免疫应答“递送信息”。 Upon recognition of danger signals, an "alarm" initiates a cascade that recruits leukocytes to the site of infection or damage, eliminates invaders, and delivers information for subsequent adaptive immune responses.

此外，PRR还可识别无感染情况下的细胞损伤（如晒伤），而非仅仅针对病原体响应，是免疫反应多样性的重要基础。 PRRs also recognize tissue damage without infection (e.g., sunburn), not only pathogens, supporting the diversity of immune responses.

促炎细胞因子与吞噬细胞招募Pro-inflammatory Cytokines and Phagocyte Recruitment

活化的巨噬细胞（MAMPs或DAMPs刺激）释放三大促炎细胞因子： Activated macrophages (stimulated by MAMPs or DAMPs) release three major pro-inflammatory cytokines:

TNF-α、IL-1和IL-6。 TNF-α, IL-1, and IL-6.

这些因子有多重作用，主要包括： These cytokines have multiple functions, mainly including:

激活其他固有免疫细胞，对适应性免疫细胞和组织细胞（如成纤维细胞）也有影响。 Activation of other innate immune cells and influence on adaptive immune cells and tissue cells (e.g., fibroblasts).

激活内皮细胞，促进白细胞渗出。 Activation of endothelial cells to promote leukocyte extravasation.

促使肝脏合成急性期蛋白（如CRP），启动炎症全身反应。 Stimulate the liver to produce acute-phase proteins (like CRP), initiating a systemic inflammatory response.

促进造血系统激活和骨重塑。 Promote hematopoiesis activation and bone remodeling.

引起临床症状如发热和厌食（主要为IL-1）。 Cause clinical symptoms such as fever and anorexia (mainly IL-1).

高水平时可导致脓毒性休克。 At high concentrations, can lead to septic shock.

TNF-α、IL-1、IL-6是慢性炎症、自身免疫和消化道疾病等相关治疗的重要靶点。 TNF-α, IL-1, and IL-6 are major therapeutic targets for chronic inflammatory, autoimmune, and digestive diseases.

炎症反应与吞噬细胞招募过程Inflammatory Response and Phagocyte Recruitment Sequence

炎症反应分为血管反应和细胞反应两个阶段。 The inflammatory response consists of a vascular phase and a cellular phase.

组织损伤或病原体入侵时，肥大细胞和巨噬细胞最先被激活。 Upon tissue injury or pathogen invasion, mast cells and macrophages are activated first.

肥大细胞释放组胺，导致血管扩张和通透性增强。 Mast cells release histamine, causing vasodilation and increased permeability.

巨噬细胞分泌TNF-α、IL-1、IL-6等促炎因子。 Macrophages secrete TNF-α, IL-1, IL-6, and other pro-inflammatory factors.

促炎因子作用下，白细胞（以中性粒细胞为主）被招募至炎症部位。 Under the action of these cytokines, leukocytes (mainly neutrophils) are recruited to the site of inflammation.

吞噬细胞招募四步过程Four-step Process of Phagocyte Recruitment

第一步：内皮激活，由TNF-α、IL-1和组胺介导，促使内皮细胞发生功能和结构变化。 Step 1: Endothelium activation mediated by TNF-α, IL-1, and histamine, inducing both functional and structural changes in endothelial cells.

第二步：白细胞“滚动”（rolling），主要依赖L-选择素（CD62L）和Sialyl-Lewis X分子实现，白细胞在血管内逐渐减速。 Step 2: Leukocyte rolling, mainly dependent on L-selectin (CD62L) and Sialyl-Lewis X; leukocytes slow down as they roll along the vessel wall.

第三步：停止（stopping），趋化因子（chemokines）和整合素的激活使白细胞与内皮牢固结合。 Step 3: Stopping, where chemokines and activation of integrins enable firm adhesion of leukocytes to the endothelium.

第四步：穿越血管壁（diapedesis），白细胞通过整合素介导的信号穿过内皮，进入组织完成炎症部位聚集。 Step 4: Diapedesis, where leukocytes cross the endothelium via integrin-mediated signals, moving into tissue to accumulate at the site of inflammation.

老师补充讲解Teacher’s Additional Notes

老师强调，巨噬细胞为炎症反应的“指挥者”，通过分泌强力促炎因子，迅速放大危险信号，召集各路免疫细胞。 The teacher emphasized that macrophages act as "commanders" of the inflammatory response, rapidly amplifying danger signals and summoning various immune cells by secreting potent pro-inflammatory factors.

白细胞招募是动态序列，包括信号放大、内皮活化和分子的协同作用。每一环节缺失都可能导致防御失败或炎症过度。 Leukocyte recruitment is a dynamic sequence involving signal amplification, endothelial activation, and molecular cooperation. Defects at any step may lead to failed defense or excessive inflammation.

药物靶向TNF-α、IL-1等因子，有助于精准调控炎症，是临床治疗自身免疫和慢性炎症性疾病的核心策略。 Targeted drugs against TNF-α, IL-1, etc., help precisely modulate inflammation and are core strategies for treating autoimmune and chronic inflammatory diseases.

中性粒细胞的多重杀菌机制与核心步骤The Multiple Microbicidal Strategies of the Neutrophil and Core Mechanisms

中性粒细胞是最具攻击性的免疫细胞之一，因其具有多种高效清除病原体的机制。 Neutrophils are among the most aggressive immune cells due to their diverse and effective pathogen-clearing strategies.

这些机制在保护宿主免受感染的同时，也可能导致局部组织附带性损伤。 While these processes protect the host from infection, they may also cause collateral local tissue damage.

一、中性粒细胞的主要杀菌机制Main Microbicidal Mechanisms of Neutrophils

趋化（Chemotaxis）：趋化因子引导中性粒细胞迅速定向迁移至感染和炎症部位。 Chemotaxis: Chemokines guide neutrophils to migrate rapidly towards the sites of infection and inflammation.

吞噬作用（Phagocytosis）：通过识别、包裹和吞噬病原体，实现定点杀灭。 Phagocytosis: Neutrophils recognize, engulf, and destroy pathogens at precise locations.

脱颗粒（Degranulation）：释放颗粒中含有的抗菌酶（如弹性蛋白酶、乳铁蛋白、溶菌酶等）直接杀灭病原体。 Degranulation: Release of antimicrobial enzymes from granules (e.g., elastase, lactoferrin, lysozyme) directly kills pathogens.

氧爆发（Oxidative Burst）：利用NADPH氧化酶催化产生大量活性氧（ROS），造成病原体氧化性损伤。 Oxidative Burst: NADPH oxidase catalyzes the creation of abundant reactive oxygen species (ROS), inflicting oxidative damage to pathogens.

NETosis（胞外捕网死亡）：抛射细胞DNA及颗粒蛋白，形成胞外捕网（NETs），包裹和限制病原体。 NETosis: Ejects cellular DNA and granule proteins to form extracellular traps (NETs) that ensnare and restrict pathogens.

细胞因子合成（Cytokine Synthesis）：产生多种细胞因子，调节免疫细胞协同应答。 Cytokine Synthesis: Produces various cytokines to coordinate and tune the immune response.

二、吞噬作用的详细步骤Detailed Steps of Phagocytosis

识别（Recognition）：通过受体识别包被吞噬标签（opsonins，如IgG、C3b）的病原体。 Recognition: Pathogens tagged by opsonins (e.g., IgG, C3b) are recognized by neutrophil receptors.

依附（Attachment）：病原体附着于中性粒细胞表面。 Attachment: The pathogen adheres to the neutrophil membrane.

包裹（Surrounding）：细胞膜延伸包裹病原体，形成吞噬泡。 Surrounding: The cell membrane extends to enclose the particle, forming a phagosome.

形成吞噬体（Phagosome Formation）：病原体被完全包裹于吞噬体内。 Phagosome Formation: The pathogen becomes fully enclosed within the phagosome.

吞噬体与颗粒融合（Fusion with Granules）：吞噬体与含抗菌酶的颗粒融合。 Fusion with Granules: The phagosome fuses with granules containing antimicrobial enzymes.

酸化与消化（Acidification & Digestion）：融合后的结构快速酸化，利用酶降解和杀死病原体。 Acidification & Digestion: The fused phagolysosome acidifies rapidly and uses enzymatic degradation to kill the pathogens.

三、脱颗粒与氧爆发Degranulation and Oxidative Burst

脱颗粒：释放各种抗菌分子（如弹性蛋白酶、溶菌酶、乳铁蛋白、髓过氧化物酶等）于吞噬泡或细胞外，协助病原体清除。 Degranulation: Discharges antimicrobial molecules, including elastase, lysozyme, lactoferrin, and myeloperoxidase, into the phagosome or extracellular space to aid in pathogen clearance.

氧爆发过程： Oxidative burst process:

O2 通过NADPH氧化酶生成超氧化物（O2-），继而生成过氧化氢（H2O2），在髓过氧化物酶作用下可进一步形成次氯酸（HOCl）和氯胺等强氧化杀菌物质。 O2 is converted to superoxide (O2-) by NADPH oxidase, then to hydrogen peroxide (H2O2), and finally to hypochlorous acid (HOCl) and chloramines through myeloperoxidase activity, providing strong microbicidal effects.

四、NETosis机制与意义Mechanism and Significance of NETosis

中性粒细胞将染色体DNA及颗粒蛋白质释放到胞外，形成网状结构（NETs），可包陷、限制并杀灭微生物。 Neutrophils extrude chromosomal DNA and granule proteins extracellularly, forming web-like NETs that entrap, restrict, and kill microorganisms.

NETosis是吞噬或脱颗粒无法彻底清除病原体时的一种重要补充机制，但可能加剧炎症及组织损伤。 NETosis is a critical backup when phagocytosis or degranulation are insufficient, but it may exacerbate inflammation and tissue injury.

五、老师补充讲解总结Teacher’s Additional Notes

老师强调，中性粒细胞的“氧爆发”及脱颗粒功能极为强大，是先天免疫最前线的主力，但其副作用是过度活化时易导致组织损伤和炎症持续性加剧。 The teacher highlighted that the “oxidative burst” and degranulation of neutrophils are extremely potent, placing them at the frontline of innate immunity, but overactivation leads to tissue injury and chronic inflammation.

NETosis不仅对大型微生物或高密度细菌感染有效，也是自身免疫类疾病的相关机制之一。 NETosis is effective against large or high-density infections and is also implicated in mechanisms of autoimmune diseases.

所有中性粒细胞杀菌方式都存在协同关系，具体启动哪一机制取决于病原体类型和炎症环境。 All neutrophil killing methods are synergistic; the choice of mechanism depends on pathogen type and the inflammatory microenvironment.

补体系统与炎症反应Complement System and Inflammatory Response

补体系统定义：约含30种血浆蛋白，占血浆蛋白总量约5%。 Definition of the complement system: Composed of about 30 plasma proteins, accounting for approximately 5% of total plasma proteins.

补体系统通过复杂的酶级联反应激活，协同免疫防御。 The complement system is activated through complex enzymatic cascades, coordinating immune defense.

有三种激活途径：经典途径、替代途径、凝集素（Lectin）途径。 There are three activation pathways: the classical pathway, the alternative pathway, and the lectin pathway.

三条途径最终都会导致C3蛋白的裂解为C3a和C3b。 All three pathways ultimately induce the cleavage of C3 protein into C3a and C3b.

补体激活后产生多种效应，包括靶细胞破坏。 After activation, the complement system produces various effects, including target cell destruction.

补体系统的激活与功能Complement Activation and Functions

经典途径通常由抗原-抗体复合物介导，激活C1复合物。 The classical pathway is usually triggered by antigen-antibody complexes, activating the C1 complex.

替代途径可以被微生物表面直接激活，无需抗体。 The alternative pathway can be directly triggered by microbial surfaces, independent of antibodies.

凝集素途径通过凝集素识别病原体表面糖分子激活补体系统。 The lectin pathway is activated via lectins that recognize carbohydrate patterns on pathogen surfaces.

三条途径均引发C3裂解，C3a和C5a为过敏毒素，具有炎症、趋化等作用。 All three pathways trigger C3 cleavage; C3a and C5a (anaphylatoxins) mediate inflammation and chemotaxis.

C3b为主要调理素，促进吞噬细胞识别和清除病原体（调理化作用）。 C3b acts as a major opsonin, enhancing phagocyte recognition and clearance of pathogens (opsonization).

终末复合物MAC（膜攻击复合物）由C5b和多分子C9组成，穿透靶细胞膜导致裂解。 The terminal complex MAC (membrane attack complex), composed of C5b and multiple C9 molecules, penetrates target cell membranes, leading to lysis.

补体系统与炎症反应Complement and Inflammation

组织损伤或感染后，补体被激活，释放C3a、C5a等介质。 After tissue injury or infection, complement is activated, releasing mediators like C3a and C5a.

C3a和C5a可激活肥大细胞，促进组胺释放，增强血管通透性。 C3a and C5a can activate mast cells, promoting histamine release and increasing vascular permeability.

C3a、C5a还具趋化作用，促进中性粒细胞等炎症细胞向损伤部位迁移。 C3a and C5a also have chemotactic functions, attracting neutrophils and other inflammatory cells to the injury site.

巨噬细胞被病原体及补体片段激活后，分泌TNF-α、IL-1、IL-6等炎症因子，进一步放大全身炎症反应。 Macrophages activated by pathogens and complement fragments secrete TNF-α, IL-1, and IL-6, amplifying the systemic inflammatory response.

中性粒细胞可形成NETs（中性粒细胞胞外捕网），协同补体清除病原体。 Neutrophils can form NETs (neutrophil extracellular traps), working with complements to eliminate pathogens.

炎症反应的终止与组织修复Resolution of Inflammation and Tissue Repair

当病原体被清除后，炎症反应逐步转入终止和修复阶段。 After pathogens are eliminated, the inflammatory response switches to resolution and tissue repair.

M2型巨噬细胞吞噬凋亡中性粒细胞（凋亡细胞清除作用），促进组织修复。 M2 macrophages engulf apoptotic neutrophils (efferocytosis), promoting tissue healing.

M2型巨噬细胞分泌TGFβ和IL-10等抗炎因子，抑制炎症并助于修复。 M2 macrophages secrete anti-inflammatory factors like TGFβ and IL-10 to suppress inflammation and aid repair.

在老师补充中，强调补体系统是连接固有免疫与获得性免疫的桥梁，对清除感染、调控炎症和组织修复均有重要作用。 In the teacher’s comments, it was emphasized that the complement system bridges innate and adaptive immunity, being crucial for pathogen clearance, inflammation regulation, and tissue repair.

细胞因子与炎症Cytokines and Inflammation

促炎性细胞因子主要包括IL-1、TNFα和IL-6，具有启动和放大炎症反应的关键作用。 The main pro-inflammatory cytokines are IL-1, TNFα, and IL-6, which are critical for initiating and amplifying inflammation.

IL-1：激活内皮细胞和免疫细胞，可导致发热。 IL-1: Activates endothelium and immune cells, and can cause fever.

TNFα：激活内皮细胞和免疫细胞，提高血管通透性。 TNFα: Activates endothelium and immune cells, increases vascular permeability.

IL-6：激活淋巴细胞，使肝脏产生炎症相关分子。 IL-6: Activates lymphocytes, causes the liver to produce inflammatory molecules.

趋化因子如IL-8能够吸引中性粒细胞至炎症部位，在炎症防御中起关键作用。 Chemokines such as IL-8 attract neutrophils to the site of inflammation, playing a key role in inflammatory defense.

抗炎性细胞因子主要包括IL-10和TGFβ，两者能够抑制促炎反应并促进组织修复。 Main anti-inflammatory cytokines are IL-10 and TGFβ, both suppress pro-inflammatory responses and promote tissue repair.

IL-10：促进修复，抑制促炎细胞因子的产生和免疫细胞活化。 IL-10: Promotes tissue repair, downregulates pro-inflammatory cytokines and cell activation.

TGFβ：同样主要作用为促进修复、减少细胞活化和抑制炎症。 TGFβ: Also primarily promotes repair, diminishes cell activation, and suppresses inflammation.

录音强调，这些细胞因子的“动态平衡”对于炎症的适当进行和结束至关重要，过度或不足都容易导致疾病（如免疫缺陷或自身免疫/慢性炎症）。 The lecture highlights that a “dynamic balance” of these cytokines is crucial for proper progression and resolution of inflammation; excess or deficiency may result in disease (such as immunodeficiency or autoimmunity/chronic inflammation).

固有抗病毒反应Innate Antiviral Response

任何有核细胞一旦检测到病毒（通过模式识别受体，PRR），会产生I型干扰素（主要为IFNα/β），作为细胞间的警报信号。 Any nucleated cell detecting a virus (via pattern recognition receptors, PRRs) produces type I interferons (mainly IFNα/β), which act as alarm signals between cells.

IFNα/β由浆细胞样树突状细胞(plasmacytoid dendritic cells)大量产生，是体内最重要的天生抗病毒分子。 IFNα/β is produced in large amounts by plasmacytoid dendritic cells, and are the most important innate antiviral molecules in the body.

IFNα/β作用于周围任意有核细胞，激活干扰素刺激基因（ISGs）表达，提升细胞抗病毒能力。 IFNα/β act on nearby nucleated cells and activate expression of interferon-stimulated genes (ISGs), enhancing cellular antiviral capacity.

录音补充：这些机制启动极为迅速且不依赖特异性抗原，是控制病毒初期扩散的关键。 Additional lecture note: These mechanisms are activated extremely rapidly and are antigen-non-specific, which is crucial for controlling early viral spread.

ISG编码蛋白的抗病毒作用Antiviral Effects of Proteins Encoded by ISG

I型干扰素诱导的ISG基因产物，可阻断病毒侵入（如解包和入胞）、逆转录、转录、翻译及装配等多个关键步骤。 Type I interferon-induced ISG gene products block multiple key steps of viral lifecycle, such as entry/uncoating, reverse transcription, transcription, translation, and assembly.

特定ISG蛋白还能抑制病毒出芽、释放和新病毒颗粒的产生。 Certain ISG proteins also inhibit viral budding, release, and production of new virions.

I型干扰素还能增强树突状细胞的I类MHC分子表达和共刺激信号，从而启动适应性免疫反应。 Type I IFN also increases dendritic cell class I MHC expression and co-stimulatory signals, thus initiating adaptive immune responses.

I型干扰素还能增强NK细胞的细胞毒性，协助清除病毒感染细胞。 Type I IFN enhances NK cell cytotoxicity, aiding in the elimination of virus-infected cells.

适应性免疫应答的启动Starting Adaptive Response

感染/炎症引发后，树突状细胞（DC）摄取抗原并迁移到二级淋巴器官（如淋巴结）。 After infection/inflammation, dendritic cells (DC) take up antigens and migrate to secondary lymphoid organs (e.g., lymph nodes).

树突状细胞在淋巴结内以MHC II分子呈递给初始CD4+ T细胞，以MHC I分子呈递给初始CD8+ T细胞，激活二者。 In lymph nodes, DCs present antigens to naive CD4+ T cells via MHC II and to naive CD8+ T cells via MHC I, activating both.

DC也可帮助激活初始B细胞，从而启动抗体反应。 DCs can also help activate naive B cells, thus initiating the antibody response.

树突状细胞的亚型与功能Dendritic Cells: Subtypes and Functions

不同类型的树突状细胞（如cDC1、cDC2、pDC、Mo-DC）具有不同的受体表达谱和功能，导致不同的免疫应答。 Different types of dendritic cells (e.g., cDC1, cDC2, pDC, Mo-DC) have distinct receptor expression profiles and functions, resulting in different immune responses.

各亚型具备不同的交叉呈递、MHC II依赖性呈递、诱导细胞毒性T细胞及不同Th细胞亚型、分泌各类细胞因子等能力。 Each subtype has varying abilities for cross-presentation, MHC II-based presentation, induction of cytotoxic T cells and Th cell subsets, and secretion of different cytokines.

MHC/HLA分子相关肽的加工与呈递路径Peptides Associated to MHC/HLA: Processing and Presentation Pathways

MHC I通路主要将内源性/胞质蛋白加工后通过TAP转至内质网，结合MHC I，呈递给CD8+ T细胞，触发细胞毒性反应。 The MHC I pathway mainly processes endogenous/cytoplasmic proteins, transfers peptides to the ER via TAP, binds them to MHC I, and presents them to CD8+ T cells, triggering cytotoxic responses.

MHC II通路则对外源性蛋白进行内吞-溶酶体消化，合成后与MHC II分子结合，呈递给CD4+ T细胞，激发细胞因子反应。 The MHC II pathway digests exogenous proteins via endocytosis and lysosomes, then combines them with newly synthesized MHC II molecules, presenting them to CD4+ T cells, eliciting cytokine responses.

老师强调，MHC/HLA分子的独特多样性和肽结合特异性，是个体免疫应答特异性的基础。 The teacher emphasizes that MHC/HLA molecules’ unique diversity and peptide-binding specificity are the foundation for individual immune response specificity.

HLA相关肽的加工与呈递路径Peptides Associated to HLA Molecules: Processing and Presentation Pathways

MHC分子（HLA）的核心功能是将抗原肽呈递给T细胞受体（TCR），是激活特异性免疫反应的基础。 The core function of MHC (HLA) molecules is to present antigen peptides to T cell receptors (TCR), serving as the basis for activating specific immune responses.

一、MHC/HLA分子的两类抗原呈递通路Two Main Antigen Presentation Pathways of MHC/HLA

I类HLA分子通路Class I HLA Pathway： Class I HLA Pathway:

来源于细胞内蛋白（自体蛋白或病毒/肿瘤抗原），蛋白在胞质内被蛋白酶体降解为肽段。 Originates from intracellular proteins (self or viral/tumor antigens), which are degraded by proteasomes in the cytoplasm into peptides.

肽段通过TAP转运到内质网，与新合成的I类HLA分子结合。 Peptides are transported by TAP into the endoplasmic reticulum, where they bind newly synthesized class I HLA molecules.

I类HLA-肽复合物在细胞表面表达，被CD8+ T细胞识别，引发细胞毒性反应。 The class I HLA-peptide complex is expressed on the cell surface and recognized by CD8+ T cells, triggering a cytotoxic response.

所有有核细胞均表达I类HLA分子。 All nucleated cells express class I HLA molecules.

II类HLA分子通路Class II HLA Pathway： Class II HLA Pathway:

主要针对胞外蛋白或微生物，经内吞作用进入细胞并在内体/溶酶体中被降解。 Targets mainly extracellular proteins or microbes, internalized and degraded within endosomes/lysosomes.

II类HLA分子合成初期由不变链（li）阻止与内源肽结合，后内吞体与II类HLA囊泡融合，降解不变链，外源来源肽段取而代之结合。 Class II HLA is initially protected by an invariant chain (li) to prevent premature peptide binding; after fusion with endosomes, the invariant chain is degraded and exogenous peptides bind.

II类HLA-肽复合物迁移至细胞表面，被CD4+ T细胞识别，诱导细胞因子应答。 The class II HLA-peptide complex migrates to the cell surface and is recognized by CD4+ T cells, inducing cytokine responses.

仅由专业抗原递呈细胞表达（树突状细胞、B细胞、单核/巨噬细胞）。 Expressed only by professional antigen-presenting cells (dendritic cells, B cells, monocytes/macrophages).

二、HLA分子的遗传与多样性Genetics and Diversity of HLA Molecules

HLA分子属于多基因、多态性和共显性表达系统。 The HLA system is polygenic, highly polymorphic, and codominantly expressed.

I类HLA基因有3个位点（A, B, C），II类有更多（DP, DQ, DR等的B1/A1亚基），所有等位基因通常以单倍型方式遗传。 Class I HLA genes have 3 loci (A, B, C); class II have more (DP, DQ, DR with B1/A1 subunits). Alleles are usually inherited as haplotypes.

HLA分子多态性极高，公开数据库已知超过24000种等位基因，多态位点集中于肽结合区域和与TCR接触表面。 HLA molecules are extremely polymorphic—with over 24,000 known alleles. Polymorphic sites are concentrated at the peptide-binding groove and the surface interacting with the TCR.

这种多样性保证个体间抗原递呈能力差异化，是群体防御多样病原体的重要保证。 This diversity provides inter-individual differences in antigen presentation, crucial for population-level defense against diverse pathogens.

三、HLA-肽-TCR三元复合体的结构与识别Structural Aspects and Recognition by TCR

TCR通过识别HLA-肽复合体的特异区域完成免疫激活，TCR与HLA肽结合槽中的肽氨基酸及HLA的多态区域共同作用。 TCRs activate immunity by specifically recognizing regions of the HLA-peptide complex, interacting with both peptide residues in the HLA groove and polymorphic regions on the HLA.

肽-HLA-TCR复合物形成时，肽嵌于HLA的沟槽结构，TCR的互补决定区（CDR）精准接触肽和HLA表面。 The peptide sits within the HLA groove while TCR complementarity-determining regions (CDRs) precisely contact both peptide and HLA surfaces.

这种三元复合体的精确配对关系决定了免疫应答的特异性与多样性。 The precise interaction of this ternary complex determines the specificity and repertoire of the immune response.

四、老师补充讲解要点Key Teacher’s Comments

老师强调，I类/II类HLA递呈内外源性抗原，分别启动细胞毒性与辅助型T细胞反应，是细胞免疫与体液免疫互补桥梁。 The teacher highlights that class I/class II HLA present endogenous/exogenous antigens, respectively triggering cytotoxic and helper T cell responses, bridging cellular and humoral immunity.

HLA的高度多态性是失配型器官移植排斥反应的根本原因，同时为预防不同感染提供遗传基础。 HLA polymorphism underlies organ transplantation rejection due to mismatching, but also provides genetic variability for defending against diverse infections.

课上还提到，专业抗原递呈细胞能够高效激活初始T细胞，因此是适应性免疫反应起点的核心。 Lecture notes emphasize that professional APCs efficiently activate naive T cells, making them central to initiating adaptive immunity.

062025-11-21

免疫应答
两种应答模式 Immune response: two response modes

听课笔记 Lecture Notes

一、免疫力基础 Immunity Basics

免疫力是生物体抵抗病原体感染和防癌的能力。 Immunity refers to the organism’s ability to resist infection by pathogens and protect against cancer.

二、两大免疫系统 Two Main Immune Systems

1
先天性免疫 Innate Immunity

出生即有，无需此前暴露。 Present at birth, requires no prior exposure.

识别
通过识别病原体共有、保守的分子模式（模式识别受体PRR）。 Recognition: Detects conserved, “predefined” molecular patterns shared by classes of pathogens (pattern recognition receptors, PRR).
反应
防御机制启动迅速，无需提前接触。 Response: Rapid deployment of defense mechanisms, no prior exposure required.

组成细胞（老师补充）：主要为骨髓来源细胞（如嗜中性粒细胞、单核细胞、巨噬细胞、树突状细胞等），细胞质大、具吞噬能力；一旦释放入血即完成终末分化，无再分裂能力，寿命短。 Cell types (teacher supplement): Mainly myeloid cells (e.g., neutrophils, monocytes, macrophages, dendritic cells), have a large cytoplasm and phagocytic ability; after release into the bloodstream, they are terminally differentiated and cannot proliferate, with short lifespans.

每个细胞表达多种PRR类型，可识别多种病原。 Each cell expresses multiple types of PRR, enabling recognition of diverse microbes.

效应具备立刻执行能力（效应分子和机制已准备好）。 Cells are immediately equipped with effector functions upon release.

先天性免疫无真正的免疫记忆，反应速度和强度一生中相对恒定。 Generally, innate immunity does not generate immunological memory; its response speed and intensity remain relatively constant throughout life.

激发炎症反应（产生促炎介质），并能唤醒、调节适应性免疫反应。 Can trigger inflammation (production of pro-inflammatory mediators) and initiate/shapes the adaptive immune response.

2
适应性免疫 Adaptive Immunity

随非自身抗原暴露而获得。 Shaped through exposure to non-self antigens.

识别
针对高度特异、预先不确定的分子结构（抗原，非自身）；识别依靠BCR（B细胞受体）、TCR（T细胞受体）。 Recognition: Detects highly specific, non-predefined molecular structures (antigen, non-self); recognition relies on BCR (B cell receptor) and TCR (T cell receptor).

每个初始淋巴细胞表达唯一的BCR或TCR（特异性完全不同）。 Each naïve lymphocyte expresses one unique BCR or TCR specificity.

受体多样性通过随机（体细胞重组）过程产生。 Receptor diversity is generated randomly (via somatic recombination).

新生成的BCR/TCR需经历选择以确保功能和自我耐受。 Newly formed BCRs/TCRs undergo selection to ensure functionality and self-tolerance.

初次反应延迟（~8-10天），需先识别抗原后形成效应及记忆淋巴细胞。 Primary response is delayed (naive to effector takes approx. 8-10 days); requires prior exposure to antigen.

二次反应更快（~4天）、更强，可产生高亲和力抗体，依赖长寿记忆细胞。 Memory response is faster (~4 days), stronger, produces higher-affinity antibodies, relying on long-lived memory cells.

效应方式包括体液免疫（可溶性分子参与）和细胞免疫（需要细胞间直接作用）。 Effector mechanisms include humoral (soluble molecules acting locally/systemically) and cell-mediated (requiring direct cell-to-cell interactions).

三、两类免疫间协同 Cooperation Between the Two Systems

大多数免疫反应同时涉及先天和适应性成分。 Most immune responses involve both innate and adaptive components.

先天免疫通过炎症和信号将危险信息传递给适应性免疫，实现联动。 Innate immunity triggers inflammation and signals (“danger”) to inform/adapt the adaptive system.

树突状细胞于两类免疫间起桥梁作用，既巡逻外周组织，识别危险信号后迁往二级淋巴器官呈递抗原，激活适应性应答。 Dendritic cells act as a bridge: they patrol peripheral tissues, recognize danger signals, then migrate to secondary lymphoid organs to present antigens and activate adaptive responses.

四、总结 Summary

免疫应答是一个动态、多级联动过程，起初由迅速但无特异记忆的先天免疫负责，随后特异性强、具备记忆的适应性免疫接管。 The immune response is a dynamic, multi-linked process, initiated by the rapid but non-specific innate immunity and followed by highly specific and memory-capable adaptive immunity.

细胞成分、识别机制、反应速度和是否具备记忆，是二者本质区别。 Cell types, recognition mechanisms, response speed, and memory capacity highlight the essential differences between these two systems.

适应性免疫应答的基本特征Cardinal features of adaptive immune responses

1
特异性 Specificity

每个淋巴细胞通过唯一的BCR或TCR识别特定抗原。 Each lymphocyte recognizes a specific antigen via a unique BCR or TCR.

即使是两个肽中单个氨基酸的变化，也可能导致该BCR或TCR不再识别。 Even a single amino acid difference between two peptides can abolish recognition by this BCR or TCR.

记住
一个淋巴细胞只表达一种抗原受体特异性。 Remember: one lymphocyte expresses only one antigen receptor specificity.
2
多样性 Diversity

免疫系统可产生极为庞大的受体种类，以识别地球上几乎所有抗原。 The immune system can generate an enormous variety of receptors to recognize nearly any antigen on earth.

可生成最多10¹¹种不同BCR，以及10¹⁶种不同TCR。 Up to 10¹¹ different BCRs and 10¹⁶ different TCRs can be generated.

人类基因组的基因数在2×10⁴到3×10⁴之间。 The number of genes in the human genome ranges between 2×10⁴ and 3×10⁴.

3
自身耐受性 Nonreactivity to self

通过中枢及外周机制，清除或控制对自身有反应性的淋巴细胞以维持免疫耐受。 Multiple central and peripheral mechanisms eliminate or control self-reactive lymphocytes to maintain immune tolerance.

4
克隆扩增 Clonal expansion

通过克隆扩增，抗原特异性淋巴细胞数目增加，以应对感染。 Clonal expansion increases the number of antigen-specific lymphocytes to match and control infections.

5
专一化 Specialization

不同类型的免疫反应针对不同入侵病原体进行优化。 Immune responses are optimized to the type of invading pathogen.

6
收缩与稳态 Contraction and homeostasis

抗原清除后，活化的效应细胞减少，仅保留一小部分长寿的记忆淋巴细胞。 After antigen clearance, the expanded pool of effector cells contracts, and a small subset of long-lived memory lymphocytes is maintained.

7
记忆 Memory

记忆保证免疫系统在再次遇到同一抗原时可作出更快更强的反应。 Memory ensures a faster and stronger response upon re-exposure to the same antigen.

基于功能状态的淋巴细胞分布 Lymphocyte distribution based on functional status

1
淋巴细胞的产生与分化 Lymphopoiesis and Differentiation

淋巴细胞的前体细胞在骨髓中产生（包括T、B细胞的前体）。 Lymphocyte precursors are generated in the bone marrow (including precursors for both T and B cells).

部分前体在骨髓内发育为B细胞；另一些迁移至胸腺继续分化为T细胞。 Some precursors become B cells in the bone marrow; others migrate to the thymus to continue differentiation as T cells.

淋巴细胞在中枢淋巴器官（骨髓与胸腺）完成初步分化，形成初始（naïve）T、B细胞。 Lymphocytes complete preliminary differentiation in central lymphoid organs (bone marrow and thymus), forming naïve T and B cells.

2
淋巴细胞的迁移与分布 Migration and Distribution

初始T、B细胞进入外周血液与二级淋巴器官（如淋巴结、脾脏）。 Naïve T and B cells enter the peripheral blood and secondary lymphoid organs (lymph nodes, spleen, etc.).

3
抗原驱动的激活与分化 Antigen-dependent activation and differentiation

在二级淋巴器官，初始淋巴细胞在抗原作用下被激活，部分分化为效应细胞，参与病原体清除；部分则成为记忆淋巴细胞。 In secondary lymphoid organs, naïve lymphocytes are activated by antigen exposure; some differentiate into effector cells to clear pathogens, others become memory lymphocytes.

4
效应功能的实现 Effector Function Realization

B细胞可分化为浆细胞，大量分泌抗体；T细胞可分化为辅助性、细胞毒性等亚型，协作或直接杀伤靶细胞。 B cells can differentiate into plasma cells and secrete large amounts of antibodies; T cells can become helper and cytotoxic subtypes to cooperate or directly kill target cells.

效应B/T淋巴细胞返回外周，参与病原体的清除。 Effector B/T lymphocytes return to peripheral sites to participate in pathogen elimination.

5
记忆淋巴细胞的形成 Formation of Memory Lymphocytes

极少数分化细胞会成为长寿的记忆T、B淋巴细胞，分布于全身，保证二次应答的迅速与高效。 A tiny subset of differentiated cells becomes long-lived memory T and B lymphocytes, distributed throughout the body to ensure a rapid and effective secondary response.

补充细节 Additional Details（根据课堂讲解）

免疫系统之所以能识别极其多样的抗原，是由于BCR/TCR的结构多样性由随机体细胞重组实现；人类基因数量有限（约三万个），但可通过基因重组产生千万亿种不同的受体。 The vast receptor diversity enabling recognition of countless antigens is achieved by random somatic recombination of BCR/TCR genes; although the human genome has only about 30,000 genes, recombination produces trillions of receptors.

“自身耐受”通过中枢和外周多种检查机制保证，及时清除/抑制自身反应性受体细胞，防止自身免疫。 Self-tolerance is enforced by multiple central and peripheral checkpoints to eliminate or suppress self-reactive immune cells, preventing autoimmunity.

免疫应答的最终目标是清除危险因素并恢复机体稳态（homeostasis）。 The ultimate goal of the immune response is to eliminate danger and restore homeostasis.

免疫细胞分布的化学因子调控机制及免疫细胞比例与适应性反应分区总结 The Role of Chemokines in Immune Cell Distribution & Cell Proportions and Compartmentalization of Adaptive Response

1
免疫细胞分布由趋化因子及其受体引导 Immune cell distribution is guided by chemokines and their corresponding receptors

趋化因子分为稳态型与炎症型，分别调控细胞在正常和炎症条件下的迁移与定位。 Chemokines are classified as homeostatic or inflammatory, regulating cell migration and localization under physiological and inflammatory conditions.

骨髓、胸腺、血液、脾脏、淋巴结、外周组织之间的细胞迁移均由各种趋化因子及相应受体决定。 Chemokine and receptor interactions direct immune cell movement among bone marrow, thymus, blood, spleen, lymph nodes, and peripheral tissues.

例如，T细胞、B细胞等表达CCR7、CXCR4、CXCR5等受体，介导它们在各组织间的分布和归巢。 For example, T and B cells express receptors such as CCR7, CXCR4, and CXCR5, which mediate their distribution and homing to specific tissues.

树突状细胞、嗜酸性粒细胞、单核细胞等也依赖多种受体（CCR2、CCR6、CX3CR1等）迁移至炎症发生部位或淋巴器官。 Dendritic cells, eosinophils, monocytes, and others rely on combinations (CCR2, CCR6, CX3CR1, etc.) to migrate to inflamed sites or lymphoid organs.

外周组织（如皮肤、肠道）通过上调趋化因子的表达，招募特定免疫细胞应对感染和损伤。 Peripheral tissues (e.g., skin, gut) upregulate chemokines to selectively recruit immune cells in response to infection or injury.

老师强调，免疫细胞的定向迁移依赖于化学因子-受体轴，既有动态调节也有局部精细化的归巢机制。 The instructor emphasized that immune cell trafficking depends on chemokine-receptor axes, featuring both dynamic adjustment and precise tissue-specific homing.

2
细胞因子调控免疫细胞的分化、增殖与激活 Cytokines regulate immune cell differentiation, proliferation, and activation

主要细胞因子家族包括生长因子、趋化因子、白介素、肿瘤坏死因子（TNF）、干扰素（IFN）及TGF家族。 Major cytokine families include growth factors, chemokines, interleukins, tumor necrosis factor (TNF), interferons (IFN), and transforming growth factor (TGF) family.

造血细胞因子（如SCF、TPO、IL-3等）调控祖细胞和前体细胞向各类血细胞分化，包括红、白细胞及巨核细胞等。 Hematopoietic cytokines (such as SCF, TPO, IL-3) regulate progenitor cell differentiation into blood cell lineages, including erythrocytes, leukocytes, and megakaryocytes.

炎症相关因子（如IL-1β、IL-6、CXCL8、TNF-α等）在上皮、内皮、巨噬细胞、中性粒细胞等广泛表达，参与炎症信号放大和细胞募集。 Proinflammatory cytokines (e.g., IL-1β, IL-6, CXCL8, TNF-α) are widely expressed in epithelial/endothelial cells, macrophages, neutrophils, etc., amplifying inflammatory signaling and cell recruitment.

细胞因子的多样性及其受体在不同免疫环境下产生复杂调控网络。 Cytokine and receptor diversity create complex regulatory networks in different immunological contexts.

课堂补充
有些细胞因子（如TGF-β、干扰素）在炎症结束时有抑制并修复损伤的作用，实现免疫平衡。 Class supplement: Certain cytokines (e.g., TGF-β, interferons) also suppress and repair after inflammation, restoring immune homeostasis.
3
骨髓与外周血液中主要免疫细胞比例 Proportions of major immune cell populations in bone marrow and blood
骨髓
粒细胞（主要为中性粒细胞）占比最高，为50-70%；淋巴细胞占15%；单核细胞及树突细胞各约2%。 Bone marrow: Granulocytes (mainly neutrophils) are the most abundant (50-70%), lymphocytes 15%, monocytes and dendritic cells about 2% each.
外周血
粒细胞（中性粒细胞）35-73%；淋巴细胞占15-52%；单核细胞4-13%；树突细胞<1%。 Peripheral blood: Granulocytes (neutrophils) 35-73%, lymphocytes 15-52%, monocytes 4-13%, dendritic cells <1%.

在淋巴细胞中，T细胞占75-85%（其中CD4+约2/3，CD8+约1/3）；B细胞、NK细胞各占5-15%。 Among lymphocytes, T cells constitute 75-85% (with CD4+ about 2/3, CD8+ about 1/3), B cells and NK cells each 5-15%.

4
适应性免疫反应的分区总结 Summary of compartmentalization in the adaptive immune response

初级淋巴器官（骨髓、胸腺）：淋巴细胞产生和成熟地，发生淋巴细胞发生（lymphopoiesis）。 Primary lymphoid organs (bone marrow, thymus): Sites for generation and maturation of lymphocytes, where lymphopoiesis occurs.

Lymphopoiesis特点
Features of lymphopoiesis:  - 获取功能性BCR或TCR，每细胞唯一特异性，组成高度多样的受体库；   Acquisition of a functional BCR or TCR, one specificity per cell, generating a highly diverse receptor repertoire;  - 清除无功能或自身反应性前体（中央耐受）。   Elimination of nonfunctional or self-reactive progenitors (central tolerance).

次级淋巴器官（淋巴结、脾脏、黏膜相关淋巴组织MALT）：负责初始淋巴细胞激活。 Secondary lymphoid organs (lymph nodes, spleen, MALT): Sites where naïve lymphocytes are activated.

在二级器官内进行抗原识别、克隆扩增、分化为效应细胞和记忆细胞。 Antigen recognition, clonal expansion and differentiation to effector and memory cells occur here.

激活树突状细胞迁入二级器官，递呈抗原。 Activated dendritic cells migrate to secondary organs to present antigens.

效应T细胞迁移到感染组织，执行抗原特异性免疫应答。 Effector T lymphocytes migrate to infected tissues for antigen-specific immune responses.

效应B细胞（浆细胞）迁移到骨髓，分泌抗体进入血液。 Effector B lymphocytes (plasma cells) migrate to bone marrow and release antibodies into the blood.

总目标是高效消除危险因素，恢复机体稳态。 The overall goal is efficient elimination of danger and restoration of homeostasis.

5
课堂关键补充 Class Key Additions

教师强调了免疫细胞分布受趋化因子-受体精确引导，部分细胞因子如TGF-β具备双重作用。 The instructor highlighted that immune cell distribution is precisely directed by chemokine-receptor interactions, and certain cytokines like TGF-β have dual functions.

基于免疫环境变化，细胞比例与迁移动态高度可塑。 Cell proportions and migration dynamics are highly plastic according to immunological context.

任一异常的定向迁移都可能导致免疫失衡或疾病，如自身免疫、免疫缺陷等。 Aberrant cell trafficking may lead to immune imbalance or disease (autoimmunity, immunodeficiency, etc.).

BCR和TCR的结构及抗体多样性产生机制 Structure of BCR and TCR & Mechanism of Antibody Diversity

1
BCR和TCR的基本结构 Basic Structure of BCR and TCR

BCR（B细胞受体）由两条重链和两条轻链组成，形成Y型结构。 BCR (B cell receptor) consists of two heavy chains and two light chains, forming a Y-shaped structure.

分泌性免疫球蛋白（如IgG）主要分布于血浆，BCR型IgM则锚定于B细胞膜表面。 Secreted immunoglobulins (like IgG) are found in the plasma, while BCR-type IgM is anchored on the surface of B cells.

每条链均含有恒定区（C区）和可变区（V区），抗原结合位点位于可变区N端。 Each chain contains a constant region (C region) and a variable region (V region), with the antigen binding site located at the N-terminus of the variable region.

Fab区负责与抗原结合，Fc区负责与细胞受体或补体结合。 The Fab region binds antigen, while the Fc region binds cell receptors or complement.

TCR（T细胞受体）由α链和β链组成（也有γδ链型），结构类似Ig中的一个Fab段，但只有一个抗原结合位点。 The TCR (T cell receptor) is composed of α and β chains (or γδ chains in some cases), structurally analogous to a single Fab fragment of Ig, but with only one antigen-binding site12.

2
抗体和TCR的抗原结合原理 Antigen Recognition by Antibody and TCR

BCR/抗体可直接结合游离抗原，结合区由两条链的可变区共同组成。 BCR/antibody can directly bind free antigens; the binding site is formed by the variable regions of both chains together.

TCR只能识别由自身MHC分子呈递的抗原肽，表现为MHC限制性。 TCR only recognizes antigen peptides presented by self-MHC molecules, manifesting as MHC restriction.

不论BCR还是TCR，抗原结合区由“互补决定区”（CDR）形成——三个位于轻链，三个位于重链或β链，共6个CDR形成结合位点。 Both BCR and TCR antigen-binding sites are formed by "complementarity-determining regions" (CDRs)—three CDRs on the light chain (or α chain for TCR), three on the heavy (or β) chain, totaling six per binding site2.

3
CDR决定抗原识别的特异性与多样性 CDR Determines Specificity and Diversity

可变区N端包含3个高变区（CDR1、CDR2、CDR3），这些环状片段是直接与抗原结合的实际部位。 The N-terminal variable region contains three hypervariable regions (CDR1, CDR2, and CDR3); these loop structures directly contact antigen.

每条轻链和每条重链各有一组CDR，组成完整抗原结合位点。 Each light and each heavy chain (or each α and β chain) provides a set of CDRs, forming the complete antigen-binding site.

BCR抗体的结合对象极为广泛，包括蛋白、肽、多糖、小分子化合物、脂类等。 BCR/antibodies can recognize diverse targets: proteins, peptides, polysaccharides, small chemicals, carbohydrates, DNA, and lipids.

TCR识别物则较为单一，仅为自身MHC分子呈递的肽分子。 TCRs largely recognize only peptides presented by self MHC molecules2.

4
数量级多样性产生的分子原因 Molecular Basis for Immense Diversity

每个人体中可产生千万级不同的免疫球蛋白分子（BCR多样性10^11种，TCR多样性10^16种）。 Each person can generate hundreds of millions of unique immunoglobulins (BCR diversity: ~10^11; TCR diversity: up to ~10^16).

这种多样性主要通过V　J片段的体细胞重组实现，而人体基因总量仅三万多。 This diversity arises from random V(D)J gene segment recombination; the total number of human genes is only about 30,000.
答案 D

轻链可变区氨基酸序列分析显示，CDR1、CDR2、CDR3三处高变区为抗原识别提供基础。 Sequence analysis of the light chain variable region shows that CDR1, CDR2, and CDR3 provide the foundation for antigen specificity23.

5
三维结构解析 Structural Analysis (Class Supplement)

晶体结构研究揭示Fab区的6个CDR环（轻链/α链3个，重链/β链3个）共同形成抗原结合表面，精确识别目标。 Crystal structures show that the 6 CDR loops (3 from light/α, 3 from heavy/β) together form the antigen-binding surface, ensuring high specificity.

TCR结合的肽-MHC复合物结构进一步确认CDR在与MHC和肽的结合中扮演中心角色。 TCR-peptide-MHC complex structures further confirm the central role of CDRs in interaction with both MHC and the presented peptide2.

6
课堂补充讲解 Key Classroom Supplements

教师强调，Ig分泌型和膜型在结构上主要差异为尾部结构及定位，功能分别用于体液免疫和抗原识别激活。 Instructor emphasized that the main difference between secreted and membrane-bound Igs is in the tailpiece and localization; these serve humoral immunity and antigen-recognition/activation functions, respectively1.

课堂指出，CDR结构可比作“手指”，通过其灵活环结构精准抓取抗原表位。 The CDR structure is likened to “fingers” which capture epitopes with high precision using flexible loops.

进一步补充，虽然免疫球蛋白类别和受体多样性极高，但每个淋巴细胞仅表达一种特异性BCR或TCR，确保免疫反应的特异性和克隆选择机制。 Further, despite high diversity in immunoglobulin classes and receptors, each lymphocyte expresses only a single specificity of BCR or TCR, ensuring immune specificity and clonal selection12.

小结 Summary

BCR和TCR的结构基础决定了抗原识别的高特异性和巨大的多样性，这一分子机制为免疫系统应对所有外来抗原提供了理论和实际基础。 The structural basis of BCR and TCR accounts for the extremely high specificity and enormous diversity in antigen recognition, providing both theoretical and practical foundations for the immune system’s ability to respond to all foreign antigens

CR和TCR多样性产生的体细胞重组机制 Mechanism of Somatic Recombination in BCR and TCR Diversity

1
单个初始B细胞的BCR特异性来源于基因片段的体细胞重组 One naive B lymphocyte = a single identity of BCR generated by somatic recombination of gene segments

每个初始B细胞仅表达一种BCR，其特异性由体细胞重组产生。 Each naive B cell expresses only one BCR, and its specificity is generated by somatic recombination.

免疫球蛋白基因（重链、轻链）在胚系状态下是非功能性的，类似乐高积木，需后天组装。 Germline immunoglobulin genes (heavy and light chains) are initially non-functional, like Lego pieces, and require assembly.

每条基因含有多个V（可变）、D（多样）、J（连接）片段（重链）；或V、J片段（轻链）；每个片段各自拥有独特DNA序列。 Each gene contains multiple V (variable), D (diversity), and J (joining) segments (for heavy chain), or V and J segments (for light chain), each with unique DNA sequence.

B细胞发育过程中，每类片段各随机选择一个，并拼接成完整的功能性基因。 During B cell development, one segment from each category is randomly selected and joined to create a functional gene.

2
体细胞重组产生抗体多样性机制 Mechanisms of Antibody Diversity via Somatic Recombination

【组合多样性】重组激活酶(RAG1/2)介导不同基因片段的随机拼接。 Combinatorial diversity: RAG1/2 recombinases mediate random joining of various gene segments.  - 重链：先D与J随机组合，再与V组合（如：51个V × 25个D × 6个J = 7650种重链）。   Heavy chain: random assembly of D with J, then joining with V (e.g., 51 V × 25 D × 6 J = 7650 possible heavy chains).  - 轻链：V与J随机组合（如：40个V × 5个J = 200种轻链，kappa和lambda两种）。   Light chain: random joining of V and J segments (e.g., 40 V × 5 J = 200 possible light chains, for kappa and lambda).

【连接多样性】端基脱氧核苷转移酶(TdT)插入或删除连接处核苷酸，进一步增加多样性。 Junctional diversity: Terminal deoxynucleotidyl transferase (TdT) introduces nucleotide insertions and deletions at junctions, amplifying diversity.

【等位排斥】每个B细胞只表达一个重链和一个轻链基因，确保每个BCR的唯一性。 Allelic exclusion: Each B cell expresses only one heavy and one light chain gene, guaranteeing single BCR specificity.

【同种型排斥】每个细胞仅表达kappa或lambda一种轻链（大约2/3为kappa，1/3为lambda）。 Isotypic exclusion: Only one type of light chain (either kappa or lambda) is expressed per cell (about 2/3 kappa, 1/3 lambda).

3
重链与轻链配对产生额外的多样性 Association Diversity via Heavy/Light Chain Pairing

每个BCR是随机重链与随机轻链的组合，两者多样性相乘进一步扩展识别能力。 Each BCR is formed by the random pairing of a randomly selected heavy chain with a randomly selected light chain, further expanding recognition diversity.

4
TCR多样性同样通过基因片段体细胞重组获得 TCR Diversity is Also Generated by Somatic Recombination of Gene Segments

TCRα链基因包含V和J片段，TCRβ链基因包含V、D、J片段，结构与BCR类似。 TCR α locus contains V and J segments; TCR β locus has V, D, and J segments—structurally akin to BCR recombination.

TCR多样性感染TCRα和TCRβ链的配对及其可变区（CDR1、CDR2、CDR3）的独特组合。 TCR diversity depends on pairing of diverse TCRα and TCRβ chains and unique combinations of CDR1, CDR2, and CDR3 in their variable regions.

CDR3多样性最为丰富，直接参与与抗原（肽-MHC复合物）的结合。 CDR3 domain is the most diverse and is directly involved in binding peptide-MHC complexes.

5
课堂补充讲解 Key Classroom Highlights

老师强调，基因重组并非传统意义上的“一个基因—一种蛋白”，而是多片段拼接生成多样性。 Instructor emphasized that recombination breaks the traditional "one gene, one protein" rule, instead creating diversity through the assembly of multiple segments.

学到的概念还包括，单个B/T细胞仅表达唯一受体，且此多样性来源于体细胞重组和随机性。 Key points also include: each B/T cell expresses just a single receptor, with immense diversity stemming from somatic recombination and randomness.

实际可产生数千万种不同BCR，数以万亿种不同TCR，远超基因组内约三万个常规基因。 In reality, humans can produce tens of millions of BCRs and trillions of TCRs, vastly exceeding the ~30,000 conventional genes in the genome.

小结 Summary

抗体和TCR的多样性本质上由胚系基因片段的体细胞重组、连接多样性和链配对实现，为适应性免疫提供理论基础。 The extraordinary diversity of antibodies and TCRs is essentially achieved through somatic recombination of germline gene segments, junctional diversity, and chain pairing, forming the basis of adaptive immunity.

中枢耐受与T淋巴细胞胸腺选择 Central Tolerance and Thymic Selection of T Lymphocytes

1
BCR和TCR特异性随机产生及自我耐受机制 BCR and TCR specificities are generated randomly and how self-tolerance is achieved
BCR和TCR的特异性是通过V　J重组随机产生的。 BCR and TCR specificities are generated randomly through V(D)J recombination.
答案 D

随机生成的受体中部分会对自身抗原有反应，必须建立机制避免自身免疫反应。 Some receptors generated randomly will react with self-antigens, so mechanisms are needed to prevent autoimmunity.

问问题：BCR和TCR库如何区分自身与非自身？ Question: How is the BCR and TCR repertoire educated to distinguish self from non-self?
答

中枢耐受指淋巴细胞在初级淋巴器官（骨髓、胸腺）发育过程中，对自身抗原高度反应（自反应性）者被清除或灭活。 Central tolerance refers to the elimination or inactivation of self-reactive lymphocytes during development in primary lymphoid organs (bone marrow, thymus).

以T细胞为例，主要在胸腺内发生中枢耐受过程。 T cells, as an example, undergo central tolerance mainly in the thymus.

2
T细胞发育与胸腺选择过程概览 Overview of T Cell Development and Thymic Selection

① 骨髓产生T细胞前体，迁移至胸腺发育为胸腺细胞。 (1) T cell precursors are produced in the bone marrow and migrate to the thymus to develop into thymocytes.

② 早期胸腺细胞（DN细胞）不表达CD3、CD4、CD8和TCR。 (2) Early thymocytes (DN, double negative) do not express CD3, CD4, CD8, or TCR.

③ 经过基因重排，形成TCR，进入CD4+CD8+双阳性（DP）阶段。 (3) After gene rearrangement, they express TCR and enter the CD4+CD8+ double positive (DP) stage.

④ DP细胞通过两条分化途径：一部分分化为γδT细胞，多数继续为αβT细胞。 (4) DP cells can become γδT cells or, mostly, continue as αβT cells.

⑤ 阳性选择（Positive Selection）：功能性TCR与胸腺皮质上皮细胞的自身MHC结合可促进生存，否则凋亡。 (5) Positive selection: Functional TCR binding to self-MHC on cortical thymic epithelial cells promotes survival; otherwise, cells undergo apoptosis.

⑥ 决定分化方向，成为CD4+单阳性（SP）或CD8+单阳性T细胞。 (6) Lineage commitment: Cells differentiate into either CD4+ single positive (SP) or CD8+ SP T cells.

⑦ 负选择（Negative Selection）：自反应性TCR（强烈识别自身抗原）被树突状细胞递呈自身抗原诱导凋亡，避免自身免疫。 (7) Negative selection: Autoreactive T cells (strongly recognizing self antigens) bind self-antigen presented by dendritic cells and undergo apoptosis, preventing autoimmunity.

⑧ 仅存低亲和力或无自身反应的T细胞成熟，离开胸腺成为功能性外周T细胞。 (8) Only T cells with low or no self-reactivity mature and exit the thymus as functional peripheral T cells.

3
胸腺选择详细过程与分区（结合图解） Detailed Process and Regionalization of Thymic Selection (with Diagram Reference)
