皮质区（Cortex）：阳性选择，胸腺上皮细胞主要递呈自身MHC分子，测试TCR能否识别MHC（即MHC限制性）。 Cortex: Positive selection; thymic epithelial cells present self-MHC molecules, testing if TCR can recognize self-MHC (MHC restriction).

只有能识别自身MHC的DP细胞才能存活并分化，否则凋亡。 Only DP cells that can recognize self-MHC survive and differentiate; others undergo apoptosis.

髓质区（Medulla）：负选择，树突状细胞递呈多种自身抗原，强烈自反应的TCR被诱导凋亡。 Medulla: Negative selection; dendritic cells present a variety of self-antigens and strongly autoreactive TCRs are induced to undergo apoptosis.

髓质区的胸腺髓质上皮细胞表达AIRE转录因子，可启动多种外周组织抗原的表达，使T细胞对全身自身抗原耐受。 Medullary thymic epithelial cells express AIRE (AutoImmune REgulator) transcription factor, allowing expression of various peripheral tissue antigens, promoting systemic self-tolerance in T cells.

4
课堂补充讲解 Key Classroom Additions

教师强调，“教育”本质为消除无法通过自身耐受筛选的胸腺细胞，而不是“训练”其容忍自身抗原。 The instructor emphasized that "education" is essentially the elimination (not training) of thymocytes that fail self-tolerance screening.

大量T细胞在发育过程中因不符合阳性/负选择标准而死亡，仅极少数通过筛选。 A large number of T cells die during development because they do not meet positive or negative selection criteria; only very few survive selection.

若自反应细胞未被清除，可能导致自身免疫疾病。 If autoreactive cells are not cleared, they may cause autoimmune diseases.

课上还补充，B细胞也有类似于中枢耐受的过程，主要发生在骨髓中。 It was also mentioned that B cells undergo a similar central tolerance process mainly occurring in the bone marrow.

5
小结 Summary

中枢耐受通过胸腺选择过程实现，避免了大部分自反应T细胞进入外周，是免疫系统自我-非自我识别和防止自身免疫疾病的关键机制。 Central tolerance is established through thymic selection, preventing most autoreactive T cells from entering the periphery, and is a key mechanism for self-nonself discrimination and protection against autoimmunity.

B细胞耐受B cell tolerance

1
高亲和力自身抗原识别的B细胞命运 Fate of B cells with high-avidity self-antigen recognition

在骨髓中，高亲和力识别自身抗原的B细胞属于自反应性B细胞。 In the bone marrow, B cells that recognize self-antigen with high avidity are considered self-reactive B cells.

这类B细胞可通过三种主要机制被消除或修正： These B cells can be eliminated or modified via three main mechanisms:

受体编辑：B细胞尝试重新排列Ig可变区基因，表达新BCR以避免自反应性。 Receptor editing: The B cell attempts rearrangement of the Ig variable region genes to express a new BCR and avoid self-reactivity.

凋亡/克隆删除：若仍然自反应，B细胞将被诱导进入程序性细胞死亡（凋亡），被清除出体内。 Apoptosis/clonal deletion: If still self-reactive, the B cell undergoes programmed cell death (apoptosis) and is eliminated.

若受体编辑成功，B细胞发育为“非自反应性B细胞”，可进入外周组织继续发育。 If receptor editing is successful, the B cell becomes a "non-self-reactive B cell" and can continue to peripheral tissues.

这是中枢耐受（central tolerance）的经典例子。 This is a typical example of central tolerance.

2
低亲和力自身抗原识别的B细胞命运 Fate of B cells with low-avidity self-antigen recognition

对自身抗原低亲和力识别的B细胞不被直接清除。 B cells with low-avidity recognition of self-antigen are not directly deleted.

这类B细胞中，BCR表达下调，信号通路被阻断，功能被“失能化”（Anergy，无能状态）。 These B cells have reduced BCR expression, blocked signaling, and become functionally "anergic" (unresponsive state).

失能性B细胞进入外周组织，无法被激活，难以参与免疫应答。 Anergic B cells enter peripheral tissues but cannot be activated and are unlikely to participate in immune responses.

这也是中枢耐受的一种机制，防止低度自反应细胞致病。 This is also a mechanism of central tolerance to prevent mildly self-reactive cells from causing disease.

3
总结 Summary

B细胞耐受的核心在于：高亲和力自反应细胞经受体编辑或克隆删除被消灭，低亲和力自反应细胞被功能性“失能化”（Anergy）。 Central to B cell tolerance: high-avidity self-reactive cells are removed through receptor editing or clonal deletion; low-avidity self-reactive cells are functionally silenced (anergy).

成功通过筛选的非自反应B细胞进入外周，准备应对非自身抗原。 Successfully screened non-self-reactive B cells enter the periphery, ready to respond to foreign antigens.

该过程保证机体自稳性，最大限度降低自身免疫疾病风险。 This process ensures self-tolerance and minimizes the risk of autoimmune diseases.

淋巴细胞根据功能状态的分布Lymphocyte distribution based on functional status

1
淋巴细胞的“发源地”——造血与发育 Lymphopoiesis and development as the "origin" of lymphocytes

原始造血干细胞(HSC)在红骨髓中分化出淋巴细胞前体细胞。 Hematopoietic stem cells (HSC) in the red bone marrow differentiate into lymphoid progenitor cells.

B细胞在骨髓发育成熟，T细胞前体迁移至胸腺进行进一步分化（1、2阶段）。 B cells mature in the bone marrow; T cell precursors migrate to the thymus for further differentiation (steps 1 & 2).

融合课程补充：造血过程中化学因子的引导决定了细胞归巢和分布。 Additional note: Chemokine signals direct cellular homing and distribution during lymphopoiesis.

2
初始淋巴细胞和外周循环 Naïve lymphocytes and peripheral recirculation

成熟但未遇抗原的B、T细胞（naive B, T cells）离开心脏后遍布外周循环和次级淋巴器官（如淋巴结、脾等）。 Mature but antigen-inexperienced B and T cells (naïve B, T cells) leave the primary lymphoid organs and circulate in the periphery and secondary lymphoid organs (e.g., lymph nodes, spleen).

未遇抗原的初始淋巴细胞不断在血液与次级淋巴器官间循环。 Naïve lymphocytes continually recirculate between the blood and secondary lymphoid organs.

课程补充：初始淋巴细胞的寿命较短，不断被新生淋巴细胞替代，稳态由限定生长因子维持。 Further explained: Naïve lymphocytes have limited lifespan and are constantly replaced by newly generated cells, with homeostasis maintained by growth factors.

3
抗原依赖性激活与效应/记忆分化 Antigen-dependent activation and effector/memory differentiation

次级淋巴器官是初始淋巴细胞遇抗原、被激活的唯一生理环境（3阶段）。 Secondary lymphoid organs are the only physiological sites where naïve lymphocytes encounter antigens and are activated (step 3).

抗原呈递细胞，主要为树突状细胞，将抗原递送至初始T细胞区，启动克隆扩增和分化（4阶段）。 Antigen-presenting cells, mainly dendritic cells, deliver antigens to naïve T cell zones, initiating clonal expansion and differentiation (step 4).

T、B细胞可进一步分化为效应细胞（如T细胞亚群、浆细胞）和记忆细胞。 T and B cells can further differentiate into effector cells (e.g., T cell subsets, plasma cells) and memory cells.

课程补充：效应T/B细胞会迁移至感染/炎症部位，辅助或执行免疫防御，记忆细胞保障长久防护。 Addition: Effector T/B cells migrate to infection/inflammation sites for immune defense, while memory cells ensure long-term protection.

初始B淋巴细胞的激活Activation of naïve B lymphocytes

1
初始B细胞激活类型 Types of naïve B cell activation

BCR识别抗原后，B细胞激活分为两类： After BCR recognizes antigen, B cell activation is classified as:

胸腺非依赖型（thymo-independent，主要应对多糖抗原）：无需T细胞帮助，可迅速分化为IgM分泌浆母细胞，产生短寿命效应B细胞。 Thymo-independent (mainly against carbohydrate antigens): Does not require T cell help, rapidly differentiates into IgM-producing plasmablasts, giving rise to short-lived effector B cells.

胸腺依赖型（thymo-dependent，针对蛋白抗原）：需T细胞帮助，抗原被摄取、加工并递呈给效应T细胞，启动T-B合作。 Thymo-dependent (for protein antigens): Requires T cell help; antigens are taken up, processed, and presented to effector T cells to initiate T-B cooperation.

课堂补充：大多数针对蛋白质的高亲和力抗体依赖T细胞辅助。 Additional note: Most high-affinity antibodies against protein antigens depend on T cell help.

2
生成中心反应与B细胞分化 Germinal center response and B cell differentiation

在生发中心（Germinal center）反应中，B细胞经历亲和力成熟，发生类交换（生成IgG、IgA、IgE等）。 In the germinal center response, B cells undergo affinity maturation and isotype switching (producing IgG, IgA, IgE, etc.).

成熟的浆细胞长期分泌高亲和力抗体，部分B细胞分化为记忆B细胞。 Mature plasma cells secrete high-affinity antibodies long-term; some B cells differentiate into memory B cells.

课堂强调：记忆B细胞和高亲和力浆细胞是持久免疫的物质基础。 Highlight: Memory B cells and high-affinity plasma cells are the material basis for lasting immunity.

3
总结 Summary

初始B细胞经胸腺非依赖或依赖途径被激活，最终分化为短寿命效应B细胞、长寿命浆细胞和记忆B细胞。 Naïve B cells may be activated through thymo-independent or thymo-dependent pathways, eventually differentiating into short-lived effector B cells, long-lived plasma cells, and memory B cells.

生发中心相关过程提升抗体亲和力与类型多样性。 Germinal center processes increase antibody affinity and diversify antibody classes.

强调了记忆B细胞与持久免疫的重要性。 Emphasis on the importance of memory B cells and durable immunity.

T依赖性B细胞应答：生发中心反应 T-dependent B cell response: the germinal center reaction

概述：B细胞在继发性淋巴器官发生T细胞依赖性激活过程。 Overview: B cells undergo T cell-dependent activation in secondary lymphoid organs.

1
B细胞识别抗原并呈递：初始抗原（Native Ag）通过BCR被B细胞识别，随后内吞、加工，并以MHC-II分子呈递给T辅助细胞（CD4+ T细胞）。
1
B cell antigen recognition and presentation: Native antigen is recognized by B cell receptors (BCR), then internalized, processed, and presented via MHC-II to CD4+ T helper cells.
2
T细胞帮助激活B细胞：T辅助细胞通过CD40L与B细胞的CD40结合并分泌细胞因子，从而激活B细胞。 2. T cell help for B cell activation: CD4+ T helper cells activate B cells through CD40L-CD40 interactions and cytokine secretion.
3
生发中心主要反应步骤及分区： 3. Main steps of germinal center reaction and zonation:

（1）准克隆性扩增（Quasi-clonal expansion）：B细胞在暗区（dark zone）大量增殖，形成中心母细胞。 (1) Quasi-clonal expansion: B cells undergo massive proliferation in the dark zone to form centroblasts.

（2）体细胞高变（Somatic hypermutations）：BCR可变区（V区）发生AID酶介导的点突变，产生多样性。 (2) Somatic hypermutations: BCR variable region (V region) undergoes AID-mediated point mutations for diversity.

（3）亲和力成熟及选择（Affinity maturation）：在明区（light zone），B细胞与滤泡树突状细胞（FDC）竞争抗原，具有高亲和力BCR的细胞被选中存活，低亲和力者凋亡。 (3) Affinity maturation and selection: In the light zone, B cells compete for antigen presented by FDCs, high-affinity BCR cells are selected to survive, low-affinity ones undergo apoptosis.

（4）抗体类别转换和终末分化（Class switch & terminal differentiation）：获选B细胞可发生抗体类别转换（如IgM→IgG/IgA/IgE），并分化为记忆B细胞或长寿浆细胞。 (4) Class switch and terminal differentiation: Selected B cells can undergo isotype switch (e.g., IgM to IgG/IgA/IgE) and differentiate into memory B cells or long-lived plasma cells.

分区结构： Structure of zones:

暗区（Dark zone）：细胞密度高，克隆性扩增区域。 Dark zone: High cell density, site of clonal expansion.

明区（Light zone）：细胞密度低，不识别抗原的细胞发生凋亡。 Light zone: Low cell density, apoptosis of cells not recognizing antigen.

生发中心产生的细胞类型： Types of cells generated from germinal center:

记忆B细胞（Memory B cells）：保留BCR，经过类别转换，并在再次遇到抗原时可快速响应。 Memory B cells: Retain BCR, have undergone isotype switching, and respond rapidly upon re-exposure.

浆细胞（Plasma cells，长寿）：大量分泌高亲和力抗体。 Plasma cells (long-lived): Secrete large quantities of high-affinity antibodies.

核心机制补充（老师讲解）： Core mechanisms (from lecture):

高亲和力BCR选择关键在于抗原竞争，高亲和力B细胞可多次获得T细胞辅助。 Key to high-affinity BCR selection is antigen competition, high-affinity B cells can receive T cell help multiple times.

类别转换和亲和力成熟依赖T细胞辅助；只有获得帮助的B细胞才能存活及分化。 Class switch and affinity maturation depend on T cell help; only B cells that receive help survive and differentiate.

生发中心反应可多次发生，记忆B细胞终生可对抗原作进一步亲和力成熟。 Germinal center reactions can occur multiple times; memory B cells can undergo further affinity maturation throughout life.

重要补充：TCR不会在外周发生高变，只BCR可通过生发中心反应不断优化抗体亲和力。 Important note: TCRs are never hypermutated in the periphery; only BCRs optimize affinity via germinal center reactions.

专有名词解释： Terminology:

AID酶：活化诱导胞苷脱氨酶，介导BCR可变区C→U突变，促进高变。 AID enzyme: Activation-induced cytidine deaminase, mediates C to U mutations in BCR variable regions for hypermutation.

FDC：滤泡树突状细胞，呈递抗原并辅助高亲和力B细胞选择。 FDC: Follicular dendritic cell, presents antigen and assists high-affinity B cell selection.

B细胞活化的概要 Summary of B Lymphocyte Activation →

B细胞的初始活化场所是次级淋巴器官。 The activation of naïve B cells takes place in secondary lymphoid organs.

初始B细胞通过BCR与特定抗原结合后被激活。 The BCR of a naïve B lymphocyte is activated upon binding a specific antigen (Ag).

如无额外刺激，激活的B细胞仅增殖并分化为短寿命的IgM分泌浆母细胞，属于T非依赖性应答。 Without further stimuli, activated B cells proliferate and differentiate into short-lived IgM-producing plasmablasts (T-independent response).

若抗原为蛋白，B细胞内吞、加工并将其呈递给T辅助细胞，引发T依赖性应答（即生发中心反应）： If the antigen is a protein, it is endocytosed, processed, and presented to T-helper lymphocytes, leading to a T-dependent response (germinal center reaction):

克隆扩增并在抗体可变区发生体细胞高突变。 Clonal expansion with somatic hypermutations in immunoglobulin variable regions.

亲和力成熟/选择过程：B细胞竞争抗原，亲和力高者被选中。 Affinity maturation/selection of modified immunoglobulins, driven by competition for the antigen.

发生抗体类别转换并生成长寿命浆细胞（IgG、IgA或IgE）和记忆B细胞。 Class switching and production of long-lived plasma cells (IgG, IgA, or IgE) and memory B cells.

再次遇到相同抗原时，记忆B细胞可快速产生更高效、高亲和力的抗体应答。 Upon re-exposure to the antigen, memory B cells enable a faster, more effective response with high-affinity antibodies.

抗原提呈与HLA分子类型 Antigen Presentation and Types of HLA Molecules →

抗体无法穿透细胞检测细胞内的病原体或非自身抗原。 Antibodies cannot penetrate cells to detect pathogens or non-self antigens inside the cells.

抗原（肽片段）在细胞表面由主要组织相容性复合物（MHC，人体称为HLA）加载并展示。 Antigens (peptides) are displayed on the cell surface, loaded onto Major Histocompatibility Complex (MHC), referred to as HLA in humans.

经典HLA分子有两类，分别呈递来自不同细胞区室的肽，与两类T细胞亚群（CD4+和CD8+）互作。 There are two classes of classic HLA molecules, each presenting peptides from two distinct cellular compartments and interacting with different T cell subsets: CD4+ and CD8+.

HLA分子相关肽的加工与提呈途径 Peptides Associated to HLA Molecules: Processing and Presentation Pathways →

I类HLA分子特性： Class I HLA Molecules:

由所有有核细胞表达。 Expressed by all nucleated cells.

主要提呈来源于胞内/胞质蛋白（如自身蛋白、病毒、肿瘤抗原）产生的肽。 Present peptides mostly derived from endogenous/cytoplasmic proteins (e.g., self proteins, viral proteins, tumor antigens).

肽经蛋白酶体降解，由TAP转运至内质网与I类HLA结合，最终表现在细胞表面，被CD8+T细胞识别，引发细胞毒应答。 After cytosolic proteins are digested by proteasomes, peptides are transported by TAP to the ER, bind to class I HLA, are presented on the cell surface and recognized by CD8+ T lymphocytes, triggering a cytotoxic response.

II类HLA分子特性： Class II HLA Molecules:

仅专业抗原提呈细胞（DCs、B细胞、单核/巨噬细胞）表达。 Expressed by professional antigen-presenting cells (DCs, B cells, mono/macrophages).

主要提呈来源于胞外蛋白（如细菌、吞噬内吞抗原）产生的肽。 Present peptides mostly derived from exogenous proteins (e.g., bacteria, endocytosed antigens).

肽在吞噬体/溶酶体中降解，随后与内质网生成的II类HLA结合，在细胞表面展示，被CD4+T细胞识别，引发细胞因子应答。 Peptides are digested in endosomes/lysosomes, bind to class II HLA in the ER, presented on the cell surface and recognized by CD4+ T lymphocytes, triggering a cytokine response.

老师补充讲解内容 Additional Key Points from Instructor →

体液免疫与细胞免疫的分工：抗体负责中和与清除胞外抗原，而细胞内抗原需依赖MHC/HLA分子与特定T细胞协同处理。 Humoral and cellular immunity complement each other: antibodies neutralize and eliminate extracellular antigens, while intracellular antigens require MHC/HLA molecules and corresponding T cell cooperation.

抗体异型（IgG亚型、IgA、IgE等）承担不同效应功能，如中和、调理促吞噬、补体活化及依赖抗体的细胞毒作用等。 Antibody isotypes (IgG subclasses, IgA, IgE) have distinct effector functions, such as neutralization, opsonization for enhanced phagocytosis, complement activation, and antibody-dependent cellular cytotoxicity.

所有人体细胞表面均有蛋白防护，抑制补体介导损伤，细菌缺乏此机制更易受攻击。 All human cells possess surface regulatory proteins that inhibit complement-mediated damage, making bacteria more susceptible due to lack of such protection.

免疫突触或超分子激活簇（SMAC）Immune synapse or supramolecular activation cluster (SMAC) →

免疫突触是T细胞与APC之间有序的接触区域，其中TCR、共受体、协同刺激分子、黏附分子和信号蛋白重新定位。 The immune synapse is the organized contact zone between the T cell and APC, where the TCR, coreceptors, costimulatory proteins, adhesion molecules, and signaling proteins relocalize.

主要分子包括：CD4或CD8、CD28-CD86、LFA1-ICAM1。 Main molecules comprise: CD4 or CD8, CD28-CD86, LFA1-ICAM1.

突触结构稳定T细胞与APC的相互作用，并组装参与受体的信号转导装置。 The synapse stabilizes T cell-APC interaction and assembles the signaling machinery for the engaged receptors.

该结构确保细胞因子和分泌颗粒能精准传递到APC或靶细胞。 This arrangement ensures precise delivery of cytokines and secretory granules to APCs or target cells.

免疫突触有助于信号分子的降解与周转，是终止T细胞活化的关键机制。 The immune synapse is essential for turnover and degradation of signaling molecules, thus terminating T cell activation on time.

结构与机制补充（结合老师讲解）Structural and Mechanistic Supplement (Based on Lecture) →

免疫突触中，TCR与其辅助分子（CD3、CD4或CD8）聚集于中央区域（cSMAC），黏附分子（如LFA-1）集中在外围（pSMAC），以保障强有力的信号传递与细胞粘附。 Within the immune synapse, TCR and CD3/CD4 or CD8 cluster in the central area (cSMAC), while adhesion molecules (like LFA-1) gather at the periphery (pSMAC), ensuring robust signal transduction and cell adhesion.

信号转导过程中，CD28等协同刺激分子激活多条下游通路（如PI3K、MAPK、NFAT、NF-κB），最终启动T细胞活化、增殖与效应功能。 During signaling, costimulatory proteins (e.g., CD28) activate numerous downstream pathways (such as PI3K, MAPK, NFAT, and NF-κB), ultimately triggering T cell activation, proliferation, and effector functions.

通过这种分区结构和信号集成，免疫突触能精准调节T细胞的命运，包括分泌因子、效应杀伤与耐受。 By this zoned architecture and integrated signaling, the immune synapse accurately regulates T cell fate, including cytokine secretion, effector killing, and induction of tolerance.

图示说明及信号例举Illustration Notes and Signal Examples →

左图显示免疫突触的空间分布：T细胞和APC之间建立分层信号区，包括cSMAC、pSMAC和dSMAC。 The left figure demonstrates the spatial layout of the immune synapse: T cell and APC interface forming layered signaling zones, including cSMAC, pSMAC, and dSMAC.

右图展示信号通路，譬如MHC-II与CD4结合、B7家族分子与CD28相互作用后激活关键信号分子（如RAS、PI3K、PLCγ等），进而影响Ca²⁺信号、NFAT、NF-κB、AP-1等转录因子。 The right diagram outlines signaling cascades, e.g., engagement of MHC-II with CD4 and B7 family with CD28 activates signaling proteins like RAS, PI3K, PLCγ, and so forth, influencing Ca²⁺ signals, NFAT, NF-κB, AP-1, and related transcription factors.

课堂补充要点Additional Key Points from Lecture →

老师强调，免疫突触的形成使得T细胞获得持续且高效的激活信号，并通过动态调控信号分子的降解与外排来终止激活，避免过度免疫反应。 The instructor emphasized that formation of the immune synapse allows T cells to receive sustained and potent activation signals, while dynamic regulation of signaling molecule degradation and export terminates activation, preventing excessive immune responses.

免疫突触也为免疫耐受的建立提供平台，在共刺激信号缺失时可诱导T细胞无反应或耐受现象。 The immune synapse also provides a platform for tolerance induction; in the absence of costimulatory signals, T cell anergy or tolerance may be established.

免疫突触或超分子激活簇（SMAC）Immune Synapse or Supramolecular Activation Cluster (SMAC)

免疫突触是T细胞和抗原递呈细胞（APC）之间有序的接触区，TCR、共受体、协同刺激分子、黏附分子和信号蛋白会在该区域重新定位。 The immune synapse is the organized contact zone between the T cell and the antigen-presenting cell (APC), where TCR, coreceptors, costimulatory proteins, adhesion molecules, and signaling proteins relocalize.

主要分子包括CD4或CD8、CD28-CD86、LFA1-ICAM1等。 Main molecules include CD4 or CD8, CD28-CD86, LFA1-ICAM1, etc.

免疫突触能够稳定T细胞与APC的结合，并组装相关受体的信号转导装置。 The immune synapse stabilizes the T cell-APC interaction and assembles the signaling machinery of involved receptors.

促使细胞因子和分泌颗粒能精准传递到APC或靶细胞。 It ensures precise delivery of cytokines and secretory granules to APCs or target cells.

促进信号分子的降解和周转，是终止T细胞活化的关键环节。 The immune synapse is key for turnover and degradation of signaling molecules, terminating T cell activation.

结构机制补充与信号通路（结合老师讲解）Structural and Mechanistic Supplement with Signaling Pathways (from Lecture)

免疫突触中心为cSMAC（TCR、CD3、CD4或CD8等集中），外圈为pSMAC（如LFA-1等集中），保证信号传导和细胞粘附。 The core of the immune synapse (cSMAC) gathers TCR, CD3, and CD4/CD8, while the peripheral ring (pSMAC) contains adhesion molecules like LFA-1, ensuring signal transduction and firm cell adhesion.

协同刺激分子（如CD28）激活多条下游信号通路（PI3K、MAPK、NFAT、NF-κB等），引导T细胞活化、增殖和效应分化。 Costimulatory proteins (e.g., CD28) activate downstream signaling pathways (such as PI3K, MAPK, NFAT, NF-κB) that lead to T cell activation, proliferation, and effector differentiation.

通过动态调控信号分子的降解与外排，免疫突触能终止T细胞活化，防止免疫反应过强。 Through dynamic regulation of degradation and export of signaling molecules, the synapse helps terminate T cell activation and prevent overactive immune responses.

T细胞表面标志物随激活的变化 Changes in T Cell Surface Markers Along Activation

T细胞在激活过程中会依次表达CD69、CD25（IL-2Rα）、CD40L和CTLA-4等标志物。 During activation, T cells sequentially express markers such as CD69, CD25 (IL-2Rα), CD40L, and CTLA-4.

CD69有助于T细胞保留在淋巴器官，避免过早迁出。 CD69 retains T cells in the lymphoid organ, preventing premature exit.

CD25（IL-2Rα）促进对IL-2的高敏反应，驱动T细胞增殖。 CD25 (IL-2Rα) enables high sensitivity to IL-2, driving T cell proliferation through autocrine growth signaling.

CD40L的表达有助于激活APC，促进免疫协同。 CD40L helps activate antigen-presenting cells, supporting immune collaboration.

CTLA-4作为负调节分子，抑制T细胞活化，最终实现免疫反应的负反馈自控。 CTLA-4 acts as a negative regulator to inhibit T cell activation, providing feedback control for the immune response.

初始CD4+T细胞分化为不同效应亚型 Differentiation of Naïve CD4+ T Lymphocytes upon Ag-Specific Activation

在次级淋巴器官，效应反应根据树突状细胞遇到的“危险信号”适应性调整，细胞因子起核心调节作用。 In secondary lymphoid organs, the effector response adapts to the danger signals encountered by dendritic cells, with cytokines playing a central regulatory role.

IL-12诱导分化为Th1，IL-4诱导分化为Th2，IL-6和IL-23诱导分化为Th17，TGF-β促分化为Treg。 IL-12 induces differentiation into Th1, IL-4 into Th2, IL-6 and IL-23 into Th17, and TGF-β into Treg.

活化/记忆型Th细胞在外周组织需再次遇到特异性抗原方能发挥效应功能。 Effector/memory Th cells in peripheral tissues must be reactivated by specific antigens to execute their effector function.

Th细胞通过产生特定细胞因子，协助和增强其他免疫细胞的功能。 T helper cells produce specific cytokines that help and enhance the function of other immune cells.

TH1和TH2淋巴细胞的效应功能 Effector Function of TH1 and Th2 Lymphocytes

TH1细胞通过产生IFN-γ，激活巨噬细胞，增强杀菌能力；促进B细胞产生IgG抗体，辅助中和、调理和补体结合。 TH1 cells produce IFN-γ to activate macrophages (enhancing microbial killing), and promote B cells to generate IgG, aiding in neutralization, opsonization, and complement binding.

TH1细胞还分泌LT和TNF，促进中性粒细胞激活和炎症反应。 TH1 cells secrete LT and TNF, promoting neutrophil activation and inflammation.

TH2细胞产生IL-4促使B细胞生成IgE，参与肥大细胞脱颗粒，以及IL-5激活嗜酸性粒细胞，主要参与抗寄生虫和过敏反应。 TH2 cells produce IL-4 for IgE production by B cells (mast cell degranulation) and IL-5 for eosinophil activation, mainly mediating anti-parasite and allergy responses.

IL-4和IL-13还可诱导巨噬细胞替代性活化，促进组织修复和抗炎。 IL-4 and IL-13 also drive alternative activation of macrophages, promoting tissue repair and suppressing inflammation.

TH17和Treg淋巴细胞的效应功能 Effector Function of TH17 and Treg Lymphocytes

TH17细胞经IL-17、IL-22信号促进组织细胞产生趋化因子、抗菌肽及增强上皮屏障，提高炎症反应和中性粒细胞聚集。 TH17 cells promote the release of chemokines, antimicrobial peptides, and increased barrier function through IL-17 and IL-22, heightening inflammation and neutrophil response.

Treg细胞通过分泌IL-10、TGF-β等抑制性细胞因子抑制其他免疫细胞活性，并能通过CTLA-4与PD-1等途径直接介导免疫调节和耐受。 Treg cells suppress the activity of other immune cells by producing inhibitory cytokines (IL-10, TGF-β) and mediate immune regulation and tolerance via CTLA-4, PD-1, etc.

Treg还能通过外泌腺苷等代谢产物，进一步抑制炎症反应，维持免疫稳态。 Treg cells also secrete adenosine and other metabolic products to further suppress inflammation and maintain immune homeostasis.

072025-12-02

超敏反应及其类型 Hypersensitivity and Its Types

超敏反应定义
当机体接触外来物质时，若免疫应答正常，能清除致病因子；若产生不当或过度的免疫反应，则称为超敏反应。 Definition of hypersensitivity: After the body encounters a foreign body, a normal immune response leads to elimination of the threat; if an inappropriate or overactive immune response occurs, it is called hypersensitivity.

经典Gell与Coombs分类分为四型： The classic Gell and Coombs classification divides hypersensitivity into four types:

I型=速发型超敏反应（immediate hypersensitivity） Type I = immediate hypersensitivity

II型=细胞毒型超敏反应（cytotoxic hypersensitivity） Type II = cytotoxic hypersensitivity

III型=免疫复合物介导型超敏反应（immune complexes-mediated hypersensitivity） Type III = immune complexes-mediated hypersensitivity

IV型=迟发型超敏反应（delayed hypersensitivity） Type IV = delayed hypersensitivity

I型或速发型超敏反应 Type I or Immediate Hypersensitivity

特点：临床症状在几分钟内出现，通常不超过1-2小时。 Features: Clinical manifestations occur within minutes (maximum one or two hours).

与遗传易感性相关，被称为特应性（atopy）。 Linked to a genetic predisposition called atopy.

发生I型反应需先有一次无症状的致敏暴露。 A first asymptomatic exposure to the allergen (sensitization) is required for the reaction.

此过程由IgE介导。 The reaction is IgE-mediated.

I型超敏反应的历史发现 History of Type I Hypersensitivity

查尔斯·里谢（1913年诺贝尔奖）通过研究海葵毒素描述了过敏反应（anaphylaxis）。 Charles Richet (Nobel 1913) described anaphylaxis while studying sea anemone venom.

主要反应见于第二次接触，表现为全身性、可致命的反应，且致敏可持续较长时间。 The reaction occurs on the second exposure, can be generalized/life-threatening, and long-lasting sensitization can develop.

历史进展
Historical milestones:

1902年，Portier与Richet首次描述过敏反应（anaphylaxis）。 Anaphylaxis: Portier and Richet (1902)

1929年，发现组胺（histamine）作用。 Role of histamine (1929)

1953年，确认肥大细胞（mast cells）功能。 Role of mast cells (1953)

1968年，明确IgE的作用。 Role of IgE (1968)

I型超敏反应机制 Mechanism of Type I Hypersensitivity

首次接触变应原（致敏）：激活树突状细胞　，呈递抗原至初始T细胞。 First contact with allergen (sensitization): Activated dendritic cells (DCs) present the antigen to naïve T cells.
答案 CD

在特定细胞因子（如IL-4）作用下，T细胞分化为Th2效应细胞，进一步激活B细胞分化为浆细胞，产生特异性IgE抗体。 Under cytokines like IL-4, T cells differentiate into Th2 effector cells, which help B cells become plasma cells producing specific IgE.

IgE抗体结合至肥大细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞表面FcεRI高亲和力受体，实现致敏。 IgE binds to the high-affinity FcεRI receptor on the surface of mast cells and basophils, leading to sensitization.

IgE及其受体 IgE and Their Receptors

IgE产生受遗传和环境因素影响，尤其与特定变应原暴露相关。 IgE production is enhanced by genetic and environmental (allergen-dependent) factors.

IgE具有独特的分子结构。 IgE has a unique immunoglobulin structure.

高亲和力受体（FcεRI）表达于肥大细胞和嗜碱性粒细胞表面。 The high-affinity receptor (FcεRI) is expressed on mast cells and basophils.

肥大细胞与嗜碱性粒细胞 Basophils and Mast Cells

嗜碱性粒细胞存在于血液中，含有大量组胺，表面高表达FcεRI。 Basophils are found in blood, contain abundant histamine, and express high levels of FcεRI.

肥大细胞主要分布于组织中，含有组胺与胰蛋白酶Tryptase，也表达FcεRI。 Mast cells are mainly in tissues, contain histamine and tryptase, and also express FcεRI.

I型过敏反应信号通路 Signaling Pathways in Type I Hypersensitivity

IgE与其受体结合并遇到抗原后，激活一系列信号通路，包括Lyn、Fyn等激酶，最终导致钙离子流入、NFAT、NF-κB等转录因子激活以及细胞脱颗粒反应。 After IgE binds to FcεRI and crosslinks with antigen, a cascade activates kinases like Lyn and Fyn, causes calcium influx, and activates transcription factors (NFAT, NF-κB) and degranulation.

结果包括细胞因子基因表达、颗粒物（如组胺）释放、脂质介质合成，最终引发典型的过敏反应症状。 This leads to gene expression of cytokines, release of granules (histamine), lipid mediator production, and the typical symptoms of allergic reactions.

I型超敏反应机制Type I Hypersensitivity Mechanism →

二次接触变应原（过敏原）可引发过敏反应。 Second contact with the allergen can trigger an allergic reaction.

I型超敏反应分为早期和晚期阶段。 Type I hypersensitivity includes early and late phases.

早期阶段
肥大细胞和/或嗜碱性粒细胞脱颗粒，释放组胺。 Early phase: Degranulation of mast cells and/or basophils, releasing histamine.

组胺作用主要包括瘙痒、黏液分泌增加、血管扩张、血管通透性增加和平滑肌收缩。 Effects of histamine: Itching, increased mucus secretion, vasodilation, increased vascular permeability, and smooth muscle contraction.

临床表现包括皮肤瘙痒、红肿、充血等。 Clinical features involve itching, swelling, redness, etc.

晚期阶段
新合成前列腺素（PG）、白三烯（LT）、血小板活化因子（PAF）；细胞因子产生及免疫细胞招募。 Late phase: Neo-synthesis of prostaglandins (PG), leukotrienes (LT), platelet-activating factor (PAF); cytokine production and immune cell recruitment.

关键细胞因子包括IL-3、IL-5（参与嗜酸性粒细胞）、IL-8、TNF和IL-4。 Key cytokines: IL-3, IL-5 (for eosinophils), IL-8, TNF, IL-4.

该过程可激活嗜酸性粒细胞、中性粒细胞、淋巴细胞等多种炎症细胞。 This process activates eosinophils, neutrophils, lymphocytes, and other inflammatory cells.

I型超敏反应的临床表现Clinical Manifestations of Type I Hypersensitivity →

皮肤表现
荨麻疹、血管性水肿、特应性皮炎。 Dermatologic: Urticaria, angioedema, atopic dermatitis.
呼吸系统表现
鼻炎、结膜炎、哮喘。 Respiratory: Rhinitis, conjunctivitis, asthma.
消化系统症状
呕吐、腹泻、腹痛。 Digestive: Vomiting, diarrhea, abdominal pain.
心血管表现
过敏性休克。 Cardiovascular: Anaphylaxis.
三大急症警示
咽喉水肿、急性重症哮喘和过敏性休克。 Three main emergencies: throat angioedema, acute severe asthma, anaphylaxis.

荨麻疹Urticaria (Superficial Cutaneous Oedema) →

可由多种原因触发，表现为红色丘疹、剧烈瘙痒和局部红斑，常见于皮肤浅层水肿。 Many causes; presents as red papules, intense itchiness, and flare (local redness), typical of superficial skin oedema.

血管性水肿Angioedema →

为皮下或粘膜深层的肿胀，可累及面部、四肢或其他部位，发生迅速、波及范围广。 Deep (hypodermic) swelling of skin and/or mucosa, often involves the face, limbs, or other locations, develops rapidly and can affect broad areas.

变应性鼻结膜炎和哮喘Rhinoconjunctivitis and Asthma →

鼻结膜炎表现为喷嚏、鼻塞、流涕、瘙痒及水样眼泪，可进展为持续症状或变应性哮喘。 Rhinoconjunctivitis features sneezing, nasal blockage, rhinorrhea, itchy/watery eyes, and may progress to persistent symptoms or allergic asthma.

哮喘为支气管收缩、炎症及粘液分泌，表现为喘息、咳嗽、呼吸困难和胸闷。 Asthma causes bronchial contraction, inflammation, and mucus secretion, with symptoms like wheeze, cough, dyspnea, and chest tightness.

哮喘可由间歇性过敏气道炎症演变为慢性持续性气道重塑。 Asthma may progress from intermittent allergic airway inflammation to chronic, persistent disease with airway remodeling.

过敏性休克Anaphylaxis →

I型超敏反应可系统性发作，表现为几分钟内致命。 Systemic manifestation of immediate hypersensitivity; can be lethal in a few minutes.

Ring及Messmer分级标准
Severity: Ring and Messmer classification.
Ⅰ级
黏膜/皮肤表现。 Grade I: Mucocutaneous manifestations.
Ⅱ级
轻度心血管症状（低血压、心动过速、气促）。 Grade II: Mild cardiovascular symptoms (low blood pressure, tachycardia, shortness of breath).
Ⅲ级
严重心血管和呼吸表现（虚脱、支气管痉挛）。 Grade III: Severe cardiovascular and respiratory effects (collapse, bronchospasm).
Ⅳ级
心脏骤停。 Grade IV: Cardiac arrest.

整合课堂讲解要点Supplement from Instructor’s Emphasis →

I型超敏反应起始于敏感个体二次接触致敏原后，IgE交联肥大细胞/嗜碱性粒细胞，快速释放炎症介质。 Type I hypersensitivity is triggered on second allergen exposure in sensitized individuals, with IgE crosslinking mast/basophil cells, rapidly releasing inflammatory mediators.

早期表现由组胺引发，晚期反应涉及新的脂质介质和细胞因子合成及多种炎性细胞募集。 Early symptoms are histamine-driven; late phase involves new lipid mediators/cytokines and broad inflammatory cell recruitment.

临床上可见皮肤（荨麻疹、血管性水肿）、呼吸（鼻炎、支气管哮喘）、消化道及循环系统急症（休克）。 Clinically manifests as dermal (urticaria, angioedema), respiratory (rhinitis, asthma), digestive, and cardiovascular emergencies (shock).

变应原触发因素与过敏诊断方法Triggers: the Allergens & Approaches of Allergy Diagnosis

一、变应原的类型 Types of Allergens

空气传播变应原包括花粉、尘螨、动物皮屑和霉菌。 Airborne allergens include pollens, dust mites, animal dander, and molds.

食物变应原常见有坚果、贝类、牛奶蛋白和蛋白。 Common food allergens are nuts, shellfish, milk proteins, and egg proteins.

药物变应原包括青霉素、肌松剂、阿司匹林和紫杉烷类。 Drug allergens involve penicillins, curare reagents, aspirin, and taxanes.

老师补充
这些变应原可通过接触、吸入或摄入方式引发I型超敏反应，部分药物和食物可形成新的表位，增强致敏性。 Instructor’s note: These allergens can induce type I hypersensitivity upon contact, inhalation, or ingestion, and some drugs/foods form new epitopes (neoantigens) that enhance sensitization 13.

二、年龄与过敏类型相关性 Age-Related Allergic Disease Features

食物过敏因年龄而异
新生儿多见牛奶过敏，婴儿以鸡蛋为主，儿童常见花生，成人则更易对贝类过敏。 Food allergy patterns vary by age: newborns—milk; infants—egg; children—peanut; adults—shellfish.

皮肤过敏（如湿疹、荨麻疹）多见于婴幼儿。 Cutaneous allergies (eczema, urticaria) are frequent in infants and young children.

呼吸道过敏如哮喘好发于儿童，过敏性鼻炎多见于青少年和青年。 Respiratory allergies—such as asthma primarily in children, and allergic rhinitis in teenagers and young adults.

药物与昆虫叮咬过敏则主要发生于成人。 Drug and insect bite allergies predominantly affect adults.

课堂强调
婴幼儿早期食物过敏多可自愈，但提示未来易发展为呼吸道过敏（变应性体质）。 Highlight: Early food allergies in infants often resolve spontaneously but signal a risk for future respiratory allergies (atopic background) 13.

三、过敏诊断流程 Diagnostic Approach to Allergies

问诊/面谈
评估个人和家族史、既往疾病、可疑致敏原及特应性体质。
Medical interview
Evaluate personal/family history, disease history, suspected allergens, and atopy.
立即型皮肤点刺试验
在皮肤表面小量引入变应原，15分钟内观察丘疹和红斑反应，阳性对照为组胺。禁忌包括严重皮肤病和近期使用抗组胺药。 2. Immediate skin prick test: Introduce small allergen quantity into epidermis, observe papule/redness within 15 min; histamine as positive control; contraindications include severe skin disease/recent antihistamine use.
血液检测
检测特异性IgE以确认致敏，包括嗜碱性粒细胞活化实验、组胺/胰蛋白酶等可溶性介质检测。 3. Blood testing: Specific IgE measurement confirms sensitization; includes basophil activation test and quantification of mediators such as histamine/tryptase.
体内激发试验
疑难或特殊病例，在医院严密监测下实施，被认为是过敏诊断的“金标准”。 4. In vivo provocation test: Gold standard for problematic/special cases, performed under hospital supervision.
课堂补充
皮肤点刺试验简便灵敏，是临床常用过敏筛查手段；部分特殊检测如嗜碱性粒细胞活化实验和组胺/胰蛋白酶测定更适合药物或严重全身过敏。 Supplement: Skin prick testing is convenient and sensitive for clinical allergy screening; specialized tests (basophil activation, mediator quantification) suit drug-related/systemic allergies 12.

四、生物诊断实验和特异性IgE检测 Biological Diagnostic Tests & Specific IgE Detection

生物诊断手段包括皮肤试验（点刺/皮内）、嗜碱性粒细胞活化实验（流式细胞术）及可溶性介质（组胺、胰蛋白酶）检测。 Biological approaches cover skin tests (prick/intradermal), basophil activation by flow cytometry, and soluble mediator (histamine, tryptase) assays.

特异性IgE检测方法有RIA、ImmunoCAP和ISAC生物芯片等多种现代手段。 Methods for specific IgE assays include RIA, ImmunoCAP, and ISAC biochip.

课堂强调
特异性IgE检测指数能客观反映致敏状态，配合临床表现和病史分析用于诊断和分型。 Emphasis: Specific IgE quantification objectively reflects sensitization and, together with history/clinical features, is key for diagnosis/subtyping 12.

五、皮肤点刺试验和皮内试验介绍 Introduction to Prick and Intradermal Skin Tests

点刺试验
微量变应原用针划入表皮，15分钟内出现丘疹和发红为阳性。对照需使用组胺（阳性）和缓冲液（阴性）。不适用严重皮肤病或近期用药者。 Prick test: A tiny quantity of allergen is pricked into the epidermis, papule and redness within 15 min are positive results. Controls include histamine (positive) and buffer (negative). Contraindicated in severe skin disease or recent antihistamine use.
皮内试验
将变应原注射入真皮层，适用于蛇毒、蜂毒或药物等迟发或特殊过敏筛查。 Intradermal test: Allergen is injected into the dermis and primarily used for venom, drug, or delayed allergies.

六、实验室IgE检测方法 Laboratory Detection of IgE

主流方法包括放射免疫分析（RIA）、免疫CAP和ISAC芯片，各具灵敏度和特异性优势。 Mainstream tests include radioimmunoassay (RIA), ImmunoCAP®, and ISAC allergen microarray, each offering sensitivity and specificity advantages.

体外过敏反应与治疗——嗜碱性粒细胞激活试验及抗过敏治疗 Anaphylaxis in vitro & Therapy—Basophil Activation Test and Anti-allergy Treatment

一、嗜碱性粒细胞激活试验原理与流程 Principle and Procedure of Basophil Activation Test

该试验用于体外检测过敏反应，特别适用于药物等变应原不可直接暴露者。 This test is used for in vitro allergy diagnosis, especially when direct exposure to allergens (like drugs) is unsafe.

试验方法是将患者外周血中嗜碱性粒细胞与可疑变应原孵育，若细胞被激活，将表达特异性激活标记。 The method involves incubating basophils from the patient’s blood with a suspected allergen; if activated, these cells will express specific activation markers.

嗜碱性粒细胞的鉴定依赖高表达的CCR3+标志。 Basophils are identified by high expression of the marker CCR3+.

激活后，细胞表面会高表达激活分子，如CD63和CD203c。 Upon activation, surface expression of markers like CD63 and CD203c significantly increases.

细胞经流式细胞术分析，根据光散射特性和激活分子表达判定是否激活。 The cells are analyzed via flow cytometry, using their scatter properties and activation marker expression to determine activation status.

二、流式细胞术数据解读 Interpretation of Flow Cytometry Results

第一步是通过抗CCR3-PE抗体圈定嗜碱性粒细胞群体。 The first step is basophil gating using anti-CCR3-PE antibody.

随后分别检测在对照组、抗IgE受体组、阳性变应原（如肌松药suxamethonium）孵育后的CD63和CD203c表达。 Next, detect CD63 and CD203c expression after incubation with negative control, anti-IgE receptor, or positive allergen (e.g. suxamethonium).

激活组可见CD63和CD203c表达显著升高，表明嗜碱性粒细胞被变应原激活，支持I型超敏反应。 Increased CD63 and CD203c in stimulated groups indicates basophil activation by allergen, supporting a type I hypersensitivity diagnosis.

三、抗过敏药物治疗 Anti-allergy Medications

对症治疗主要包括
抗组胺药（最常用）、糖皮质激素、抗白三烯类、B2-激动剂（用于哮喘）、肾上腺素（用于休克）。 Symptomatic treatment includes: antihistamines (most common), corticosteroids, anti-leukotrienes, beta-2 agonists (for asthma), and adrenaline (for anaphylactic shock).

四、抗过敏因果治疗——变应原免疫治疗（AIT） Causal Therapy: Allergen Immunotherapy (AIT)

AIT通过逐步增加变应原剂量诱导机体耐受，分为起始阶段（逐步递增剂量）、维持阶段（最大剂量维持3-5年）。 AIT gradually increases allergen exposure, inducing tolerance; includes initiation with dose escalation and maintenance phase (max dose, 3–5 years).

可采用皮下注射（SC）或舌下（SL）途径，药剂形式为滴剂或片剂。 Administered via subcutaneous injection (SC) or sublingual (SL) drops/tablets.

禁忌证包括
5岁以下儿童、妊娠、免疫功能障碍、失控的急性过敏、β受体阻断剂应用。 Contraindications: under 5 years, pregnancy, immune dysfunction, uncontrolled allergies, β-blocker therapy.

主要风险为诱发轻重不等的过敏反应。 The main risk is allergic reactions of varying severity.

疗效
黄蜂毒95%，蜂毒80%，花粉/尘螨约70%。 Efficacy: wasp venom 95%, honey bee 80%, pollens/dust mites about 70%.

五、AIT作用机制 Mechanisms of Allergen Immunotherapy (AIT)

高剂量变应原作用于树突状细胞，诱导调节性T细胞（Treg）增多并分泌IL-10、TGF-β，抑制Th2反应。 High-dose allergen acts on dendritic cells to induce regulatory T cells (Treg), which secrete IL-10 and TGF-β, suppressing Th2 response.

上调IL-10使B细胞IgE下降、IgG4及IgA升高，从而抑制过敏反应。 IL-10 upregulation by Tregs causes B cells to decrease IgE and increase IgG4, IgA—suppressing allergic responses.

抑制嗜碱性粒细胞、嗜酸性粒细胞和肥大细胞等效应细胞激活与炎症因子释放。 Activation and inflammatory mediator release from basophils, eosinophils, and mast cells are suppressed.

综合效应包括减少黏液分泌、降低气道高反应性、抑制平滑肌增生及减少上皮损伤等。 Overall effects: reduced mucus secretion, decreased bronchial hyperreactivity, inhibition of smooth muscle proliferation, and less epithelial injury.

过敏流行病学与遗传/环境因素Allergy Epidemiology & Genetic/Environmental Factors

一、过敏性疾病流行病学发展历程Epidemiological History of Allergic Diseases

时间轴展示了过敏相关疾病和主要发现的重要年份。从1873年首次描述花粉症（hay fever），1889年镍皮炎（nickel dermatitis），1902年首次正式提出“过敏”（anaphylaxis），到1914年报道哮喘，及后续发现Venom、NMBA、花生等相关性过敏反应。1966年IgE被确认，极大推动了机制研究。

The timeline shows key years in the recognition and discovery of allergy-related diseases: 1873—hay fever; 1889—nickel dermatitis; 1902—coining of “anaphylaxis”; 1914—asthma; venom anaphylaxis, NMBA anaphylaxis, peanut anaphylaxis later discovered. IgE was characterized in 1966, advancing mechanistic research.

插图显示，过敏性疾病的患病率自20世纪以来急剧上升。1929年估算患病率仅为1%，而2000年后部分地区已达30%-40%。

The graph indicates a dramatic rise in allergy prevalence since the 20th century: from 1% in 1929 to as high as 30-40% in recent decades 15.

二、过敏流行病学现状Modern Epidemiology of Allergy

欧洲和北美过敏性疾病的患病率为20%-30%。以法国为例，哮喘患者约达300万。

Prevalence in Europe and North America: 20-30%. France reports around 3 million asthma patients.

同胞间患病一致性（共患率）分析显示，非孪生/双卵双胞胎为40%，分开养育的同卵双胞胎50%，同养同卵双胞胎高达80%。

Concordance rates
40% for non-twins/dizygotic twins, 50% for monozygotic twins raised apart, 80% for monozygotic twins raised together.

说明除了遗传因素外，环境也有重要影响。

These numbers indicate both a strong genetic and strong environmental influence 15.

三、遗传易感性与易患基因Genetic Predisposition and Atopy-Related Genes

过敏为高度可遗传性疾病。若无父母过敏，风险约15%；一方过敏风险增至25%；父母均过敏风险达50%，若为同一疾病则可达80%。

Allergy is highly heritable: 15% risk if neither parent is allergic, 25% if one parent, 50-80% if both parents (especially if the same disease) .

核心相关基因包括：

HLA
如HLA-DR1（猫）、HLA-DR2/5（豚草过敏）

TCR相关基因（尘螨过敏）

FcεRI β链基因（11q13）

5号染色体相关细胞因子基因
如IL-3、IL-4、IL-13、IL-4R，以及β肾上腺素能受体基因。

Main genes involved:

HLA (e.g., HLA-DR1 for cat, HLA-DR2/5 for ragweed)

TCR genes (dust mites)

FcεRI β-chain on chromosome 11q13

Chromosome 5q cytokines IL-3, IL-4, IL-13, IL-4R, and β-adrenergic receptor genes.

四、环境因素作用与现代生活方式Environment Factors & Modern Lifestyle Influence

风险因素
孕期压力、环境污染、吸烟、城市生活、肥胖、过度卫生、电视等。
Risk factors
prebirth stress, pollutants, tobacco, urban life, obesity, excessive hygiene, television.
保护因素
多胞胎（手足多）、乡村生活、农产品摄入、家庭宠物、丰富微生物群、母乳喂养、早期适度暴露。
Protective factors
numerous siblings, countryside living, farm products, pets (especially dogs), rich microbiota, long breastfeeding, early allergen exposure.

课程强调，过敏性疾病的快速增长可能与工业化、城市化和现代生活方式紧密相关，被认为是一种“现代生活方式病”。

Instructor emphasized: the allergy epidemic is likely rooted in industrialization, urbanization, and aspects of modern lifestyle—a “modern lifestyle disease” 15.

五、课题小结与诊疗策略Conclusions and Clinical Notes

过敏是超敏反应的一种，可表现为即刻型或迟发型。即刻型涉及IgE及组胺释放。

Allergy is a form of hypersensitivity, presenting as immediate or delayed forms; immediate type involves IgE and histamine release 15.

诊断依靠问诊、皮肤试验、特异性IgE检测。

Diagnosis depends on medical history, skin tests, and specific IgE measurement.

过敏患病率迅速上升，主要归因于工业化和城市生活方式。

The prevalence of allergy is sharply increasing, largely due to industrialization and lifestyle changes.

治疗以对症抗组胺药和变应原特异性免疫疗法为主。

Treatment centers on antihistamines and allergen immunotherapy 15.

抗体相关诊断方法及免疫检测基础Antibody-based Diagnostic Tests and Immunoassay Fundamentals

一、抗体相关诊断方法类型Types of Antibody-based Diagnostic Tests

常见免疫检测技术包括
免疫固定电泳、免疫比浊、快速诊断、流式细胞术、免疫凝集、免疫荧光、免疫组织化学、ELISA、免疫印迹等。

Common immunodiagnostic techniques include: immunofixation electrophoresis, immunonephelometry, rapid diagnostic tests, flow cytometry, immunoagglutination, immunofluorescence, immunohistochemistry, ELISA, and immunoblot.

这些方法皆以抗体-抗原高特异性结合为基础，实现疾病相关标志物或病原的检测。

All these methods are based on the highly specific antibody-antigen interaction, allowing detection of disease markers or pathogens.

二、教学内容概览Lecture Outline

1
抗体、抗原与免疫复合物
1
Antibodies, antigens & immune complexes
2
无标记技术 2. Label-free methods
3
免疫标记方法 3. Immunolabeling methods
4
细胞流式术 4. Cell cytometry

三、血清免疫球蛋白种类与特性Serum Immunoglobulins (Igs) Types and Characteristics

免疫球蛋白表达于初始B细胞、记忆B细胞表面，由浆母细胞/浆细胞分泌进入血清（血清Igs）。

Immunoglobulins are present on naive and memory B cell surfaces, and are secreted into serum by plasmablasts/plasma cells.

五大同种型
IgM、IgD、IgG、IgA、IgE；其中IgG有4个亚型，IgA有2个亚型。
Five isotypes
IgM, IgD, IgG, IgA, IgE; IgG has 4 subtypes, IgA has 2.

成人血清内可存在超过1000万种不同特异性的Ig。

Adult serum contains over 10 million distinct Ig specificities.

四、多克隆与单克隆抗体免疫试剂Immunological Reagents: Polyclonal vs Monoclonal Antibodies

多克隆血清是多种抗体混合，识别同一抗原的不同表位，每种抗原-抗体对拥有不同亲和力。

Polyclonal serum is a mixture of many antibodies recognizing different epitopes of the same antigen, each with its own affinity.

多克隆生产过程包括
动物免疫、取血、IgGs分离、亲和层析提纯。

Polyclonal production: immunize animal, serum collection, fractionation and purification of IgGs via affinity chromatography.

单克隆抗体由单一浆细胞克隆产生，只识别一种表位且亲和力一致，制备中需融合浆母细胞和骨髓瘤形成杂交瘤后选取克隆。

Monoclonal antibody is produced by one plasma cell clone, binds one epitope with uniform affinity; production requires fusing plasmablasts with myeloma cells to form a hybridoma, which is then cloned/selected.

五、抗原识别与免疫复合物Antigen Recognition and Immune Complexes

抗体与抗原结合形成免疫复合物，这是多种免疫检测分析法的基础。

The binding of antibody (Ab) to antigen (Ag) forms the immune complex, which is fundamental to many immunoassays.

抗体结构包括Fab区结合抗原（识别“表位”），Fc区决定抗体功能。轻链有κ、λ两种，重链有m、d、g、a、e等类型。

Antibody structure
Fab region binds antigen (epitope), Fc region determines effector function. Light chains: kappa (κ), lambda (λ). Heavy chains: mu (m), delta (d), gamma (g), alpha (a), epsilon (e).
抗原定义
可被抗体识别的实体，其分子性质、大小、表位数量和结构差异很大。
Antigen
any entity recognized by an antibody; molecular nature, size, and number/structure of epitopes vary greatly.

六、免疫复合物构象与表达形式Presentations of Immune Complexes

免疫复合物可呈双分子（bimolecular）、多价抗原（multivalent）、格状（lattices）多种构象。

Immune complexes may be bimolecular, multivalent antigen, or lattice-like in formation.

这些复合物构象影响了免疫检测的技术策略与检测灵敏度。

The spatial forms influence immunoassay strategies and sensitivity.

七、分析目标与检测方法选择Analytical Objective and Test Selection

分析目标
需明确是检测抗原（Ag）还是抗体（Ab）。

Analytical objective: clearly define whether to detect antigen (Ag) or antibody (Ab).

质性检测（是/否）与定量检测（如抗原浓度、抗体滴度）的选择影响实验设计。

Qualitative (yes/no) vs. quantitative (e.g., Ag dose, Ab titer) testing determines assay design.

特异性（specificity）指检测能正确排除阴性样本，灵敏度（sensitivity）指检测能正确识别阳性样本。

Specificity
ability to give true negatives; Sensitivity: ability to give true positives.

八、课堂补充与老师强调Class Highlights and Instructor's Notes

实际实验室免疫检测既有无标记法（如比浊、免疫固定等），也有免疫标记法（如ELISA、流式、免疫荧光、免疫印迹等），需根据标本类型和目标选择合适技术。

In practice, both label-free (e.g., nephelometry, immunofixation) and labeled/immunotagged methods (e.g., ELISA, flow cytometry, immunofluorescence, immunoblot) are used; technique chosen depends on sample and analytical objective.

单克隆抗体灵敏度与特异性通常高于多克隆，适合精准诊断、一抗研发和治疗。

Monoclonal antibodies generally provide higher specificity/sensitivity, suitable for targeted diagnostics, research, and therapy.

无标记检测方法（Part II）Label-free Methods（Part II）

无标记检测方法概述Label-free Methods Overview

无标记检测方法是基于大分子的免疫复合物格架结构的形成。 Label-free methods are based on the formation of large immune-complex lattices.

这种方法的灵敏度较低，通常需要克/升（g/L）或毫克/升（mg/L）级别的抗原或抗体。 These methods have low sensitivity, usually requiring gram/liter (g/L) or milligram/liter (mg/L) concentrations of antigen or antibody.

免疫复合物形成 Immune-Complex Formation

抗体（Ab）和抗原（Ag）结合形成免疫复合物，能通过可见的沉淀或凝集来检测。 Antibody (Ab) and antigen (Ag) interaction leads to immune complex formation, which can be detected by visible precipitation or agglutination.

主要无标记方法与举例Major Label-free Methods and Examples

1
免疫沉淀（Immunoprecipitation）：抗原与抗体均为可溶性分子，通过形成不溶性复合物实现沉淀检测。
1
Immunoprecipitation: Both antigen and antibody are soluble, and the formation of an insoluble complex results in precipitation that is detected.

如Ouchterlony双向免疫扩散实验，中心为抗原井，周围为待测血清。若含有特异性抗体，会在抗原与抗体之间形成可见的弓形沉淀线。 Example: Ouchterlony double diffusion, with antigen in a central well and sera in surrounding wells. Positive serum shows visible precipitation arcs.

2
免疫凝集（Immunoagglutination）：抗原以颗粒形式呈现；抗体结合后导致颗粒聚集和肉眼可见的凝集反应。 2. Immunoagglutination: Antigen is presented as a particle. Antibody binding causes aggregation of particles, observable as agglutination.

常见于血型检测，如抗A、抗B、抗Duffy等抗体可直接检测红细胞表面抗原（血型卡演示）。 Used in blood typing (e.g., anti-A, anti-B, anti-Duffy antibodies, as shown in blood typing cards).

Coombs试验
检测红细胞相关自身抗体（auto-Abs），可用于自身免疫性溶血性贫血的诊断。 3. Coombs test: Detects autoantibodies against red blood cells, useful in diagnosing autoimmune hemolytic anemia.
4
活性炭颗粒包被心磷脂用于检测抗心磷脂抗体，可用于自身免疫性疾病的筛查。 4. Charcoal particles coated with cardiolipin can identify anti-cardiolipin autoantibodies for autoimmune disease screening.

无标记法灵敏度低的原因及适用范围Limitations and Applications of Label-free Methods

该类方法主要依赖大量抗原抗体的直接结合，大分子复合物才能被肉眼观察或仪器检测，因此对敏感度有限。 These methods require high concentrations of antigen and antibody to form visible complexes, so their sensitivity is limited.

适用于血清蛋白、血型、部分自身抗体的大规模筛查，但不适合低丰度靶标的检测。 Suitable for bulk serum protein analysis, blood grouping, and some autoantibody screenings; not ideal for low-abundance targets.

无标记血清免疫球蛋白检测常用方法Label-free Methods for Serum Immunoglobulin Assessment

1
免疫浊度法（Nephelometry）：用于定量检测血清免疫球蛋白。
Nephelometry
Used for quantification of serum immunoglobulins.
2
血清蛋白电泳（SPEP）：虽然不是抗体为基础的方法，但作为第一步评估血清免疫球蛋白图谱的重要手段。 2. Serum Protein Electrophoresis (SPEP): Not antibody-based, but essential for initial evaluation of serum immunoglobulin patterns.
3
免疫固定电泳（IFE）：用于鉴定和分型单克隆免疫球蛋白。 3. Immunofixation Electrophoresis (IFE): For identification and typing of monoclonal immunoglobulins.

课堂补充与老师强调 Instructor’s Supplement and Key Points

无标记检测方法原理简单、成本低，但依赖抗原抗体大规模聚集，无法检测极低浓度目标，通常作为初筛或特定用途（如血型、蛋白分型）。 Label-free diagnostics are simple and low-cost, but require mass immune-complex formation and are not sensitive to trace targets. Commonly used for first-line screening or specific tasks like blood typing and protein profiling.

传统方法如Ouchterlony双扩散实验、沉淀和凝集试验等至今仍有参考价值。 Classical techniques like Ouchterlony’s double diffusion, precipitation, and agglutination tests still hold reference value in immunology practice.

抗原特异性抗体检测及抗原检测的免疫标记方法 Immunolabeling methods for antigen-specific antibodies assessment and also for antigen detection

一、免疫标记技术类型与临床应用 Types of Immunolabeling Strategies and Clinical Applications

免疫标记检测策略主要应用于固相反应，广泛用于抗体或抗原的高灵敏检测。 Immunolabeling strategies in solid phase reactions are widely used for highly sensitive detection of antibodies or antigens.

常见方法包括酶联免疫分析（如ELISA及其类似方法）、免疫印迹（immunoblot）、免疫层析法和免疫荧光法。 Major approaches include enzyme-linked immunosorbent assays (ELISA and ELISA-like), immunoblotting (Western blot), immunochromatography, and immunofluorescence.

ELISA法和ELISA-like法适用于微量蛋白、病毒抗体、细胞因子等的定量/定性检测。 ELISA and ELISA-like tests support quantitative and qualitative detection of trace proteins, virus antibodies, cytokines, etc.

免疫印迹法常用于蛋白分型、疾病确诊，如HIV抗体分型等。 Immunoblot is frequently used for protein typing and definitive clinical diagnoses, such as HIV antibody typing.

免疫层析广泛应用于快速诊断试剂（如新冠、怀孕、HIV检测），可在数分钟内获得结果。 Immunochromatography is widely applied in rapid diagnostic kits (e.g., for COVID-19, pregnancy, HIV), yielding results within minutes.

免疫荧光技术用于组织免疫组化和血液淋巴细胞免疫分型/流式细胞术。 Immunofluorescence is used in tissue immunohistology and immunophenotyping of blood lymphocytes by flow cytometry.

二、ELISA原理与步骤 ELISA Principle and Workflow

ELISA（酶联免疫吸附测定）利用固相反应，以高灵敏度定量分析液体样本中的抗体或抗原。 ELISA (enzyme-linked immunosorbent assay) leverages solid phase reactions for highly sensitive quantitative analysis of antibodies/antigens in liquid samples.

灵敏度极高，可检测纳克每升或更低水平目标分子。 Excellent sensitivity allows detection at ng/L or even lower.

若配合标准曲线可实现定量分析。 Quantitative measurement is possible if a calibrated standard curve is used.

具体步骤 Steps for detection of specific antibodies：

1
在板孔表面包被抗原或抗体（取决于检测目标）。 Plastic wells are coated with antigen or antibody.
2
用大量非相关蛋白阻断非特异性结合位点。 Block non-specific sites with high concentrations of irrelevant proteins.
3
加入样本进行孵育，同时设置空白、阴性和标准对照。 Incubate samples and controls (blank, negative, standard).
4
用酶标试剂进行免疫标记孵育。 Immunolabel by incubating with an enzyme-labeled reagent.
5
加入底物（如显色底物），根据酶反应定量分析。 Add substrate (e.g., chromogenic), substrate conversion signals quantitation.
主要应用为
微量血清蛋白（如特异性IgG、IgM、IgE）、低丰度细胞因子/激素等的定量检测。 Main indications include quantification of serum proteins (specific IgG/IgM/IgE) and low-abundance cytokines/hormones.

ELISA包括间接法（检测标记二抗结合）、夹心法（捕获抗体+检测抗体双重识别，见右侧示意图）。 ELISA techniques include indirect detection (labeled secondary Ab) and sandwich format (capture Ab + detection Ab double recognition).

三、免疫印迹与免疫印刷 Immunoblot / Western Blot / Immunoprinting

免疫印迹（Western blot）首先通过凝胶电泳分离蛋白，随后将其转移至硝酸纤维素等膜上，再用患者血清孵育。 Immunoblot (Western blot) separates proteins by gel electrophoresis, transfers them to nitrocellulose/PVDF membranes, and then incubates with patient serum.

通过酶标二抗结合及底物反应，实现特异性条带的检测。 Detection is performed with enzyme-labeled secondary antibody and corresponding substrate.

免疫印刷（immunoprinting）为诊断提供商用现成膜，结合重组抗原直接赋形，无需电泳转膜过程。 Immunoprinting uses commercial membranes with directly spotted recombinant antigens, bypassing electrophoresis/transfer steps.

该平台常用于验证ELISA阳性结果（如HIV抗体确证）。 Used to confirm ELISA positives, e.g. for HIV antibody diagnostics.

四、免疫层析与快速诊断试剂 Immunochromatography & Rapid Diagnostic Tests (Lateral Flow)

原理为样本依靠毛细作用沿硝酸纤维素膜迁移，与标记纳米颗粒（如金属纳米金）结合生成可见信号。 Principle: sample migrates via capillary action along nitrocellulose, reacting with labeled nanoparticles (e.g. gold), producing visual signal.

几分钟内即可检测抗体或抗原，广泛应用于HIV、新冠、妊娠检测。 Detect antibody or antigen within minutes; widely used for HIV, COVID-19, pregnancy tests.

可检测液体标本（如血、尿、唾液等）。 Can be performed on liquid specimens including blood, urine, saliva.

五、免疫层析检测流程 Immunochromatography Workflow（以检测特定抗体为例）

1
样本（血清/体液）加入检测装置起始位，溶解含标签的抗原。
1
Patient sample (serum/fluid) is added at the test start site; labeled antigen dissolves.
2
毛细作用驱动样本沿膜迁移，抗体与标签抗原结合形成复合物。 2. Sample migrates via capillary flow; Ab binds labeled Ag to form immune complexes.
3
复合物被“Test”区固定的捕获抗体或抗原识别，形成检测信号。 3. Complexes are trapped at the Test region (T) by immobilized capture Ab/Ag, forming detection signal.
4.游离抗原/抗体继续向“Control”区迁移，被捕获，确保反应有效性。 4. Excess free Ag/Ab continues to Control region 　, where it is trapped to validate the test.
答案 C

六、课堂补充与强调 Instructor’s Notes and Highlights

老师强调
免疫标记法是现代实验与诊断不可或缺的高灵敏技术，能够用于大规模筛查（流行病学、疫苗、肿瘤标志物、细胞/组织分型）。 Instructor highlighted: Immunolabeling is an indispensable high-sensitivity tool in modern diagnostics and research, supporting large-scale screening (epidemiology, vaccines, tumor markers, cellular/tissue phenotyping).

ELISA及相关方法为疾病诊断与科研最重要的基础之一。 ELISA and related technologies are among the most fundamental tools in clinical diagnostics and scientific research.

免疫层析法因其操作简便和即时结果，已成为床旁检测、公共卫生等场合的首选。 Immunochromatography is favored for point-of-care and public health testing due to its simplicity and rapidity.

快速检测抗原特异性抗体的检测格式Rapid Test Formats for the detection of Ag-specific Abs

一、夹心法与间接法在快速检测中的应用 Application of Sandwich and Indirect Methods in Rapid Testing

夹心反应常用于检测所有类型的抗体（Ig各亚类），测试区使用包被抗原与被检抗体结合，再通过带标签的抗原或抗体形成“夹心”结构，实现信号放大与可视化。 The sandwich reaction is commonly used to detect all antibody classes (all Ig isotypes). In the test region, immobilized antigen captures the antibody, then a labeled antigen or antibody creates a "sandwich" complex, amplifying and visualizing the signal.

夹心法优点是可同时检测所有抗体亚型，常用于综合性抗体检测，如某些感染、疫苗应答等的快速筛查。 The advantage of the sandwich method is that it detects all antibody subclasses simultaneously, suitable for broad screening such as infections and vaccine responses.

间接法是通过抗原捕获患者血清中的特异性抗体（如IgG、IgM），然后用特异性二抗（如抗人IgG、抗人IgM）实现区分或定量。不同检测区可区分IgG和IgM，适用于判断近期或既往感染等。 The indirect method captures specific antibodies (like IgG, IgM) from the patient's serum via antigen, and uses type-specific secondary antibodies (anti-human IgG, anti-human IgM) for differentiation/quantification. Individual test regions separately identify IgG and IgM, useful for distinguishing recent from past infection.

底部图片分别示例夹心法和间接法试纸条结果显示，两条检测线、控制线表明样本中含有相应特异性抗体。 The images below illustrate the visual results of sandwich and indirect strip tests: two test lines and a control line indicate respective specific antibodies present in the sample.

多重免疫分析检测抗体或抗原 Multiplex Immunoassays for Ab or Ag Detection

多重免疫分析利用聚苯乙烯微球，每种微球带有不同荧光“指纹”，可同时识别样本中的多个分析物。 Multiplex immunoassays use polystyrene microbeads, each with a unique fluorescence "signature," enabling simultaneous identification of multiple analytes in a sample.

将不同涂有特异性抗原或抗体的微球混合，用于检测并定量几十种抗体或抗原，极大提升效率和信息量。 Mixed beads coated with specific antigens or antibodies allow detection and quantification of dozens of antibodies or antigens, vastly improving throughput and data yield.

适用实例
抗HLA抗体检测，评估器官移植前的免疫风险。 Example: Anti-HLA antibody detection for pre-transplant immune evaluation.

Ab检测时微球结合抗原，后续结合血清中被检抗体；Ag检测时微球固定抗体再捕获标本中抗原。 For antibody detection, beads are coated with antigen, then capture serum antibody; for antigen detection, beads are coated with antibody, then capture analyte.

单孔体积低至50微升，即可实现高通量多指标分析，非常适合科研及复杂诊断场景。 Only 50 μL per well is required for high-throughput, multiplexed analysis, ideal for research and complex clinical diagnostics.

免疫荧光法检测特异性抗体/抗原 Immunofluorescence Methods for Specific Ab/Ag Detection
  • A间接免疫荧光法 Indirect Immunofluorescence

间接免疫荧光广泛用于临床实验室检测与滴定自身抗体（如ANA、ANCA），特别适用于自身免疫病的诊断与监测。 Indirect immunofluorescence is widely used in clinical labs for detecting and titrating autoantibodies (ANA, ANCA), crucial for diagnosing/monitoring autoimmune diseases.

也可用于检测针对某些细菌或螺旋体（如莱姆病）等感染的特异性抗体。 This method also detects antibodies to certain bacteria or spirochetes (e.g., Lyme disease/Borrelia burgdorferi).

基本流程
抗原片段/病原体固定于载玻片，先与患者血清反应，再用带荧光的二抗标记，最后用荧光显微镜观察判断。 Workflow: Antigen/pathogen fixed on a slide, incubate with patient serum, followed by fluorochrome-conjugated secondary antibody, then visualize under a fluorescence microscope.

右图为核抗体样本以及螺旋体抗体检测的荧光显微结果。 Right panel: Fluorescent images showing nuclear antigen antibody and spirochete antibody detection.

B. 直接免疫荧光法与免疫组织化学 Direct Immunofluorescence & Immunohistology

直接免疫荧光在临床常用于检测病理组织中免疫复合物沉积，如IgG、补体于狼疮性肾炎、移植排斥、淀粉样变等。 Direct immunofluorescence is used clinically to detect immune complex deposits (e.g., IgG, complement) in pathological tissues like lupus nephritis, graft rejection, amyloidosis.

实验流程
组织切片上直接用带荧光的抗体（如抗IgG）孵育，洗涤后显微镜观察。 Procedure: Incubate tissue section with fluorochrome-conjugated Ab (e.g., anti-IgG), wash, and examine under microscopy.

典型结果如皮肤真表层的IgG条带（狼疮带试验）和肾脏肾小球的广泛IgG沉积。 Example results: IgG band at the dermal-epidermal junction in skin (lupus band test), and glomerular IgG deposits in kidney.

C. 免疫组织化学在临床诊断中的应用 Immunohistology in Clinical Diagnosis

免疫组织化学用于标记组织切片中的抗原，是肿瘤病理和器官移植等多种疾病精准诊断的重要技术。 Immunohistology labels antigens on tissue sections, serving as a vital diagnostic tool in tumor pathology, organ transplant monitoring, etc.

通过标志物（如MART-1、CD20、C4d等）鉴别肿瘤亚型、B细胞淋巴瘤、移植排斥情形。 Marker antibodies (MART-1, CD20, C4d, etc.) distinguish tumor subtypes, large B-cell lymphoma, humoral rejection in transplantation.

染色结果以棕色或荧光等信号展现病灶组织中抗原定位，辅以H&E染色加强形态判读。 Results are shown as brown/fluorescent signals for antigen localization, often combined with H&E for better morphology.

课堂补充与强调 Instructor Emphasis and Supplement

老师强调
现代免疫标记技术（夹心法、间接法、荧光及多重微球等）极大提升了临床诊断灵敏度和多样化，是自免/传染/肿瘤等疾病精准医学不可或缺工具。 Instructor emphasized: Modern immunolabeling technologies (sandwich/indirect methods, fluorescence, multiplex bead assays) greatly enhance clinical diagnostic sensitivity and diversity, being indispensable precise medical tools for autoimmunity, infectious disease, oncology, etc.

多重检测方法能在极小样本中实现多目标精准检测，适用于移植配型、复杂疾病分型和多因子监测。 Multiplex methods provide precise detection of multiple targets in small samples, ideal for transplantation typing, complicated disease characterization, and multi-factorial monitoring.

细胞流式术与荧光免疫分型Cell Cytometry and Immunophenotyping by Fluorescence

一、流式细胞术基本原理及医学意义 Basic Principle and Clinical Significance of Flow Cytometry

流式细胞术是评估造血细胞、识别血液与组织免疫细胞亚群的重要工具。 Flow cytometry is an essential tool for evaluating hematopoietic cells and identifying blood and tissue immune cell subsets.

该技术基于光散射和激发产生荧光信号（诊断常用直接免疫荧光标记法），核心为荧光染料结合到抗体。 It is based on light scatter and fluorescence (in diagnostics, direct immunofluorescence labeling), with core detection using fluorochrome-conjugated antibodies.

二、临床常规应用 Routine Clinical Applications

免疫表型分析
主要用于淋巴细胞亚群的分子表面标志物检测。 Lymphocyte immunophenotyping: focus on detecting cell surface markers of lymphocyte subsets.

HIV患者CD4+ T细胞鉴定与计数，是艾滋病诊断和疗效随访的重要指标。 Identification and counting of CD4 T-cells in HIV-infected patients is a key for AIDS diagnosis and monitoring.

淋巴增殖性疾病的诊断与随访。 Diagnosis and follow-up of lymphoproliferative disorders.

免疫缺陷及其他免疫介导疾病中免疫细胞的分型和功能分析。 Characterization of immune cells in immunodeficiency and other immune-mediated diseases.

三、实验流程及荧光标记原理 Experimental Steps and Fluorescence Labeling Principle

基础流程包括
采集血液样本，加抗体（通常带有不同荧光标签），进行标记，红细胞裂解，最后进行仪器检测与分析。 Basic workflow: collect blood sample, add labeled antibodies (with different fluorochromes), perform immunolabeling, lyse red blood cells, and analyze the sample with a flow cytometer.
常用荧光染料有
FITC（异硫氰酸荧光素）、PE（藻红蛋白）、PerCP（藻蓝蛋白）、APC（别藻蓝蛋白）；还常用Cy5、Cy7等串联染料和Alexa染料。 Commonly used fluorochromes: FITC (fluorescein isothiocyanate), PE (phycoerythrin), PerCP (peridinin chlorophyll protein), APC (allophycocyanin), with tandem dyes like Cy5, Cy7 and Alexa dyes also widely used.

抗体如Anti-CD3、Anti-CD4、Anti-CD8等特异识别白细胞亚群，由不同荧光标签区分。 Antibodies such as anti-CD3, anti-CD4 and anti-CD8 specifically recognize leukocyte subsets, differentiated by fluorochrome tag.

四、流式细胞仪光学原理与信号 Flow Cytometer Optics and Signals

样本以单细胞流经激光束，细胞与荧光标记物被激发，发射不同波长的荧光信号，由多个检测器收集。 Samples are focused into a cell stream, pass through laser beams. Cells and fluorochromes are excited, emitting fluorescence at various wavelengths, collected by multiple detectors.

信号分为三类
FSC（前向散射，反映细胞大小）、SSC（侧向散射，反映细胞颗粒度）、荧光信号（标记分子类型/表达量）。 There are three main signal types: FSC (forward scatter, cell size), SSC (side scatter, granularity), fluorescence (marker type and expression level, e.g. FITC, PE, APC).
例如
Anti-CD3-FITC发射543 nm，Anti-CD4-PE 578 nm，Anti-CD45-PerCP 680 nm，通过这些“颜色”综合分析细胞表型。 For example: anti-CD3-FITC emits at 543 nm, anti-CD4-PE at 578 nm, anti-CD45-PerCP at 680 nm; these “colors” are integrated for phenotyping.

五、数据分析与表达 Data Analysis and Representation

数据以点图（scatter plot）或直方图（histogram）形式表示。 Data are shown as dot plots or histograms.

点图
每个点代表一个细胞，X轴为FSC（大小），Y轴为SSC（颗粒度），不同群体显现为不同密集度色带（如淋巴细胞、单核细胞、粒细胞）。 Dot plot: each point represents a cell, X-axis is FSC (size), Y-axis is SSC (granularity); different cell types (lymphocytes, monocytes, granulocytes) cluster distinctly.

除了形态学分群，还可根据表型（如CD14 vs. CD45等标记）进行分区，得到功能/类型的多重细胞亚群。 Besides morphological gating, phenotype gating (e.g. CD14 vs. CD45 expression) allows classification into multiple functional subpopulations.

直方图常用于分析单一参数（如CD3表达强度），可划分不同表达区间。 Histograms are often used for single parameter analysis (e.g., CD3 expression intensity), with separate gates for dim/medium/bright positives.

六、典型数据解读与实际意义 Typical Data Interpretation and Practical Relevance

通过组合形态（FSC、SSC）和表型（荧光信号）可以精准区分、计数和功能注释不同免疫细胞亚群。 By combining morphology (FSC, SSC) and phenotype (fluorescent markers), different immune cell subpopulations can be precisely distinguished, counted, and functionally annotated.

这为临床免疫分型、疾病诊断与疗效评估提供了强大工具。 This provides a powerful tool for clinical immunophenotyping, disease diagnosis, and monitoring of therapeutic effects.

七、课堂补充与细节强调 Instructor’s Supplements and Key Details

老师强调，流式细胞术不仅可分析常规外周血细胞，还能应用于骨髓、淋巴结、肿瘤等复杂样本，具备高通量与多参数联合检测能力。 Instructor emphasized that flow cytometry applies not only to peripheral blood but also to bone marrow, lymph node, tumor, etc., offering high-throughput and multiparametric capabilities.

高敏感分辨率和多颜色检测使其在免疫缺陷、造血肿瘤、新型细胞疗法等领域成为不可替代的技术平台。 High-sensitivity, multi-color detection makes it indispensable in immunodeficiency, hematologic malignancies, and novel cell therapy fields.

流式细胞术数据表现与免疫分型分析Flow Cytometry Data Representation and Immunophenotyping Analysis

1
流式细胞术中的数据表现形式 Data Representation in Flow Cytometry

常用数据表现方式有点图（dot plot）和直方图（histogram），分别用于细胞群体的区分与单项参数分析。 Common representations include dot plots and histograms, which are used for distinguishing cell populations and analyzing single parameters, respectively.

点图中，每个点代表一个细胞，可根据形态或表型分区。 In a dot plot, each dot represents one cell, which can be gated based on morphology or phenotype.

按形态分群时，前向散射（FSC）代表细胞大小，侧向散射（SSC）代表细胞颗粒度。粒细胞、单核细胞和淋巴细胞会集中于不同区域。 For morphological gating, forward scatter (FSC) reflects cell size, and side scatter (SSC) reflects granularity. Granulocytes, monocytes, and lymphocytes cluster in distinct areas.

按表型分群时，常用不同抗体（如CD14、CD45等）区分单核细胞和白细胞，或用CD3识别T细胞。 For phenotypic gating, specific antibodies (e.g., CD14, CD45) are used to distinguish monocytes and leukocytes, or CD3 to identify T lymphocytes.

直方图可用于分析单一表型分子的表达，如示例中CD3表达可区分CD3+（T细胞）与CD3-（非T细胞）。 Histograms are useful for detecting the expression of a single phenotypic marker—e.g., CD3 expression distinguishes CD3+ (T cells) and CD3- (non-T cells).

2
淋巴细胞亚群免疫分型分析 Lymphocyte Immunophenotyping Analysis

通过连续点图门控，可逐步解析不同亚群——以CD19/CD3分区B细胞与T细胞，用CD4/CD8进一步区分T细胞亚群（CD4+辅助T细胞、CD8+细胞毒T细胞）。 By sequential dot-plot gating, B cells and T cells (with CD19/CD3) can be separated first, then T cell subpopulations further divided into CD4+ (helper) and CD8+ (cytotoxic) subsets.

NK细胞通过CD56和CD3阴性定位，并可利用CD56高低（bright/dim）再细分亚群。 NK cells are identified as CD3 negative and CD56 positive; CD56 bright and dim distinguish further NK subtypes.

各亚群占总淋巴细胞的比例可定量展示（如：B细胞11.3%，T细胞72.8%，CD4+ 31%，CD8+ 57%，NK细胞14.1%等）。 The proportions of each subset in total lymphocytes can be quantified (e.g., B cells 11.3%, T cells 72.8%, CD4+ 31%, CD8+ 57%, NK cells 14.1%).

3
淋巴细胞亚群的进一步分型与功能分析 Subpopulation and Functional Analysis

B细胞可通过IgD与CD27分为浆母细胞、记忆B细胞、边缘区样细胞和未成熟/初始B细胞。 B cells, gated by IgD and CD27, are classified into plasmablasts, memory, marginal zone (MZ)-like, and naive (immature) subsets.

CD8+T细胞通过CCR7与CD45RA可分为初始（naive）、效应记忆（effector memory, EM）和终末分化（TEMRA）等亚型。 CD8+ T cells, using CCR7 and CD45RA, are separated into naive, effector memory (EM), and terminally differentiated (TEMRA) subtypes.

4
免疫细胞功能性检测与激活实验 Immune Cell Functional Testing and Activation Assays

可对整体血样孵育激活分子后，通过细胞表面与功能性标志（如CD63、CD11b、CD107a）分析活化状态。 Whole blood is incubated with activation molecules, then immune cell populations and activation markers (CD63, CD11b, CD107a) are assessed to determine functional status.

嗜碱性粒细胞激活实验（BAT）：孵育特异性过敏原（如蜂毒）激活表面CD63上调，判断是否有特异性IgE介导的脱颗粒反应。 Basophil activation test (BAT): Incubation with specific allergen (e.g., bee venom) triggers CD63 upregulation on basophils, indicating allergen-specific IgE-mediated degranulation.

中性粒细胞活化测试
激活指标包括CD11b、CD66b3的上调及氧化爆发，常用内毒素（LPS）诱导。 Neutrophil activation test: Surface CD11b, CD66b3 upregulation and oxidative burst (after LPS stimulation) indicate activation.
NK细胞细胞毒性测试
通过检测刺激后细胞表面CD107a上调，反映其穿孔素和颗粒酶含量释放，一旦功能受损（如患者样本刺激后CD107a无明显上升）提示NK功能缺陷。 NK cytotoxicity assay: After stimulation, CD107a upregulation indicates degranulation (perforin/granzyme release); lack of CD107a increase implies NK cytotoxicity defect.
5
总结与关键知识点 Summary & Key Concepts

无标记检测主要依赖免疫复合物形成，适用于总Ig定量、浆细胞病分型，方法有比浊法、蛋白电泳、免疫固定、电凝集。 Label-free tests depend on immune complex formation; suitable for total Ig quantification, monoclonal gammopathy typing. Methods: nephelometry, SPEP, immunofixation, agglutination.

免疫标记法通过酶、荧光/金属标签获得高灵敏度，可检测特异性抗体/抗原，包括ELISA、免疫印迹、快速检测、免疫荧光及多重微球技术。 Immunolabeling uses enzyme/fluorochrome/gold labels for high sensitivity, detects specific Abs or Ags. Includes ELISA, western blot, rapid tests, immunofluorescence, multiplex assays.

流式细胞术利用荧光抗体识别和定量多种免疫细胞亚群，是免疫分型、免疫缺陷/肿瘤/病毒感染诊断和随访、功能测试的核心手段。 Flow cytometry uses fluorescent antibodies to identify/quantify immune cell subsets, essential for immunophenotyping, diagnosing/following immunodeficiencies, hematological malignancies, HIV monitoring, activation tests.

检测方法的选择依据分析物种类、灵敏度需求及临床情境和标准化程度。 Method choice depends on analyte type, required sensitivity, clinical context, and degree of standardization.

082025-12-09

免疫耐受与自身免疫病的发病机制→Immune tolerance and pathophysiology of autoimmune diseases

本章主要围绕机体如何区分自身与非自身、如何建立免疫耐受以及一旦耐受失衡会如何导致自身免疫病展开。

This chapter focuses on how the body distinguishes self from non‑self, how immune tolerance is established, and how loss of tolerance leads to autoimmune diseases.

总体结构上，本部分先做引言，然后依次讨论免疫耐受、耐受破坏与自身免疫、危险因素，并以系统性红斑狼疮和多发性硬化两个疾病为例。

In terms of structure, this part starts with an introduction, then covers immune tolerance, tolerance breakdown and autoimmunity, risk factors, and finally two example diseases: systemic lupus erythematosus and multiple sclerosis.

自我与非自我
基本概念→Self vs non‑self: basic concepts

Paul Ehrlich 在 1938 年提出“horror autotoxicus”的概念，强调正常情况下机体不会攻击自身，这是免疫系统必须遵守的基本原则。

In 1938 Paul Ehrlich proposed the concept of “horror autotoxicus”, emphasizing that under normal conditions the body must avoid attacking itself, which is a fundamental rule for the immune system.

正常免疫要求准确识别“自身”和“非自身”：能有效识别并清除外来抗原时，机体得到保护。

Normal immunity requires accurate discrimination between “self” and “non‑self”: when foreign antigens are correctly recognized and eliminated, the organism is protected.

一旦这种区分机制出现异常，原本应被容忍的自身抗原会被当作“非自身”攻击，从而导致自身抗原被破坏。

If this discrimination becomes abnormal, self‑antigens that should be tolerated are instead treated as “non‑self” and attacked, leading to destruction of self‑components.

“自身免疫”在工作定义上，是指个体免疫系统被激活并针对自身一个或多个成分，从而引起的一系列临床后果。

Working‑wise, “autoimmunity” is defined as the clinical consequences of activation of an individual’s immune system against one or more of its own constituents.

“免疫学自身”与“免疫学非自身”→Immunological self vs immunological non‑self

“免疫学自身”不仅包括机体固有的结构性自身抗原，还包括在生理条件下被视为无害并被免疫系统耐受的某些环境成分。

“Immunological self” includes not only the constitutive structural antigens of the organism but also certain environmental components that are harmless under physiological conditions and become tolerated by the immune system.

具体来说，自身部分包括机体细胞和组织中持续存在的自身蛋白等自身抗原。

More specifically, the self part comprises self‑antigens such as proteins that are constitutively expressed in the body’s cells and tissues.

环境成分部分则涵盖了通常无害的外源抗原，例如食物抗原、吸入性抗原以及与我们共生的肠道微生物群等。

The environmental component includes usually harmless external antigens such as food antigens, inhaled antigens, and the commensal microbiota living with us.

“免疫学非自身”指的是
在淋巴细胞成熟过程中没有接触到，或者在机体稳态状态下本不应遇到的细胞或分子。

“Immunological non‑self” refers to cells or molecules that adaptive immune cells are not exposed to during their maturation, or that they are not expected to encounter in the body at steady state.

适应性免疫系统被设计为优先对这些“免疫学非自身”成分产生反应，而对“免疫学自身”则通过各种耐受机制主动抑制攻击。

The adaptive immune system is designed to preferentially respond to such “immunological non‑self” components, while responses against “immunological self” are actively restrained by tolerance mechanisms.

获得性免疫耐受的发现→Discovery of acquired immunological tolerance

Burnet 和 Medawar 因发现“获得性免疫耐受”在 1960 年获得诺贝尔奖，他们证明免疫系统在发育过程中可以被“教育”，对某些抗原保持不反应。

Burnet and Medawar received the Nobel Prize in 1960 for their discovery of “acquired immunological tolerance”, showing that the immune system can be “educated” during development to remain unresponsive to certain antigens.

老师强调，免疫耐受是一个主动调控过程，而不是单纯的“什么都不做”或被动缺乏反应。

The lecturer emphasized that immune tolerance is an active regulatory process, rather than merely “doing nothing” or a passive lack of response.

适应性免疫细胞对自身的识别与耐受建立→Self‑recognition by adaptive immune cells and tolerance establishment

课上特别指出，适应性免疫系统的细胞，尤其是 T 淋巴细胞和 B 淋巴细胞，在本质上具有识别自身分子的能力。

The lecture highlighted that cells of the adaptive immune system, especially T lymphocytes and B lymphocytes, inherently have the capacity to recognize self molecules.

B 细胞和 T 细胞分别通过 BCR 和 TCR 与抗原结合，而这些受体的基因并不是事先为特定病原体“写好”的。

B cells and T cells recognize antigens through BCRs and TCRs respectively, and the genes encoding these receptors are not pre‑written for specific pathogens.

在骨髓和胸腺等原发淋巴器官中，BCR 和 TCR 的基因片段通过随机组合和连接产生多样化受体，这种随机重排意味着理论上可以识别几乎任何结构。

In primary lymphoid organs such as bone marrow and thymus, gene segments encoding BCRs and TCRs are randomly combined and joined, and this random rearrangement means that, in theory, receptors can recognize almost any structure.

这种机制带来的好处是极高的受体多样性，使机体能够应对未知或从未接触过的病原体。

The benefit of this mechanism is extremely high receptor diversity, enabling the organism to respond to unknown pathogens it has never previously encountered.

但代价在于，其中一部分随机生成的受体会正好识别并结合自身结构，从而形成潜在的自身反应性克隆。

The cost, however, is that some randomly generated receptors will happen to bind self structures, creating potentially self‑reactive clones.

老师在这里引出了耐受的必要性
既要保证受体库足够多样，又必须通过后续的筛选和调控机制，防止这些自身反应性细胞真正发动攻击。

At this point the lecturer introduced the necessity of tolerance: while maintaining a highly diverse receptor repertoire, the immune system must also use subsequent selection and regulatory mechanisms to prevent these self‑reactive cells from mounting real attacks.

因此，PPT 上“Recognition of self”和“Tolerance establishment: active process”这两点是紧密相连的：免疫细胞对自身的识别在生理上是真实存在的，而免疫耐受则是主动把这种识别“引导”成无害甚至有益的状态。

Thus the PPT items “Recognition of self” and “Tolerance establishment: active process” are tightly linked: physiological self‑recognition by immune cells is real, and immune tolerance actively channels this recognition into a harmless, sometimes even beneficial, state.

接下来课程将进一步展开中枢耐受和外周耐受的具体机制，说明机体是如何在不同发育阶段和解剖部位层层把关，避免自身免疫病发生。

Subsequent parts of the course will further detail the mechanisms of central and peripheral tolerance, explaining how the body implements checkpoints at different developmental stages and anatomical sites to prevent autoimmune disease.

免疫耐受与T细胞中枢耐受建立→Immune tolerance and central tolerance of T cells

本部分围绕“免疫耐受”展开，重点解释先天与适应性免疫的衔接、BCR/TCR如何在原发淋巴器官中随机重排产生高多样性及高频自身反应性，以及胸腺中T细胞发育、正负选择和中枢耐受的具体机制。

This part focuses on “immune tolerance”, explaining the link between innate and adaptive immunity, how BCR/TCR are randomly rearranged in primary lymphoid organs to generate high diversity and frequent self‑reactivity, and how T cells develop in the thymus, undergo positive/negative selection, and establish central tolerance.

先天免疫与适应性免疫的时间轴衔接→Temporal relationship between innate and adaptive immunity

老师先借图快速回顾
感染后最早启动的是先天免疫，包括上皮屏障、吞噬细胞（如中性粒细胞、巨噬细胞）、树突状细胞、补体和NK细胞，作用时间是感染后数小时内。

The lecturer first used the figure to review that immediately after infection innate immunity is activated, including epithelial barriers, phagocytes such as neutrophils and macrophages, dendritic cells, complement, and NK cells, acting within hours after infection.

随后才逐步启动适应性免疫
树突状细胞携带抗原激活T淋巴细胞，进而帮助B淋巴细胞产生抗体，以及产生效应T细胞；这一阶段通常在感染后几天才达到峰值。

Adaptive immunity is then progressively engaged: dendritic cells carrying antigens activate T lymphocytes, which in turn help B lymphocytes produce antibodies and generate effector T cells, and this phase usually peaks several days after infection.

老师强调，这里主要是提醒大家：后面讨论的BCR/TCR重排和胸腺教育，都是为了让适应性免疫在被激活时既足够多样、又不攻击自身。

The lecturer stressed that this slide mainly reminds us that the upcoming topics of BCR/TCR rearrangement and thymic education ensure that once adaptive immunity is activated it is both highly diverse and yet does not attack self.

BCR和TCR在原发淋巴器官中的随机重排→Random rearrangement of BCR and TCR in primary lymphoid organs

老师先澄清淋巴细胞的发育部位
所有淋巴细胞的发育都始于骨髓，B细胞的整个发育过程在骨髓内完成，而T细胞在骨髓起始、随后迁入胸腺完成分化。

The lecturer first clarified lymphocyte developmental sites: all lymphocytes start their development in the bone marrow, B cells complete their entire development there, whereas T cells start in the bone marrow and then migrate to the thymus to complete differentiation.

在胚系状态下，任何细胞一开始其实都没有可直接使用的TCR或BCR编码基因，也就是说，“完整受体基因”并不是预先写好的。

In the germline state no cell initially has ready‑to‑use genes for TCR or BCR, meaning that “complete receptor genes” are not pre‑written.

以TCR为例
其由α链和β链组成，β链基因由V、D、J和C四类基因片段构成，α链由V、J和C三类片段构成，这些片段在基因组中各有多拷贝。

Taking the TCR as an example: it is composed of an alpha and a beta chain, the beta chain gene consists of V, D, J, and C segments, and the alpha chain gene consists of V, J, and C segments, each represented by multiple copies in the genome.

在T细胞前体的分化过程中，这些V、D、J片段会通过重组酶介导的随机重排被“抽取”并连接：β链从多个V、D、J片段中各选一个组合，α链从多个V和J片段中各选一个组合。

During T‑cell progenitor differentiation these V, D, and J segments are randomly chosen and joined by recombination enzymes: the beta chain selects one V, one D, and one J from many copies, and the alpha chain selects one V and one J from many copies.

连接点处还会随机添加或删除核苷酸，产生“连接多样性”，进一步放大受体的潜在组合数；最终每个T细胞前体都会获得一套独一无二的α链和β链基因。

At the junctions nucleotides are randomly added or removed, generating “junctional diversity” that further amplifies possible receptor combinations, so that each T‑cell progenitor ultimately acquires a unique alpha and beta chain gene set.

上述重排过程先发生在β链，成功表达β链后再进行α链重排，最后两条随机生成的链配对，形成特异性TCR。

This rearrangement process first occurs in the beta chain, and only after successful beta chain expression does alpha chain rearrangement proceed, with the two randomly generated chains finally pairing to form a specific TCR.

BCR的原理相同，只是发生在骨髓中的B细胞发育阶段，通过重排重链和轻链基因片段获得多样性。

The same principle applies to the BCR, except that it occurs during B‑cell development in the bone marrow through rearrangement of heavy and light chain gene segments.

老师在录音中强调
这种“随机生成”最大的好处，是让受体理论上能够识别几乎任何结构，包括人类从未接触过、祖先也未遇到过的病原体抗原，而并非由固定基因一一对应特定病原体。

The lecturer emphasized in the recording that the great advantage of this “random generation” is that receptors can in principle recognize almost any structure, including pathogen antigens never encountered by humans or their ancestors, rather than being hard‑wired for specific pathogens.

但这种随机性也带来两个主要问题
一是有些受体可能结构不完整或不能正常表达，二是相当一部分受体会恰好识别自身结构，成为“自身反应性”BCR或TCR。

However this randomness also causes two major problems: first, some receptors may be structurally incomplete or not properly expressed, and second, a substantial fraction of receptors will by chance recognize self structures, becoming “autoreactive” BCRs or TCRs.

因此PPT特别在橙色框中标出“高频率的自身反应性BCR和TCR”，意思是：在未经过筛选的原始受体库中，自身反应性克隆出现的频率其实很高。

Hence the PPT highlights in an orange box that there is a “high frequency of autoreactive BCR and TCR”, meaning that in the unscreened primary receptor pool self‑reactive clones are actually very common.

这直接引出“免疫耐受”的必要性
既然随机重排不可避免地产生大量自身反应性淋巴细胞，就必须通过后续的中枢和外周耐受机制主动处理这些细胞。

This directly leads to the need for “immune tolerance”: since random rearrangement unavoidably generates many self‑reactive lymphocytes, subsequent central and peripheral tolerance mechanisms must actively handle these cells.

中枢耐受与外周耐受的区分→Distinction between central and peripheral tolerance

老师在讲到这里时，顺带给出定义：发生在胸腺和骨髓这样的原发淋巴器官、在淋巴细胞发育阶段就对自身反应性克隆进行筛除或改造的过程，称为“中枢耐受”。

At this point the lecturer defined that processes occurring in primary lymphoid organs such as thymus and bone marrow, where self‑reactive clones are eliminated or modified during lymphocyte development, are called “central tolerance”.

而当这些细胞已经离开原发淋巴器官、进入外周二级淋巴器官甚至效应位点后，通过无反应、调节性细胞等方式继续控制自身反应性的过程，则称为“外周耐受”。

Once cells have left primary lymphoid organs and entered peripheral secondary lymphoid organs or effector sites, further control of self‑reactivity through non‑responsiveness or regulatory cells is termed “peripheral tolerance”.

本部分PPT和录音集中讨论的是胸腺内T细胞的中枢耐受，外周耐受将在后面单独展开。

The current PPT slides and recordings focus on central tolerance of T cells in the thymus, while peripheral tolerance will be discussed later.

T细胞中枢耐受的两大机制
克隆删除与调节性T细胞诱导→Two main mechanisms of T‑cell central tolerance: clonal deletion and regulatory T‑cell induction

PPT在“Central tolerance of T Cells in the thymus”页面中用橙色框列出两种核心机制：克隆删除（recessive）和调节性T细胞诱导（dominant）。

On the “Central tolerance of T Cells in the thymus” slide the orange box lists two core mechanisms: clonal deletion (recessive) and regulatory T‑cell induction (dominant).

克隆删除是隐性（recessive）机制：当某个T细胞克隆的TCR对自身抗原亲和力过高时，该克隆在胸腺内经历凋亡，从库中被直接移除。

Clonal deletion is a recessive mechanism: when a T‑cell clone’s TCR has excessively high affinity for self antigens, that clone undergoes apoptosis in the thymus and is physically removed from the repertoire.

调节性T细胞诱导则是显性（dominant）机制：一部分对自身抗原有中等亲和力的T细胞并不被删除，而是被“改造”为调节性T细胞，在外周主动抑制其他潜在自身反应性细胞。

Regulatory T‑cell induction is a dominant mechanism: some T cells with intermediate affinity for self antigens are not deleted but are instead converted into regulatory T cells that actively suppress other potentially self‑reactive cells in the periphery.

老师强调，这两种机制共同工作：前者清除“危险度最高”的克隆，后者则通过主动调节在整体水平上维持耐受。

The lecturer emphasized that these mechanisms act together: the first removes the “most dangerous” clones, while the second maintains tolerance at the population level through active regulation.

胸腺结构与T细胞发育阶段→Thymic structure and stages of T‑cell development

胸腺由包膜包裹，通过小梁分隔成多个小叶，每个小叶又分为外侧的皮质区和内侧的髓质区；皮质中主要有皮质胸腺上皮细胞cTEC，髓质中有髓质胸腺上皮细胞mTEC及树突状细胞和其他抗原呈递细胞。

The thymus is enclosed by a capsule and subdivided by trabeculae into lobules, each with an outer cortex and inner medulla; the cortex mainly contains cortical thymic epithelial cells (cTEC), whereas the medulla contains medullary thymic epithelial cells (mTEC), dendritic cells, and other antigen‑presenting cells.

来自骨髓的早期T细胞前体通过血管进入胸腺皮质，初始阶段既不表达CD4也不表达CD8，被称为“双阴性（DN, CD4-CD8-）”细胞，可进一步细分为DN1、DN2、DN3等阶段。

Early T‑cell progenitors from the bone marrow enter the thymic cortex via blood vessels and initially express neither CD4 nor CD8, being called “double‑negative” (DN, CD4-CD8-) cells, further divided into DN1, DN2, DN3 stages.

在DN阶段，细胞开始进行TCR基因重排（先β链后α链），成功后逐渐上调CD4和CD8，转变为同时表达两者的“双阳性（DP, CD4+CD8+）”胸腺细胞。

During the DN stages cells begin TCR gene rearrangement (beta chain first, then alpha), and after successful rearrangement they up‑regulate both CD4 and CD8, becoming “double‑positive” (DP, CD4+CD8+) thymocytes.

随后在胸腺皮质和髓质中，这些DP细胞会经历一系列“教育”步骤，即正选择和负选择，最终只保留单阳性（SP, CD4+CD8-或CD4-CD8+）成熟T细胞离开胸腺。

Subsequently in the cortex and medulla these DP cells undergo an “education” process of positive and negative selection, and only single‑positive (SP, CD4+CD8- or CD4-CD8+) mature T cells are allowed to exit the thymus.

MHC与抗原呈递
正负选择的基础→MHC and antigen presentation: the basis of positive and negative selection
老师通过提问引导大家回顾
CD4+T细胞主要识别MHC II呈递的抗原肽，而CD8+T细胞识别MHC I呈递的抗原肽。

By questioning the class the lecturer reviewed that CD4+ T cells mainly recognize antigenic peptides presented by MHC class II, whereas CD8+ T cells recognize peptides presented by MHC class I.

MHC分子本质上是细胞表面的一类蛋白，经典存在于抗原呈递细胞上，用来“展示”来源于蛋白质的多肽片段。

MHC molecules are essentially cell‑surface proteins classically found on antigen‑presenting cells, used to “display” peptide fragments derived from proteins.

老师特别补充
MHC并不只呈递病原体的肽段，同样也可以呈递细胞自身蛋白降解产生的肽段，因此胸腺中T细胞在发育时实际上是通过识别“自身MHC+自身肽”来接受筛选。

The lecturer specifically added that MHC molecules do not only present pathogen‑derived peptides; they also present peptides from self proteins, so developing T cells in the thymus are in fact tested on “self MHC plus self peptide”.

TCR识别的真正对象是“MHC分子与其携带的多肽共同构成的复合物”，这也是后续正负选择判定“是否能识别、识别多强”的基础。

The true target of the TCR is the complex of “MHC molecule plus bound peptide”, which is the basis on which positive and negative selection evaluate whether and how strongly a thymocyte can recognize.

胸腺中的正选择
保留能识别自身MHC的细胞→Positive selection: preserving cells that recognize self MHC

在胸腺皮质区，cTEC在表面同时表达MHC I和MHC II，并加载来源于自身蛋白的多肽，用以测试DP胸腺细胞的TCR。

In the thymic cortex cTECs express both MHC class I and class II on their surface and load them with peptides derived from self proteins to test the TCRs of DP thymocytes.

如果某个DP细胞的TCR完全不能识别任一型MHC‑肽复合物，就无法获得生存信号，会死亡，这被称为“死亡忽略（death by neglect）”。
