If a DP cell’s TCR fails to recognize any MHC‑peptide complex at all, it does not receive survival signals and dies, a process called “death by neglect”.

如果TCR能以适度强度识别MHC I+肽，该细胞受到正选择并逐渐下调CD4、保留CD8，分化为单阳性CD8+细胞；若适度识别的是MHC II+肽，则保留CD4、下调CD8，分化为单阳性CD4+细胞。

If the TCR recognizes MHC I plus peptide with appropriate strength the cell undergoes positive selection and gradually down‑regulates CD4 while retaining CD8, becoming a CD8+ single‑positive cell; if it appropriately recognizes MHC II plus peptide it retains CD4, down‑regulates CD8, and becomes a CD4+ single‑positive cell.

正选择的核心目的是
筛掉完全“不识别自身MHC”的无用克隆，并确定每个T细胞未来是走CD4还是CD8谱系。

The core purpose of positive selection is to discard clones that cannot recognize self MHC at all and to assign each T cell to either the CD4 or CD8 lineage.

胸腺中的负选择与Treg诱导
删除高亲和自身反应性克隆→Negative selection and Treg induction: removing highly self‑reactive clones

完成正选择后，单阳性T细胞继续迁入胸腺髓质，在mTEC和树突状细胞等多种抗原呈递细胞面前再次测试其TCR。

After positive selection single‑positive T cells migrate to the thymic medulla, where their TCRs are tested again by mTECs and dendritic cells as antigen‑presenting cells.

如果TCR与自体MHC‑肽复合物的结合极为强烈，就会触发负选择：细胞发生凋亡，从而完成“克隆删除”，这是预防严重自身免疫的关键步骤。

If a TCR binds self MHC‑peptide complexes too strongly negative selection is triggered: the cell undergoes apoptosis, completing “clonal deletion”, which is crucial for preventing severe autoimmunity.

若识别信号处于中间强度，既不是完全无反应，也不是过强，部分T细胞会被诱导向调节性T细胞谱系分化，成为未来在外周维持耐受的“主动刹车”。

If the recognition signal is of intermediate strength—neither absent nor excessively strong—some T cells are induced to differentiate into regulatory T‑cell lineages, becoming an “active brake” that maintains tolerance in the periphery.

因此，胸腺中的正负选择共同实现T细胞中枢耐受：正选择确保所有输出T细胞都能识别自身MHC，负选择和Treg诱导则限制其对自身抗原的有害反应。

Thus positive and negative selection together establish T‑cell central tolerance in the thymus: positive selection ensures all exported T cells recognize self MHC, while negative selection and Treg induction limit harmful responses to self antigens.

本段小结→Mini‑summary of this section

这一系列PPT和讲解说明
由于BCR/TCR在原发淋巴器官中通过随机重排产生极高多样性，导致自身反应性克隆高频存在，因此必须在胸腺中通过结构化的发育阶段、MHC‑依赖的正负选择、克隆删除和Treg诱导等机制，主动建立T细胞的中枢免疫耐受，为后续外周耐受打下基础。

These slides and explanations show that because BCRs and TCRs are generated with extremely high diversity through random rearrangement in primary lymphoid organs, self‑reactive clones are frequent, so the thymus must, via structured developmental stages, MHC‑dependent positive and negative selection, clonal deletion, and Treg induction, actively establish central immune tolerance of T cells, thereby laying the foundation for subsequent peripheral tolerance.

组织特异性抗原耐受与Aire/Fezf2及Treg在中枢耐受中的作用ITolerance to tissue‑specific antigens and the roles of Aire/Fezf2 and Tregs in central tolerance

这一组PPT主要说明
机体如何对“组织特异性抗原”（TSAs）建立中枢耐受，历史认识与现实差异，胸腺髓质上皮细胞mTEC的异位表达作用，以及Aire和Fezf2两个关键转录调控因子和胸腺Treg在预防多种自身免疫病中的核心地位。

This set of slides explains how the body establishes central tolerance to tissue‑specific antigens (TSAs), contrasting historical and current views, highlighting the ectopic expression function of medullary thymic epithelial cells (mTECs), and detailing how the transcriptional regulators Aire and Fezf2 and thymic Tregs prevent many autoimmune diseases.

一、组织特异性抗原（TSAs）耐受：从“外周”到“胸腺”IFrom peripheral tolerance to thymic tolerance for TSAs

最初的“历史假说”认为
很多只在特定组织表达的抗原（如胰岛、甲状腺、皮肤、眼、睾丸等的组织特异性抗原），在胸腺中并不存在，因此对它们的耐受只能在外周建立。

The initial historical hypothesis held that many antigens expressed only in specific tissues, such as in pancreas, thyroid, skin, eye, or testis, are absent from the thymus, so tolerance to them must be established solely in the periphery.

老师在录音里强调，以前大家认为胸腺里T细胞在发育、选择时，只能看到“普遍表达”的自体蛋白（例如肌动蛋白、微管蛋白、一些常见代谢酶），而对真正“组织特异性”的抗原是完全“看不到”的。

The lecturer emphasized in the recording that it was long thought that developing T cells in the thymus only see “ubiquitous” self proteins, such as actin, tubulin, and common metabolic enzymes, and are completely blind to truly tissue‑specific antigens.

然而现实情况是
胸腺髓质胸腺上皮细胞（mTECs）会“异位表达”大量来源于各种外周组织的TSAs，把它们在胸腺中临时“展现出来”，供发育中的T细胞进行负选择或转分化为Treg。

In reality, medullary thymic epithelial cells (mTECs) “ectopically express” many TSAs normally found in peripheral organs, temporarily displaying them in the thymus so that developing T cells can undergo negative selection or be converted into Tregs.

PPT中的饼图展示
在mTECs中可以检测到来自大脑、肠道、肝脏、甲状腺、皮肤表皮、乳腺、肾脏、胃、心脏、肺、睾丸等多种组织的转录本，说明这些本应只在对应器官表达的基因，在mTEC里也被低水平地打开。

The pie chart in the slide illustrates that transcripts originating from brain, intestine, liver, thyroid, epidermis, mammary gland, kidney, stomach, heart, lung, testis, and many other tissues are detectable in mTECs, indicating that genes normally restricted to these organs are also weakly expressed in mTECs.

这种“异位表达”意味着
胸腺实际上在投射出一个“免疫学自我的影子”，让正在受教育的T细胞在发育期就见到多种外周组织的抗原，从而在中枢阶段就对这些TSAs建立耐受。

This ectopic expression means that the thymus actually projects an “immunological self shadow,” enabling developing T cells to encounter many peripheral tissue antigens early on and thereby to establish tolerance to TSAs at the central level.

二、Aire
在胸腺中投射“自我阴影”的自身免疫调节因子IAire: autoimmune regulator projecting a self shadow in the thymus

接下来的“Tolerance to TSAs”文献截图部分，老师讲的是Aire蛋白的发现及其功能：Aire是一种在胸腺mTEC中表达的转录调控因子，全称为autoimmune regulator。

In the “Tolerance to TSAs” paper screenshot, the lecturer discussed the discovery and function of Aire, a transcriptional regulator expressed in thymic mTECs, whose full name is autoimmune regulator.

研究者对mTEC进行转录组分析，发现其中存在大量通常只在脑、肠道、胃、卵巢、视网膜等特定器官中表达的mRNA，从而提出：是某种因子驱动了mTEC里这些TSAs的“异位表达”。

Researchers performed transcriptomic analyses of mTECs and found abundant mRNAs normally restricted to brain, intestine, stomach, ovary, retina, and other specific organs, suggesting that some factor drives ectopic TSA expression in mTECs.

后续工作表明，这个关键因子就是Aire：Aire阳性的mTEC中，大量组织特异性基因被激活并翻译成抗原肽，经MHC呈递给发育中的T细胞。

Subsequent work showed that this key factor is Aire: in Aire‑positive mTECs, numerous tissue‑specific genes are activated and translated into antigenic peptides that are presented via MHC to developing T cells.

PPT中的组织切片一列显示
在Aire敲除小鼠（KO）中，唾液腺、卵巢、视网膜等多个器官出现密集的淋巴细胞浸润和严重的组织破坏，而野生型（WT）小鼠组织结构完整，提示Aire缺失会导致系统性自身免疫。

The tissue sections in the slide show that Aire knockout (KO) mice exhibit dense lymphocytic infiltrates and severe tissue destruction in salivary gland, ovary, retina, and other organs, whereas wild‑type (WT) mice maintain intact tissue architecture, indicating that loss of Aire causes systemic autoimmunity.

在人类中，Aire基因突变导致APECED或APS‑1综合征（autoimmune polyendocrinopathy candidiasis ectodermal dystrophy / autoimmune polyendocrine syndrome）：患者出现多腺体内分泌器官同时被免疫攻击、反复黏膜念珠菌感染以及外胚层组织损害等。

In humans, mutations in the Aire gene cause APECED or APS‑1 (autoimmune polyendocrinopathy candidiasis ectodermal dystrophy / autoimmune polyendocrine syndrome), in which patients develop autoimmune attacks against multiple endocrine organs, recurrent mucocutaneous candidiasis, and ectodermal tissue damage.

因此，Aire的生理作用可以概括为：在mTEC中激活大量TSAs的表达，提供胸腺内的“自我抗原库”，从而通过负选择清除高度自身反应性的T细胞，并促进对这些TSAs特异性Treg的生成，以预防机体对外周组织的自身免疫攻击。

Therefore the physiological role of Aire can be summarized as activating the expression of a wide range of TSAs in mTECs, thereby creating an intrathymic self‑antigen library that enables negative selection of highly self‑reactive T cells and promotes generation of TSA‑specific Tregs, preventing autoimmune attacks on peripheral tissues.

三、Aire的分子机制
从染色质调控到耐受诱导IMolecular mechanism of Aire: from chromatin regulation to tolerance induction
“AIRE
from transcriptional regulation to tolerance induction” 这一页图示，放大了一个mTEC细胞核：Aire定位在染色质附近，通过调控染色质开放，驱动多个组织特异性基因的转录。
The “AIRE
from transcriptional regulation to tolerance induction” slide schematically enlarges the nucleus of an mTEC, with Aire positioned at chromatin regions, where it regulates chromatin accessibility and drives transcription of multiple tissue‑specific genes.

老师解释，Aire本身更像一个“转录平台”或“协同因子”：它并不是简单地直接结合所有TSAs基因的启动子，而是招募一系列参与表观遗传调控的蛋白，去打开平时处于“关闭状态”的染色质区域。

The lecturer explained that Aire acts more as a “transcriptional platform” or co‑factor: rather than directly binding all TSA gene promoters, it recruits various epigenetic regulators to open chromatin regions that are usually in a repressed state.

在Aire存在时，这些被打开的TSAs基因被转录、翻译，其蛋白被加工为肽段加载到MHC分子上，由mTEC或相关树突状细胞呈递给发育中的胸腺细胞。

When Aire is present, these opened TSA genes are transcribed and translated, their proteins are processed into peptides loaded onto MHC molecules, and these complexes are presented by mTECs or associated dendritic cells to developing thymocytes.

图下方的示意告诉我们，识别这些TSAs太强的胸腺细胞会经历克隆删除；而识别强度处于中等范围的部分细胞，则会向Treg谱系偏转（clonal deviation），最后输出到外周，专门负责抑制对相同TSAs的自身免疫反应。

The lower scheme indicates that thymocytes whose TCRs bind these TSAs too strongly undergo clonal deletion, whereas those with intermediate affinity deviate into the Treg lineage (clonal deviation), eventually exiting to the periphery to suppress autoimmune responses against the same TSAs.

整体上，Aire将“转录调控”与“耐受诱导”直接连接起来：没有Aire，就缺少丰富的TSAs表达，负选择和Treg诱导的靶标变少，从而大幅提高自身免疫病（如1型糖尿病、甲状旁腺病变、角膜和视网膜炎症等）的风险。

Overall Aire directly links transcriptional regulation to tolerance induction: without Aire, the repertoire of TSAs is impoverished, leaving fewer targets for negative selection and Treg induction, and markedly increasing the risk of autoimmune diseases such as type 1 diabetes, parathyroid disease, and ocular inflammation of the cornea and retina.

四、Fezf2
与Aire平行的第二套“TSAs开关”IFezf2: a second TSA switch acting in parallel to Aire

Cell期“Fezf2 orchestrates a thymic program of self‑antigen expression for immune tolerance”这页图告诉我们：除了Aire之外，还有另一条独立但互补的通路，由Fezf2蛋白主导。

The Cell article “Fezf2 orchestrates a thymic program of self‑antigen expression for immune tolerance” shows that, in addition to Aire, there is another independent but complementary pathway driven by the Fezf2 protein.

Fezf2是一种转录因子，同样在mTEC中表达，它可以直接结合部分组织限制性抗原基因的调控区域，直接促进这些TSAs的转录（图中标作Fezf2‑dependent TRA）。

Fezf2 is a transcription factor also expressed in mTECs that directly binds regulatory regions of certain tissue‑restricted antigen genes and directly promotes their transcription (labeled as Fezf2‑dependent TRA in the figure).

与之相对，Aire则更多通过“表观遗传方式”间接打开染色质（图中Aire‑dependent TRA），两者分别控制一部分不同的TSAs集合，但最终都通过MHC呈递参与负选择。

In contrast Aire more often acts indirectly through epigenetic mechanisms to open chromatin (Aire‑dependent TRA in the figure); the two factors control partially distinct TSA sets but both end up contributing to negative selection through MHC presentation.

老师在录音中补充
如果缺失Aire，会出现APS‑1样多器官自身免疫；而Fezf2缺陷的小鼠则倾向于针对另一组抗原发生自身免疫（如关节、脑或其他器官），说明这两条通路在“覆盖面”和靶器官上具有互补性。

The lecturer added that Aire deficiency leads to APS‑1‑like multi‑organ autoimmunity, whereas Fezf2‑deficient mice tend to develop autoimmunity against a different set of antigens, such as those in joints or brain, showing that these pathways are complementary in coverage and target organs.

后面“Complementary actions of Aire and Fezf2 in central tolerance induction”这张图进一步用疾病示例说明：Aire相关的TSAs包括眼抗原、胰岛素等，关联自身免疫性葡萄膜炎和1型糖尿病等；Fezf2相关TSAs包括与干燥综合征、类风湿关节炎、重症肌无力、帕金森病等相关的抗原。

The subsequent “Complementary actions of Aire and Fezf2 in central tolerance induction” slide further illustrates this with disease examples: Aire‑related TSAs include ocular antigens and insulin associated with autoimmune uveitis and type 1 diabetes, whereas Fezf2‑related TSAs include antigens implicated in Sjögren’s syndrome, rheumatoid arthritis, myasthenia gravis, and Parkinson’s disease.

因此，中枢耐受中对TSAs的“全景覆盖”依赖于Aire和Fezf2两套程序共同工作：它们扩大了胸腺中可被呈递的TSAs谱系，从而最大限度地删除或调控潜在针对这些组织的自身反应性克隆。

Thus panoramic coverage of TSAs in central tolerance depends on the combined action of Aire and Fezf2, which together broaden the repertoire of TSAs presented in the thymus and thereby maximize deletion or regulation of potentially self‑reactive clones against these tissues.

五、胸腺中Treg的诱导
TSAs特异性“主动刹车”IThymic Treg induction: TSA‑specific active brakes

“Treg induction in the thymus”这一页总结了：在胸腺髓质中，TCR与CD28等共刺激信号的强度梯度，决定了胸腺细胞的命运——常规T细胞、Treg，还是被负选择删除。

The “Treg induction in the thymus” slide summarizes how, in the thymic medulla, the gradient of TCR plus CD28 co‑stimulatory signal strength determines thymocyte fate as conventional T cells, Tregs, or cells destined for deletion.

如果TCR对自体MHC‑肽复合物几乎无感或太弱，细胞不会被很好保留；如果识别极强，则触发负选择和凋亡，属于我们前面说的克隆删除。

If a TCR is almost unresponsive or too weakly reactive to self MHC‑peptide complexes, the cell is not efficiently retained; if the signal is extremely strong, negative selection and apoptosis are triggered, corresponding to clonal deletion discussed earlier.

处于“中等强度”的那一批胸腺细胞，在适当的细胞因子环境（尤其是IL‑2和TGF‑β）以及共刺激背景下，会启动FoxP3的表达，分化为自然调节性T细胞（胸腺型Treg）。

Those thymocytes receiving intermediate‑strength signals, in the appropriate cytokine milieu (particularly IL‑2 and TGF‑β) and co‑stimulatory context, up‑regulate FoxP3 and differentiate into natural regulatory T cells (thymic Tregs).

老师在录音里提到，典型的Treg表型是CD4阳性、FOXP3阳性并高表达IL‑2受体α链CD25，它们依赖IL‑2存活，但自身并不大量产生IL‑2，而是“消耗”周围IL‑2来限制效应T细胞扩张。

The lecturer mentioned that Tregs typically display CD4 positivity, FOXP3 expression, and high levels of the IL‑2 receptor alpha chain CD25; they depend on IL‑2 for survival but do not produce much IL‑2 themselves, instead “consuming” local IL‑2 to limit effector T‑cell expansion.

这些胸腺来源的Treg一旦进入外周，每次遇到自己特异的TSAs时会被激活，释放抑制性细胞因子（如IL‑10、TGF‑β等）、表达CTLA‑4等抑制性分子，或者通过直接细胞毒、代谢竞争等方式，抑制周围的自身反应性效应T细胞和其他免疫细胞。

Once exported to the periphery, these thymus‑derived Tregs are reactivated whenever they encounter their cognate TSAs and then release inhibitory cytokines such as IL‑10 and TGF‑β, express inhibitory molecules like CTLA‑4, or use direct cytotoxicity and metabolic competition to suppress surrounding self‑reactive effector T cells and other immune cells.

老师还特别指出
缺陷或数量不足的Treg会显著增加多发性硬化、结肠炎、系统性自身炎症等疾病的风险，因此“克隆删除+Treg诱导”被视为中枢耐受中两种互补且同等重要的策略。

The lecturer further pointed out that defective or insufficient Tregs markedly increase the risk of diseases such as multiple sclerosis, colitis, and systemic autoinflammatory conditions, so “clonal deletion plus Treg induction” are regarded as two complementary and equally crucial strategies in central tolerance.

六、小结
TSAs、Aire、Fezf2与Treg共同构筑的中枢耐受网络ISummary: a central tolerance network built by TSAs, Aire, Fezf2, and Tregs

这一组PPT与录音共同传达的关键信息是：胸腺并非只处理“普遍自体抗原”，而是借助mTEC对多种组织特异性抗原的异位表达，在中枢阶段就为大部分外周组织建立起耐受基础。

Together the slides and recording convey that the thymus does not deal only with ubiquitous self antigens; by using mTEC‑mediated ectopic expression of many TSAs, it lays the groundwork for tolerance to most peripheral tissues at the central level.

Aire和Fezf2是驱动这一过程的两大核心转录调控因子，它们各自通过不同机制（表观遗传调控或直接转录激活）打开不同谱系的TSAs基因，使这些抗原在mTEC中得到呈递。

Aire and Fezf2 are the two major transcriptional regulators driving this process, each using different mechanisms (epigenetic regulation or direct transcriptional activation) to open distinct sets of TSA genes for presentation in mTECs.

在这些TSAs的参与下，发育中的胸腺细胞一方面通过强反应克隆的删除被清除，另一方面通过中等亲和力克隆向Treg谱系偏转，产生对相应组织具有专一抑制功能的调节性T细胞。

With these TSAs present, developing thymocytes are both cleared by deletion of strongly self‑reactive clones and diverted into the Treg lineage from intermediate‑affinity clones, generating regulatory T cells that specifically protect the corresponding tissues.

若Aire或Fezf2缺陷，或Treg诱导与功能出现问题，就会出现以多内分泌腺、关节、神经系统、眼等为靶器官的多种自身免疫病，说明这一中枢耐受网络是维持机体“免疫学自身”完整性的关键屏障。

If Aire or Fezf2 is defective or Treg induction and function are impaired, diverse autoimmune diseases arise targeting endocrine glands, joints, the nervous system, and the eye, underscoring that this central tolerance network is a key barrier for preserving the integrity of the immunological self.

淋巴细胞中枢与外周耐受机制ILymphocyte central and peripheral tolerance mechanisms

一、B细胞中枢耐受
在骨髓内完成的“第一道筛选”ICentral tolerance of B cells: the first selection step in the bone marrow

B细胞中枢耐受发生在骨髓中，从前B细胞到未成熟B细胞这一发育窗口内，是防止强烈自身反应性BCR输出的关键。

Central tolerance of B cells occurs in the bone marrow during the developmental window from pre‑B cells to immature B cells and is crucial to prevent the export of strongly autoreactive BCRs.

老师强调，BCR直接识别天然抗原（蛋白、多糖、DNA、磷脂等），不需要像TCR那样依赖MHC‑肽复合物，因此在骨髓微环境中只要周围有自体结构，候选BCR就有机会被“立刻测试”。

The lecturer stressed that BCRs directly recognize native antigens such as proteins, polysaccharides, DNA, and phospholipids without needing MHC–peptide complexes like TCRs, so in the bone‑marrow environment any surrounding self structures can immediately test candidate BCRs.

1
受体编辑（receptor editing）：优先尝试“改造”而非立刻删除IReceptor editing: trying to remodel before deleting

如果在骨髓中，未成熟B细胞的BCR高亲和力识别到自身抗原，会首先启动“受体编辑”：主要通过再次重排轻链基因，替换原来自身反应性的轻链。

If, in the bone marrow, an immature B cell’s BCR recognizes a self antigen with high affinity, “receptor editing” is triggered first, mainly by rearranging the light‑chain gene again to replace the original autoreactive light chain.

重链通常在发育早期已经成功验证，因此此时主要“推倒重来”的是轻链；通过再次V‑J重排，有机会生成一个不再识别自身的BCR，从而保留这个B细胞。

The heavy chain is usually validated earlier in development, so what is effectively “rebuilt” at this stage is the light chain; through new V–J rearrangements the cell may generate a BCR that no longer recognizes self and can therefore be rescued.

受体编辑的意义在于
在不牺牲整个克隆的前提下，最大化利用已经投入成本的B细胞前体，同时降低自身反应性克隆的数量。

The significance of receptor editing is that it salvages B‑cell precursors in which developmental resources have already been invested while reducing the number of autoreactive clones.

2
克隆删除（clonal deletion）：编辑失败时的“硬性淘汰”IClonal deletion: hard elimination when editing fails

若多轮受体编辑后BCR仍然强烈识别自身抗原，则该B细胞会通过凋亡被删除，这就是B细胞的克隆删除。

If multiple rounds of receptor editing still leave the BCR strongly self‑reactive, the B cell undergoes apoptosis, which constitutes clonal deletion for B cells.

老师指出，这一步类似T细胞在胸腺中的负选择：直接从库中清除最危险、亲和力最高的自身反应性克隆，防止其进入循环。

The lecturer noted that this is analogous to negative selection in the thymus for T cells, directly clearing the most dangerous, highest‑affinity self‑reactive clones from the repertoire.

3
无反应（anergy）：在离开骨髓前“关机但不杀死”IAnergy: switching off but not killing before exit from the marrow

还有一部分B细胞在骨髓中只受到持续弱信号或不完全激活，这类自身反应性B细胞常被“安静化”，进入功能性失活状态，即无反应。

A proportion of B cells in the bone marrow receive only chronic weak signals or incomplete activation; these autoreactive B cells are often silenced into a functionally inactive state called anergy.

无反应状态的细胞在表面受体和信号通路上被“调低音量”，对抗原刺激反应迟钝，很难被完全激活，这一步通常在B细胞离开骨髓前完成。

Anergic cells down‑tune their surface receptors and signaling pathways, making them sluggish to antigenic stimulation and difficult to fully activate, and this step is usually completed before the B cells leave the bone marrow.

随后这些细胞以“过渡性B细胞”身份进入外周淋巴器官，若继续遇到自身抗原和调节信号，仍可以被进一步删除或维持在无反应状态。

They then enter peripheral lymphoid organs as “transitional B cells,” where further exposure to self antigens and regulatory cues can either delete them or maintain them in an anergic state.

二、中枢耐受不完全
为何还需要外周耐受ICentral tolerance is incomplete: why peripheral tolerance is still required

老师在“Central tolerance”这一页强调：无论是胸腺中的T细胞，还是骨髓中的B细胞，中枢耐受都“不可能做到百分之百”。

On the “Central tolerance” slide the lecturer emphasized that central tolerance can never be one hundred percent complete, whether for T cells in the thymus or B cells in the bone marrow.

部分亲和力较低、或未曾接触到相应自身抗原的自身反应性B细胞和T细胞，仍然可以从原发淋巴器官“漏出”，进入外周循环。

Some self‑reactive B and T cells with lower affinity, or those that have not yet encountered their specific self antigens, can “leak” out of primary lymphoid organs into peripheral circulation.

因此体内必须再设置一层外周耐受机制，包括T细胞和B细胞层面的多种调控，来持续监控和约束这些潜在危险克隆。

The body therefore needs an additional layer of peripheral tolerance, with multiple mechanisms at both T‑cell and B‑cell levels, to continually monitor and restrain these potentially dangerous clones.

三、T细胞外周耐受的总体机制
预防激活与控制反应IPeripheral tolerance of T cells: preventing activation and controlling responses

“Peripheral tolerance of T cells”总览页将机制分为两大类：一类主要是预防自身反应性T细胞被激活（无反应、外周克隆删除），另一类是在它们被部分激活后继续控制反应强度（免疫豁免、调节性T细胞、免疫偏移）。

The overview slide on “Peripheral tolerance of T cells” divides mechanisms into two groups: those that prevent activation of self‑reactive T cells (anergy and peripheral clonal deletion) and those that control response intensity once they are partially activated (immune privilege, regulatory T cells, and immune deviation).

老师提醒，外周耐受和中枢耐受是串联的：前者弥补后者的“漏网之鱼”，也能应对感染、炎症或组织损伤时新暴露的自身抗原。

The lecturer reminded the class that peripheral and central tolerance act in series: peripheral mechanisms catch the “escapees” from central tolerance and also deal with newly exposed self antigens during infection, inflammation, or tissue damage.

四、T细胞外周无反应
有信号1没信号2的“假激活”IAnergy of T cells: pseudo‑activation with signal 1 but no signal 2

在“Peripheral tolerance of T cells – Anergy”图中，自身反应性T细胞通过TCR识别树突状细胞表面的MHC‑肽复合物，获得所谓“信号1”。

In the “Peripheral tolerance of T cells – Anergy” figure, a self‑reactive T cell receives “signal 1” when its TCR recognizes an MHC–peptide complex on the surface of a dendritic cell.

但此时树突状细胞处于未完全激活状态，MHC和共刺激分子（如CD80/CD86）的表达水平较低，几乎不提供“信号2”。

However the dendritic cell is not fully activated at this time and expresses low levels of MHC and co‑stimulatory molecules such as CD80/CD86, so it delivers almost no “signal 2”.

结果是T细胞既没有收到完整激活所需的双信号，又感知到一定抗原，从而进入一种长期功能性失活状态：不再增殖，也几乎不产生IL‑2，这就是外周无反应。

As a result the T cell perceives antigen without receiving the pair of signals needed for full activation and enters a long‑lasting functionally inactive state: it does not proliferate and produces little or no IL‑2, which defines peripheral anergy.

五、T细胞外周克隆删除
Fas/FasL介导的凋亡IPeripheral clonal deletion of T cells: Fas/FasL‑mediated apoptosis

“Deletion/apoptosis”这一页使用与无反应类似的示意，但在自身反应性T细胞一侧标出了Fas/FasL信号通路。

The “Deletion/apoptosis” slide uses a similar scheme to anergy but highlights the Fas/FasL pathway on the self‑reactive T‑cell side.

当自身反应性T细胞在外周持续或反复遇到自身抗原，而环境中缺乏足够共刺激和存活因子时，Fas/FasL相互作用被激活，诱导该T细胞发生凋亡。

When a self‑reactive T cell repeatedly encounters its self antigen in the periphery without adequate co‑stimulation and survival factors, Fas–FasL interactions are activated and induce apoptosis of that T cell.

这种“外周克隆删除”可以看作是对中枢耐受的再补刀：把仍然具有危险亲和力、并在外周获得持续信号的自身反应性克隆彻底清除出T细胞库。

This peripheral clonal deletion can be viewed as a second blow after central tolerance, definitively removing self‑reactive clones that still have dangerous affinity and receive chronic stimulation in the periphery from the T‑cell repertoire.

六、免疫豁免（免疫学“无知”）：通过空间隔离实现耐受IImmune privilege (immunological ignorance): tolerance via spatial segregation

“Immune privilege = ignorance”页面指出，有些组织特异性抗原在胸腺中呈递非常有限，属于“TSAs poorly presented in the thymus”。

The “Immune privilege = ignorance” slide notes that certain tissue‑specific antigens are “TSAs poorly presented in the thymus,” meaning their thymic display is very limited.

同时，某些解剖部位（如眼、脑、睾丸、胎盘等）被归类为免疫豁免区：血‑组织屏障严密、炎症倾向低、局部产生免疫抑制因子，使得淋巴细胞难以进入或即便进入也难以被完全激活。

At the same time certain anatomical sites such as the eye, brain, testis, and placenta are considered immune‑privileged: they have tight blood–tissue barriers, low inflammatory tone, and local production of immunosuppressive factors, making lymphocyte entry and full activation difficult.

在这些情境下，机体对相应抗原形成的是一种“无知型耐受”：不是主动抑制反应，而是淋巴细胞很少真正看到这些抗原，从而不触发免疫攻击。

Under these conditions the body establishes a form of “ignorance‑based tolerance”: instead of actively suppressing responses, lymphocytes simply rarely encounter these antigens and therefore do not initiate immune attacks.

七、免疫调节 = 调节性T细胞
外周耐受的核心主动机制IImmune regulation = regulatory T cells: the central active mechanism of peripheral tolerance

“Immune regulation = Regulatory T cells”图分为天然Treg和诱导型Treg两部分，说明外周耐受依赖两类来源不同但功能相似的调节性T细胞。

The “Immune regulation = Regulatory T cells” figure distinguishes natural Tregs and induced Tregs, showing that peripheral tolerance relies on two populations of regulatory T cells with different origins but similar functions.

天然Treg多在胸腺中由中等亲和力识别自身抗原的CD4+T细胞分化而来；诱导型Treg则是在外周遇到抗原时，在TGF‑β等细胞因子环境下由常规CD4+T细胞转化生成。

Natural Tregs mainly arise in the thymus from CD4+ T cells that recognize self antigens with intermediate affinity, whereas induced Tregs are generated in the periphery from conventional CD4+ T cells under cytokine conditions such as TGF‑β.

这两类Treg共同特点是表达CD4、CD25以及转录因子Foxp3，是外周维持耐受、抑制自身反应性T细胞和其他免疫细胞的“专业抑制细胞”。

Both types typically express CD4, CD25, and the transcription factor Foxp3 and act as professional suppressor cells that maintain peripheral tolerance and restrain self‑reactive T cells and other immune cells.

调节性T细胞的抑制机制多样
包括分泌抑制性细胞因子（IL‑10、TGF‑β、IL‑35）、表达CTLA‑4等负性共刺激分子、通过改变代谢微环境（如消耗IL‑2）、直接释放穿孔素和颗粒酶杀伤效应T细胞，以及与其他T细胞竞争树突状细胞表面资源等。

Regulatory T cells use multiple suppressive mechanisms: secreting inhibitory cytokines such as IL‑10, TGF‑β, and IL‑35; expressing negative co‑stimulatory molecules like CTLA‑4; altering the metabolic microenvironment by consuming IL‑2; directly killing effector T cells via perforin and granzymes; and competing with other T cells for access to dendritic‑cell surface resources.

老师特别强调，Treg数量或功能的缺陷，会显著增加多发性硬化、炎症性肠病和系统性自身免疫疾病等的发生风险，说明它们是外周耐受中最关键的主动“刹车装置”之一。

The lecturer emphasized that defects in Treg number or function markedly increase the risk of multiple sclerosis, inflammatory bowel disease, and systemic autoimmune diseases, showing that Tregs are among the most critical active “brakes” in peripheral tolerance.

八、IPEX综合征
外周Treg崩溃的极端表现IIPEX syndrome: an extreme example of peripheral Treg collapse

另一张“Peripheral tolerance of T cells – Regulatory T cells”详细介绍了IPEX综合征，这是一个由FoxP3基因突变引起的X连锁疾病。

Another “Peripheral tolerance of T cells – Regulatory T cells” slide details IPEX syndrome, an X‑linked disease caused by mutations in the FoxP3 gene.

IPEX的全称是“免疫失调、多腺体内分泌病、肠病、X连锁综合征”，其核心本质是胸腺及外周Treg谱系承诺尚在，但调节功能严重异常甚至耗竭。

IPEX stands for immune dysregulation, polyendocrinopathy, enteropathy, X‑linked syndrome, whose core nature is that although thymic and peripheral Treg lineage commitment exists, regulatory function is severely impaired or depleted.

PPT中展示
患者携带FoxP3突变后，Treg细胞数量减少且FOXP3表达不稳定，导致效应CD4+T细胞过度增殖，并向Th2和Th17等炎症谱系偏移，大量产生IL‑4、IL‑5、IL‑13和IL‑17。

The slide shows that with FoxP3 mutations patients have reduced numbers of Tregs and unstable FOXP3 expression, leading to hyperproliferation of effector CD4+ T cells that skew toward inflammatory lineages such as Th2 and Th17, producing large amounts of IL‑4, IL‑5, IL‑13, and IL‑17.

同时，成熟自身反应性B细胞扩增，产生多种自身抗体和高水平IgE，引发肠道、胰腺、皮肤、肾脏、甲状腺甚至胎儿相关组织的广泛自身免疫损伤。

At the same time mature autoreactive B cells expand, generating many autoantibodies and high IgE levels, which cause widespread autoimmune damage in the gut, pancreas, skin, kidneys, thyroid, and even fetal‑related tissues.

老师用IPEX例子说明
一旦外周调节性T细胞这一层耐受“崩盘”，机体会迅速进入多器官、多系统的严重自身免疫状态。

Using IPEX as an example the lecturer illustrated that once the peripheral Treg layer of tolerance collapses, the body rapidly develops severe multi‑organ, multi‑system autoimmunity.

九、免疫偏移（immune deviation）：通过调整T辅助细胞谱系来减轻自身损伤IImmune deviation: mitigating self damage by shifting T‑helper lineages

“Immune deviation”这一页强调的是细胞因子环境的改变可以让T辅助细胞（TH）在不同亚群之间重新“选边站队”。

The “Immune deviation” slide emphasizes that changes in the cytokine milieu can make T‑helper cells switch allegiance among different subsets.

例如IL‑12和IFN‑γ驱动TH1反应，产生更多IFN‑γ和TNF，有利于清除细胞内病原，但若过强则与自身免疫有关；而IL‑4则偏向TH2，促进IL‑4、IL‑5、IL‑13分泌，使反应从细胞毒型转向更偏向体液/过敏型。

For example IL‑12 and IFN‑γ drive TH1 responses that produce more IFN‑γ and TNF, which help clear intracellular pathogens but, if excessive, are linked to autoimmunity, whereas IL‑4 pushes toward TH2 differentiation with IL‑4, IL‑5, and IL‑13 secretion, shifting reactions from cytotoxic patterns to more humoral or allergy‑type responses.

TGF‑β联合IL‑6或其他因子则有利于形成TH17，分泌IL‑17A等，在某些病理状态下既能帮助对抗感染，也可能参与自身免疫；通过人为或自然改变这些细胞因子组合，可以在一定程度上“转移”免疫攻击的形式，降低对关键组织的破坏。

TGF‑β together with IL‑6 or other factors favors TH17 development and IL‑17A production, which can help fight infections but may also participate in autoimmunity; by naturally or therapeutically modulating these cytokine combinations it is sometimes possible to “redirect” immune attacks into less destructive forms for critical tissues.

十、B细胞外周耐受
补充骨髓之外未见到的自身抗原IPeripheral tolerance of B cells: complementing what bone marrow cannot see

“Peripheral tolerance of B cells”页面首先指出，B细胞在骨髓中不可能遇到体内所有自身抗原，尤其是很多组织特异性的膜蛋白或分泌蛋白。

The “Peripheral tolerance of B cells” slide first notes that B cells in the bone marrow cannot encounter all self antigens in the body, particularly many tissue‑specific membrane or secreted proteins.

因此，对这类自身抗原的耐受需要在外周建立，主要依赖两种机制：克隆删除和无反应。

Tolerance to such self antigens must therefore be established in the periphery and mainly relies on clonal deletion and anergy.

当自身反应性B细胞在外周高亲和力结合到组织特异性自身抗原，并获得强烈且持续的BCR信号时，会触发凋亡，从而完成外周层面的克隆删除。

When an autoreactive B cell in the periphery binds a tissue‑specific self antigen with high affinity and receives strong sustained BCR signaling, apoptosis is induced, completing clonal deletion at the peripheral level.

如果B细胞虽然能结合自身抗原，但缺乏T细胞提供的“第二信号”（例如CD40L‑CD40相互作用或足够的细胞因子支持），则会被固定在无反应状态，既存活但难以被激活、分化和产生抗体。

If a B cell can bind its self antigen yet receives no “second signal” from T cells, such as CD40L–CD40 interaction or adequate cytokine support, it becomes anergic, surviving but being difficult to activate, differentiate, or produce antibodies.

老师总结道
B细胞的耐受是“骨髓中枢筛选 + 外周二次把关”的组合，既利用受体编辑和克隆删除减少强烈自身反应性克隆，也通过无反应等方式在外周长期压制残余的自身反应性B细胞。

The lecturer concluded that B‑cell tolerance is a combination of central screening in the bone marrow plus secondary checkpoints in the periphery, using receptor editing and clonal deletion to reduce strongly self‑reactive clones and long‑term anergy to suppress residual autoreactive B cells.

耐受破坏与自身免疫发生ITolerance breakdown and autoimmunity

这一部分从前面“免疫耐受”的机制出发，转向讨论耐受是如何被打破的：哪些淋巴细胞会被错误激活、哪些清除与调节环节失效、哪些来源的自身抗原则被暴露出来，最终共同推动自身免疫病的发生。

This part moves from the mechanisms of “immune tolerance” to how tolerance is broken: which lymphocytes are wrongly activated, which clearance and regulatory steps fail, and which sources of self antigens become exposed, together driving autoimmune diseases.

一、总体框架
耐受破坏是多环节、多因素同时失控的结果→Overall picture: tolerance breakdown is failure of multiple steps and factors
PPT首先用一个总表列出几类关键机制
无知型自身反应淋巴细胞被激活、无反应型淋巴细胞被再激活、自身反应淋巴细胞凋亡缺陷、抗原呈递细胞共刺激分子上调、调节性T细胞缺陷以及细胞因子平衡失调。

The first slide lists key mechanisms: activation of ignorant autoreactive lymphocytes, re‑activation of anergic lymphocytes, apoptosis defects in autoreactive lymphocytes, upregulation of co‑stimulatory molecules on antigen‑presenting cells, defects in regulatory T cells, and dysregulation of cytokine balance.

底部的蓝色框强调
自身免疫并非单一原因，而是“多个因素共同作用”的结果，这也解释了为什么同样的遗传背景下，有些人发病、有些人不发病。

The blue box at the bottom stresses that autoimmunity is not due to a single cause but results from “the conjunction of several factors,” explaining why some people become ill while others with similar genetics do not.

二、“无知型”自身反应淋巴细胞的激活
原本应该永远接触不到抗原→Activation of “ignorant” autoreactive lymphocytes: cells that should never see their antigen

所谓“ignorant autoreactive lymphocytes”指的是那些带有自身反应性TCR或BCR，但在生理状态下几乎从不遇到对应自身抗原的克隆，它们在体内容许存在却功能上处于“无知状态”。

“Ignorant autoreactive lymphocytes” are clones bearing autoreactive TCRs or BCRs that under physiological conditions almost never encounter their cognate self antigens, so they are allowed to exist but remain functionally ignorant.

原因通常是这些组织特异性抗原被“隔离”在免疫豁免部位或封闭组织中，例如脑实质、眼内、睾丸、某些腺体深部等，平时既不容易被树突状细胞携出，也不易被淋巴细胞直接接触。

This is usually because their tissue‑specific antigens are sequestered in immune‑privileged or closed sites, such as brain parenchyma, the eye, testis, or deep glandular tissues, which are normally inaccessible to dendritic cells or lymphocytes.

老师在录音中举例
如果脑内发生感染或出血，屏障被破坏，大量脑抗原被释放并被树突状细胞摄取，就可能激活本来“应该一直无知”的针对脑抗原的自身反应性T细胞。

In the recording the lecturer gave the example that brain infection or hemorrhage can break the barrier, release brain antigens for dendritic cell uptake, and activate self‑reactive T cells that were supposed to remain ignorant toward brain antigens.

PPT指出，无知型细胞是否被激活取决于几个因素：抗原的种类和剂量、暴露次数、体内已被激活的T细胞数量以及受累组织中MHC与共刺激分子的上调程度。

The slide notes that activation of ignorant cells depends on the nature and dose of the antigen, the number of exposures, the number of already activated T cells, and how much MHC and co‑stimulatory molecule expression is upregulated in the affected tissues.

在严重感染或组织损伤时，局部炎症信号会诱导组织细胞和浸润的APC大量上调MHC和CD80/CD86等共刺激分子，从而把本来“安静呈递自身抗原”的环境，转变为足以完全激活自身反应性T细胞的“危险环境”。

During severe infection or tissue damage, local inflammatory signals induce tissue cells and infiltrating APCs to upregulate MHC and co‑stimulatory molecules such as CD80/CD86, turning what was a quiet environment passively presenting self antigens into a “dangerous environment” capable of fully activating self‑reactive T cells.

三、无反应型自身反应淋巴细胞在炎症中被“救活”→Rescue of anergic autoreactive lymphocytes under inflammation

前面外周耐受部分已经讲过，无反应（anergy）的T细胞或B细胞通常是在只收到“信号1”（TCR或BCR识别抗原）而几乎没有“信号2”（共刺激）的情况下产生的，会长期表现为不分裂、不分泌IL‑2、难以激活。

Earlier we saw that anergic T or B cells arise when they receive “signal 1” (TCR or BCR recognition of antigen) without “signal 2” (co‑stimulation), leaving them long‑term non‑proliferative, IL‑2‑deficient, and hard to activate.

当前这组PPT强调
在强烈炎症条件下（如急性感染），这种无反应状态是可以被逆转的。

These slides now emphasize that under strong inflammatory conditions such as acute infection, this anergic state can be reversed.

一方面，病原相关分子模式（PAMPs）和损伤相关分子（DAMPs）通过Toll样受体等触发树突状细胞成熟，使其高表达共刺激分子（CD80/CD86）、CD40以及分泌促炎细胞因子。

On one hand pathogen‑associated molecular patterns (PAMPs) and damage‑associated molecules (DAMPs) stimulate dendritic cells via Toll‑like and other receptors, driving their maturation with high expression of co‑stimulatory molecules (CD80/CD86), CD40, and secretion of pro‑inflammatory cytokines.

这样，原本只提供“信号1”的APC现在可以提供完整的“信号1+信号2”，其中典型的共刺激轴包括CD40L/CD40以及CD28与CD80/CD86的结合。

Thus APCs that previously gave only “signal 1” now deliver full “signal 1 plus signal 2,” with classical co‑stimulatory axes such as CD40L binding CD40 and CD28 binding CD80/CD86.

另一方面，炎症环境中大量细胞因子和特异抗原的再刺激，可直接促使部分无反应T细胞重新进入增殖和效应分化程序，相当于把“已经被关机的自身反应克隆”重新启动。

On the other hand abundant cytokines and renewed antigen stimulation in inflammation can directly push some anergic T cells back into proliferation and effector differentiation, effectively rebooting “switched‑off autoreactive clones.”

老师特别提醒
这有两层含义——炎症既可能使本来应被诱导成无反应的细胞一开始就被完全激活，也可能在无反应已经形成之后，把它们从无反应状态中拯救出来，两种情况都为自身免疫埋下隐患。

The lecturer stressed two implications: inflammation can both prevent proper induction of anergy in the first place and rescue cells from an already established anergic state, and both scenarios set the stage for autoimmunity.

四、凋亡缺陷
无法清除自身反应性淋巴细胞→Apoptosis default: failure to delete autoreactive lymphocytes

这一页“Apoptosis default in autoreactive lymphocytes”突出两类凋亡通路：外源性凋亡和内源性凋亡，只要任一通路受损，都可能让本应被删除的自身反应性克隆长期存活。

The “Apoptosis default in autoreactive lymphocytes” slide highlights two apoptosis pathways, extrinsic and intrinsic; defects in either can allow self‑reactive clones that should be deleted to persist.

外源性凋亡主要依赖Fas/FasL通路：Fas（CD95）是T细胞等表达的死亡受体，FasL多由活化T细胞或其他细胞表达，两者结合后招募下游分子并激活半胱天冬酶级联反应，诱导细胞程序性死亡。

Extrinsic apoptosis relies mainly on the Fas/FasL pathway: Fas (CD95) is a death receptor on T cells and others, FasL is expressed on activated T cells and other cells, and their interaction recruits downstream molecules to activate caspase cascades that trigger programmed cell death.

内源性凋亡则与线粒体通路和Bcl‑2家族成员相关，其中促凋亡的Bim被特别标出，是调控淋巴细胞生死、维持耐受的重要分子。

Intrinsic apoptosis involves mitochondrial pathways and Bcl‑2 family members; the pro‑apoptotic protein Bim is highlighted as a key regulator of lymphocyte survival and tolerance.

PPT指出
这两条凋亡通路都与系统性红斑狼疮的发病机制有关——如果凋亡不足，自身反应性淋巴细胞得以积累，同时大量凋亡残骸又得不到及时清除，形成自身抗原库。

The slide notes that both apoptosis pathways are linked to systemic lupus erythematosus pathophysiology—insufficient apoptosis lets autoreactive lymphocytes accumulate, and uncleared apoptotic debris creates a reservoir of self antigens.

在Fas基因突变的小鼠中，可以看到系统性血管炎、关节炎、脾肿大、淋巴细胞异常增殖以及肾脏中大量自身抗体沉积，表现出严重的系统性自身免疫表型。

In Fas‑mutant mice there is systemic vasculitis, arthritis, splenomegaly, abnormal lymphoproliferation, and extensive autoantibody deposition in the kidneys, all features of severe systemic autoimmunity.

缺失Bim的小鼠同样出现淋巴结肿大、脾肿大、血清高γ球蛋白血症和大量自身抗体，PPT特别说明Bim在维持无反应性B细胞和清除自身反应性B细胞存活方面至关重要。

Mice lacking Bim likewise show lymphadenopathy, splenomegaly, hypergammaglobulinemia, and many autoantibodies; the slide emphasizes that Bim is crucial for maintaining anergic B cells and eliminating autoreactive B cells.

五、其他耐受破坏因素
共刺激上调、Treg缺陷与细胞因子失衡→Other breakdown factors: co‑stimulation upregulation, Treg defects, and cytokine imbalance
总览PPT中还列出三点
APC上共刺激分子上调、调节性T细胞缺陷、细胞因子平衡失调，这三者在炎症背景下往往同时存在并互相放大。

The overview slide also lists three items: upregulation of co‑stimulatory molecules on APCs, defects in regulatory T cells, and dysregulation of cytokine balance, which often coexist and amplify each other in inflammatory settings.

当树突状细胞、巨噬细胞在慢性炎症中持续受到PAMPs/DAMPs刺激时，会长时间高表达CD80/CD86、CD40等，使得即使是低亲和力自身反应性T细胞，一旦遇到抗原，也能得到强烈“信号2”而被激活。

When dendritic cells and macrophages are chronically stimulated by PAMPs or DAMPs they maintain high CD80/CD86 and CD40 expression, so even low‑affinity autoreactive T cells can receive strong “signal 2” and be activated once they see antigen.

如果前面讲到的胸腺Treg诱导或外周Treg维持出现缺陷（如FOXP3突变、Treg数量不足或功能不稳定），本应被Treg“压制”的这些自身反应性克隆就失去了关键的刹车。

If thymic Treg induction or peripheral Treg maintenance is defective, for example through FOXP3 mutations, insufficient Treg numbers, or unstable Treg function, these autoreactive clones lose a key braking mechanism.

此外，促炎细胞因子（如IL‑6、TNF‑α、IFN‑γ、IL‑17等）和抗炎细胞因子（如IL‑10、TGF‑β等）之间的平衡被打破，会推动TH1/TH17等致病谱系扩张、抑制Treg和抗炎环境，从而进一步放大自身免疫反应。

Moreover, imbalance between pro‑inflammatory cytokines such as IL‑6, TNF‑α, IFN‑γ, and IL‑17 and anti‑inflammatory cytokines like IL‑10 and TGF‑β promotes expansion of pathogenic TH1/TH17 lineages and weakens Tregs and regulatory milieus, further amplifying autoimmune responses.

六、自身抗原从何而来
持续周转、损伤和清除缺陷→Where do auto‑antigens come from: turnover, damage, and clearance defects

“Sources of auto‑Ags?”部分总结了免疫系统在任何时刻都暴露于一定量的自身抗原，这些抗原在耐受破坏时会成为攻击靶点。

The “Sources of auto‑Ags?” section summarizes that the immune system is constantly exposed to some level of self antigens, which become targets once tolerance breaks.

首先，健康组织在正常更新过程中也会不断脱落、细胞经历凋亡，这是一个基础的自身抗原来源；在耐受正常时，这些凋亡细胞会被安静、非炎症性地清除。

First, healthy tissues shed components and undergo apoptosis during normal turnover, providing a baseline source of self antigens that are quietly cleared under intact tolerance.

其次，病原体抗原与自身抗原之间可能存在结构相似性，形成“分子模拟”（molecular mimicry），这种交叉反应会在后面的几页重点展开。

Second, pathogen antigens may structurally resemble self antigens, creating “molecular mimicry,” a type of cross‑reactivity elaborated on in later slides.

第三，自身抗原本身可能因基因突变或化学修饰、翻译后修饰（如柠檬酸化、氧化、糖基化等）而“变形”，被免疫系统当作新的非自我抗原识别。

Third, self antigens may themselves be altered by gene mutation or chemical and post‑translational modifications, such as citrullination, oxidation, or glycosylation, making them appear as novel non‑self antigens.

第四，组织损伤（创伤、缺血、慢性炎症、感染等）会大量释放细胞内成分，包括以前从未暴露给免疫系统的隐蔽抗原或“隐匿表位”。

Fourth, tissue injury from trauma, ischemia, chronic inflammation, or infection releases large quantities of intracellular components, including previously hidden antigens or cryptic epitopes.

最后，如果凋亡细胞和中性粒细胞网等残骸清除系统本身存在缺陷，这些自身成分会在体内堆积，长期刺激免疫系统，是系统性红斑狼疮等疾病的重要病理基础。

Finally, if clearance systems for apoptotic cells and neutrophil extracellular traps are defective, these self components accumulate and chronically stimulate the immune system, forming an important pathological basis for diseases such as systemic lupus erythematosus.

七、分子模拟与交叉反应
由感染引发的自身免疫→Molecular mimicry and cross‑reaction: infection‑triggered autoimmunity

“Cross reaction = Molecular mimicry”大图通过三个板块展示了从感染到自身免疫的渐进过程：A为分子模拟本身，B为旁观者激活，C为表位扩展。

The large “Cross reaction = Molecular mimicry” diagram uses three panels to show the progression from infection to autoimmunity: A for molecular mimicry, B for bystander activation, and C for epitope spreading.

在“分子模拟”情形下，病毒或细菌蛋白被APC加工成肽并呈递给特异性T细胞，这一步是正常的抗感染反应。

In molecular mimicry, viral or bacterial proteins are processed into peptides by APCs and presented to specific T cells, which is a normal anti‑infective response.

问题在于
某些病原体肽段在空间构象或氨基酸序列上，与某种自身抗原表位高度相似，使得同一个TCR或BCR既可以识别病原肽，也能识别对应的自身肽。

The problem arises when certain pathogen peptides are structurally or sequentially very similar to self epitopes, so that the same TCR or BCR recognizes both the pathogen peptide and the corresponding self peptide.

当感染时，病原抗原在强烈炎症环境中被呈递，T细胞或B细胞会被完全激活，形成效应细胞和记忆细胞；感染清除后，这些记忆克隆依然存在。

During infection the pathogen antigen is presented in a highly inflammatory context, fully activating T or B cells and generating effectors and memory cells that persist after the infection is cleared.

此时，如果机体某个组织中表达的自身抗原“长得很像”先前的病原肽，这些记忆细胞再次遇到抗原时就会错误地攻击自身组织，引发自身免疫。

If later a tissue expresses a self antigen that “looks like” the previous pathogen peptide, these memory cells will mistake the self antigen for the pathogen and attack the tissue, initiating autoimmunity.

旁观者激活（bystander activation）进一步放大反应：感染部位释放大量细胞因子和危险信号，激活局部APC，使其呈递更多来源于受损组织的自身抗原，并激活原本没有分子模拟关系的其他自身反应性淋巴细胞。

Bystander activation further amplifies the response: the infected site releases many cytokines and danger signals that activate local APCs to present more self antigens from damaged tissues, thereby activating additional autoreactive lymphocytes that do not rely on mimicry.

表位扩展（epitope spreading）则是一个正反馈过程：最初针对某一个表位的自身反应造成组织损伤，释放出更多不同的自身抗原和新表位，这些新表位又刺激新的自身反应性克隆，病变范围和抗体谱随时间不断扩展。

Epitope spreading is a positive‑feedback process in which an initial autoimmune response against one epitope causes tissue damage, releasing more self antigens and new epitopes that in turn stimulate new autoreactive clones so that lesions and autoantibody repertoires expand over time.

老师在录音中强调，多发性硬化、类风湿关节炎和系统性红斑狼疮等疾病中，都可以观察到表位扩展现象：随着病程进展，患者血清中自身抗体种类和靶抗原越来越多。

The lecturer emphasized that in multiple sclerosis, rheumatoid arthritis, and systemic lupus erythematosus, epitope spreading is observed clinically as an increasing variety of autoantibodies and target antigens as the disease progresses.

八、分子模拟在具体疾病中的例子→Examples of molecular mimicry in specific diseases

第二张分子模拟PPT给出了NEJM综述表格，总结了一些被提出的“病原‑自身抗原”对应关系和可能涉及的免疫细胞类型。

The second molecular mimicry slide shows a NEJM review table summarizing proposed pathogen–self antigen pairs and the immune cell types involved.

例如，在1型糖尿病中，一个重要自身抗原是胰岛β细胞的GAD65；表中提出某些柯萨奇病毒P2‑C蛋白与GAD65存在结构相似性，T细胞可能在对病毒反应后，交叉识别胰岛抗原。

For type 1 diabetes an important autoantigen is the beta‑cell enzyme GAD65; the table suggests that Coxsackievirus P2‑C proteins resemble GAD65 so that T cells initially primed against the virus may cross‑recognize islet antigens.

在类风湿关节炎中，HLA‑DRB1及一些热休克蛋白60被认为是自身靶标，而结核分枝杆菌的65 kDa热休克蛋白等可能提供结构近似的外源抗原，既可激活T细胞也可激活B细胞。

In rheumatoid arthritis HLA‑DRB1 and heat‑shock protein 60 are candidate autoantigens, while Mycobacterium tuberculosis 65‑kDa heat‑shock proteins may provide similar foreign antigens that activate both T and B cells.

多发性硬化中，髓鞘碱性蛋白（MBP）是经典自身抗原，多种病毒抗原被怀疑与其存在分子模拟关系；脊柱关节病则与HLA‑B27和多种革兰阴性菌蛋白之间的交叉反应有关。

In multiple sclerosis myelin basic protein is a classical autoantigen, and several viral antigens are suspected to mimic it; in spondyloarthropathies, HLA‑B27 and proteins from various Gram‑negative bacteria are thought to cross‑react.

Graves病中，甲状腺刺激激素受体（TSHR）作为自身抗原，可能与某些耶尔森菌表面蛋白发生B细胞层面的分子模拟，促成刺激型自身抗体的产生。

In Graves’ disease the thyroid‑stimulating hormone receptor is the autoantigen, and certain Yersinia proteins may mimic it at the B‑cell level, favoring the emergence of stimulatory autoantibodies.

老师提醒
这些分子模拟关系有的证据充分（尤其是在动物模型中），有的在人类中仍具争议，但从整体上支持“感染可以通过分子模拟和表位扩展，成为自身免疫疾病的重要触发因素”这一观点。

The lecturer cautioned that some of these mimicry links are well supported, especially in animal models, while others remain controversial in humans, but overall they support the idea that infections, through molecular mimicry and epitope spreading, are major triggers of autoimmune disease.

九、本部分小结
从耐受到自身免疫的关键跨越→Summary of this section: the critical leap from tolerance to autoimmunity

综合这一组PPT和讲解，耐受破坏可以概括为三个层面：一是本该“看不到抗原”或“保持无反应”的自身反应淋巴细胞被激活；二是本应被Fas/Bim等通路删除的克隆因凋亡缺陷而存活；三是感染、组织损伤和清除功能障碍持续提供并放大自身抗原来源。

Taken together, tolerance breakdown can be summarized at three levels: first, autoreactive lymphocytes that should remain ignorant or anergic become activated; second, clones that should be deleted by Fas-, Bim-, and related pathways survive because of apoptosis defects; third, infections, tissue damage, and clearance failures continuously generate and amplify self antigens.

在这些基础上，共刺激分子上调、Treg数量或功能受损以及细胞因子谱向促炎偏移，共同将一次偶发的激活事件转变为持续的自身免疫反应。

On top of this, upregulated co‑stimulation, impaired Treg numbers or function, and a shift of cytokine profiles toward inflammation together convert an occasional activation event into a sustained autoimmune response.

老师用这部分为后面的“危险因素”和具体疾病（系统性红斑狼疮、多发性硬化）的病理生理学作铺垫：真正的临床自身免疫病，往往是遗传易感、环境触发、耐受机制缺陷和抗原特异性等多重因素长期叠加的结果。

The lecturer used this section to prepare for the upcoming “risk factors” and disease‑specific pathophysiology (systemic lupus erythematosus and multiple sclerosis), emphasizing that clinical autoimmune diseases usually arise from long‑term convergence of genetic susceptibility, environmental triggers, defective tolerance mechanisms, and specific antigenic targets.

七、分子模拟与交叉反应
由感染引发的自身免疫→Molecular mimicry and cross‑reaction: infection‑triggered autoimmunity

“Cross reaction = Molecular mimicry”大图通过三个板块展示了从感染到自身免疫的渐进过程：A为分子模拟本身，B为旁观者激活，C为表位扩展。

The large “Cross reaction = Molecular mimicry” diagram uses three panels to show the progression from infection to autoimmunity: A for molecular mimicry, B for bystander activation, and C for epitope spreading.

在“分子模拟”情形下，病毒或细菌蛋白被APC加工成肽并呈递给特异性T细胞，这一步是正常的抗感染反应。

In molecular mimicry, viral or bacterial proteins are processed into peptides by APCs and presented to specific T cells, which is a normal anti‑infective response.

问题在于
某些病原体肽段在空间构象或氨基酸序列上，与某种自身抗原表位高度相似，使得同一个TCR或BCR既可以识别病原肽，也能识别对应的自身肽。

The problem arises when certain pathogen peptides are structurally or sequentially very similar to self epitopes, so that the same TCR or BCR recognizes both the pathogen peptide and the corresponding self peptide.

当感染时，病原抗原在强烈炎症环境中被呈递，T细胞或B细胞会被完全激活，形成效应细胞和记忆细胞；感染清除后，这些记忆克隆依然存在。

During infection the pathogen antigen is presented in a highly inflammatory context, fully activating T or B cells and generating effectors and memory cells that persist after the infection is cleared.

此时，如果机体某个组织中表达的自身抗原“长得很像”先前的病原肽，这些记忆细胞再次遇到抗原时就会错误地攻击自身组织，引发自身免疫。

If later a tissue expresses a self antigen that “looks like” the previous pathogen peptide, these memory cells will mistake the self antigen for the pathogen and attack the tissue, initiating autoimmunity.

旁观者激活（bystander activation）进一步放大反应：感染部位释放大量细胞因子和危险信号，激活局部APC，使其呈递更多来源于受损组织的自身抗原，并激活原本没有分子模拟关系的其他自身反应性淋巴细胞。

Bystander activation further amplifies the response: the infected site releases many cytokines and danger signals that activate local APCs to present more self antigens from damaged tissues, thereby activating additional autoreactive lymphocytes that do not rely on mimicry.

表位扩展（epitope spreading）则是一个正反馈过程：最初针对某一个表位的自身反应造成组织损伤，释放出更多不同的自身抗原和新表位，这些新表位又刺激新的自身反应性克隆，病变范围和抗体谱随时间不断扩展。

Epitope spreading is a positive‑feedback process in which an initial autoimmune response against one epitope causes tissue damage, releasing more self antigens and new epitopes that in turn stimulate new autoreactive clones so that lesions and autoantibody repertoires expand over time.

老师在录音中强调，多发性硬化、类风湿关节炎和系统性红斑狼疮等疾病中，都可以观察到表位扩展现象：随着病程进展，患者血清中自身抗体种类和靶抗原越来越多。

The lecturer emphasized that in multiple sclerosis, rheumatoid arthritis, and systemic lupus erythematosus, epitope spreading is observed clinically as an increasing variety of autoantibodies and target antigens as the disease progresses.

八、分子模拟在具体疾病中的例子→Examples of molecular mimicry in specific diseases

第二张分子模拟PPT给出了NEJM综述表格，总结了一些被提出的“病原‑自身抗原”对应关系和可能涉及的免疫细胞类型。

The second molecular mimicry slide shows a NEJM review table summarizing proposed pathogen–self antigen pairs and the immune cell types involved.

例如，在1型糖尿病中，一个重要自身抗原是胰岛β细胞的GAD65；表中提出某些柯萨奇病毒P2‑C蛋白与GAD65存在结构相似性，T细胞可能在对病毒反应后，交叉识别胰岛抗原。

For type 1 diabetes an important autoantigen is the beta‑cell enzyme GAD65; the table suggests that Coxsackievirus P2‑C proteins resemble GAD65 so that T cells initially primed against the virus may cross‑recognize islet antigens.

在类风湿关节炎中，HLA‑DRB1及一些热休克蛋白60被认为是自身靶标，而结核分枝杆菌的65 kDa热休克蛋白等可能提供结构近似的外源抗原，既可激活T细胞也可激活B细胞。

In rheumatoid arthritis HLA‑DRB1 and heat‑shock protein 60 are candidate autoantigens, while Mycobacterium tuberculosis 65‑kDa heat‑shock proteins may provide similar foreign antigens that activate both T and B cells.

多发性硬化中，髓鞘碱性蛋白（MBP）是经典自身抗原，多种病毒抗原被怀疑与其存在分子模拟关系；脊柱关节病则与HLA‑B27和多种革兰阴性菌蛋白之间的交叉反应有关。

In multiple sclerosis myelin basic protein is a classical autoantigen, and several viral antigens are suspected to mimic it; in spondyloarthropathies, HLA‑B27 and proteins from various Gram‑negative bacteria are thought to cross‑react.

Graves病中，甲状腺刺激激素受体（TSHR）作为自身抗原，可能与某些耶尔森菌表面蛋白发生B细胞层面的分子模拟，促成刺激型自身抗体的产生。

In Graves’ disease the thyroid‑stimulating hormone receptor is the autoantigen, and certain Yersinia proteins may mimic it at the B‑cell level, favoring the emergence of stimulatory autoantibodies.

老师提醒
这些分子模拟关系有的证据充分（尤其是在动物模型中），有的在人类中仍具争议，但从整体上支持“感染可以通过分子模拟和表位扩展，成为自身免疫疾病的重要触发因素”这一观点。

The lecturer cautioned that some of these mimicry links are well supported, especially in animal models, while others remain controversial in humans, but overall they support the idea that infections, through molecular mimicry and epitope spreading, are major triggers of autoimmune disease.

九、本部分小结
从耐受到自身免疫的关键跨越→Summary of this section: the critical leap from tolerance to autoimmunity

综合这一组PPT和讲解，耐受破坏可以概括为三个层面：一是本该“看不到抗原”或“保持无反应”的自身反应淋巴细胞被激活；二是本应被Fas/Bim等通路删除的克隆因凋亡缺陷而存活；三是感染、组织损伤和清除功能障碍持续提供并放大自身抗原来源。

Taken together, tolerance breakdown can be summarized at three levels: first, autoreactive lymphocytes that should remain ignorant or anergic become activated; second, clones that should be deleted by Fas-, Bim-, and related pathways survive because of apoptosis defects; third, infections, tissue damage, and clearance failures continuously generate and amplify self antigens.

在这些基础上，共刺激分子上调、Treg数量或功能受损以及细胞因子谱向促炎偏移，共同将一次偶发的激活事件转变为持续的自身免疫反应。

On top of this, upregulated co‑stimulation, impaired Treg numbers or function, and a shift of cytokine profiles toward inflammation together convert an occasional activation event into a sustained autoimmune response.

老师用这部分为后面的“危险因素”和具体疾病（系统性红斑狼疮、多发性硬化）的病理生理学作铺垫：真正的临床自身免疫病，往往是遗传易感、环境触发、耐受机制缺陷和抗原特异性等多重因素长期叠加的结果。

The lecturer used this section to prepare for the upcoming “risk factors” and disease‑specific pathophysiology (systemic lupus erythematosus and multiple sclerosis), emphasizing that clinical autoimmune diseases usually arise from long‑term convergence of genetic susceptibility, environmental triggers, defective tolerance mechanisms, and specific antigenic targets.

自身免疫病的危险因素IRisk factors for autoimmune diseases

这一部分在大纲中对应“Risk factors”，老师用它把前面讲的耐受机制和后面要讲的SLE、多发性硬化连接起来，强调自身免疫病一定是多种危险因素共同作用的结果，而不是单一原因。

This part corresponds to “Risk factors” in the outline; the teacher uses it to bridge the tolerance mechanisms discussed earlier with SLE and MS, stressing that autoimmune diseases always result from multiple risk factors acting together rather than a single cause.

一、整体框架
内源性与外源性危险因素→Endogenous vs exogenous risk factors
PPT首先把危险因素分成两大类
内源性危险因素包括遗传和表观遗传，外源性危险因素主要是环境，二者共同决定疾病的“多因素性”。

The slides first divide risk factors into two main groups: endogenous factors including genetics and epigenetics, and exogenous factors mainly encompassing the environment, together defining the diseases as “multifactorial”.

同一个因素既可能提高发病风险，也可能在某些组合下起到保护作用，所以老师特别用“induction / protection”提示：同一条通路上既可以有危险等位基因，也可以有保护等位基因。

The teacher also notes that a given pathway can harbor both risk and protective variants, highlighted by the “induction / protection” arrows to show that the same pathway can either promote or protect against disease depending on the alleles present.

二、遗传学证据
多基因易感与双生子研究→Genetic evidence: polygenic susceptibility and twin studies

PPT指出，已有超过30个基因与不同自身免疫病相关，因此大部分自身免疫病是“多基因（polygenic）”而不是单基因疾病。

The slides state that more than 30 genes have been linked to autoimmune disorders, so most such diseases are “polygenic” rather than monogenic.

同卵双生子资料表显示
类风湿关节炎同卵双生子一致率约12–32%，异卵4–9%；1型糖尿病同卵30–54%，异卵0–13%；多发性硬化同卵9–26%，异卵0–4%；克罗恩病同卵约44%，异卵约4%。

The concordance table shows that for rheumatoid arthritis the monozygotic twin concordance is about 12–32% vs 4–9% for dizygotic twins; for type 1 diabetes 30–54% vs 0–13%; for multiple sclerosis 9–26% vs 0–4%; and for Crohn’s disease about 44% vs 4%.

这些数据说明
基因明显增加风险，但远未达到100%一致，提示环境和免疫耐受状态仍然非常重要。

These figures demonstrate that genes clearly increase risk but fall far short of 100% concordance, indicating that environment and tolerance mechanisms remain very important.

三、HLA基因
最强的遗传危险位点→HLA genes as major risk loci

PPT单独列出“HLA关联”，强调HLA（人类MHC）等位基因对自身抗原肽的呈递能力不同，是很多自身免疫病中最强的遗传危险因素。

A separate “HLA association” slide emphasizes that HLA (human MHC) alleles differ in their ability to present self peptides and constitute the strongest genetic risk factors in many autoimmune diseases.

脊柱关节病中，HLA‑B27在健康人群约8%，在强直性脊柱炎患者中高达90–95%，说明特定等位基因明显富集于患者。

In spondyloarthropathy HLA‑B27 is present in about 8% of healthy people but 90–95% of patients with ankylosing spondylitis, showing strong enrichment of specific alleles in patients.

类风湿关节炎中，HLA‑DRB1在健康人约28%，在RA患者约70%；1型糖尿病中，HLA‑DR3或DR4在健康人约40%，在患者约95%。

In rheumatoid arthritis HLA‑DRB1 occurs in about 28% of healthy individuals but 70% of RA patients; in type 1 diabetes HLA‑DR3 or DR4 appears in roughly 40% of healthy people but in 95% of patients.

乳糜泻中，HLA‑DR3‑DQ2在健康人约30%，在乳糜泻患者约90%；多发性硬化在不同人群分别与HLA‑DR2‑Dw2或HLA‑DR6相关。

In celiac disease HLA‑DR3‑DQ2 is found in about 30% of healthy individuals but 90% of patients; multiple sclerosis is associated with HLA‑DR2‑Dw2 or HLA‑DR6 in different populations.

老师在讲解中强调
HLA本质上改变的是“哪些自身肽更容易被呈递并被TCR看到”，从而改变某些自身反应性T细胞被激活的机会。

The lecturer stressed that HLA essentially determines “which self peptides are more efficiently presented and seen by TCRs,” thereby modifying the likelihood that certain autoreactive T cells will be activated.

四、其他遗传通路
补体、共刺激、信号转导与细胞因子→Other genetic pathways: complement, co‑stimulation, signaling, cytokines

“GENETICS”细化页把非HLA危险基因按功能通路归类，老师重点强调它们怎样削弱耐受或增加自身抗原负荷。

The detailed “GENETICS” slide groups non‑HLA risk genes by functional pathways, and the teacher highlighted how they weaken tolerance or increase self‑antigen load.

补体基因方面，C1q、C2、C4缺陷与系统性红斑狼疮高度相关，因为补体负责清除免疫复合物和凋亡细胞，缺陷会让自身抗原长期滞留。

For complement genes, deficiencies of C1q, C2, or C4 are strongly associated with SLE because complement is needed to clear immune complexes and apoptotic cells, and defects allow self antigens to persist.

共刺激通路中，CTLA‑4、CD28、ICOS、CD80等基因与1型糖尿病、Graves病、RA和SLE相关，反映“信号2”的强弱直接决定自身反应性T细胞是被删除、无反应，还是被完全激活。

In co‑stimulatory pathways, CTLA‑4, CD28, ICOS, and CD80 are associated with type 1 diabetes, Graves’ disease, RA, and SLE, reflecting that the strength of “signal 2” determines whether autoreactive T cells are deleted, rendered anergic, or fully activated.

T细胞信号转导与活化方面，PTPN22等基因调节TCR信号阈值，其多态性会让T细胞更易或更难被抗原激活，因此与多种自身免疫病易感有关。

For T‑cell signaling and activation, genes like PTPN22 regulate TCR signaling thresholds; polymorphisms can make T cells easier or harder to activate by antigen, which is linked to susceptibility in several autoimmune diseases.

TNF受体通路上，TNFα受体信号的上调有利于慢性炎症维持，与克罗恩病、RA、SLE和1型糖尿病等炎症性疾病相关。

Within TNF‑receptor pathways, upregulated TNFα‑receptor signaling promotes chronic inflammation and is associated with Crohn’s disease, RA, SLE, and type 1 diabetes.

Fc受体基因的改变会影响机体对免疫复合物的“识别和吞噬效率”，从而调节含自身抗体复合物在体内的堆积程度。

Changes in Fc‑receptor genes affect the efficiency with which the body recognizes and phagocytoses immune complexes, modulating the accumulation of autoantibody‑containing complexes.

细胞因子相关基因（如IL1RL1、IL2、IL6等）通过改变细胞因子网络，使免疫反应更偏向促炎或抗炎，从而影响炎症性肠病、1型糖尿病和RA等的风险。

Cytokine‑related genes such as IL1RL1, IL2, and IL6 alter cytokine networks, biasing immune responses toward pro‑ or anti‑inflammatory states and thereby influencing risks of IBD, type 1 diabetes, RA, and others.

五、性激素影响
女性高发与生理时期相关→Influence of sex hormones: female predominance and life stages

“INFLUENCE OF SEXUAL HORMONES”页首先指出：绝大多数自身免疫病在女性中的患病率高于男性，并展示了不同疾病的女男比，多数≥2:1，SLE和Sjögren综合征约9:1。

The “INFLUENCE OF SEXUAL HORMONES” slide first notes that most autoimmune diseases are more prevalent in women than men, with female‑to‑male ratios of at least 2:1 for many, and about 9:1 for SLE and Sjögren’s syndrome.

对于系统性红斑狼疮，老师强调三点：一是生育期（高雌激素阶段）患病率最高；二是口服避孕药和妊娠都可能诱发或加重病情；三是绝经后疾病活动度通常下降。

For SLE the lecturer stressed three points: prevalence peaks during reproductive years when estrogen is high; oral contraceptives and pregnancy can trigger or exacerbate disease; and activity generally decreases after menopause.

多发性硬化方面
大部分患者在青春期后才出现首发症状；妊娠期特别是中晚期，复发率往往下降；而产后数月内容易出现病情加重或复发。

For multiple sclerosis, most patients develop first symptoms after puberty; relapse rates often decline during pregnancy, especially later on, but exacerbations frequently occur in the months postpartum.

老师解释，雌激素能通过其受体直接刺激B、T淋巴细胞的活化和存活，而孕期整体环境更偏免疫抑制，这些激素的动态变化与女性不同时期的发病风险密切对应。

The lecturer explained that estrogens directly stimulate B‑ and T‑lymphocyte activation and survival via their receptors, whereas the overall pregnancy milieu is more immunosuppressive; these hormonal dynamics closely match women’s varying disease risks across life stages.

六、环境危险因素与“卫生假说”
肠道菌群与蠕虫感染→Environmental risk factors and the hygiene hypothesis: microbiota and helminths
“ENVIRONMENT
HYGIENE HYPOTHESIS”圆图把家庭规模、兄弟姐妹数量、饮食、宠物、职业、消毒卫生、分娩方式、疫苗、微生物多样性等众多因素都连到中心的“hygiene hypothesis”。
The “ENVIRONMENT
HYGIENE HYPOTHESIS” diagram connects family size, number of siblings, diet, pets, occupation, sanitation, mode of delivery, vaccination, and microbial diversity to a central “hygiene hypothesis” node.
文字页首先强调肠道微生物群
西方生活方式限制了人群对多种微生物的暴露，改变儿童肠道定植模式，使免疫系统长期处于“训练不足”的状态。

The text slide then emphasizes the gut microbiome: Western lifestyles limit exposure to diverse microbes and alter colonization patterns in children, leaving the immune system “under‑trained.”

这种“微生物贫乏”会增加过敏和自身免疫病的概率，被老师概括为“缺失的免疫偏转”，意思是免疫系统没能在早期建立适当的偏向和制衡。

This “microbial poverty” increases the likelihood of allergies and autoimmune diseases, summarized by the teacher as “missing immune deviation,” meaning that early life fails to establish proper immune skewing and counterbalances.

实验上，将多种肠道细菌定植到无菌NOD‑MyD88‑缺陷小鼠，可以显著降低1型糖尿病的发生率，说明合适的微生物信号能够重塑耐受。

Experimentally, colonizing germ‑free NOD‑MyD88‑deficient mice with various gut bacteria markedly reduces type 1 diabetes incidence, indicating that appropriate microbial signals can re‑shape tolerance.

PPT接着提出蠕虫感染的“保护性”可能：流行病学上常见蠕虫感染高的地区某些自身免疫病发病率较低。

The slides then raise the potential protective role of helminth infections, as epidemiology frequently shows lower prevalence of certain autoimmune diseases where helminth burden is high.

蠕虫通过诱导T细胞向Th2极化，增强IL‑4、IL‑5、IL‑13等分泌，从而在一定程度上抑制原本在自身免疫和自炎症中被放大的Th1/Th17反应。

Helminths induce Th2 polarization of T cells with increased IL‑4, IL‑5, and IL‑13, thereby partially suppressing Th1 and Th17 responses that are typically exaggerated in autoimmune and autoinflammatory conditions.

老师提醒，这只是相关性和机制假说，目前仍在争论中，所以PPT最后特别写“hypothesis still debated”，并不意味着感染寄生虫是可取的治疗方式。

The teacher cautioned that this is based on correlations and mechanistic hypotheses that remain debated, hence the slide’s concluding note “hypothesis still debated,” and it does not mean that helminth infections are an acceptable therapy.

七、小结
危险因素如何与耐受机制汇合→Summary: how risk factors converge with tolerance mechanisms

综合这一组“Risk factors”幻灯片，老师的核心信息是：遗传背景决定免疫系统的“底盘”，性激素和表观遗传在一生中不断调节反应阈值，而环境（尤其是微生物与生活方式）塑造免疫系统早期的训练轨迹。

Taken together, these “Risk factors” slides convey that genetics define the basic “chassis” of the immune system, sex hormones and epigenetics continuously tune response thresholds throughout life, and the environment—especially microbes and lifestyle—shapes early training trajectories.

当这些内外因素以不利组合叠加时，就会使中枢和外周耐受的多重机制更容易出现漏洞，一旦再遇到感染、组织损伤等触发事件，就更可能跨越阈值发展成临床可见的自身免疫性疾病。

When these internal and external factors combine unfavorably, they render multiple central and peripheral tolerance mechanisms leakier, so that subsequent triggers such as infections or tissue damage are more likely to push the system over the threshold into clinically apparent autoimmune disease.

自身免疫病与系统性红斑狼疮概述IAutoimmune diseases and systemic lupus erythematosus overview

这一组PPT从整体上先回顾自身免疫病的类型和致病机制，然后以系统性红斑狼疮为例，详细讲解其临床特点、自身抗原来源、清除缺陷、B细胞和树突状细胞的核心作用以及肾脏等器官的组织损伤。

This group of slides first reviews the types and pathogenic mechanisms of autoimmune diseases, then uses systemic lupus erythematosus as an example to detail its clinical features, sources of auto‑antigens, clearance defects, the central roles of B cells and dendritic cells, and tissue lesions such as renal damage.

一、自身免疫病的分类
按受累器官和发病频率划分IClassification of autoimmune diseases: by affected organs and frequency

PPT先用全身人体示意图，将自身免疫病按“常见、较少见和罕见”以及按受累器官进行分类，帮助建立“哪些疾病归入自身免疫”的整体地图。

The slides start with a whole‑body diagram that classifies autoimmune diseases as common, less common, or rare, and maps them to affected organs to build a global picture of which diseases fall under autoimmunity.

常见疾病包括
系统性红斑狼疮（系统性结缔组织病）、自身免疫性甲状腺炎（AIT）、银屑病（皮肤）、1型糖尿病（胰腺）、乳糜泻（小肠）和类风湿关节炎（关节）等。

Common diseases include systemic lupus erythematosus (a systemic connective tissue disease), autoimmune thyroiditis (AIT), psoriasis (skin), type 1 diabetes (pancreas), celiac disease (intestine), and rheumatoid arthritis (joints).

较少见的有Addison病（肾上腺皮质损伤）、APS2等联合内分泌综合征；罕见的则有APS1和IPEX等多腺体、全身性免疫失调综合征。

Less common diseases include Addison’s disease (adrenal cortical failure) and APS2 polyendocrine syndromes, whereas rare conditions include APS1 and IPEX, which are multi‑gland and systemic immune dysregulation syndromes.

另一个PPT从“自身抗原分布范围”角度，把疾病分成“非器官特异性疾病”和“器官特异性疾病”两大类。

Another slide classifies diseases into “non‑organ specific disorders” and “organ‑specific disorders” according to the distribution of their auto‑antigens.

非器官特异性疾病中，自身抗原存在于多种器官，典型是结缔组织病：系统性红斑狼疮、类风湿关节炎、抗磷脂综合征、系统性硬化、干燥综合征、肌炎等，以及以血管损伤为主的血管炎（如ANCA相关小血管炎）。

In non‑organ specific disorders, auto‑antigens are present in multiple organs; typical connective tissue diseases include SLE, RA, antiphospholipid syndrome, systemic sclerosis, Sjögren’s syndrome, and myositis, plus vasculitides such as ANCA‑associated small‑vessel vasculitis.

器官特异性疾病中，自身抗体的靶抗原主要局限在单一器官或组织，如：甲状腺（TG、TPO、TSH受体）、肠道（麦胶蛋白、组织型转谷氨酰胺酶）、肝脏（LKM1、肌动蛋白等）、胰腺（GAD、IA2、胰岛β细胞抗原）、皮肤（BP180、BP230、桥粒芯糖蛋白）、胃腺细胞、周围和中枢神经系统抗原、横纹肌乙酰胆碱受体以及红细胞表面抗原等。

In organ‑specific disorders, targets of auto‑antibodies are largely confined to one organ or tissue, such as the thyroid (TG, TPO, TSH receptor), intestine (gliadin, tissue transglutaminase), liver (LKM1, actin, mitochondrial antigens), pancreas (GAD, IA2, β‑cell antigens), skin (BP180, BP230, desmogleins), gastric parietal cells, peripheral and central nervous system antigens, skeletal muscle acetylcholine receptor, and red blood cell surface antigens.

二、自身免疫病的不同致病机制→Different mechanisms of pathogenesis in autoimmune diseases

老师用一个“Autoimmune diseases – Different mechanisms of pathogenesis”的PPT总结了四条主要致病通路：自身抗体、免疫复合物、CD4 T细胞和细胞因子。

The lecturer summarized four main pathogenic pathways on a slide titled “Autoimmune diseases – Different mechanisms of pathogenesis”: autoantibodies, immune complexes, CD4 T cells, and cytokines.

第一类是自身抗体直接介导的激活或阻断作用，例如Graves病中抗TSH受体抗体具有“激动样”作用，持续刺激甲状腺；重症肌无力中抗乙酰胆碱受体抗体阻断神经‑肌肉接头；以及自身免疫性溶血性贫血中，针对红细胞抗原的溶血性抗体。

The first category is direct activation or blocking by autoantibodies, such as agonistic anti‑TSH receptor antibodies in Graves’ disease that continuously stimulate the thyroid, anti‑acetylcholine receptor antibodies in myasthenia gravis that block neuromuscular transmission, and hemolytic antibodies against red cell antigens in autoimmune hemolytic anemia.

第二类是免疫复合物介导的损伤，以系统性红斑狼疮为代表：自身抗体与可溶性自身抗原形成可沉积的免疫复合物，引发补体激活和炎症。

The second category is immune‑complex‑mediated injury, typified by SLE, where autoantibodies form depositable immune complexes with soluble self antigens, triggering complement activation and inflammation.

第三类是CD4 T细胞主导的细胞介导自身免疫，如桥本甲状腺炎中大量自身反应性CD4 T细胞浸润甲状腺，或多发性硬化中髓鞘自身反应性CD4 T细胞攻击中枢神经系统。

The third category is CD4 T‑cell‑mediated autoimmunity, such as heavy infiltration of the thyroid by autoreactive CD4 T cells in Hashimoto’s disease or attack on CNS myelin by myelin‑specific CD4 T cells in multiple sclerosis.

第四类是以细胞因子为病理“放大器”，例如TNFα在克罗恩病和类风湿关节炎中维持慢性炎症，从而解释了TNFα靶向生物制剂的疗效。

The fourth category involves cytokines as pathological amplifiers, for example TNFα maintaining chronic inflammation in Crohn’s disease and RA, which explains the efficacy of anti‑TNF biologics.

老师特别提示
这些机制在具体疾病中往往并存，只是侧重不同；在SLE里免疫复合物和I型干扰素轴尤为关键，而在多发性硬化里CD4 T细胞和细胞因子网络更突出。

The teacher noted that these mechanisms often coexist in a given disease with different emphases; in SLE immune complexes and type I interferon are especially critical, whereas in MS CD4 T cells and cytokine networks dominate.

三、系统性红斑狼疮的基本特点→Key clinical features of systemic lupus erythematosus

PPT首先把系统性红斑狼疮归类为“非器官特异性结缔组织疾病”，说明其自身抗原和病变累及多器官，而不仅仅局限于某一个组织。

The slides first classify SLE as a “non‑organ specific connective tissue disease,” indicating that its autoantigens and lesions involve multiple organs rather than a single tissue.

常见临床表现包括
发热、乏力、关节炎样关节痛、典型蝶形红斑等皮肤损害、胸膜炎以及肾功能受损等。

Common clinical manifestations include fever, fatigue, arthritis‑like joint pain, characteristic malar “butterfly” rash and other skin lesions, pleurisy, and renal dysfunction.

疾病具有高度多形性（polymorphic），在不同患者间受累器官和症状组合差异很大，因此诊断往往依赖一系列临床和免疫学标准的综合判断。

The disease is highly polymorphic; organ involvement and symptom combinations vary widely among patients, so diagnosis relies on a composite of clinical and immunologic criteria.

性别分布明显偏向女性，女男比例约9:1，好发年龄为20–30岁年轻女性，这与前面讲到的雌激素刺激B、T淋巴细胞的作用相呼应。

There is a marked female predominance with a female‑to‑male ratio of about 9:1, and onset peaks in women aged 20–30 years, echoing the earlier point that estrogens stimulate B and T lymphocytes.

四、凋亡与清除缺陷
SLE中自身抗原的主要来源→Apoptosis and clearance defects: main sources of auto‑antigens in SLE

“Systemic lupus erythematosus – Clearance deficiency”的大图展示：左侧是凋亡及其失败性清除，右上是中性粒细胞NETosis，中心是自身抗体和免疫复合物形成，底部是I型干扰素驱动的重度器官损伤。

The large “Systemic lupus erythematosus – Clearance deficiency” figure shows apoptosis and failed clearance on the left, neutrophil NETosis on the upper right, autoantibody and immune‑complex formation in the center, and type I interferon‑driven severe organ damage at the bottom.

凋亡本身是生理性细胞死亡方式，正常情况下细胞碎片会被巨噬细胞安静吞噬并清除，不引起炎症；但在SLE中，凋亡细胞过多或清除能力不足，使这些细胞进入“继发性坏死”阶段，形成所谓“继发性坏死细胞（SNECs）”。

Apoptosis is a physiological cell‑death pathway whose debris is normally quietly phagocytosed by macrophages without inflammation; in SLE, however, excessive apoptosis or impaired clearance allows cells to progress to secondary necrosis, becoming so‑called secondary necrotic cells (SNECs).

这些SNECs会释放大量核酸和核蛋白，暴露原本应被包裹在细胞核内的DNA、RNA、组蛋白和染色质复合物，为后续自身抗体和免疫复合物的形成提供了丰富的自身抗原。

SNECs release large amounts of nucleic acids and nuclear proteins, exposing DNA, RNA, histones, and chromatin complexes that should remain sequestered in the nucleus, thereby providing abundant self antigens for subsequent autoantibody and immune‑complex formation.

另一侧的NETosis表示中性粒细胞在某些刺激下释放网状结构（NETs），同样富含DNA和核蛋白；若DNase I活性不足或清除不及时，这些NETs也会成为持续存在的自身抗原库。

On the other side, NETosis depicts neutrophils releasing DNA‑rich extracellular traps that also contain nuclear proteins; if DNase I activity is insufficient or clearance is slow, these NETs likewise form a persistent self‑antigen reservoir.

后续文字PPT明确提出
“凋亡细胞是自身抗原的主要来源”，SLE中既有“凋亡过多”，也有“细胞成分清除缺陷”。

A subsequent text slide explicitly states that “apoptotic cells are the main source of auto‑antigens,” with SLE showing both excessive apoptosis and defective clearance of cellular components.

清除缺陷的分子基础包括补体C1q、C2、C4突变，导致免疫复合物和凋亡残骸清除不足；以及若小鼠敲除c‑Mer、MFG‑E8、SAP、TIM‑4等参与识别和吞噬凋亡细胞的分子，也会自发出现类似SLE的表现。

The molecular basis of clearance defects includes mutations in complement components C1q, C2, and C4 that impair removal of immune complexes and apoptotic debris, and mouse knockouts of c‑Mer, MFG‑E8, SAP, or TIM‑4—molecules involved in recognizing and engulfing apoptotic cells—that spontaneously develop SLE‑like syndromes.

另一方面，若凋亡晚期细胞膜完整性丧失，会释放多种危险信号（HMGB1、微粒体、小体等）并伴随自身抗原被化学修饰，形成所谓“新表位”，更容易被免疫系统当作非自我识别。

In addition, when late apoptotic cells lose membrane integrity they release danger signals such as HMGB1 and microparticles, and their self antigens may undergo chemical modification to form “neo‑epitopes” that the immune system more readily mistakes for non‑self.

老师在“Tolerance vs Immunity”图上说明：在耐受一侧，凋亡细胞通过“找我信号”和“吃我信号”被巨噬细胞以非炎症方式清除；而在炎症一侧，晚期凋亡细胞丧失膜完整性、释放危险信号及修饰的自身抗原，促使巨噬细胞转为激活状态，从而导致耐受崩溃。

Using the “Tolerance vs Immunity” figure, the teacher explained that on the tolerance side apoptotic cells are cleared non‑inflammatorily by macrophages via “find‑me” and “eat‑me” signals, whereas on the immunity side late apoptotic cells lose membrane integrity, release danger signals and modified self antigens, convert macrophages into activated inflammatory cells, and thereby break tolerance.

五、自身抗体与B细胞在SLE中的核心作用→Central roles of autoantibodies and B cells in SLE

在清除缺陷提供大量核抗原背景下，B细胞被持续刺激并发生高活化，这是“Systemic lupus erythematosus – Antinuclear antibodies”那几页的核心信息。

Against the background of clearance defects providing abundant nuclear antigens, B cells are chronically stimulated and become hyperactivated, which is the key message of the “Systemic lupus erythematosus – Antinuclear antibodies” slides.

首先，几乎所有SLE患者都可以检测到抗核抗体（ANA），包括针对染色质及其成分的抗体（双链DNA、单链DNA、RNA、组蛋白、核小体）以及针对可溶性核抗原的抗体（SSA、SSB、Sm、RNP等）。

First, virtually all SLE patients test positive for antinuclear antibodies, including antibodies against chromatin and its components (double‑stranded DNA, single‑stranded DNA, RNA, histones, nucleosomes) and antibodies against soluble nuclear antigens such as SSA, SSB, Sm, and RNP.

这些自身抗体与持续存在的核抗原结合，形成循环或局部免疫复合物，后续在肾小球、血管壁等处沉积，是组织损伤的直接起点。

These autoantibodies bind persistent nuclear antigens to form circulating or local immune complexes that deposit in glomeruli and vessel walls, constituting the direct starting point of tissue injury.

PPT中“Central role for B cells”部分强调：过量自身抗原驱动BCR持续受刺激，并通过增加BLyS产生提高B细胞存活阈值，从而让原本应被删除的自身反应性B细胞得以长期存活。

The “Central role for B cells” text highlights that excessive self antigens chronically stimulate BCRs and, via increased BLyS production, raise B‑cell survival thresholds, allowing autoreactive B cells that should have been deleted to persist.

同时，BCR和内吞小体内的TLR7/9可共同识别核酸抗原，实现“TLR与BCR的协同激活”，甚至在缺乏T细胞帮助时也能直接激活B细胞，形成T细胞非依赖性通路。

Meanwhile, BCRs and endosomal TLR7/9 co‑recognize nucleic acid antigens, providing “co‑engagement of TLR and BCR” that can directly activate B cells even in the absence of T‑cell help, creating T‑cell‑independent activation routes.

被激活的B细胞不仅产生大量自身抗体，还分泌多种细胞因子（IL‑4、IL‑6、IL‑10、TNFα等），进一步放大炎症和自身免疫反应。

Activated B cells not only secrete large quantities of autoantibodies but also produce cytokines such as IL‑4, IL‑6, IL‑10, and TNFα, which further amplify inflammation and autoimmunity.

六、肾脏等组织损伤
免疫复合物沉积和补体激活→Renal and other tissue lesions: immune‑complex deposition and complement activation

“Tissular lesions”几张PPT集中展示了SLE最具代表性的器官损伤——免疫复合物性肾小球肾炎及相关血管炎。

The “Tissular lesions” slides focus on SLE’s hallmark organ damage—immune‑complex glomerulonephritis and related vasculitis.

免疫复合物在肾小球毛细血管壁和系膜区沉积，激活补体级联反应，产生大量C3a、C5a等炎症介质，既直接损伤内皮细胞，又大量招募中性粒细胞和单核‑巨噬细胞。

Immune complexes deposit along glomerular capillary walls and mesangial regions, activating complement cascades that generate C3a and C5a, directly injuring endothelial cells and massively recruiting neutrophils and monocyte–macrophages.

PPT中的荧光图像显示，在受累肾小球内有条带状或颗粒状的免疫复合物沉积，提示免疫复合物和补体是肾病理的主导因素。

The fluorescent images on the slides show band‑like or granular deposits in diseased glomeruli, indicating that immune complexes and complement dominate the renal pathology.

更新的示意图进一步分成三个层面
A层面是补体激活和粒细胞脱颗粒，B层面是适应性免疫激活（巨噬细胞/DC和Th1/Th17浸润与细胞因子分泌），C层面是肾脏固有细胞的应激反应与重构（足细胞损伤、系膜细胞增生、基质沉积和新月体形成）。

A more recent diagram divides this into three levels: panel A shows complement activation and granulocyte degranulation, panel B shows adaptive immunity with macrophage/DC and Th1/Th17 infiltration and cytokine release, and panel C shows intrinsic renal responses such as podocyte injury, mesangial cell proliferation, extracellular matrix deposition, and crescent formation.

除了肾脏外，PPT还提到自身抗体可直接作用于其他靶分子，例如抗NMDA受体抗体导致中枢神经精神症状，抗红细胞和抗血小板抗体导致贫血和血小板减少。

Beyond the kidney, the slides also mention direct actions of autoantibodies on other targets, such as anti‑NMDA receptor antibodies causing neuropsychiatric manifestations and anti‑RBC or anti‑platelet antibodies leading to anemia and thrombocytopenia.

七、树突状细胞与IFNα
SLE中关键放大环路→Dendritic cells and IFNα: a key amplification loop in SLE

在“Systemic lupus erythematosus – Dendritic cells and IFNα”PPT中，老师强调浆细胞样树突状细胞（pDC）是体内IFNα的主要来源。

On the “Systemic lupus erythematosus – Dendritic cells and IFNα” slide, the lecturer stressed that plasmacytoid dendritic cells (pDCs) are the main source of IFNα in the body.

pDC通过TLR7/9识别免疫复合物中的核酸，或直接识别病毒核酸（如EBV），同时通过Fcγ受体结合含自身抗体的复合物，在雌激素环境影响下大量分泌IFNα。

pDCs recognize nucleic acids in immune complexes or viral genomes such as EBV via TLR7/9 and bind autoantibody‑containing complexes via Fcγ receptors, and under estrogenic influence they produce large amounts of IFNα.

在SLE患者中，IFNα生成明显过多，原因包括遗传易感、病毒感染以及持续存在的免疫复合物和核酸；这种高IFNα状态被认为是SLE的一个“特征性分子标志”。

In SLE patients IFNα production is markedly increased due to genetic susceptibility, viral infections, and persistent immune complexes and nucleic acids; this high IFNα milieu is considered a molecular hallmark of SLE.

IFNα能强烈激活树突状细胞、B细胞、T细胞和NK细胞，促进抗原呈递、克隆扩增、分化和自身抗体生成，形成一个自我维持的“放大环路”。

IFNα strongly activates dendritic cells, B cells, T cells, and NK cells, enhancing antigen presentation, clonal expansion, differentiation, and autoantibody production, thereby creating a self‑sustaining amplification loop.

最后一张“to sum up”图综合展示了这一环路：UV和病毒等触发凋亡与NETosis，释放核抗原；这些核抗原与自身抗体形成免疫复合物，激活pDC产生IFNα；IFNα再激活B细胞、T细胞和NK细胞，伴随BLyS上调和补体参与，共同驱动炎症和组织损伤。

The final “to sum up” diagram synthesizes this loop: UV light and viruses trigger apoptosis and NETosis, releasing nuclear antigens; these antigens form immune complexes with autoantibodies that activate pDCs to produce IFNα, and IFNα in turn activates B cells, T cells, and NK cells, with BLyS upregulation and complement participation to drive inflammation and tissue damage.

八、SLE中特异性自身抗原来源与诱因→Specific sources of auto‑antigens and triggers in SLE

“Sources of auto‑Ag / Other factors influencing lupus”PPT列出了几类特别与SLE相关的自身抗原来源和环境因素。

The “Sources of auto‑Ag / Other factors influencing lupus” slide lists several SLE‑specific sources of auto‑antigens and environmental factors.

紫外线照射会损伤角质形成细胞，引起皮肤细胞大批凋亡，从而显著增加凋亡细胞负荷，是典型的皮肤诱发因素。

Ultraviolet radiation damages keratinocytes and induces massive apoptosis of skin cells, markedly increasing apoptotic burden and serving as a classic cutaneous trigger.

EB病毒感染通过其核抗原EBNA‑1与自身抗原SSA/Sm之间的分子模拟，既提供外源危险信号，又通过交叉反应促进产生针对核抗原的自身抗体。

Epstein–Barr virus infection contributes through molecular mimicry between its nuclear antigen EBNA‑1 and self antigens such as SSA/Sm, providing both exogenous danger signals and cross‑reactive stimulation for anti‑nuclear autoantibodies.

“其他影响因素”中，雌激素通过ERα直接刺激B、T淋巴细胞活化，是解释女性高发的重要一环；某些硅化物暴露可导致多克隆免疫激活；特定药物如肼屈嗪、普鲁卡因胺通过抑制DNA甲基化，可诱导药物性狼疮。

Among “other factors,” estrogens stimulate B and T lymphocytes via ERα and help explain female predominance; exposure to some forms of silicon can cause polyclonal immune activation; and drugs such as hydralazine and procainamide inhibit DNA methylation and can induce drug‑induced lupus.

九、小结
系统性红斑狼疮的病理生理学主线→Summary: main pathophysiological axis of SLE

综合这些PPT和讲解，系统性红斑狼疮可以概括为：在遗传和激素易感背景下，凋亡和NETosis产生大量核抗原，而补体和吞噬系统的清除缺陷使这些抗原长期滞留。

Taken together, SLE can be summarized as follows: on a background of genetic and hormonal susceptibility, apoptosis and NETosis generate large amounts of nuclear antigens, and defects in complement and phagocytic clearance allow these antigens to persist.

B细胞在核抗原和TLR信号双重刺激下高度活化，产生广谱抗核自身抗体；这些抗体与抗原形成免疫复合物，在肾小球和血管壁等处沉积并激活补体，引发炎症和器官损伤。

B cells become highly activated under dual stimulation from nuclear antigens and TLR signals, producing broad‑spectrum antinuclear autoantibodies; these form immune complexes that deposit in glomeruli and vessel walls, activate complement, and trigger inflammation and organ damage.

浆细胞样树突状细胞通过识别含核酸的免疫复合物大量分泌IFNα，驱动并放大DC、B、T、NK等多种细胞的激活，形成正反馈环路，使疾病从一次触发演变为长期、系统性的自身免疫性结缔组织病。

Plasmacytoid dendritic cells sense nucleic acid‑containing immune complexes and secrete large amounts of IFNα, driving and amplifying activation of DCs, B cells, T cells, and NK cells, creating a positive‑feedback loop that turns an initial trigger into a chronic systemic autoimmune connective tissue disease.

092025-12-09（第 2 讲）

同种异基因器官与造血干细胞移植的免疫学 Immunology of Allogeneic Organ Transplantation and Allogeneic Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation (HSCT)

一、考试与课程安排说明 Exam format and course organization

• 期末考试将采用传统笔试形式，包括每一节课各两道题，其中既有开放性问答题，也有选择题，用来检查对每节课核心知识点的理解和记忆。 The final evaluation will be a classical written exam, with two questions per lesson, including open questions and multiple-choice questions, to assess understanding and recall of each lecture’s key points.

• 考题主要围绕课件幻灯片上的“总体性、基础性信息”，不会深挖复杂的研究细节，但需要熟悉每张关键幻灯片上的主线内容。 The questions will mainly target the general, fundamental information on the slides rather than complex research details, but you need to know the main messages of each important slide.

• 老师说明之后几周自己事务较多，可能对邮件回复不够及时，现在已恢复正常，如果自学课件或PDF资料时有疑问，可以发邮件，一般会在同日或两日内回复。 The lecturer mentioned being busy in the previous weeks and not always replying quickly to emails, but now responses should be faster; if you have questions while studying the slides or PDFs, you can email and usually get an answer within one or two days.

• 本次课并非本模块最后一讲，下周还会有两节课：一节是自身免疫病的发病机制，一节是免疫缺陷疾病的内容，本节课则主要聚焦同种异基因移植的免疫学。 This session is not the last one of the module; next week there will be two additional lectures on the pathophysiology of autoimmune diseases and on immunodeficiencies, while the present lecture focuses mainly on the immunology of allogeneic transplantation.

二、本课总体学习目标 Overall learning objectives of this lecture

• 理解并能描述同种异基因免疫反应的发生机制，即机体如何识别并攻击来自他人的细胞或组织。 Understand and be able to describe the mechanisms of allogeneic immune responses, that is, how the body recognizes and attacks cells or tissues coming from another individual.

• 掌握实体器官移植排斥反应的免疫学基础，包括超急性排斥、急性排斥和慢性排斥三种类型。 Grasp the immunological basis of rejection reactions in solid organ transplantation, including hyperacute, acute, and chronic rejection.

• 识别参与移植物排斥的主要细胞毒机制：CD8阳性T细胞直接细胞毒、抗体依赖的细胞介导细胞毒　、以及抗体介导的补体依赖性细胞毒。 Identify the principal cytotoxic mechanisms involved in graft rejection: direct cell cytotoxicity mediated by CD8 positive T lymphocytes, antibody-dependent cell-mediated cytotoxicity (ADCC), and antibody-mediated complement-dependent cytotoxicity.
答案 ACD
