• 知道免疫抑制治疗的关键分子靶点，为后续学习各类免疫抑制药物机制打下基础。 Recognize the main molecular targets of immunosuppressive therapies, preparing for later study of how different immunosuppressive drugs work.

• 理解造血干细胞移植中移植物抗宿主病（GVHD）的免疫病理学机制，与实体器官移植中的“宿主抗移植物”反应加以对比。 Understand the immunopathology underlying graft-versus-host disease (GVHD) in hematopoietic stem cell transplantation, and contrast it with the host-versus-graft reactions in solid organ transplantation.

三、课程结构与内容框架 Structure and main topics of the lecture

• 课程首先介绍法国生物医学署这一国家级机构及其在移植体系中的调控作用。 The lecture first introduces the French Agency of Biomedicine and its regulatory role in the national transplant system.

• 随后给出一系列关键术语定义
移植、同基因（自体、同卵双生）移植物、同种异基因移植物、异种移植物以及同种异基因抗原。 It then provides key definitions: transplantation, autografts and syngeneic grafts, allografts, xenografts, and alloantigens.

• 接下来讲解同种异基因反应产生的原因，即供受者之间的遗传差异如何引发正常但在临床上“不受欢迎”的免疫排斥。 Next, it explains why allogeneic immune responses occur, that is, how genetic differences between donor and recipient elicit a normal, but clinically unwanted, rejection response.

• 课程随后引入免疫抑制治疗的概念，说明其如何阻断这种免疫攻击，但也带来感染、肿瘤和毒性等不良反应。 The lecture then introduces the concept of immunosuppressive therapy, describing how it blocks these immune attacks but also causes side effects such as infections, malignancies, and drug toxicities.

• 最后开始系统介绍三大类同种异基因抗原：ABO血型抗原、HLA分子与次要组织相容性抗原，为后续排斥反应和免疫学检测做铺垫。 Finally, it begins a systematic presentation of the three major groups of alloantigens: ABO blood group antigens, HLA molecules, and minor histocompatibility antigens, which lay the foundation for understanding rejection and pre‑transplant immunological testing.

四、法国生物医学署及其在移植中的作用 French Agency of Biomedicine and its role in transplantation

• 法国生物医学署是负责人体相关“要素”（器官、组织、细胞等）医学、科学及伦理管理的国家机构。 The French Agency of Biomedicine is the national body in charge of medical, scientific, and ethical regulation of human body elements such as organs, tissues, and cells.

• 该机构负责管理全国器官、组织和细胞供体及受者的登记系统，维护相应数据库。 It manages national registries for organ, tissue, and cell donors and patients, maintaining the related databases.

• 它制定器官分配和移植优先顺序的规则，确保分配过程公平、透明并符合医学与伦理标准。 It sets the rules for transplant priorities and organ allocation, ensuring that distribution is fair, transparent, and aligned with medical and ethical standards.

• 生物医学署积极推动器官、组织及骨捐献的宣传和发展，以增加供体数量和移植机会。 The agency actively promotes organ, tissue, and bone donation in order to increase the donor pool and transplant opportunities.

• 它还负责除血液（由EFS管理）之外的各类人体来源材料的生物安全控制，确保采集、保存与使用过程的安全。 It also oversees biosafety control for human body–derived materials other than blood, which is managed by another body, ensuring safety in collection, storage, and use.

• 该机构监管医学辅助生殖、产前诊断，并批准胚胎与干细胞研究项目，体现其在生命医学各领域的综合监管职能。 The agency regulates medically assisted procreation and prenatal diagnosis, and authorizes embryonic and stem cell research projects, reflecting its broad oversight across biomedical fields.

• 在移植领域，它不仅监管法规，还持续监测全国移植活动情况并发布每年的器官移植统计数据。 In the field of transplantation, it not only regulates the rules but also monitors transplant activity nationwide and publishes annual statistics on organ transplantation.

五、法国器官移植活动概况 Organ transplantation activity in France

• 统计数据显示，法国每年进行的器官移植以肾移植为主，其次是肝移植，心脏、心肺、肺、胰腺和肠移植的例数相对较少。 According to the statistics presented, kidney transplantation accounts for most organ transplants in France, followed by liver transplantation, while heart, heart–lung, lung, pancreas, and intestine transplants are less frequent.

• 去年法国共完成约六千多例器官移植，其中肾移植约三千七百余例，是远远最常见的移植类型。 Last year there were a little over 6,000 organ transplants in France, with around 3,700 of them being kidney transplants, making kidney transplantation by far the most common type.

• 数据中用括号标出的数字代表活体供者的移植例数，例如肾移植中有数百例来自活体供肾，整体活体捐献数量仍然不算高。 The numbers shown in brackets correspond to transplants from living donors; for example, several hundred kidney transplants were performed using living donors, but overall the absolute number of living donations remains modest.

• 老师强调，与美国等国家相比，法国活体器官捐献的比例明显较低，而美国有超过一半的某些器官移植（如肾移植）来自活体供者，这在一定程度上与社会和文化特征有关。 The lecturer emphasized that, compared with countries like the United States where more than half of some organ transplants such as kidneys come from living donors, the proportion of living donation in France is significantly lower, partly due to social and cultural factors.

• 在法国，允许活体捐献的器官主要是成对或可部分切除的器官，如肾脏、肝脏部分及肺叶等，以保证供者自身功能安全。 In France, organs that can be donated by living donors are mainly paired or partially resectable organs such as kidneys, parts of the liver, or lung lobes, ensuring that the donor’s own function remains adequate.

• 法国法规严格限定活体供者的范围，通常要求是患者的近亲属（父母、兄弟姐妹）或与受者稳定共同生活至少三年的伴侣，以防止器官买卖和非法中介。 French regulations strictly limit who may serve as a living donor, usually close relatives such as parents or siblings, or a partner living with the recipient for at least three years, in order to prevent organ trafficking and unethical brokerage.

• 相关规则的根本目的在于保护供受者双方的权益，确保捐献完全出于知情、自愿和无经济利益驱动。 These rules ultimately aim to protect both donor and recipient, ensuring that donation is fully informed, voluntary, and not driven by financial incentives.

六、关键术语与移植类型 Key definitions and types of grafts

• “移植”是指将细胞、组织或器官从身体的一处转移到另一处，通常是在不同个体之间完成。 Transplantation is defined as transferring cells, tissues, or organs from one site to another, typically between different individuals.

• “自体移植”（autograft）指同一个人不同部位之间的移植，例如取自下肢血管移植到心脏冠状动脉，供者与受者为同一人。 An autograft refers to a graft within the same individual, such as using a blood vessel from the leg to bypass a coronary artery, where donor and recipient are the same person.

• “同基因移植”（isograft 或 syngeneic graft）是指在基因完全相同的个体之间的移植，如同卵双生子之间，此时几乎不存在抗原差异。 An isograft or syngeneic graft is performed between genetically identical individuals, such as identical twins, in which there are essentially no antigenic differences.

• “同种异基因移植”（allograft）是本课重点，指在同一物种内、但基因不同的个体之间进行的组织或器官移植，例如大多数人类器官移植。 An allograft, the main focus of this lecture, is transplantation of tissues between genetically different individuals of the same species, which corresponds to most human organ transplants.

• “同种异基因抗原”（alloantigen）指在受者看来属于“非自身”的任何抗原，只要它能在移植情境中触发同种异基因免疫反应。 An alloantigen is any non‑self antigen from the donor that can trigger an allogeneic immune response in the recipient.

• “异种移植”（xenograft）指不同物种之间的移植，例如猪器官移植到人，这目前尚未成为常规临床实践，但已有一些探索性临床试验。 A xenograft is a transplant between different species, such as a pig organ transplanted into a human; this is not yet standard clinical practice, but exploratory clinical trials and experimental grafts have already begun.

问七、同种异基因移植为何会被排斥？ Why are allografts rejected?
答

• 以同种异基因肾移植为例，供者和受者在遗传上存在差异，因此供者器官中的许多蛋白质与受者自身蛋白的氨基酸序列不同。 Taking a renal allogeneic transplant as an example, the donor and recipient are genetically different, so many proteins in the donor organ have amino acid sequences distinct from those of the recipient’s own proteins.

• 这些遗传多态性导致供者器官表面表达的“同种异基因抗原”被受者功能正常的免疫系统识别为“非己”，从而激活免疫反应。 These genetic polymorphisms create alloantigens on the donor organ that are recognized by the recipient’s functional immune system as non‑self, thereby activating an immune response.

• 激活后的免疫系统通过T细胞、抗体及补体等多种效应机制，针对供者抗原发动攻击，最终可导致移植物功能受损甚至完全丧失，即移植排斥。 Once activated, the immune system employs effectors such as T cells, antibodies, and complement to attack the donor antigens, which can damage or destroy the graft and result in transplant rejection.

• 从免疫学角度看，这其实是机体对“非自身”的一种正常保护性反应，但在临床上却是不希望发生的，因为目标器官本身是挽救生命或改善生活质量的治疗手段。 From an immunological point of view, this is a normal protective response against non‑self, but clinically it is undesirable because the transplanted organ is intended to save life or improve quality of life.

八、免疫抑制治疗在同种异基因移植中的作用 Role of immunosuppressive therapy in allogeneic transplantation

• 为了避免移植物被上述正常免疫反应迅速破坏，临床上必须使用免疫抑制药物来削弱或阻断针对供者抗原的免疫应答。 To prevent the graft from being rapidly destroyed by these normal immune responses, clinicians must use immunosuppressive drugs to dampen or block immune reactions against donor antigens.

• 在肾等同种异基因器官移植中，免疫抑制治疗的目标是维持一个相对“安静”的免疫环境，使移植物得以长期存活并维持功能。 In renal and other allogeneic organ transplants, the goal of immunosuppressive therapy is to create a relatively quiescent immune environment that allows long‑term graft survival and function.

• 然而，免疫抑制并非没有代价：持续抑制免疫系统会增加感染的风险，也会提高恶性肿瘤发生率，并带来药物自身的器官毒性等不良反应。 However, immunosuppression is not without cost: sustained suppression of the immune system increases susceptibility to infections, raises the risk of malignancies, and introduces drug‑related organ toxicities and other adverse effects.

• 因此，临床实践需要在“避免排斥”和“避免过度免疫抑制”之间寻找平衡，这也是后续课程中讨论免疫抑制药物靶点和策略的核心出发点。 Therefore, clinical practice must balance preventing rejection with avoiding excessive immunosuppression, which is the core rationale for later discussions on the targets and strategies of immunosuppressive drugs.

九、三大类同种异基因抗原 Three major groups of alloantigens

1
ABO血型抗原 ABO blood group antigens

• ABO抗原不仅表达在红细胞表面，还广泛存在于血管内皮等多种细胞上，因此在固体器官移植，尤其是肾移植中具有重要意义。 ABO antigens are expressed not only on red blood cells but also widely on various cells such as vascular endothelial cells, which makes them highly relevant in solid organ transplantation, particularly in kidney grafts.

• 不表达某种ABO抗原的个体会天然产生针对该抗原的IgM类天然抗体（如A型个体产生抗B，B型个体产生抗A），这在输血学中已非常熟知。 Individuals who do not express a given ABO antigen naturally produce IgM antibodies against that antigen, such as anti‑B in group A and anti‑A in group B individuals, a principle well known from transfusion medicine.

• 如果在器官移植中忽视ABO相容性，将含有不相容ABO抗原的器官植入受者体内，受者体内预存的天然抗体会立刻结合供者血管内皮，引发补体激活和血栓形成，导致超急性排斥。 If ABO compatibility is ignored in organ transplantation and an organ with incompatible ABO antigens is implanted, the recipient’s pre‑existing natural antibodies immediately bind donor vascular endothelium, activate complement and thrombosis, and cause hyperacute rejection.

2
HLA分子（主要组织相容性复合体，MHC） HLA molecules (major histocompatibility complex, MHC)

• HLA分子是最重要的一类同种异基因抗原，在人类中极度多态，不同个体之间HLA型差异巨大。 HLA molecules are the most important group of alloantigens and are extremely polymorphic in humans, leading to substantial HLA differences between individuals.

• HLA的生理功能是将来源于细胞内外的多肽片段呈递给T细胞受体，从而启动或调节T细胞免疫应答。 Physiologically, HLA molecules present peptides from intra‑ and extracellular sources to T‑cell receptors, thereby initiating or modulating T‑cell immune responses.

• 因为HLA高度多态，供受者间的HLA不相容会触发非常强烈的T细胞反应，是多数同种异基因移植排斥反应的主要驱动力。 Due to their high polymorphism, HLA mismatches between donor and recipient elicit very strong T‑cell responses and are the main drivers of most allogeneic transplant rejections.

• 课件中还提到次要组织相容性抗原（mHAgs），它们的多态性也可引起排斥，但通常效应弱于HLA，是在HLA基本匹配时仍可导致免疫反应的另一层来源。 The slides also mention minor histocompatibility antigens (mHAgs), whose polymorphisms can also provoke rejection, usually with weaker effects than HLA, and they represent an additional source of immune responses even when HLA matching is relatively good.

十、小结 Summary of this section

• 本节内容从课程目标与考试要求出发，引入同种异基因器官与造血干细胞移植这一主题，并强调其核心是“机体对非自身组织的免疫识别与控制”。 This section began with the course objectives and exam format, introduced the theme of allogeneic organ and hematopoietic stem cell transplantation, and emphasized that the core issue is how the body recognizes and controls immune responses to non‑self tissues.

• 通过法国生物医学署和全国移植数据的例子，展示了同种异基因移植在现实医疗体系中的规模、监管方式及伦理约束。 Using the French Agency of Biomedicine and national transplant statistics as examples, it illustrated the scale of allogeneic transplantation in real healthcare systems, together with its regulatory and ethical framework.

• 然后梳理了移植相关的关键术语，区分自体、同基因、同种异基因与异种移植，并提出“同种异基因抗原”这一后续所有机制分析的基本概念。 It then clarified transplantation‑related terminology, distinguishing autografts, syngeneic grafts, allografts, and xenografts, and introduced alloantigens as the fundamental concept for all subsequent mechanistic discussions.

• 最后解释了同种异基因移植被排斥的原因，以及免疫抑制治疗在阻断正常免疫反应与维持移植物之间所扮演的双刃剑角色，并初步概括了三大类同种异基因抗原中ABO与HLA的关键作用。 Finally, it explained why allografts are rejected, described immunosuppressive therapy as a double‑edged sword that both protects the graft and increases risks, and provided an initial overview of the crucial roles of ABO and HLA among the three major groups of alloantigens.

同种异基因免疫反应
同种异基因抗原类型与T/B细胞激活机制 Allogeneic immune response: types of alloantigens and activation of T/B lymphocytes

一、三类同种异基因抗原及其不相容 Three groups of alloantigens and their incompatibility

三类主要同种异基因抗原包括
ABO血型抗原、HLA分子以及次要组织相容性抗原。 The three main groups of alloantigens are ABO blood group antigens, HLA molecules, and minor histocompatibility antigens.

ABO血型抗原不仅存在于红细胞，也广泛表达在血管内皮等多种细胞上，因此在固体器官移植中非常关键。 ABO blood antigens are expressed not only on red blood cells but also broadly on vascular endothelial and other cell types, making them crucial in solid organ transplantation.

个体如果不表达某种ABO抗原，就会天然产生对应的IgM抗体（如A型个体有抗B，B型个体有抗A）；若肾等器官ABO不相容，这些天然抗体会立即结合移植物血管内皮，激活补体并形成血栓，导致超急性排斥。 Individuals lacking a given ABO antigen naturally produce IgM antibodies against it (for example, group A individuals have anti‑B, group B individuals have anti‑A); if a kidney or other organ is ABO‑incompatible, these natural antibodies immediately bind graft endothelium, activate complement and cause thrombosis, leading to hyperacute rejection.

HLA分子（MHC）极度多态，供受者间HLA不相容是同种异基因移植中最强的免疫反应驱动力，因此被称为“主要组织相容性抗原”。 HLA molecules (MHC) are highly polymorphic, and HLA mismatches between donor and recipient are the main drivers of powerful immune responses in allogeneic transplantation, hence they are called “major histocompatibility antigens.”

课件示意目前已经鉴定出超过二万四千种HLA等位基因，图中分别列出了不同HLA位点（如DP、DQ、DR、B、C、A）对应的蛋白等位基因数量，说明HLA多态性极其丰富。 The slide indicates that more than 24,000 HLA alleles have been identified, listing the numbers of protein alleles for different loci (such as DP, DQ, DR, B, C, A), which illustrates the extreme polymorphism of HLA.

次要组织相容性抗原（minor histocompatibility antigens，mHAgs）不是HLA本身，而是其他供者蛋白中与受者存在氨基酸序列差异的部分；虽然每个分子诱导的反应较弱，但在HLA已基本匹配时，它们仍可驱动排斥反应。 Minor histocompatibility antigens (mHAgs) are not HLA molecules themselves but are segments of other donor proteins whose amino acid sequences differ from the recipient’s; although each individual response is weaker, they can still drive rejection when HLA is otherwise well matched.

二、次要组织相容性抗原的来源与机制 Minor histocompatibility antigens: origin and mechanism

mHAg来源于除HLA以外的供者蛋白，这些蛋白因为正常的遗传多态性而在供受者之间存在一个或多个氨基酸差异。 mHAgs arise from donor proteins other than HLA that differ from the recipient’s proteins by one or more amino acids due to normal genetic polymorphism.

受者对自身蛋白衍生的多肽已经建立了免疫耐受，因此对“自体多肽”不会产生T细胞应答。 The recipient’s immune system is tolerant to peptides derived from self proteins, so it does not mount T‑cell responses against these self peptides.

当供者蛋白中某个氨基酸发生多态性改变时，移植物细胞会在自身HLA分子上呈递一个稍有不同的多肽片段，这个“变异多肽”可能被部分受者TCR高亲和力识别，从而被视为“外源抗原”。 When a donor protein has a polymorphic amino acid substitution, graft cells present a slightly altered peptide on their HLA molecules, and this “variant peptide” may bind strongly to some recipient TCRs, being perceived as a foreign antigen.

由于受者胸腺中从未用这种变异多肽进行负选择，机体缺乏对此的耐受，结果是出现针对mHAg的T细胞克隆扩增与效应应答，进而参与移植物排斥。 Because this variant peptide was not involved in negative selection in the recipient’s thymus, the immune system lacks tolerance to it; T‑cell clones specific for the mHAg can therefore expand and exert effector functions, contributing to graft rejection.

教师特别强调，mHAg可以来自许多不同基因，我们尚未完全了解全部谱系；典型例子是性连锁多态，如Y染色体编码蛋白，因此“男性供者→女性受者”的移植中，Y相关蛋白常作为经典的次要组织相容性抗原。 The lecturer highlighted that mHAgs can originate from many different genes, and their full spectrum is still not completely known; a classic example is sex‑linked polymorphism such as Y‑chromosome encoded proteins, so in “male donor → female recipient” transplants, Y‑related proteins often behave as typical minor histocompatibility antigens.

三、同种异基因免疫反应的启动
从组织损伤到DC激活 Initiation of the allogeneic immune response: from tissue damage to dendritic cell activation

移植的同种异基因器官在取出、保存和再灌注过程中经历缺血再灌注等损伤，首先激活固有免疫反应，引起局部炎症。 The transplanted allogeneic organ undergoes ischemia‑reperfusion and other injuries during procurement, storage, and reperfusion, which first activate the innate immune system and induce local inflammation.

这些损伤相关信号和炎症环境促使供者或受者来源的树突状细胞成熟并被激活，它们摄取供者抗原（包括HLA和mHAg），上调共刺激分子。 Damage‑associated signals and the inflammatory milieu promote maturation and activation of donor‑ or recipient‑derived dendritic cells, which capture donor antigens (including HLA and mHAgs) and up‑regulate costimulatory molecules.

活化的树突状细胞携带这些抗原迁移到二级淋巴器官（如淋巴结、脾脏），在那里启动抗原特异性的T和B淋巴细胞反应。 Activated dendritic cells carrying these antigens then migrate to secondary lymphoid organs (such as lymph nodes and spleen), where they initiate antigen‑specific responses of T and B lymphocytes.

四、初始T细胞的同种异基因激活步骤与药物靶点 Activation steps of naïve T cells in allogeneic responses and drug targets

课件给出了“初始T淋巴细胞激活的经典五步”，这同样适用于许多效应和记忆T细胞： The slides present the “five classic steps” in activation of naïve T lymphocytes, which are also partly valid for many effector and memory T cells:

TCR激活
特异性识别HLA/抗原复合物（即HLA分子上呈递的供者抗原肽），通过TCR/CD3复合体传递“信号1”。 TCR activation: specific recognition of an HLA/antigen complex (a donor peptide presented on an HLA molecule) delivers “signal 1” via the TCR/CD3 complex.
2
共刺激（costimulation）：如CD28与APC表面的CD80/CD86结合、CD40‑CD154相互作用，为T细胞提供“信号2”，没有共刺激则T细胞呈无反应或耐受。 Costimulation: interactions such as CD28 with CD80/CD86 on APCs and CD40–CD154 provide “signal 2”; without costimulation, T cells become anergic or tolerant.
细胞因子信号
以IL‑2为核心，T细胞通过IL‑2受体α链CD25和JAK3等通路获得“信号3”，驱动增殖与分化。 Cytokine signaling: centered on IL‑2, T cells receive “signal 3” through the IL‑2 receptor α‑chain CD25 and pathways involving JAK3, driving proliferation and differentiation.
克隆扩增与分化
在mTOR等代谢/细胞周期通路支持下，T细胞发生大量克隆扩增并分化为效应Th细胞或细胞毒性T细胞。 Clonal expansion and differentiation: supported by mTOR and cell‑cycle pathways, T cells undergo extensive clonal expansion and differentiate into effector Th cells or cytotoxic T cells.
迁移与效应功能
效应T细胞在趋化因子受体和黏附分子的引导下迁移到炎症部位（移植物），执行直接或间接的细胞毒作用。 Migration and effector function: guided by chemokine receptors and adhesion molecules, effector T cells migrate to the inflammatory site (the graft) and exert direct or indirect cytotoxic effects.

下方信号通路示意图用粉色框标出多个关键分子（如TCR/CD3、钙调神经磷酸酶calcineurin、CD28、CD154、CD25、JAK3、mTOR、细胞周期蛋白、S1P受体、CD52等），提示这些都是临床免疫抑制药物的重要靶点。 The signaling diagram below highlights in pink several key molecules (such as TCR/CD3, calcineurin, CD28, CD154, CD25, JAK3, mTOR, cell‑cycle regulators, the S1P receptor, and CD52), indicating that these are important targets of clinical immunosuppressive drugs.

教师说明，本课程仅要求理解这些“靶点存在于T细胞激活路径上”，具体每种药物的机制将在后续专门的免疫抑制治疗课程中详细讲解。 The lecturer noted that in this session you only need to understand that these targets lie along T‑cell activation pathways, while detailed mechanisms of each drug will be covered in a separate lecture on immunosuppressive therapy.

五、初始B细胞的同种异基因激活与供者特异性抗体 Activation of naïve B cells and generation of donor‑specific antibodies

B细胞的同种异基因应答是典型的“T依赖性应答”，课件同样列出五个关键步骤： The B‑cell allogeneic response is a typical “T‑dependent response,” and the slides again list five key steps:

BCR激活
初始B细胞通过其BCR直接识别并结合特异性供者抗原（如供者HLA或其他同种异基因抗原）。 BCR activation: naïve B cells directly recognize and bind specific donor antigens (such as donor HLA or other alloantigens) through their B‑cell receptors (BCRs).
由抗原特异性效应Th细胞提供共刺激
B细胞呈递相同抗原肽给CD4⁺Th细胞，获得CD40‑CD40L等信号作为共刺激。 Costimulation from antigen‑specific effector Th cells: B cells present the same antigenic peptides to CD4⁺ Th cells and receive signals such as CD40–CD40L as costimulation.
3
Th细胞分泌的细胞因子（如IL‑4、IL‑21等）进一步促进B细胞活化、同种型转换和亲和力成熟。 Cytokines secreted by Th cells (such as IL‑4 and IL‑21) further promote B‑cell activation, isotype switching, and affinity maturation.
4
B细胞克隆扩增与分化为浆细胞和记忆B细胞，形成长期的免疫记忆。 B cells then undergo clonal expansion and differentiate into plasma cells and memory B cells, establishing long‑term immune memory.
5
浆细胞迁移至骨髓等部位，持续产生针对供者抗原的高亲和力IgG，即“供者特异性抗体”（donor‑specific antibodies，DSA）。 Plasma cells migrate to bone marrow and other sites, where they continuously produce high‑affinity IgG against donor antigens, known as donor‑specific antibodies (DSA).
右侧示意图显示，这些IgG可以激活补体系统，也可通过抗体依赖的细胞介导细胞毒　招募NK细胞等效应细胞共同破坏移植物。 The diagram on the right shows that these IgG antibodies can activate the complement system and mediate antibody‑dependent cell‑mediated cytotoxicity (ADCC), recruiting NK cells and other effector cells to damage the graft.
答案 ACD
六、效应阶段
同种异基因免疫反应如何攻击移植物 Effector phase: how allogeneic immune responses damage the graft

“Allogeneic immune response in action”一页将T细胞和抗体的效应整合在一起，左侧肾移植物周围出现大量效应Th细胞、细胞毒性T淋巴细胞（CTL）及同种异基因抗体，伴随明显炎症。 The “Allogeneic immune response in action” slide integrates T‑cell and antibody effects, depicting many effector Th cells, cytotoxic T lymphocytes (CTLs), and alloantibodies around the transplanted kidney, together with marked inflammation.

上方框图强调“内皮和基质损伤”
供者血管内皮和间质细胞表面表达的HLA被CTL识别后，CTL直接介导细胞毒作用；Th1细胞分泌IFN‑γ和IL‑2等细胞因子，既维持CTL的活性，也招募并激活NK细胞，共同放大组织损伤。 The upper box focuses on “endothelial and stromal lesions”: donor vascular endothelial and stromal cells presenting HLA are recognized by CTLs, which directly mediate cytotoxicity; Th1 cells secrete cytokines such as IFN‑γ and IL‑2 that sustain CTL activity and recruit/activate NK cells, amplifying tissue damage.
下方框图强调“内皮损伤”
供者内皮表面的HLA及非HLA抗原与受者供者特异性抗体结合，触发C1q结合和补体级联反应，在内皮细胞表面沉积C4d等补体片段。 The lower box emphasizes “endothelial lesions”: donor endothelial HLA and non‑HLA antigens bind recipient donor‑specific antibodies, triggering C1q binding and complement cascades, with deposition of complement split products such as C4d on endothelial surfaces.

被补体或抗体“标记”的内皮随后成为单核细胞、巨噬细胞、中性粒细胞和NK细胞等的攻击靶点，通过抗体依赖的细胞介导细胞毒进一步破坏血管壁和移植物结构。 Endothelium tagged by complement or antibodies then becomes a target for monocytes, macrophages, neutrophils, and NK cells, which carry out antibody‑dependent cell‑mediated cytotoxicity and further damage vessel walls and graft structures.

总的来说，适应性免疫反应所驱动的这些细胞毒和炎症过程，使原本由手术和缺血造成的组织损伤持续加重，最终表现为急性或慢性排斥。 Overall, these cytotoxic and inflammatory processes driven by adaptive immunity exacerbate the initial surgical and ischemic injuries, ultimately manifesting as acute or chronic rejection.

七、免疫抑制治疗的多重靶点概览 Overview of multiple targets for immunosuppressive treatments

“Multiple targets for immunosuppressor treatments”一页把前面提到的T/B细胞激活通路与具体药物联系起来，左图以T细胞为中心，右图以B细胞和浆细胞为中心。 The “Multiple targets for immunosuppressor treatments” slide links the previously described T‑ and B‑cell activation pathways to specific drugs, with the left panel centered on T cells and the right on B cells and plasma cells.

T细胞侧的主要药物靶点包括
阻断TCR/CD3信号的抗CD3单抗、抑制钙调神经磷酸酶的环孢素和他克莫司、干预共刺激的CTLA‑4‑Ig或抗CD154抗体、阻断IL‑2/IL‑2R通路的抗CD25抗体、抑制JAK3和mTOR信号的小分子药物，以及抑制细胞周期和核苷酸合成的传统免疫抑制剂（如硫唑嘌呤、MPA等）。 On the T‑cell side, major drug targets include anti‑CD3 monoclonal antibodies that block TCR/CD3 signaling, calcineurin inhibitors such as cyclosporine and tacrolimus, CTLA‑4‑Ig or anti‑CD154 antibodies that interfere with costimulation, anti‑CD25 antibodies that block the IL‑2/IL‑2R pathway, small‑molecule inhibitors of JAK3 and mTOR, and classical immunosuppressants such as azathioprine and MPA that inhibit the cell cycle and nucleotide synthesis.

图中还标出了针对CD52和S1P受体的药物，它们通过耗竭淋巴细胞或阻止淋巴细胞再循环来降低整体T细胞活性。 The figure also highlights drugs targeting CD52 and the S1P receptor, which reduce overall T‑cell activity by depleting lymphocytes or blocking their recirculation.

B细胞侧，示意图提示可通过抗CD20单抗（如利妥昔单抗）耗竭成熟B细胞，通过甲氨蝶呤等药物抑制增殖，通过抗CD38或抗CD319单抗、蛋白酶体抑制剂硼替佐米等直接作用于浆细胞，以减少同种异基因抗体的产生。 On the B‑cell side, the diagram suggests several strategies: depleting mature B cells with anti‑CD20 antibodies such as rituximab, inhibiting proliferation with drugs like methotrexate, and directly targeting plasma cells with anti‑CD38 or anti‑CD319 antibodies and proteasome inhibitors such as bortezomib, thereby reducing alloantibody production.

教师再次强调，你目前需要掌握的是“免疫抑制药物几乎可以在T/B细胞激活的每一个关键环节设立靶点”，具体药名和使用方案属于后续课程内容。 The lecturer again emphasized that at this stage you mainly need to understand that “immunosuppressive drugs can target almost every key step in T‑ and B‑cell activation,” while the exact drug names and regimens will be detailed in later courses.

八、本组PPT小结 Local summary for this set of slides

同种异基因移植中的免疫学不相容主要由三类抗原驱动：ABO血型、HLA和次要组织相容性抗原，其中HLA不相容最强，mHAg则在HLA匹配情况下补充驱动排斥。 Immunological incompatibility in allogeneic transplantation is mainly driven by three classes of antigens—ABO blood groups, HLA, and minor histocompatibility antigens—with HLA mismatches being the strongest and mHAgs providing additional drivers of rejection even when HLA is well matched.

供者器官的损伤和炎症首先激活树突状细胞，它们在二级淋巴器官启动初始T/B细胞；T细胞经三信号激活后克隆扩增并迁移到移植物，B细胞在T细胞帮助下产生供者特异性IgG。 Injury and inflammation in the donor organ first activate dendritic cells, which in secondary lymphoid organs prime naïve T and B cells; after receiving three signals, T cells clonally expand and migrate to the graft, while B cells, with T‑cell help, generate donor‑specific IgG.

效应Th细胞、CTL、补体和抗体依赖的细胞介导细胞毒共同作用，造成内皮和基质细胞严重损伤，使炎症和组织坏死不断放大，表现为急性或慢性排斥。 Effector Th cells, CTLs, complement, and antibody‑dependent cell‑mediated cytotoxicity act together to inflict severe damage on endothelial and stromal cells, amplifying inflammation and tissue necrosis and manifesting clinically as acute or chronic rejection.

多种免疫抑制药物正是针对这些激活和效应步骤设计，通过在不同环节“拦截”T/B细胞信号通路和抗体生成，来降低这种免疫学上“正常但临床上不希望发生”的同种异基因免疫反应。 Multiple immunosuppressive drugs are designed to intervene at these activation and effector steps, intercepting T‑ and B‑cell signaling pathways and antibody formation to dampen this immunologically “normal but clinically unwanted” allogeneic immune response.

固体器官移植的免疫学方面
排斥反应 Immunological aspects of solid organ transplantation: rejection responses
一、本组PPT定位
围绕“排斥反应”的评估与风险控制 Focus of these slides: evaluation and risk control around rejection responses

本组幻灯从“移植前免疫学评估→供者选择与HLA不相容→最终交叉配型→移植后随访与并发症”四个环节，搭起理解固体器官移植排斥反应的临床框架。

These slides build a clinical framework for understanding rejection in solid organ transplantation, moving through four steps: pre‑transplant immunological evaluation, donor selection and HLA incompatibilities, the final cross‑match test, and post‑transplant follow‑up with complications.

二、移植前免疫学评估
尽量降低免疫学风险 Pre‑transplant immunological evaluation: minimizing immunological risk
1
评估对象与总体目的 Object of evaluation and overall goal

评估对象是候选受者（病人），通常在进入等待名单前后进行系统检查，目标是“在允许移植的前提下，尽可能降低免疫学风险”。

The evaluation targets the candidate recipient (patient) and is performed around the time of wait‑list enrolment, with the goal of allowing transplantation while minimizing immunological risk as far as possible.

2
免疫学探索的两大核心内容 Two core items of immunological work‑up

第一项是HLA分型，第二项是检测受者是否已经带有抗HLA抗体，这两项直接决定未来排斥风险和供者匹配策略。

The first is HLA typing, and the second is detecting whether the recipient already carries anti‑HLA antibodies; together they determine future rejection risk and guide donor matching strategies.

HLA分型
明确受者表达哪些HLA等位基因 HLA typing: defining which HLA alleles the recipient expresses

临床上通常利用PCR对8–10个位点进行HLA分型，至少包括HLA‑A、HLA‑B、HLA‑C、HLA‑DRB1以及DQB1，有时还会加入其他DRB位点。

In clinical practice, HLA typing is usually performed by PCR on 8–10 loci, at least including HLA‑A, HLA‑B, HLA‑C, HLA‑DRB1 and DQB1, sometimes with additional DRB loci.

PPT示例强调这是“低分辨率分型”，即只确定较“粗略”的等位基因家族，不是每一个精细亚型，高分辨率分型需要更复杂的分子方法或测序，在某些情形才会使用。

The slide stresses that this is “low‑resolution typing,” defining broad allele families rather than every fine subtype; high‑resolution typing requires more complex molecular methods or sequencing and is reserved for particular situations.

4
检测受者体内预存抗HLA抗体 Detecting pre‑existing anti‑HLA antibodies in the recipient

老师特别强调，某些病人已经被“免疫化”，即以前暴露过他人的HLA分子，因而体内已有抗HLA抗体，这会显著增加排斥风险。

The lecturer emphasized that some patients are already “immunized,” meaning they have previously been exposed to foreign HLA molecules and now carry anti‑HLA antibodies, which markedly increases rejection risk.

常见的免疫化情境包括
既往输血、先前的同种异基因移植，以及妊娠（母亲可对胎儿父系HLA产生免疫）。

Common immunizing circumstances include prior blood transfusions, previous allotransplants, and pregnancies, in which the mother may become immunized against the fetus’s paternal HLA.

实验方法演变
从微量淋巴细胞毒试验到Luminex/ELISA Evolution of laboratory methods: from microlymphocytotoxicity to Luminex/ELISA

传统的“微量淋巴细胞毒性试验”曾用于检测抗HLA抗体：将患者血清与表达已知HLA的细胞系、补体和染料共孵育，观察细胞是否被补体溶解；现在这一方法在多数中心已不再常规使用。

Historically, the microlymphocytotoxicity test was used: patient serum was incubated with cell lines expressing known HLA molecules, complement, and a vital dye, and complement‑mediated lysis revealed anti‑HLA antibodies; this is no longer routinely used in most centers.

目前主流检测依赖ELISA和Luminex等固相法：在固相载体表面固定一系列已知HLA分子，然后加入患者血清，如含有相应特异性IgG，则会结合其上。

Current practice mainly relies on solid‑phase techniques such as ELISA and Luminex, in which panels of known HLA molecules are immobilized on a solid surface and then incubated with patient serum; any specific IgG antibodies present will bind to their corresponding antigens.

Luminex技术要点
如何识别抗HLA谱 Luminex key points: how the anti‑HLA profile is identified

Luminex体系中使用带有不同荧光组合的微珠，每一类微珠表面包被一种特定的HLA分子，通过荧光条形码即可区分数十到上百种微珠。

In a Luminex system, beads carrying different combinations of fluorochromes are used, each bead type being coated with a specific HLA molecule, so that tens to hundreds of bead specificities can be distinguished by their fluorescence barcode.

将受者血清加入这些微珠混合物中，如果存在针对某一HLA的抗体，它就会特异性结合相应微珠表面，随后再加带荧光标记的抗人IgG二抗，通过流式细胞仪读取信号，即可判断病人针对哪些HLA等位基因有抗体。

Recipient serum is added to the bead mixture; if antibodies against a given HLA are present, they bind specifically to the corresponding beads, and a fluorochrome‑labeled anti‑human IgG secondary antibody is then added; analysis by flow cytometry reveals which bead (and thus which HLA allele) has been recognized.

由于HLA等位基因总数巨大，微珠上只选择各人群中最常见的部分等位基因，因此检测结果能覆盖主要临床相关抗体，但无法穷尽所有可能的HLA抗体。

Because the overall number of HLA alleles is enormous, only the most frequent alleles in a given population are represented on the bead panel, so the assay captures most clinically relevant antibodies but cannot test against every possible HLA specificity.

7
同时评估感染风险与其他医学问题 Parallel evaluation of infectious risk and other medical issues

在免疫学评估之外，医生还要通过血清学筛查来预判感染并发症，例如是否对HIV、HBV、HCV等病毒呈阳性，以及其他潜在病原体的风险。

In addition to the immunological work‑up, physicians perform serological screening to anticipate infectious complications, for example checking for seropositivity to HIV, HBV, HCV and other potential pathogens.

此外还需完成全面的“其他医学检查”，评估心肺功能、原发疾病活动度等，以确定病人总体上是否适合接受移植手术和长期免疫抑制。

Comprehensive “other medical check‑ups” are also required, assessing cardiac and pulmonary function, activity of the underlying disease, and other factors to determine whether the patient is globally fit for transplantation and long‑term immunosuppression.

三、潜在供者
HLA不相容与mismatch判定 Potential donor: defining HLA incompatibilities or mismatches
何谓“HLA mismatch”
从受者视角出发 Definition of HLA “mismatch” from the recipient’s perspective

当为某一受者寻找潜在供者时，会将双方的HLA分型结果进行逐位点比对；凡是“供者表达而受者自身不表达”的HLA等位基因，对受者免疫系统来说都是外来抗原，统称为“HLA不相容”或“mismatch”。

When a potential donor is considered for a given recipient, their HLA typings are compared locus by locus; any HLA allele that is present in the donor but absent in the recipient is foreign to the recipient’s immune system and is called an HLA incompatibility or mismatch.

PPT示例
6个等位基因分型下的“两处mismatch” Slide example: two mismatches with six alleles typed

示例中，受者在HLA‑A位点有A01和A24，在HLA‑B位点有B39和B44，在DRB1位点有DRB101和DRB102；供者则为A01，B09，B44，DRB101和DRB111。

In the example, the recipient carries A01 and A24 at the HLA‑A locus, B39 and B44 at HLA‑B, and DRB101 and DRB102, whereas the donor has A01, B09, B44, DRB101 and DRB111.

从受者角度看，B09和DRB1*11是“多出来的供者等位基因”，因此构成两处HLA mismatch；PPT特别指出这是“低分辨率分型”的示意。

From the recipient’s point of view, B09 and DRB1*11 are donor alleles not present in the recipient, thus constituting two HLA mismatches, and the slide specifies that this is an example based on low‑resolution typing.

3
低分辨率与高分辨率分型对匹配难度的影响 Impact of low‑ versus high‑resolution typing on matching difficulty

老师说明，如果改用高分辨率HLA分型（精确到更多数字位、反映更细的氨基酸差异），理论上的理想匹配变得极其困难，在多数供者–受者对中不可实现。

The lecturer explained that if high‑resolution typing is used, specifying more digits and finer amino‑acid differences, perfect HLA identity becomes extremely difficult to achieve and is often unattainable for most donor–recipient pairs.

因此，临床实践中通常接受“存在一定数量mismatch”的现实，再通过免疫抑制治疗来控制由此产生的免疫反应。

Clinical practice therefore accepts a certain number of mismatches as inevitable and relies on immunosuppressive therapy to control the resulting immune responses.

4
不同器官对HLA匹配程度的要求不同 Different organs require different degrees of HLA matching

在肾移植中，由于透析可以暂时代替肾功能，通常有更多时间去等待HLA匹配更好的供者，从而力求减少mismatch数目。

In kidney transplantation, dialysis can temporarily replace renal function, so there is usually more time to wait for a better‑matched donor and thus to minimize the number of mismatches.

而心脏移植往往属于紧急情况，难以长时间等待高匹配度供者，此时会在确保基本条件的前提下接受较多HLA不相容，通过加强免疫抑制来弥补。

In contrast, heart transplantation is often an emergency, leaving little time to wait for a highly compatible donor; in that case, more HLA incompatibilities are accepted as long as basic conditions are met, and stronger immunosuppression is used to compensate.

四、最终交叉配型实验
排除“已存在的抗供者抗体” Final cross‑match test: ruling out pre‑existing anti‑donor antibodies
1
确定潜在供者后的三步原则 Three principles once a potential donor is identified

一旦锁定潜在供者，首先必须确认ABO血型相容，其次在具体器官的紧急程度允许范围内“尽量争取最佳HLA相容性”，最后才是进行最终的交叉配型实验。

Once a potential donor has been identified, the team must first confirm ABO compatibility, then aim for the best possible HLA compatibility allowed by the urgency for that organ, and finally perform the last step, the cross‑match test.

PPT将“HLA mismatch数目”简单定义为“供者有而受者没有的HLA等位基因个数”，并强调在后续的实验中要特别关注受者是否已经对这些等位基因产生了IgG抗体。

The slide defines the number of HLA mismatches as the number of HLA alleles present in the donor but not in the recipient and stresses that subsequent testing should focus on whether the recipient has already generated IgG antibodies against these donor alleles.

2
交叉配型实验的目的 Purpose of the cross‑match test

交叉配型在移植手术前“最后一刻”进行，核心问题是：受者血清中是否存在针对该具体供者细胞的抗体（主要是IgG型抗HLA或其他抗原）。

The cross‑match test is performed at the very last stage before surgery to answer one key question: does the recipient’s serum contain antibodies, mainly IgG against HLA or other antigens, that recognize this particular donor’s cells.

3
实验步骤概述 Experimental steps in the cross‑match

实验将供者淋巴细胞与受者血清混合孵育，如果受者血清中存在抗供者的IgG，它们会结合到供者淋巴细胞表面。

Donor lymphocytes are incubated with recipient serum, and if anti‑donor IgG antibodies are present, they bind to antigens on the donor lymphocyte surface.

随后加入带荧光标记的抗人IgG抗体，通过流式细胞仪检测细胞是否变为荧光阳性，从而间接显示受者是否携带抗供者IgG。

A fluorochrome‑conjugated anti‑human IgG antibody is then added, and flow cytometry is used to detect whether the cells have become fluorescent, indirectly revealing the presence of anti‑donor IgG in the recipient.

判读原则
交叉配型必须为阴性 Interpretation: the cross‑match must be negative

如果交叉配型阳性，说明术前受者体内已经存在可以识别并攻击供者细胞的抗体，一旦移植立即接触到内皮和血管，将触发极快速、严重的抗体介导损伤。

A positive cross‑match indicates that the recipient already carries antibodies capable of recognizing and attacking donor cells, which, once they encounter the graft endothelium and vasculature, can rapidly trigger severe antibody‑mediated damage.

因此临床上要求“交叉配型必须为阴性”才允许手术进行；若为阳性，则视为禁忌组合，需要更换供者或采用其他策略。

Therefore, a negative cross‑match is mandatory before proceeding with transplantation; a positive result defines a forbidden donor–recipient combination, requiring another donor or alternative strategy.

五、移植后评估
功能、免疫抑制与并发症 Post‑transplant evaluation: function, immunosuppression, and complications
1
移植物功能恢复 Resumption of graft function

术后首先要确认移植物功能是否恢复，例如肾移植要观察尿量和肌酐变化，心脏移植要关注心排量与血流动力学表现等，这是所有后续评估的基础。

The first post‑operative concern is whether graft function has resumed, such as monitoring urine output and creatinine for kidney transplants or cardiac output and hemodynamics for heart transplants, which forms the basis for all further evaluation.

免疫抑制
预防与治疗兼顾的长期策略 Immunosuppression as a long‑term preventive and curative strategy

PPT明确指出，免疫抑制既用于“预防性”抑制排斥，也用于在出现急性排斥时“治疗性”加强，以挽救移植物功能。

The slide states that immunosuppression has both a preventive role, continuously suppressing rejection, and a curative role, being intensified when acute rejection episodes occur to rescue graft function.

移植受者几乎都会在“整个生命过程中”持续处于某种程度的免疫抑制状态，根据时间、排斥风险和并发症不断调整药物组合和剂量。

Transplant recipients remain under some degree of immunosuppression “throughout their whole life,” with drug regimens and doses adjusted over time according to rejection risk and emerging complications.

免疫学并发症
排斥发作 Immunological complications: rejection episodes

尽管有免疫抑制，仍然可能发生不同形式的排斥发作，PPT将其统称为“免疫学并发症”，提示后续课程会区分超急性、急性和慢性排斥等具体类型。

Despite ongoing immunosuppression, various forms of rejection may still occur; the slide groups these under “immunological complications,” indicating that subsequent lectures will distinguish hyperacute, acute, and chronic rejection in detail.

感染并发症
病毒再激活与机会性感染 Infectious complications: viral reactivation and opportunistic infections

由于长期抑制免疫，受者更易发生病毒再激活，如HCV、HBV、CMV等，也易受到供者携带病原体的影响。

Because of chronic immunosuppression, recipients are more prone to viral reactivations such as HCV, HBV, and CMV and to infections transmitted from the donor.

PPT特别提到机会性感染和院内感染，例如肺孢子菌和念珠菌等真菌或原虫，可在免疫力低下时引发严重肺炎或全身感染。

The slide specifically mentions opportunistic and nosocomial infections, such as Pneumocystis and Candida, which can cause severe pneumonia or systemic infections in immunocompromised patients.

5
药物毒性与肿瘤相关并发症 Drug toxicity and tumor‑related complications

免疫抑制药物本身可对多个器官产生毒性，如肾毒性、肝毒性或骨髓抑制等，这些被单独归类为“毒性并发症”。

Immunosuppressive drugs themselves can damage several organs, for example causing nephrotoxicity, hepatotoxicity, or bone‑marrow suppression, and these effects are listed separately as “toxicity of immunosuppressors.”

长期免疫抑制还可促进淋巴细胞异常增殖，形成“淋巴增殖综合征”，并增加多种恶性肿瘤（统称为“癌症”）的发生风险，需要长期监测。

Long‑term immunosuppression can also drive abnormal lymphocyte proliferation, leading to “lymphoproliferative syndromes,” and increases the risk of various malignancies, collectively referred to as “cancer,” which necessitates long‑term surveillance.

6
原发疾病本身对预后的影响 Impact of the patient’s underlying disease

PPT提醒，即便更换了器官，受者原本的基础疾病仍然存在，它既可能再次损害新移植物，也可能因全身状态恶化而影响移植长期结局。

The slide notes that even after organ replacement, the patient’s underlying disease persists and may again damage the new graft or worsen the overall condition, thereby affecting long‑term transplant outcomes.

六、本组内容小结 Local summary for this set of slides

本组PPT围绕“排斥反应”这一核心主题，从移植前的免疫学评估和感染风险筛查讲起，强调HLA分型与抗HLA抗体检测是量化免疫学风险、指导供者选择的基础。

Centered on the theme of “rejection responses,” these slides begin with pre‑transplant immunological evaluation and infectious risk screening, highlighting HLA typing and anti‑HLA antibody detection as the basis for quantifying immunological risk and guiding donor selection.

随后通过具体例子解释了“HLA mismatch”概念，以及不同器官在紧急程度不同的情况下对匹配程度的不同要求。

They then use concrete examples to explain the concept of an “HLA mismatch” and show how different organs, depending on urgency, require different degrees of HLA compatibility.

在最终交叉配型阶段，重点是排除“已经存在的抗供者IgG抗体”，因此要求交叉配型结果必须为阴性，否则不能实施移植。

At the stage of the final cross‑match, the key is to exclude “pre‑existing anti‑donor IgG antibodies,” so the cross‑match result must be negative; otherwise, transplantation cannot proceed.

移植后评估则围绕“功能恢复—终身免疫抑制—多维并发症管理”展开，其中免疫学排斥发作、机会性感染、药物毒性、淋巴增殖和肿瘤，以及原发疾病持续存在，构成长期管理的主要挑战。

Post‑transplant evaluation focuses on graft function recovery, lifelong immunosuppression, and multi‑dimensional complication management, with rejection episodes, opportunistic infections, drug toxicity, lymphoproliferative syndromes and cancer, and the persistent underlying disease forming the main long‑term challenges.

同种异基因器官排斥的类型与机制ITypes of Organ Rejection in Allogeneic Transplantation – Timing and Mechanisms

一、器官排斥反应三大类型
按时间与机制划分 Three main types of organ rejection: classified by time and mechanism
超急性排斥
发生在移植血流重建后立即到约5天内，多数在24小时内即可出现。

Hyperacute rejection: occurs immediately after re‑establishing blood flow up to about 5 days, most often within the first 24 hours.

急性排斥
包括急性细胞性排斥和抗体介导的急性排斥，一般在术后5天到1年多这一时间窗内发生。
Acute rejection
includes acute cellular rejection and antibody‑mediated acute rejection, usually occurring between about day 5 and more than 1 year after transplantation.
慢性排斥与慢性移植物功能失常
多在术后90天以后逐渐出现，可持续数年，表现为进展性、不可逆的功能下降。

Chronic rejection and chronic allograft dysfunction: typically appear after 90 days and may progress over several years, leading to gradual and irreversible loss of graft function.

三种排斥的共同点是都由同种异基因免疫反应驱动，但其潜在病理机制不同：超急性主要是预成抗体和补体，急性可由T细胞或抗体主导，慢性则多为持续低度免疫损伤加上非免疫因素共同作用。

All three types are driven by allogeneic immune responses, but they differ in underlying mechanisms: hyperacute rejection is mainly mediated by pre‑formed antibodies and complement, acute rejection may be T‑cell‑ or antibody‑dominated, and chronic rejection usually reflects persistent low‑grade immune injury combined with non‑immunological factors.

二、超急性排斥
几乎立刻发生的抗体介导损伤 Hyperacute rejection: immediately occurring antibody‑mediated damage
典型诱因
受者体内预先存在抗“供者”抗体 Main triggers: pre‑formed anti‑donor antibodies

超急性排斥出现在已被“同种异基因免疫化”的病人中，关键是术前就有针对供者抗原的预成抗体，主要为IgG，也可包括IgM。

Hyperacute rejection occurs in patients who are already “allo‑immunized,” that is, they possess pre‑formed antibodies against donor antigens before surgery, mainly IgG but sometimes also IgM.

这些抗体可分为三类
抗HLA抗体、抗ABO血型抗体（ABO也表达在内皮和上皮细胞上）、以及针对其他同种异基因抗原如抗内皮细胞抗体。

These antibodies fall into three main categories: anti‑HLA antibodies, anti‑ABO blood group antibodies (ABO is expressed on endothelial and epithelial cells as well as red cells), and antibodies against other alloantigens such as anti‑endothelial antibodies.

病理机制
抗体固定在内皮同种异基因抗原上 Pathologic mechanism: antibody binding to endothelial alloantigens

当受者血液开始灌注移植物时，这些预成抗体立即结合在移植器官血管内皮细胞表面的同种异基因抗原上。

When the recipient’s blood first perfuses the graft, these pre‑formed antibodies immediately bind to alloantigens on the surface of endothelial cells in the transplanted organ.

抗体结合后激活经典途径补体，引发补体级联反应，生成C3b、C5b‑9等效应分子，直接导致内皮细胞溶解与死亡。

Antibody binding activates the classical complement pathway, triggering complement cascades and generating effectors such as C3b and C5b‑9, which directly lyse and kill endothelial cells.

同时，补体片段和免疫复合物可通过抗体依赖的细胞介导细胞毒　招募中性粒细胞、巨噬细胞和NK细胞，对内皮进行进一步攻击。
答案 ACD
At the same time, complement split products and immune complexes promote antibody‑dependent cell‑mediated cytotoxicity 　, recruiting neutrophils, macrophages and NK cells to further damage the endothelium.
答案 ACD

内皮受损暴露基底膜并激活凝血系统，出现凝血活化、血栓形成、血管腔阻塞、局部缺血和坏死，即“血栓形成、缺血性坏死”的连锁反应。

Endothelial injury exposes basement membranes and activates the coagulation system, leading to coagulation, thrombosis, vascular occlusion, local ischemia and necrosis – a cascade of “thrombosis and ischemic necrosis.”

3
临床表现与结局 Clinical picture and outcome

超急性排斥极少见，发生率不足1%，几乎都与未被发现的强阳性交叉配型或ABO/HLA严重不相容有关。

Hyperacute rejection is now very rare, with an incidence below 1%, and is almost always associated with an undetected strongly positive cross‑match or major ABO/HLA incompatibility.

一旦发生，移植物迅速肿胀、变色并失去功能，临床上往往在手术台上或术后极短时间内即可判断，几乎没有有效治疗，只能紧急摘除移植物。

Once it occurs, the graft rapidly swells, changes color and loses function, and clinicians can usually recognize it on the operating table or very soon afterwards; there is essentially no effective treatment, and urgent removal of the graft is required.

因此预防至关重要
必须事先进行系统的抗HLA抗体筛查和阴性的交叉配型，严格避免ABO不相容移植。

Prevention is therefore crucial: systematic screening for anti‑HLA antibodies and a negative cross‑match, together with strict avoidance of ABO‑incompatible transplants, are mandatory.

三、急性细胞性排斥
以T细胞为主导的急性损伤 Acute cellular rejection: T‑cell‑mediated acute injury
1
临床特征 Clinical features

急性细胞性排斥通常在术后几天到数月出现，典型表现为发热和移植器官的“功能衰竭”或快速功能恶化，如肾小球滤过率下降、心输出量减低等。

Acute cellular rejection typically occurs from days to several months after surgery and presents with fever plus functional failure or rapid deterioration of the graft, such as reduced glomerular filtration in kidney grafts or decreased cardiac output in heart grafts.

2
诱因与机制 Cause and mechanism

其根本原因是受者抗供者T淋巴细胞的激活，尤其是CD8阳性的细胞毒性T细胞，也有CD4辅助T细胞参与。

The fundamental cause is activation of recipient anti‑donor T lymphocytes, particularly CD8⁺ cytotoxic T cells, with participation of CD4⁺ helper T cells.

这些效应T细胞在同种异基因抗原刺激下克隆扩增，随后浸润到移植物间质和实质，在局部释放细胞毒分子和炎症细胞因子。

These effector T cells clonally expand in response to alloantigens and then infiltrate the graft stroma and parenchyma, where they release cytotoxic molecules and inflammatory cytokines.

3
组织学诊断 Histological diagnosis

确诊依赖移植物活检，光镜下可见实质细胞损伤和间质炎症，特点是单个核细胞、尤其是T淋巴细胞浸润。

Definitive diagnosis requires a graft biopsy, which shows parenchymal cell damage and interstitial inflammation characterized by mononuclear cell, particularly T‑cell, infiltration.

4
治疗与预后 Treatment and prognosis

教师强调，急性细胞性排斥在临床上相当常见，约半数以上受者一生中会经历至少一次，但通常可以通过加强免疫抑制治疗逆转。

The lecturer stressed that acute cellular rejection is quite common, with more than half of recipients experiencing at least one episode, but it is usually reversible if immunosuppression is intensified.

治疗措施包括短期加大糖皮质激素剂量、调整或增加T细胞靶向免疫抑制药物等，大多数病例在治疗后移植物功能可以恢复。

Treatment consists of short‑term high‑dose corticosteroids and adjustment or escalation of T‑cell‑targeted immunosuppressive drugs, and in most cases graft function can be restored.

四、抗体介导的急性排斥
血管为主的损伤 Antibody‑mediated acute rejection: predominantly vascular injury
1
临床特征 Clinical features

抗体介导的急性排斥与急性细胞性排斥的临床表现类似，同样有发热和移植器官功能衰竭，因此单凭临床症状难以区分。

Antibody‑mediated acute rejection shares similar clinical manifestations with cellular acute rejection, including fever and graft functional failure, so it cannot be distinguished on clinical grounds alone.

病因
供者特异性抗体 Cause: donor‑specific antibodies

其核心病因是供者特异性抗体（DSA）沉积在移植物血管内皮表面，这些抗体主要为IgG类抗HLA或其他内皮抗原抗体。

The key cause is deposition of donor‑specific antibodies (DSA) on endothelial surfaces in the graft vasculature, mainly IgG antibodies targeting donor HLA or other endothelial antigens.

这些抗体结合后激活补体，特别是C4裂解产生的C4d片段会沿毛细血管和小血管壁沉积，成为一个重要的免疫组织化学标志。

When these antibodies bind, they activate complement; a notable feature is deposition of the C4d fragment along capillary and small‑vessel walls, which serves as an important immunohistochemical marker.

3
诊断要点 Diagnostic clues
诊断依赖移植物活检
病理切片中可见血管腔内及周围的C4d阳性染色，提示局部补体激活和抗体参与。

Diagnosis relies on graft biopsy: histological sections show C4d‑positive staining in capillaries and small vessels, indicating local complement activation and antibody involvement.

同时可见血管损伤（vascular lesions），如内皮肿胀、炎性细胞黏附和血管壁结构破坏。

Vascular lesions are also observed, such as endothelial swelling, inflammatory cell adhesion, and structural damage to vessel walls.

实验室上常伴有循环DSA滴度升高，与组织学改变相互印证。

Laboratory tests often reveal elevated circulating DSA titers, corroborating the histological findings.

4
治疗原则 Treatment principles

治疗以针对B细胞和T细胞的免疫抑制方案为主，包括强化现有免疫抑制、使用B细胞/浆细胞靶向药物，以及在部分中心联合血浆置换等手段。

Treatment centers on immunosuppressive regimens targeting both B and T cells, including intensifying baseline therapy, adding B‑cell or plasma‑cell‑directed drugs, and in some centers combining this with plasmapheresis.

教师提到，这部分药物的详细机制会在专门的免疫抑制治疗课程中展开，这里应重点理解“抗体介导排斥主要损伤血管，需要抑制B细胞和抗体产生”。

The lecturer noted that detailed mechanisms of these drugs will be discussed in a dedicated immunosuppression lecture; here you should mainly remember that “antibody‑mediated rejection primarily injures blood vessels and requires suppression of B cells and antibody production.”

五、慢性排斥与慢性移植物功能失常 Chronic graft rejection and chronic allograft dysfunction

临床本质
缓慢进展、不可逆的功能恶化 Clinical nature: slowly progressive, irreversible deterioration

慢性排斥表现为移植物功能的逐渐、进行性下降，本质上是长期低度免疫损伤累积的结果，一旦纤维化形成一般不可逆。

Chronic rejection manifests as gradual, progressive loss of graft function and essentially represents the cumulative effect of long‑standing low‑grade immune injury; once fibrosis is established it is usually irreversible.

病因
多因素叠加 Causes: multiple factors

免疫学因素方面，关键是“亚临床、慢性（持续性）的同种异基因免疫反应”，即可能没有明显急性发作，但持续存在轻度T细胞和/或抗体介导的损伤。

On the immunological side, the key is “subclinical, chronic (persistent) alloimmune reaction,” meaning there may be no overt acute episodes yet mild T‑cell and/or antibody‑mediated damage persists over time.

非免疫学因素同样重要，包括高血压、免疫抑制药物本身的毒性、代谢性疾病等，这些都可加速血管和间质的损伤。

Non‑immunological factors are also important, including hypertension, toxic effects of immunosuppressive drugs, and metabolic diseases, all of which accelerate vascular and interstitial injury.

3
典型病理改变 Typical pathological changes

血管方面可见“闭塞性动脉硬化”，即血管壁增厚、管腔狭窄，统称为移植物血管病变（vasculopathy）。

Vascularly, “occlusive arteriosclerosis” is observed, with thickened vessel walls and narrowed lumina, a pattern often termed graft vasculopathy.

实质和间质方面则出现进行性纤维化与小管萎缩，表现为“纤维化‑小管萎缩”型改变，最终导致有效功能单位大量丢失。

In the parenchyma and interstitium, progressive fibrosis and tubular atrophy occur, leading to extensive loss of functional units.

4
治疗与预防 Treatment and prevention

在慢性排斥阶段，已经形成的纤维化基本不可逆，现有治疗多只能减慢进展，难以恢复原有功能。

At the stage of chronic rejection, established fibrosis is essentially irreversible, and current treatments can at best slow progression rather than restore previous function.

因此教师强调，关键是通过良好控制急性排斥发作和非免疫危险因素，尽量避免或延后慢性排斥的发生。

The lecturer therefore emphasized that the key is to control acute rejection episodes and non‑immune risk factors as well as possible, thereby preventing or delaying the onset of chronic rejection.

六、不同器官移植物的平均生存期 Average lifespan of transplanted organs

课件列出多种器官的平均功能存活时间，反映在现代免疫抑制和监测条件下，各类移植大致预期寿命：

The slides list average functional survival times for several organs, reflecting the approximate life expectancy of different grafts under current immunosuppression and monitoring:

肾移植
约10–15年，但也有个别病例存活超过40年。
Kidney grafts
about 10–15 years on average, with exceptional cases surviving more than 40 years.
肝移植
大约10年。
Liver grafts
around 10 years.
心脏移植
约10–13年。
Heart grafts
approximately 10–13 years.
肺移植
平均约5.7年。
Lung grafts
average around 5.7 years.
肠移植
约3年。
Intestine grafts
about 3 years.
胰腺移植
约8年。
Pancreas grafts
about 8 years.

这些数值受多种因素影响，包括器官本身的易损性、慢性排斥发生率、患者基础疾病和感染并发症等。

These figures are influenced by many factors, including the intrinsic vulnerability of each organ, the incidence of chronic rejection, the patient’s underlying disease, and infectious complications.

七、猪肾异种移植
EXPAND临床试验与现状 Kidney xenograft from pigs: EXPAND clinical trial and current status
1
异种肾移植的最新临床尝试 Recent clinical attempts at kidney xenotransplantation

老师在最后展示了近期猪肾异种移植的人体案例，例如2024年在MGH进行的首例带基因修饰的猪肾移植，肾功能良好，但病人约在术后第51天因心源性原因去世。

The lecturer presented recent human cases of pig kidney xenotransplantation, such as the first genetically modified pig‑kidney transplant at MGH in 2024, where graft function was good but the patient died from cardiac causes around day 51.

之后还有在NYU和MGH的病例，部分患者可脱离透析，移植物分别在约第130天因排斥被切除，以及维持功能约271天后因功能下降而被移除。

Subsequent cases at NYU and MGH showed that some patients could come off dialysis, with grafts removed around day 130 due to rejection in one case and after about 271 days of declining function in another.

这些案例提示，目前猪肾在人体内尚未获得“多年级别”的长期生存，仍处于探索性阶段。

These cases suggest that pig kidneys have not yet achieved multi‑year survival in humans and that the field remains exploratory.

2
EXPAND试验概要 Outline of the EXPAND trial

EXPAND临床试验是评估基因改造猪肾在终末期肾病患者中应用的研究，初步计划纳入6名受试者，如前期结果良好可扩展至50人。

The EXPAND clinical trial is designed to evaluate genetically modified pig kidneys in end‑stage renal disease patients, initially planning to enroll 6 participants with the possibility of expansion to 50 if early results are favorable.

课件举例的公告显示，某例在2025年11月完成的UKidney移植手术，使用的是带有多重基因编辑的猪肾。

The slide cites an announcement of a UKidney transplant performed in November 2025 using a pig kidney with multiple gene edits.

3
猪源肾脏的基因工程改造 Genetic engineering of porcine kidneys

为了减少排斥和器官损伤，猪肾通常被进行多位点基因编辑：敲除主要异种抗原相关基因、失活猪内源性逆转录病毒（PERVs）、并插入某些人源基因以改善免疫耐受和内皮保护。

To reduce rejection and organ damage, pig kidneys undergo multiple genetic edits: knocking out genes encoding major xenogeneic antigens, inactivating porcine endogenous retroviruses (PERVs), and inserting selected human genes to improve immune tolerance and endothelial protection.

在某些方案中，还会联合移植猪胸腺组织（“肾+胸腺”复合移植物），试图通过重塑T细胞发育来诱导更持久的耐受。

In some protocols, porcine thymic tissue is co‑transplanted with the kidney (“kidney + thymus” grafts) in an attempt to reshape T‑cell development and induce more durable tolerance.

总体而言，到目前为止尚无证据表明猪肾在人体内可以实现多年长期存活，因此所有临床尝试都被定位为高度监控下的探索性研究。

Overall, there is still no evidence that porcine kidneys can survive for many years in humans, so all current clinical attempts are considered highly monitored exploratory studies.

八、同种异基因免疫反应小结
排斥的控制与未解决问题 Summary of allogeneic immune responses: controlling rejection and remaining challenges

老师在总结中再次强调，同种异基因免疫反应是由输血和移植等医疗行为“人为创造”的情境，本质上是受者免疫系统对“非自身”的一种正常防御性反应，但在移植背景下却是不希望出现的结果。

In the summary, the lecturer reiterated that allogeneic immune responses are circumstances “created by medicine” through transfusions and transplants; fundamentally, they are normal defensive reactions of the recipient’s immune system against non‑self, but in the transplant setting they are unwanted outcomes.

通过尽量减少供受者间的遗传差异（血型、HLA、次要组织相容性抗原）以及避免预存抗体，可以明显降低排斥风险。

By minimizing genetic differences between donor and recipient in blood groups, HLA and minor histocompatibility antigens, and by avoiding pre‑existing antibodies, the risk of rejection can be significantly reduced.

急性排斥发作（包括细胞性和抗体介导性）在当前多种免疫抑制方案下通常可以得到较好控制和治疗。

Acute rejection episodes, whether cellular or antibody‑mediated, are generally well controlled and treatable with current immunosuppressive regimens.

慢性排斥则是更棘手的问题
可以通过优化急性事件管理和危险因素控制减缓进展，但一旦形成显著纤维化，目前缺乏逆转手段。

Chronic rejection is more problematic: its progression can be slowed by optimal management of acute events and risk factors, but there is currently no effective way to reverse established fibrosis.

九、免疫耐受
未来研究方向 Immune tolerance: a future research direction

最后一页课件标注“Immune tolerance”并说明本课程中不详细讨论，只指出诱导真正的移植免疫耐受是当前研究的热点和远期目标。

The final slide titled “Immune tolerance” states that this topic is not discussed in detail in the present course, but notes that inducing true immune tolerance to transplants is a major current research focus and a long‑term goal.

当前的免疫抑制更多是“被动压制免疫反应”，带来感染和肿瘤等副作用；未来希望通过更精准的细胞和分子调控，让受者在无需或极少免疫抑制药物的情况下长期接受移植物。

Current immunosuppression mostly “passively suppresses immune responses,” leading to infections and malignancies; future strategies aim to use more precise cellular and molecular control so that recipients can accept grafts long‑term with little or no immunosuppressive medication.

因此，同种异基因移植免疫学的终极目标，是在防止排斥的同时建立真正的耐受状态，从而延长移植物寿命并减少并发症负担。

Thus, the ultimate goal of allogeneic transplant immunology is to prevent rejection while establishing genuine tolerance, thereby prolonging graft lifespan and reducing the burden of complications.

组织与细胞移植及造血干细胞移植ITissue and Cell Transplantation and Hematopoietic Stem Cell Transplantation

一、组织与细胞移植的总体概念 Overall concept of tissue and cell transplantation

组织和细胞移植指的是
除实体器官外，将角膜、皮肤、血管、心脏瓣膜、骨、肌腱、韧带以及造血干细胞等从供者转移到受者体内，用来替代或修复受损结构与功能。

Tissue and cell transplantation refers to the transfer, in addition to solid organs, of cornea, skin, blood vessels, heart valves, bone, tendons, ligaments, and hematopoietic stem cells from a donor to a recipient in order to replace or repair damaged structures and functions.

与实体器官移植相比，某些组织（如角膜）免疫学风险较低，而复合组织（如手、面部）和造血干细胞移植则涉及极复杂的免疫学问题，尤其是同种异基因免疫反应和移植物抗宿主病。

Compared with solid organ transplantation, some tissues such as cornea carry relatively low immunological risk, whereas composite tissues such as hands or faces and hematopoietic stem cell transplantation involve highly complex immunology, especially allogeneic immune responses and graft‑versus‑host disease.

二、不同组织移植的免疫特点 Immunological features of different tissue grafts

角膜移植
免疫豁免组织 Corneal transplantation: an immune‑privileged tissue

角膜几乎不含血管和免疫细胞，被视为“免疫豁免组织”，因此排斥风险明显降低，是唯一一个在很多国家都有正式等候名单、临床效果非常好的组织移植。

The cornea is almost avascular and devoid of immune cells, and is considered an “immune‑privileged tissue,” so the risk of rejection is markedly reduced; it is the only tissue transplant for which there is often an official waiting list and consistently excellent clinical outcomes.

皮肤移植
免疫学高度复杂 Skin grafts: highly complex immunologically

自体皮肤移植（同一患者不同部位之间）几乎没有免疫学问题，是严重烧伤修复的标准做法。

Autologous skin grafts, transferred between different sites of the same patient, are essentially free of immunological issues and are standard treatment for severe burns.

同种异基因皮肤则通常只作短期治疗性覆盖材料，多经固定或处理以抑制免疫细胞增殖，用于烧伤后数天到一两周内提供生长因子、保护创面，等待受者自身皮肤再生。

Allogeneic skin is usually used only as a short‑term therapeutic dressing; it is often fixed or processed to prevent immune cell proliferation and is applied for days to a couple of weeks after burns to supply growth factors and protect the wound until the recipient’s own skin regenerates.

3
复合组织移植（如手、面部）：极高的免疫排斥风险 Composite tissue transplantation (e.g. hand, face): very high rejection risk

手或面部移植属于复合组织移植，包含皮肤、肌肉、骨、血管、神经等多种高度免疫原性成分，因此同种异基因免疫反应极为强烈，排斥风险可接近“几乎必然发生”。

Hand or face transplantation is a form of composite tissue transplantation that includes skin, muscle, bone, blood vessels, and nerves, all highly immunogenic, so the allogeneic immune response is extremely strong and the risk of rejection approaches “almost inevitable.”

这类移植需要终生、强度很高的免疫抑制治疗，其免疫学难度明显大于单一器官如肾或肝。

Such grafts require lifelong, intensive immunosuppressive therapy, and their immunological complexity is clearly greater than that of single organs such as kidney or liver.

三、造血干细胞移植
指征与基本目的 Hematopoietic stem cell transplantation (HSCT): indications and main goals

造血干细胞移植的核心目的是“重建一个新的造血和免疫系统”，用于替换一个本身异常或被刻意摧毁的造血系统。

The core purpose of hematopoietic stem cell transplantation is to “rebuild a new hematopoietic and immune system” to replace one that is intrinsically abnormal or has been deliberately destroyed.

一类指征是先天性或遗传性造血/免疫缺陷等，使原有造血系统“先天异常”；另一类是恶性疾病（如白血病、淋巴瘤、骨髓增生异常综合征等）治疗中通过大剂量化疗/放疗“有意摧毁”患者造血系统。

One set of indications is congenital or inherited defects of hematopoiesis or immunity, where the original system is “intrinsically abnormal”; another is malignant diseases such as leukemia, lymphoma, or myelodysplastic syndromes, in which high‑dose chemotherapy or radiotherapy intentionally destroys the patient’s hematopoietic system.

四、造血干细胞来源及其差异 Sources of HSCs and their differences

1
骨髓 Bone marrow

传统来源是骨髓，需要在全身麻醉下多点穿刺（约数十次），总量可超过1升骨髓，然后从中分选CD34阳性造血干/祖细胞。

The traditional source is bone marrow, obtained under general anesthesia via dozens of punctures that yield more than 1 liter of marrow, from which CD34‑positive hematopoietic stem and progenitor cells are sorted.

外周血干细胞
G‑CSF动员 Peripheral blood stem cells mobilized by G‑CSF

供者先接受粒细胞集落刺激因子G‑CSF注射数天，使CD34阳性细胞从骨髓进入外周血，然后通过单采术（apheresis）收集外周血单个核细胞，再分选CD34阳性细胞。

Donors receive injections of granulocyte colony‑stimulating factor (G‑CSF) for several days, which mobilizes CD34‑positive cells from marrow into peripheral blood; leukapheresis is then performed to collect peripheral blood mononuclear cells and CD34‑positive cells are sorted from them.

这种方式创伤较小，是目前临床最常用的造血干细胞来源之一。

This method is less invasive and is now one of the most commonly used clinical sources of hematopoietic stem cells.

3
脐带血 Umbilical cord blood

脐带血是在新生儿出生后从脐带和胎盘采集的血液，含有造血干细胞和免疫细胞，但总体细胞数较少。

Umbilical cord blood is collected from the umbilical cord and placenta after birth and contains hematopoietic stem cells and immune cells, but the total cell number is relatively low.

脐带血的免疫系统比较“幼稚”，记忆T细胞很少，因此发生GVHD的风险相对较低，但因为细胞数目有限，造血和免疫重建速度往往更慢。

The immune system in cord blood is relatively “naïve,” with few memory T cells, so the risk of GVHD is comparatively lower, but because cell numbers are limited, hematopoietic and immune reconstitution tends to be slower.

4
不同来源意味着不同“质量” Different sources imply different “qualities”

老师强调，不同来源的造血干细胞具有不同的造血能力（重建速度）和免疫学特性（记忆T细胞含量），这会直接影响植入成功率、GVHD风险和感染风险。

The lecturer emphasized that different HSC sources have different hematopoietic capacities (speed of reconstitution) and immunological characteristics (content of memory T cells), which directly affect engraftment success, GVHD risk, and susceptibility to infections.

临床上通常要求CD34阳性细胞剂量至少达到 3 x 10^6 / kg 体重，低于这一剂量会显著增加植入失败和长期免疫重建不良的风险。

Clinically, the CD34‑positive cell dose is usually required to be at least 3 x 10^6 per kilogram of body weight; doses below this threshold markedly increase the risk of graft failure and poor long‑term immune reconstitution.

五、供者匹配
基于HLA与遗传背景的分型 Donor matching: based on HLA and overall genetic background

供者按照HLA及整体遗传相容程度可分为几类，从免疫学最理想到风险最高大致排序如下。

Donors can be classified according to HLA and overall genetic compatibility, roughly ordered from immunologically ideal to highest risk.

基因完全相同且有血缘关系的供者
同卵双生 Genetically identical and related donors: identical twins

同卵双生子之间在HLA和其他基因上几乎完全相同，是理想但极其少见的供者类型。

Identical twins share virtually the same HLA and other genes and represent an ideal but very rare donor category.

2
HLA基因上完全相同且有血缘关系的供者 HLA‑identical and related donors

来自同一家族、HLA 10/10完全相同的兄弟姐妹等，属于“基因上高度相容又有血缘”的供者，是造血干细胞移植中最常追求的类型。

These are family members such as siblings with a complete 10/10 HLA match, representing “highly compatible genetically and related” donors and being the most sought‑after type in HSCT.

3
表型完全相同但无血缘关系的供者 Phenotypically identical but unrelated donors

通过国际骨髓库数据库寻找的“10/10 HLA匹配”的非亲属志愿者，HLA层面高度相容，但其余基因背景差异较大，因此仍然存在显著的同种异基因免疫反应风险。

These are unrelated volunteer donors from international registries who are 10/10 HLA‑matched; they are highly compatible at the HLA level but differ more widely in the rest of the genome, so significant allogeneic immune responses remain possible.

4
半相合同源供者（haploidentical） Haploidentical related donors

如父母、子女或部分兄弟姐妹，只与受者共享约一半的HLA等位基因（约5/10匹配），既是重要的供者来源，又伴随较高的排斥和GVHD风险。

Examples include parents, children, or some siblings who share about half of the HLA alleles (roughly 5/10 match); they constitute an important donor pool but carry higher risks of rejection and GVHD.

5
无关脐带血供者 Unrelated cord blood donors

来自脐带血库的供者要求至少达到5/6 HLA匹配即可使用，因为其免疫系统较“幼稚”，可以在一定程度上容忍HLA不完全相合。

Cord blood units from registries are accepted with at least a 5/6 HLA match because their immune systems are “naïve” and can tolerate some HLA mismatch.

老师强调，第3类和第4类供者同种异基因免疫反应风险最高；总体原则是：HLA不相合越少，排斥和GVHD风险越低。

The lecturer stressed that donor categories 3 and 4 carry the highest risk of allogeneic immune reactions; in general, the fewer HLA mismatches, the lower the risks of rejection and GVHD.

六、造血干细胞移植的管理步骤 Steps for managing an HSCT

移植前4周
评估与预处理 Pre‑transplant (about 4 weeks): assessment and conditioning

首先对受者和供者进行全面临床评估，包括基础疾病状态、器官功能、感染危险和HLA匹配情况。

The first step is a comprehensive clinical assessment of both recipient and donor, including underlying disease status, organ function, infectious risks, and HLA matching.

随后进行“预处理（conditioning）”，通过大剂量化疗和/或放疗摧毁受者原有造血和免疫系统，同时在骨髓中“腾出空间”以利新移植物定植，并帮助控制原发恶性疾病。

This is followed by “conditioning,” using high‑dose chemotherapy and/or radiotherapy to destroy the recipient’s original hematopoietic and immune system, create space in the bone marrow for engraftment of the graft, and help control the underlying malignancy.

移植期约6周
输注与极度易感阶段 Transplant phase (around 6 weeks): infusion and highly vulnerable period

造血干细胞通过静脉输注进入体内，本质上是一种“血液输注”，通常被称为“移植日day 0”。

Hematopoietic stem cells are infused intravenously, essentially as a “blood transfusion,” and this day is typically referred to as “day 0.”

此后约3周内患者需隔离在无菌病房，因为此时处于骨髓极度抑制或再生障碍状态（aplastic phase），几乎没有白细胞，对细菌、病毒和真菌高度易感，也是预处理急性毒性反应集中的时期。

For about three weeks afterwards the patient is isolated in a sterile room because they are in a phase of severe bone‑marrow aplasia, with almost no white blood cells and extreme susceptibility to bacterial, viral, and fungal infections; this is also when acute toxicities of conditioning are most pronounced.

随着供者造血干细胞开始植入，逐渐出现白细胞恢复和免疫系统重建的早期迹象。

As donor stem cells begin to engraft, early signs of white blood cell recovery and immune system reconstitution gradually appear.

移植后第一年
密切随访与持续免疫抑制 Post‑transplant first year: close follow‑up and ongoing immunosuppression

在移植后第一年，患者需要每周或每月门诊随访，监测血象、免疫重建、感染和GVHD信号。

During the first post‑transplant year, patients require weekly or monthly follow‑up visits to monitor blood counts, immune reconstitution, infections, and signs of GVHD.

此阶段患者持续接受免疫抑制药物（主要用于预防和治疗GVHD）以及广谱抗生素/抗真菌药物等的预防用药，同时其免疫系统在缓慢、逐步地重建。

In this period, patients remain on immunosuppressive drugs, mainly to prevent and treat GVHD, as well as prophylactic broad‑spectrum antibiotics and antifungals, while their immune system is slowly and progressively reconstituted.

GVHD的风险在这一年内始终存在，需要通过临床症状和实验室指标反复评估。

The risk of GVHD persists throughout this first year and must be repeatedly assessed using clinical manifestations and laboratory parameters.

远期随访
疫苗接种与减停免疫抑制 Long‑term follow‑up: vaccinations and tapering immunosuppression

进入长期随访阶段后（每年随访1–2次），如果病情稳定，可逐步减停环孢素等免疫抑制药物，一般在移植后1–2年内尝试完全停药。

In the long‑term follow‑up phase (with one or two visits per year), if the patient remains stable, immunosuppressive drugs such as cyclosporine can be gradually tapered, with complete discontinuation usually attempted one to two years post‑transplant.

由于新的免疫系统是“重新长成”的，所有儿童期疫苗接种计划需要重新开始，包括破伤风、小儿麻痹、肺炎球菌、流感等疫苗。

Because the new immune system has essentially “grown from scratch,” the entire childhood vaccination schedule must be repeated, including tetanus, polio, pneumococcal, influenza, and other vaccines.

在儿童和青少年中，还需长期评估对生长、青春期发育和生育力的远期影响。

In children and adolescents, long‑term effects on growth, puberty, and fertility also need to be monitored.

七、HSCT后的免疫重建时间线 Immune reconstitution after HSCT: timeline

1
从预处理到粒细胞恢复 From conditioning to neutrophil recovery

预处理一般在移植前5–10天完成；day 0为造血干细胞输注日。

Conditioning usually ends 5–10 days before transplantation, and day 0 is the day of HSC infusion.

在自体移植时，约day 12–15可见中性粒细胞恢复；在同种异基因移植中，中性粒细胞恢复多发生在移植后约20天起，持续数天到数周。

In autologous transplants, neutrophil recovery is typically seen around days 12–15, whereas in allogeneic transplants it usually begins around day 20 and can take several days to weeks.

临床上将白细胞计数连续3天达到大于500 / 微升（即0.5 x 10^9 / 升）视为“摆脱再生障碍（recovery from aplasia）”和移植物基本植入成功的标志。

Clinically, a white blood cell count exceeding 500 per microliter (0.5 x 10^9 per liter) for three consecutive days is taken as evidence of “recovery from aplasia” and basic engraftment of the graft.

2
先天免疫与后天免疫的重建 Rebuilding innate and adaptive immunity

在粒细胞恢复后，数周到数月内会逐步重建NK细胞等先天淋巴细胞群。

After neutrophil recovery, innate lymphocyte populations such as NK cells are reconstituted over the following weeks to months.

真正完整的B细胞和T细胞库需要数月到数年才能完全建立，在此期间患者一直处于不同程度的免疫缺陷状态。

A fully diverse B‑cell and T‑cell repertoire takes months to years to develop, and during this time the patient remains in a state of varying degrees of immunodeficiency.

同种异基因移植后存在“嵌合体（chimerism）”概念，即受者血液和免疫细胞可以完全或部分由供者造血干细胞来源，需要通过基因或分子检测加以监测。

After allogeneic transplantation, the concept of “chimerism” applies, meaning that the recipient’s blood and immune cells are fully or partly derived from donor HSCs, which is monitored using genetic or molecular assays.

八、免疫重建与免疫耐受
为何最终可以停用免疫抑制 Reconstitution and immune tolerance: why immunosuppression can ultimately be stopped

新的T细胞来自供者HSC，在受者的胸腺中经历发育和“教育”，与正常个体一样接受负选择和正选择。

New T cells arise from donor HSCs but undergo development and “education” in the recipient’s thymus, undergoing negative and positive selection just as in any normal individual.

这些新生的初始T细胞只对“受者自身抗原+自身HLA”保持低亲和力耐受，因此对受者的正常组织是耐受的，相当于一个“重新对自身耐受”的新免疫系统。

These newly generated naïve T cells are tolerant to “recipient self antigens plus self HLA,” so they do not attack the recipient’s normal tissues, effectively constituting a new immune system that is re‑tolerant to self.

正因为如此，在移植后1–2年，当新免疫系统基本重建并完成耐受建立时，就有可能逐步停用环孢素等免疫抑制剂，而不再像实体器官移植那样终生依赖免疫抑制。

For this reason, once the new immune system has largely reconstituted and established tolerance one to two years after transplantation, cyclosporine and other immunosuppressants can often be gradually discontinued, unlike solid organ transplants that usually require lifelong immunosuppression.

需要注意的是，输注时一并进入的成熟记忆T细胞并不会经历胸腺再教育，它们可能参与早期的GVHD反应，因此如何在保留有益效应（如抗肿瘤）的同时控制GVHD，是当前研究的重要方向。

It should be noted that mature memory T cells infused with the graft do not undergo thymic re‑education and may drive early GVHD; balancing their beneficial effects, such as anti‑tumor activity, against the risk of GVHD is a major current research focus.

九、HSCT后的主要并发症
感染与GVHD Main complications after HSCT: infections and GVHD
1
感染性并发症 Infectious complications

由于预处理造成的骨髓再生障碍和免疫系统重建不完全，再加上免疫抑制药物的使用，患者极易发生机会性感染。

Because of bone‑marrow aplasia induced by conditioning, incomplete immune reconstitution, and the use of immunosuppressive drugs, patients are highly prone to opportunistic infections.

常见病原包括巨细胞病毒CMV、EB病毒、肺孢子菌以及多种真菌，这些感染往往症状非典型、病情凶险、进展迅速。

Common pathogens include cytomegalovirus (CMV), Epstein–Barr virus (EBV), Pneumocystis, and various fungi; these infections often present atypically, are severe, and progress rapidly.

预防措施包括
严格的病房隔离、广谱抗菌/抗真菌预防用药，以及频繁监测以便尽早发现和处理感染。

Preventive measures include strict isolation, extensive antimicrobial and antifungal prophylaxis, and close monitoring to detect and treat infections as early as possible.

免疫学并发症
排斥与移植物抗宿主病 Immunological complications: rejection and graft‑versus‑host disease

在造血干细胞移植中，“经典意义上的排斥反应”相对少见，因为受者原有免疫系统在预处理过程中已被深度摧毁，但若预处理不足，仍可能出现对供者造血细胞的排斥。

In HSCT, classic rejection of the graft is relatively rare because the recipient’s original immune system has been profoundly destroyed by conditioning, although inadequate conditioning can still lead to rejection of donor hematopoietic cells.

更加常见也更具挑战的是移植物抗宿主病（graft versus host disease，GVHD）：是随移植物一同输注的免疫健全T淋巴细胞攻击受者组织，尤其是各种上皮组织。

Far more common and challenging is graft‑versus‑host disease (GVHD), caused by immunocompetent T lymphocytes transferred with the graft that attack the recipient’s tissues, particularly various epithelia.

GVHD可分为急性和慢性两种形式
急性GVHD多发生在移植后最初几个月，主要表现为皮疹、消化道症状和肝功能异常；慢性GVHD则呈进行性纤维化，临床上类似硬皮病等自身免疫性疾病。

GVHD occurs in acute and chronic forms: acute GVHD usually arises in the first months after transplantation, presenting mainly as skin rash, gastrointestinal symptoms, and liver dysfunction, while chronic GVHD is characterized by progressive fibrosis and clinically resembles autoimmune diseases such as scleroderma.

GVHD的预防和一线治疗都依赖免疫抑制剂，尤其是糖皮质激素，同时配合钙调神经磷酸酶抑制剂等其他药物。

Prevention and first‑line treatment of GVHD rely on immunosuppressive agents, particularly corticosteroids, often combined with calcineurin inhibitors and other drugs.

老师指出，在造血干细胞移植领域，GVHD仍然是核心难题之一，如何在维持抗肿瘤“移植物抗白血病效应”的同时降低GVHD，是临床和研究共同关注的重点。

The lecturer pointed out that GVHD remains one of the central challenges in HSCT, and that a major clinical and research focus is reducing GVHD while preserving the graft‑versus‑leukemia effect against malignant cells.
